### `.env.example`  ·  22 lines

````bash
# ── Firebase Web SDK (client) ────────────────────────────────────────────────
# Project settings → General → Your apps → Web app → SDK setup and configuration
NEXT_PUBLIC_FIREBASE_API_KEY=
NEXT_PUBLIC_FIREBASE_AUTH_DOMAIN=
NEXT_PUBLIC_FIREBASE_PROJECT_ID=
NEXT_PUBLIC_FIREBASE_MESSAGING_SENDER_ID=
NEXT_PUBLIC_FIREBASE_APP_ID=

# 1 = use Firebase Local Emulators and disable IndexedDB persistence (dev only)
NEXT_PUBLIC_USE_EMULATORS=0

# ── Server only (never prefix with NEXT_PUBLIC_) ─────────────────────────────
# Base64 of a service-account JSON, used by firebase-admin in /api/ai/*
FIREBASE_SERVICE_ACCOUNT=
GEMINI_API_KEY=
GEMINI_MODEL=gemini-3.5-flash-lite
AI_DAILY_LIMIT=100
AI_ENABLED=true

# Optional Groq fallback (OpenAI-compatible, tool calling). Leave empty to disable.
GROQ_API_KEY=
GROQ_MODEL=openai/gpt-oss-120b
````

### `README.md`  ·  127 lines

````markdown
# Tutor Manager (مدير المدرس)

Mobile-first, offline-first PWA for **one** private tutor (≈9–15 students, 4–7 groups): weekly schedule, fast attendance, per-student session counters, cash payments, dues, statistics and an AI assistant (text + voice).

- **Stack:** Next.js 16 (App Router, TypeScript) · Tailwind CSS v4 · Firebase Auth + Firestore · Vercel · Gemini
- **Status:** Stages 0–10 implemented; awaiting Firebase Firestore API enablement, Vercel deploy and the real-week phone trial before tagging `v1.0`. See `~/.opencode/plan/tutor-manager-pwa-stages.md` for the full staged plan.
- **Spec:** `masterPlan.md` (current), `Tutor Manager (مدير المدرس) — Complete Build Plan.md` (superseded Flutter spec, kept for screen details).

## Setup

```bash
npm install
cp .env.example .env.local   # fill in the Firebase web config
npm run dev                  # http://localhost:3000
```

### Firebase project (one-time)

1. Create a Firebase project, add a **Web app**, copy the config into `.env.local`.
2. Authentication → Sign-in method → enable **Email/Password** (only provider in v1).
3. Authentication → Settings → Authorized domains → add `localhost` and your Vercel domain.
4. Firestore → create the database (production mode).
5. Deploy rules + indexes:

```bash
firebase use --add           # select your project
firebase deploy --only firestore:rules,firestore:indexes
```

### Local emulators

Needs **JDK 21+** (firebase-tools refuses older Java):

```bash
npm run emulators            # Auth :9099, Firestore :8080, UI :4000
# in .env.local: NEXT_PUBLIC_USE_EMULATORS=1
npm run dev
```

`npm run test:emulator` boots the emulators and runs the Firestore rules tests
against `firestore.rules`.

### AI assistant (Stage 9)

Server-only keys in `.env.local`:

```bash
FIREBASE_SERVICE_ACCOUNT=<base64 service-account JSON>
GEMINI_API_KEY=<key>
GEMINI_MODEL=gemini-3.5-flash-lite   # fast free model (larger free quota)
AI_DAILY_LIMIT=100
AI_ENABLED=true              # false hides the assistant button

# Optional text-only fallback — leave empty to disable
GROQ_API_KEY=
GROQ_MODEL=openai/gpt-oss-120b
```

The route is `POST /api/ai/assistant`. It verifies a Firebase ID token, enforces
a per-tutor daily cap, answers only from read tools, and returns **proposals**
that the UI applies only after you confirm. Voice uses direct inline audio where
Gemini accepts the MIME type, and falls back to `ar-EG` Web Speech → text
otherwise; see `docs/spikes.md` for the Spike B verdict table. Provider ladder:
Gemini primary → the other Gemini model → Groq (text only, when `GROQ_API_KEY`
is set). The hydrated Firestore state is loaded once per request and shared by
all tool calls.

`NEXT_PUBLIC_USE_EMULATORS=1` also disables IndexedDB persistence (emulator data is ephemeral, and stale cache there causes confusing bugs). Offline behaviour is verified against a real project, not the emulator.

## Scripts

| Command | What it does |
| --- | --- |
| `npm run dev` | Dev server |
| `npm run build` | Production build (Turbopack) |
| `npm start` | Serve the production build |
| `npm run lint` | ESLint |
| `npm run typecheck` | `tsc --noEmit` |
| `npm test` | Domain unit tests (Vitest) |
| `npm run test:watch` | Domain tests in watch mode |
| `npm run emulators` | Auth + Firestore emulators |
| `npm run test:emulator` | Firestore rules tests against the emulator |

## Architecture (short version)

```
src/
  app/            routes: (auth) sign-in/onboarding, (app) today/schedule/students/
                  groups/attendance/dues/stats/settings/dev, api/ai (Stage 9)
  components/     ui/ design-system kit, features/ cross-screen pieces
  domain/         PURE TypeScript: dateKey, sessionId, money, counters,
                  balanceCalculator, groupPosition  ← Stage 1
  data/           firebase client, auth, TutorDataProvider, mappers, services
  i18n/           ar (source of truth) + en, typed, RTL provider
```

Rules that must not break:

1. `domain/` imports nothing from React, Next, Firebase or the UI.
2. Screens never touch `firebase/firestore`; only `TutorDataProvider` reads and `data/services/*` writes.
3. `counterService` is the **only** writer of `sessionsCounted` / `sessionsPaid`.
4. Writes use `writeBatch` (offline-safe). **Never** `runTransaction` (requires network).
5. Dates are `yyyy-MM-dd` keys in `Africa/Cairo`. Never `toISOString().slice(0, 10)`.
6. Money is whole integers; digits are Latin (`123`) with `tabular-nums`.
7. All user-facing copy lives in `src/i18n`.

### Offline model

`TutorDataProvider` subscribes to six tiny collections (settings, groups, students, sessions, attendance, payments). Firestore's IndexedDB cache hydrates the app on reload and queues batched writes while offline; the UI renders computed balances (not cached counters), so it is correct instantly. Cached counters are reconciled on ready/reconnect.

### Next 16 + PWA note

Next 16 builds with Turbopack by default, but `@serwist/next` needs webpack.
`npm run build` therefore uses `next build --webpack`, and `postcss.config.mjs`
keeps Tailwind v4 working in webpack builds. The service worker is generated at
`public/sw.js`, precaches the app shell and `/offline`, and keeps `/api/*` and
Firebase endpoints network-only. Dev stays on Turbopack with Serwist disabled.

## Docs

- `docs/PROJECT_GUIDE.md` — **الدليل الشامل**: كل طبقة وكل ملف وإزاي بيشتغل
- `docs/versions.md` — pinned versions, environment notes (JDK 21 for emulators)
- `docs/spikes.md` — offline + Gemini-audio verification checklists
- `docs/manual-tests.md` — device test checklist
- `docs/release-checklist.md` — Firebase/Vercel deploy + real-week trial steps
- `scripts/gemini-audio-probe.mjs` — one-shot MIME probe for Spike B
- `/dev` and `/dev/style` — development-only seed tools and the design gallery
````

### `Tutor Manager (مدير المدرس) — Complete Build Plan.md`  ·  344 lines

````markdown
# Tutor Manager (مدير المدرس) — Complete Build Plan

*Version 1.0 · For vibe coding · Flutter + Firebase · Part A: English · Part B: العربية (further down)*

---

# PART A — ENGLISH

## 1. Overview

A mobile app for ONE private tutor (science, about 9–15 students, 4–7 groups) to run his teaching: weekly schedule, fast attendance after each lesson, a session counter per student, payment tracking (cash, usually after every 4 sessions), dues, statistics, and an AI assistant (text + voice) that answers questions and records attendance from speech.

Built first for a tutor the developer knows. It may be offered to other tutors later, so keep the code clean, but do not build multi-tutor features now.

**Goals**

- Replace the notebook's calculations, not the notebook itself. Recording a lesson must take under 30 seconds.
- The tutor never forgets who owes money.
- Calm, simple, beautiful UI.
- Works offline, syncs through Firebase so he can open it on a second phone.

**Non-goals for v1**

- Parent or student accounts, online payments, automatic messages to parents, multi-tutor or centre accounts, homework or grades, video lessons.

## 2. Core business rules (source of truth)

These rules decide everything. Implement them as a pure Dart class (`BalanceCalculator`) with unit tests, with no Flutter or Firebase imports.

1. **Cycle length L = 4 sessions** (a setting, default 4). One lesson per week, so a cycle is about a month.
2. **The counter belongs to the student, not the group.** A student who joins late starts at 0. A student who moves to another group keeps his counters and payment status.
3. **A session counts for a student** when he is present, or when he is absent and the setting `absenceCounts` is true. Default is true (absence counts); the tutor can change it in Settings. Cancelled sessions never count. Extra sessions (rare, exam periods) count like regular ones.
4. **Payments** are cash, full amount, normally after the student finishes 4 sessions, sometimes earlier. Each payment stores the amount actually paid, the date, `sessionsCovered` (default 4) and an optional note.
5. **Price is per student.** Some students pay less. Expected amount per cycle = `pricePerSession × L`. A payment stores the real amount, so old records never change when the price changes later.
6. **balance = sessionsCounted − sessionsPaid**
   - `balance >= L` → **DUE**. `dueCycles = floor(balance / L)`. Amount due = `dueCycles × L × pricePerSession`.
   - `0 < balance < L` → in progress, not due yet.
   - `balance == 0` → settled.
   - `balance < 0` → **paid ahead** by `abs(balance)` sessions. Such a student must never appear as unpaid when he reaches session 4.
7. **cyclePosition** = `counted == 0 ? 0 : ((counted − 1) mod L) + 1`, shown as 'session 3 of 4'. It is based on counted sessions, not on payments, so a student who paid early at session 3 still shows '3 of 4 · paid ✓'.
8. **Group position** = the most common cyclePosition among its active students with counted > 0 (tie → the higher one). Students whose position differs are flagged, for example 'Ahmed 2/4'.
9. `sessionsCounted` and `sessionsPaid` are cached fields on the student. The source of truth is attendance + payments + settings. After any edit, call `recomputeStudent(studentId)`.
10. Past attendance can be edited; counters are recomputed afterwards.
11. Makeup for a missed lesson is informal (explained in the next lesson). Do not model it.

## 3. Tech stack and architecture

- **Flutter** (stable), Material 3 as the base, fully custom theme. Arabic (RTL) is the default UI language, English is available.
- **State:** `flutter_riverpod`. **Routing:** `go_router`.
- **Firebase:** Auth (Google sign-in + email), Cloud Firestore with offline persistence enabled, Cloud Functions (callable) for the AI.
- **Packages:** `firebase_core`, `firebase_auth`, `cloud_firestore`, `cloud_functions`, `google_sign_in`, `intl`, `google_fonts` (Cairo), `speech_to_text` (on-device, locale `ar_EG`), `fl_chart`, `flutter_localizations`.
- **AI:** a callable Cloud Function calls a Claude model through the Anthropic API. The API key lives in Firebase Secret Manager and never inside the app. The model name is an environment variable.

**Folder structure**

```
lib/
  core/        theme/, widgets/ (glass_card, status_chip...), utils/, l10n/
  domain/      balance_calculator.dart (pure Dart), models/
  data/        repositories/ (groups, students, sessions, attendance, payments, settings)
  features/    auth/, today/, schedule/, attendance/, groups/, students/,
               payments/, dues/, stats/, ai/, settings/
  app.dart, main.dart
functions/     index.ts (aiAssistant), tools.ts
```

**Rules for the codebase**

- UI never talks to Firestore directly; only repositories do.
- All user-facing strings go through l10n (ar + en).
- All money is stored as integers (whole currency units). Currency label comes from Settings (default EGP / ج.م).
- Every list screen has loading, empty and error states.

## 4. Data model (Firestore)

Everything lives under `users/{uid}`.

```
users/{uid}
  settings: { absenceCounts: true, cycleLength: 4, language: 'ar',
              currency: 'EGP', weekStartsOn: 6 /* Saturday */ }

users/{uid}/groups/{groupId}
  name, subject?, slots: [{ weekday: 0-6, time: 'HH:mm', durationMin }],
  active: true, createdAt

users/{uid}/students/{studentId}
  name, phone? (parent), groupId, pricePerSession, startedAt,
  active: true, note?,
  sessionsCounted: 0, sessionsPaid: 0        // cached, see rule 9

users/{uid}/sessions/{groupId_yyyymmdd[_n]}
  groupId, date (timestamp), time, type: 'regular'|'extra',
  status: 'scheduled'|'completed'|'cancelled', note?

users/{uid}/attendance/{sessionId_studentId}
  sessionId, studentId, groupId (at that time), date,
  status: 'present'|'absent'

users/{uid}/payments/{paymentId}
  studentId, date, amount, sessionsCovered, note?
```

**Indexes:** attendance (studentId, date desc); sessions (date asc, groupId); payments (studentId, date desc).

**Security rules:** a signed-in user can read and write only documents under `users/{request.auth.uid}/`. Everything else is denied.

**Session generation:** sessions are created from group `slots`. On app start (and when a group's slots change), idempotently create sessions for the next 14 days using the deterministic id above, so running it twice never duplicates. Extra sessions get a `_n` suffix. The tutor can cancel any session or add an extra one for any group and date.

## 5. Screens

Navigation: bottom bar with 4 tabs (**Today, Schedule, Students, Stats**) plus **More** (Groups, Dues, Settings). A floating mic/sparkle button opens the AI assistant from any tab.

### S1. Splash and Sign-in

- Logo, 'Continue with Google' and email/password. First launch → short onboarding: create first group, add first students (skippable).
- States: loading, wrong password, no network (offline sign-in works if previously signed in).

### S2. Today (home)

- Header: greeting, today's date (Gregorian + weekday in Arabic).
- **Today's lessons**: card per session (group name, time, number of students, 'session 3/4' badge, status: scheduled / completed / cancelled). Tap → Attendance (S3).
- **Reminder card** 'Reaching session 4 today': students whose next counted session is their 4th and who are not paid ahead, listed per group (this is the reminder the tutor asked for: today is session 4 for X and Y, did they pay?).
- **Due now card**: number of students with `balance >= L` and total amount; tap → Dues (S8).
- Quick actions: add extra session, record payment.
- Empty state: 'No lessons today' with a calm illustration.

### S3. Attendance (the most important screen)

- Opened from a session card. Title: group, date, time, 'session 3/4' badge.
- List of the group's active students. **Everyone is Present by default.** Tapping a row toggles Absent (row turns soft red, light haptic). Each row shows name, cyclePosition ('2 of 4'), and a payment chip (Paid ahead / Due / —).
- Mic button: tutor says 'Ahmed and Hamada were absent'; names get highlighted as Absent; a confirm bar shows the result before saving.
- Buttons: **Save lesson** (primary), **Cancel lesson**, **Add note**.
- On save: write attendance docs, set session `completed`, recompute counters in one batch, then show a result sheet: 'Saved. 2 students reached a due payment' with a shortcut to record payment.
- Editable later: reopening a completed session shows saved states; editing triggers recompute.
- Edge cases: student joined after the session date (hidden), student inactive (hidden), working offline (saves locally, syncs later).

### S4. Schedule

- Toggle Day / Week. Week view is a 7-column or vertical list of days with lesson blocks (group colour, time). Day view lists lessons with status.
- Actions: add extra session, cancel a session, jump to date.

### S5. Groups (More → Groups)

- **List:** name, student count, next lesson, group position badge ('session 3/4') and a small warning if some students differ.
- **Group detail:** schedule slots, student list with each student's position and payment state, flagged outliers, 'Add student', 'Edit', 'Archive'.
- **Group form:** name, weekly slots (weekday + time, multiple), subject (optional).

### S6. Students

- **List:** search, filter (All / Due / Paid ahead / Archived), grouped by group or flat. Row: name, group, position, status chip.
- **Student detail:** name, parent phone (tap to call or WhatsApp), group, price per session, start date; counter ring ('3 of 4'); balance and status; tabs: **Attendance history**, **Payments history**; buttons: Record payment, Move group, Edit, Archive.
- **Student form:** name, parent phone, group, price per session, start date, optional note. Starting counter field (default 0) for students who already have history.
- **Move group:** pick new group; counters stay; attendance history keeps old group ids.

### S7. Record payment (bottom sheet)

- Fields: student (prefilled), amount (prefilled = expected for dueCycles, or one cycle if none due), sessions covered (default 4), date (default today), note.
- Shows a live preview: 'After this payment: paid ahead 1 session' or 'Settled'.
- Warns (does not block) if amount differs from the expected amount.

### S8. Dues

- Sorted list of students with `balance >= L`: name, group, sessions completed since last payment, amount due, 'Record payment' button. Total at the top.
- Second section: 'Almost due' (balance = L−1) so the tutor can prepare.
- Row action: draft a polite reminder text for the parent (AI), copy it, open WhatsApp. Never sent automatically.

### S9. Statistics

- Month picker. Cards: **Collected**, **Outstanding** (sum of amounts due now), **Expected** this month, **Attendance rate**.
- Charts (`fl_chart`): income per month (last 6 months), attendance rate per group (bars).
- Lists: students with most absences, students most often late to pay (days between cycle end and payment), top groups by income.
- All numbers computed in plain Dart/Firestore queries, never by the AI.

### S10. AI assistant

- Bottom sheet or full screen chat. Input: text field + mic. Suggestion chips: 'Who hasn't paid?', 'Who was absent this week?', 'How much did I collect this month?', 'Who reaches session 4 this week?'.
- Two behaviours: **answer questions** (read-only) and **prepare actions** (attendance, payment, cancel session). An action is always shown as a confirmation card with Confirm / Edit / Cancel. Nothing is written without a tap.
- Details in section 6.

### S11. Settings

- Absence counts toward the cycle (switch, default ON), cycle length (default 4), currency label, week start day, language (Arabic / English), account (email, sign out), data export (CSV/JSON, v1.1), about.
- Changing 'absence counts' recomputes all students (show a confirm dialog).

## 6. AI design

**Principle:** the AI understands language; plain code does the maths and the writing to the database.

**Flow**

1. The app sends `{ text, locale, today, groups:[{id,name}], students:[{id,name,groupId}] }` to the callable function `aiAssistant`. The list is small (about 15 students), so it is cheap.
2. The function calls the model with a system prompt and a fixed set of **tools**. The model chooses a tool and arguments; it never invents numbers.
3. Read tools run in the function against Firestore (admin SDK, scoped to the caller's uid) and return JSON. The model phrases the answer from that JSON in the tutor's language.
4. Action tools do NOT write. They return a proposed action `{type, params}`; the app renders a confirmation card and performs the write through the normal repositories after the tutor taps Confirm.

**Tools**

- Read: `get_dues()`, `get_almost_due()`, `get_absences({studentId?, groupId?, from, to})`, `get_income({from, to})`, `get_group_status({groupId})`, `get_student_summary({studentId})`, `get_today_overview()`.
- Propose: `propose_attendance({groupId, date, absentStudentIds[]})`, `propose_payment({studentId, amount, sessionsCovered, date})`, `propose_cancel_session({groupId, date})`, `draft_parent_reminder({studentId})`.

**Name matching:** if a spoken name matches more than one student, return `needs_clarification` with the candidates and ask 'Which Ahmed?'. Never guess. If a name matches nobody, say so.

**System prompt (summary):** You are the assistant inside a private tutor's management app. Reply in the same language and dialect the tutor used (Egyptian Arabic by default), briefly. Use tools for every number and never calculate from memory. Never claim an action was done; you only propose it. If the request is ambiguous, ask one short question.

**Voice:** `speech_to_text` on device with locale `ar_EG` converts speech to text, then it follows the same path as typed text. Show the recognised text so the tutor can fix it before sending.

**Safety and cost:** rate-limit the function per user, cap input length, never send phone numbers to the model, log errors only.

## 7. Design system

**Mood:** calm, warm, quiet. Soft beige and sand, one muted sage accent, frosted-glass cards. It should feel like good stationery, not a tech product.

**Hard rules**

- No gradients, no purple/blue AI glow, no neon, no glowing orbs.
- Glass effect is subtle: translucent white cards with blur over a flat warm background.
- One accent colour, used sparingly.

**Background:** flat `#F3EDE3`. To give the glass something to blur, place 2–3 large solid blurred circles (sand `#E6DBC9`, sage `#CFDCD3`, clay `#EBD5C7`) at 40–60% opacity behind the content. Solid colours only.

| Token | Value | Use |
| --- | --- | --- |
| background | #F3EDE3 | app background |
| surface-glass | white 55% + blur 18 | cards, sheets |
| glass-border | white 70%, 1px | card outline |
| text | #2E2B27 | main text |
| text-muted | #7A7468 | secondary text |
| primary | #6B8F7B | buttons, active tab |
| primary-dark | #4F7261 | pressed, links |
| accent | #C58B6D | highlights, charts |
| success | #6B8F7B | present, paid |
| warning | #D8A24A | almost due |
| danger | #C45B52 | absent, due |
| info | #6F8FA6 | paid ahead |

Status chips use the same colours at 15% opacity as fill with the full colour as text.

**Typography:** Cairo (supports Arabic and Latin). Title 22/700, section 17/600, body 15/400, caption 12.5/400. Numbers use tabular figures.

**Shape and space:** card radius 20, buttons 14, chips fully round. 4-pt grid (8 / 12 / 16 / 24). Minimum tap target 48 dp. Card shadow: 0 8 24 rgba(60,50,40,0.08).

**Components:** `GlassCard`, `StatusChip`, `CounterRing` (thin ring showing 3 of 4), `StudentRow`, `SessionCard`, `BigPrimaryButton`, `EmptyState` (single-colour line illustration), `ConfirmCard` (AI actions).

**Motion:** 200 ms ease-out, no bounce. Press scale 0.98. Light haptic when toggling attendance. Sheets slide up softly.

**Accessibility:** contrast at least 4.5:1 for text, never rely on colour alone (absent also shows an icon and the word), support system font scaling, full RTL mirroring.

**Dark mode:** v1.1 (deep warm brown `#1F1B17`, same accent).

## 8. Build plan (about 4 weeks)

**Week 1 — Foundation**

- Create the Flutter project, Firebase (Auth, Firestore, Functions), theme, l10n (ar/en, RTL), router, `GlassCard`.
- Sign-in, settings document, security rules.
- Groups and students: full CRUD with repositories.
- *Done when:* he can sign in, add 5 groups and 12 students, close and reopen the app offline and see them.

**Week 2 — Sessions and attendance**

- Session generation from slots, Schedule (day/week), cancel and extra session.
- `BalanceCalculator` + unit tests for every scenario in section 9.
- Attendance screen with default-present, save, recompute.
- *Done when:* all tests pass and a lesson can be recorded in under 30 seconds.

**Week 3 — Money and dashboard**

- Payments sheet, student detail histories, Dues screen.
- Today screen with reminder and due cards, group position badges and outlier flags.
- *Done when:* the early-payment and late-joiner cases behave exactly as in section 9.

**Week 4 — Stats, AI, polish**

- Statistics screen and charts.
- `aiAssistant` function, tools, chat UI, voice, confirmation cards.
- Empty/error states, haptics, app icon, splash, Android release build, internal test with the tutor.
- *Done when:* the tutor used it for one real week without opening the notebook for calculations.

**After v1:** dark mode, CSV/JSON export and backup, iOS release, yearly statistics, multi-tutor support if he decides to offer it to others.

## 9. Test scenarios (unit tests for BalanceCalculator)

Assume L = 4, price = 50, `absenceCounts = true` unless stated.

1. New student, 4 present sessions, no payment → counted 4, paid 0, balance 4 → DUE, amount 200, position 4.
2. Pays at session 3 (covers 4) → counted 3, paid 4, balance −1 → paid ahead 1, position 3 'paid ✓'. After session 4 → balance 0, settled. After session 8 → balance 4 → DUE.
3. Absent once, `absenceCounts = true` → absence counts as a session. With `false` → it does not; position and balance stay.
4. Group is at session 3; a student joined last week with counted 1 → group shows 3/4, student flagged '1/4'.
5. Student moves group at counted 3 → counters unchanged, next session in the new group is his 4th.
6. Student unpaid for 6 sessions → balance 6, dueCycles 1, amount 200, position 2. After paying 4 → balance 2, position 2, not due.
7. Discounted student price 40 → due amount 160.
8. Cancelled session → nobody's counter changes.
9. Extra session in exam week → counts for present students.
10. Edit an old absence to present → counters recomputed.
11. Offline: mark attendance, close app, reconnect → data synced, no duplicates.
12. Two students both named 'Ahmed' + voice command 'Ahmed was absent' → AI asks which one.

## 10. Master prompt for the vibe-coding tool

Copy this first, then paste the relevant section of this document with each step.

```
You are building 'Tutor Manager', a Flutter + Firebase app for ONE private
tutor (about 15 students, 4-7 groups). Read the whole spec I give you and
follow it exactly.

Rules:
- Business rules in section 2 are the source of truth. Put them in a pure
  Dart BalanceCalculator with unit tests for every scenario in section 9
  BEFORE building UI.
- Architecture: riverpod + go_router; UI never calls Firestore directly,
  only repositories do. Firestore offline persistence ON.
- Arabic RTL by default, all strings in l10n (ar + en). Font: Cairo.
- Design: follow section 7 exactly. No gradients, no purple/blue AI look.
  Warm beige background, frosted glass cards, one sage accent.
- Money = integers. Counters are cached on the student and recomputed from
  attendance + payments + settings after every change.
- Do not add features that are not in the spec. Ask me if something is unclear.
- Work one week of the build plan at a time. After each step, tell me what
  to test by hand.
```

**Suggested step prompts**

1. 'Do Week 1: project setup, theme, l10n, auth, groups and students CRUD.'
2. 'Do the BalanceCalculator and its unit tests only. Show me the test output.'
3. 'Do session generation, Schedule and the Attendance screen (S3).'
4. 'Do payments, student detail, Dues and Today screens.'
5. 'Do Statistics.'
6. 'Do the aiAssistant Cloud Function with the tools in section 6, then the chat UI with voice and confirmation cards.'
7. 'Polish: empty/error states, haptics, icon, splash, release build.'

## 11. Decisions to confirm with the tutor

- Does an absence count toward the 4 sessions? (default in the app: yes, changeable in Settings)
- Is the first lesson of a new student counted, or is there a free trial?
- What are the real per-student prices for the discounted students?
- Which weekday does his week start on, and what are the lesson times for each group?
- Does he want to enter old data (students already mid-cycle)? The student form has a starting counter for this.

---

*End of Part A. Part B (العربية) follows.*
````

### `docs/PROJECT_GUIDE.md`  ·  409 lines

````markdown
# دليل مشروع Tutor Manager (مدير المدرس) — شرح كامل

> هذا الملف هو المرجع الواحد لكل حاجة في المشروع: إيه اللي بيحصل، فين، بإيه، وليه اتعمل كده.
> آخر تحديث: 26 نوفمبر 2026 — بعد كل Stage 0–10 + تحسينات AI/Vercel.

---

## 1) المشروع في سطرين

تطبيق PWA ** RTL بالعربية المصرية** لمدرس خاص واحد (9–15 طالب، 4–7 مجموعات).
يغطي: الحصص، الحضور، عداد الحصص لكل طالب، الدفعات، المستحقات، الإحصائيات، ومساعد ذكي بالصوت/النص.

**الهدف:** المدرس يفتح التطبيق الصبح يلاقي: حصص النهاردة + مين قرب يدفع + مين عليه فلوس، من غير ما يفتح دفتر ورقي.

---

## 2) الحقائق السريعة

| حاجة | القيمة |
|---|---|
| Live URL | `https://tutor-organizer.vercel.app` |
| GitHub | `https://github.com/Mohamed0-0Tarek/TutorOrganizer` |
| Firebase project | `tutororganizer` |
| Firestore location | `me-central1` (Doha]— أقرب منطقة لمصر) |
| Framework | Next.js 16.4.6 — App Router + TypeScript |
| React | 19.3.0 |
| Tailwind | 4.3.3 (Turbopack في dev / PostCSS في build) |
| Firebase client | 13.4.0 |
| Firebase Admin | 14.5.1 (Firestore فقط — لا نستورد `auth` منه، انظر §13) |
| AI SDK | `@google/genai` 3.6.1 + `jose` 6.2.18 للتحقق من التوكن |
| Charts | `recharts` 3.11.1 |
| Icons | `lucide-react` 1.79.0 |
| Validation | `zod` 4.6.7 |
| PWA | `@serwist/next` + `serwist` 9.5.15 |
| Tests | `vitest` 5.1.1 + `@firebase/rules-unit-testing` 6 |
| Node | `24.x` (مثبّت في `engines`) |

### الأوامر

```bash
npm run dev            # Turbopack — Serwist متوقف في التطوير
npm run build          # next build --webpack  (لازم webpack عشان الـ service worker)
npm start              # تشغيل نسخة الإنتاج محليًا
npm run typecheck      # tsc --noEmit
npm run lint           # eslint
npm test               # 88 unit tests
npm run test:emulator  # 20 test (قواعد + تكامل) على emulators
npm run emulators      # Auth :9099 + Firestore :8080 + UI :4000
```

---

## 3) القواعد الذهبية (لو كسرتها هتقع في مشاكل)

1. `src/domain/**` **خالص TypeScript**: مفيش React، Next، Firebase، ولا متصفح. كل الدوال قابلة للاختبار لوحدها.
2. **الشاشات ما بتكتبش Firestore مباشرةً.** القراءة كلها في `TutorDataProvider`، والكتابة كلها في `src/data/services/*`.
3. `counterService` هو **الوحيد** اللي بيكتب `sessionsCounted` / `sessionsPaid`.
4. كل الكتابات بـ `writeBatch` (آمنة للـ offline). **ممنوع `runTransaction`** — محتاج نت ومعندناش.
5. التواريخ كلها `yyyy-MM-dd` في **Africa/Cairo**. ممنوع `toISOString().slice(0,10)`.
6. الفلوس **integers** دايمًا (ج.م)، والأرقام بتتعرض بـ Latin digits (123) مع `tabular-nums`.
7. **كل كلمة بيشوفها المستخدم** في `src/i18n/ar.ts` + `en.ts`. مفيش نصوص hardcoded في الشاشات.
8. المساعد AI **ما بيكتبش أبدًا من غير بطاقة اقتراح + ضغط «تنفيذ»**.

---

## 4) هيكل المجلدات

```
src/
  app/                 #Routes (App Router)
    (auth)/            # sign-in, onboarding — بدون bottom bar
    (app)/             # اليوم، الجدول، الطلاب، المجموعات، الحضور، المستحقات، الإحصائيات، الإعدادات، dev
    api/ai/assistant/  # الـ route الخلفي للمساعد (Node runtime)
    offline/           # صفحة fallback للـ service worker
    manifest.ts        # PWA manifest
    sw.ts              # مصدر الـ service worker (Serwist)
    globals.css        # الـ design tokens + وصفة الـ glass
    layout.tsx         # الـ root: Cairo font + providers
    error.tsx          # Error boundary عام
  components/
    ui/                # مكتبة القطع المشتركة (glass card, chips, sheet...)
    features/          # قطع خاصة: forms, BalanceChip, PaymentSheet, AiSheet...
  domain/              # المنطق الخالص (لا تبعيات)
  data/
    firebase/          # client (lazy) + admin (server)
    ai/                # tools, providers, schema — المساعد
    auth/              # AuthProvider
    provider/          # TutorDataProvider + hooks
    services/          # كل الكتابات على Firestore
    mappers.ts         # Firestore -> domain types
    paths.ts           # كل مساراتFirestore في مكان واحد
  i18n/                # ar/en + provider + locale store
  lib/                 # helper صغيرة (cn, vibrate, weekdays, groupColor...)
test/
  domain/              # unit tests للمنطق الخالص
  ai/                  # schema + privacy tests
  integration/         # emulator tests (CRUD, sessions, attendance, payments, AI)
  emulator/            # firestore.rules tests
docs/                  # spikes, manual-tests, versions, release-checklist, هذا الملف
scripts/               # gemini-audio-probe.mjs
firebase.json / firestore.rules / firestore.indexes.json
next.config.ts / postcss.config.mjs / vitest*.mts / .env.example
```

---

## 5) طبقة `domain/` — كل دالة وظيفتها

| الملف | بيعمل إيه بالظبط |
|---|---|
| `types.ts` | كل الـ interfaces: `Group`, `Student`, `Session`, `Attendance`, `Payment`, `TutorSettings`, `StudentBalance`. فيه `DEFAULT_SETTINGS`: `absenceCounts=true`, `cycleLength=4`, `weekStartsOn=6` (السبت)، العملة EGP/ج.م. |
| `dateKey.ts` | `dateKeyOf`, `todayKey`, `addDaysKey`, `weekdayOfKey`, `formatArabicDate`, `formatTime`. كل الحسابات على UTC كـ `yyyy-MM-dd` عشان Cairo والـ DST ما يbosوا الحساب. |
| `sessionId.ts` | ID حتمي: `{groupId}_{yyyyMMdd}` للحصة العادية، `_{n}` للإضافية. `attendanceId = {sessionId}_{studentId}`. `nextExtraSessionNumber` بيرجع **أصغر رقم حر ≥2**. الـ deterministic ID هو اللي بيمنع التكرار offline. |
| `money.ts` | `expectedCycleAmount(price,L) = price × L`، `formatMoney` بالـ en-US digits، assertions إن المبلغ integer موجب. |
| `counters.ts` | **المرجع الوحيد** لحساب `counted` و`paid`: `initial + attendance(completed && >= startedAtKey && present أو absent مع absenceCounts) - dedupe`, و`paid = initialPaid + Σ sessionsCovered`. |
| `balanceCalculator.ts` | `balance = counted - paid` → DUE / IN_PROGRESS / SETTLED / PAID_AHEAD. `cyclePosition` من الـ counted فقط. `needsReminder` = «الحصة الجاية هي رقم 4/8/…» مع استبعاد PaidAhead و DUE. |
| `groupPosition.ts` | موقف المجموعة = أكثر `cyclePosition` تكرار بين الطلاب النشطين (التعادل ← الأعلى)، والباقي `outliers` بأسمائهم. |
| `sessionGenerator.ts` | `GENERATION_HORIZON_DAYS = 14`. يخطط الحصص من slots المجموعات النشطة: ينشئ اللي مفقود، يحدّث time للحصص المستقبلية المجدولة بس، **ما يرجّعش ملغية ولا يلمس الإضافية**. |
| `stats.ts` | `collectedInMonth`, `outstandingNow`, `expectedForMonth`, `attendanceRate`, `incomeByMonth(6)`, `attendanceByGroup`, `topAbsences`, `slowestPayers`, `topGroupsByIncome`. كلها pure. |
| `validation.ts` | تحليل أسماء، وقت `HH:mm`، مدة 15–180، فلوس صحيحة، عدادات، تاريخ، تليفون. بترجع `{ok:true,value}` أو `{ok:false,error}` والـ error key بيتترجم في i18n. |
| `aiSanitize.ts` | `redactPhones` / `redactStrings`: بيشيل أي رقم تليفون من كل اللي بيتبعت لـ Gemini/Groq. الأرقام القصيرة والتواريخ والـ IDs ما بتتلمسش. |

---

## 6) طبقة `data/` — القراءة والكتابة

### 6.1 `firebase/client.ts`

Singleton كسول ( lazy ) لأن `next build` لازم يشتغل من غير env. لو `NEXT_PUBLIC_USE_EMULATORS=1`:
- بيربط Auth بـ `127.0.0.1:9099` و Firestore بـ `8080`
- بيستخدم `memoryLocalCache()` بدل `persistentLocalCache` (عشان الكاش القديم ما يبوظش تجارب الإيموليتر).

في الإنتاج: `persistentLocalCache({tabManager: persistentMultipleTabManager()})` → IndexedDB + مزامنة بين التبويبات.

### 6.2 `firebase/admin.ts`

Server-only. بيعمل `initializeApp` بـ service account من `FIREBASE_SERVICE_ACCOUNT` (base64 JSON). **متعمدًا ما بيستوردش `firebase-admin/auth`** لأنه على Vercel بيحمل `jwks-rsa → jose` بـ `require` CommonJS وبيكسر الـ route (`ERR_REQUIRE_ESM`). بدل كده `verifyTutorToken` بيعمل `await import('jose')` ويتحقق من الـ JWT مقابل Google JWKS (`securetoken@system.gserviceaccount.com`) مع `iss`/`aud` الصح. في الإيموليتر بيDecode الـ payload غير الموقّع فقط.

### 6.3 `TutorDataProvider.tsx`

**مصدر الحقيقة الوحيد في الواجهة.** 6 `onSnapshot` على:

```
users/{uid}                       -> settings
users/{uid}/groups
users/{uid}/students
users/{uid}/sessions
users/{uid}/attendance
users/{uid}/payments
```

بيعمل `markLoaded` / `markCache` لكل source، و`status` (`loading|ready|error`)، و`online` من أحداث `online/offline`. بيحسب `sessionsById` و`balances` بـ `useMemo`، وبيعمل:
- `createDefaults` مرة واحدة لو مستند الإعدادات مش موجود.
- `ensureGenerated` عند ready + focus + visibility + online.
- `reconcileCounters` على كل snapshot → self-heal للعدادات المخزنة.

الشاشات بتقرأ منه عبر `src/data/provider/hooks.ts`: `useTutorData`, `useStudentBalance`, `useGroupStatus`, `useDueStudents`, `useTodaySessions`, `useToday`.

### 6.4 الخدمات (services)

| ملف | مسؤوليته |
|---|---|
| `settingsService` | إنشاء/defaults + تحديث الإعدادات. |
| `groupService` | إنشاء/تعديل/أرشفة مجموعة (يمرّر patch عبر `definedFields` عشان `undefined` ما يبوظش `updateDoc`). |
| `studentService` | إنشاء/تعديل/نقل/أرشفة طالب. |
| `enrollmentService` | يلفّ عمليات الطالب ويستدعي `reconcileStudent` بعدها (العدادات تفضل متسقة). |
| `counterService` | **الكاتب الوحيد** للعدادات المخزنة؛ `persist` / `reconcile` / `reconcileStudent` بـ writeBatch. |
| `sessionGenerator` | `ensureGenerated` مع in-flight guard لكل uid (يمنع تكرار الـ batch مع كذا trigger). |
| `sessionService` | إلغاء حصة (ولو كان لها حضور بيحدّث العدادات في نفس الـ batch)، حصة إضافية، تعديل وقت/ملاحظة، حذف إضافية. |
| `attendanceService` | `saveLesson`: كل حضور + حالة الحصة + العدادات **في batch واحد**، وبيرجّع `newlyDue`. و`setAttendanceStatus` لتعديل حصة محفوظة. |
| `paymentService` | `record/update/remove` — كل واحد بيكتب الدفعة ويعيد حساب عداد الطالب في batch واحد. |
| `devSeedService` | بيانات تجريبية + تبديل غياب + إفساد العدادات (للـ self-heal) + مسح. `/dev` مخفي في الإنتاج. |
| `cleanPatch.ts` | يشيل مفاتيح `undefined` من أي patch قبل `updateDoc`. |

---

## 7) الـ routes (الشاشات)

### `(auth)` — بدون bottom bar
- `sign-in`: إيميل/كلمة سر تسجيل ودخول، mapping لأخطاء Firebase على كلمات عربية من i18n، عرض واضح لو Firebase مش متعداد.
- `onboarding`: خطوتين (مجموعة + طالب) مع تخطي.

### `(app)` — الـ shell
`layout.tsx` فيه AuthGuard + `TutorDataProvider` + `Backdrop` + `OfflineBar` + skeletons + `BottomBar` + More sheet + Floating AI button + PWA prompts.
كل شاشة ليها loading/empty/error.

| Route | الوظيفة |
|---|---|
| `/` (Today) | تحية حسب الوقت + تاريخ Cairo، حصص اليوم مع badge موقف المجموعة، كارت «وصلوا للرابعة»، كارت المستحقات، إجراءات سريعة. |
| `/schedule` | يوم/أسبوع، DateStepper، إلغاء حصة بملاحظة، إضافة حصة إضافية، رسوم بيانية لا — charts في Stats بس. |
| `/attendance/[sessionId]` | جدول الطلاب، اللمسة الواحدة لتغيير حاضر/غايب + vibration، ملاحظة، شريط حفظ ثابت، result sheet بالـ newly-due، تحذير قبل الخروج مع تغييرات مش محفوظة. |
| `/students` | بحث + 4 فلاتر (الكل/عليهم دفعات/مدفوع مقدمًا/مؤرشفين) + CountRing. |
| `/students/[id]` | الرصيد المحسوب لحظيًا، أزرار تليفون/واتساب، تعديل/نقل/أرشفة، سجل الحضور مع رقم الدورة، سجل الدفعات مع تعديل/حذف. |
| `/students/new`, `/students/[id]/edit` | نموذج الطالب + قسم «بيانات سابقة» (`initialCounted`, `initialPaid`) + اختيار المجموعة. |
| `/groups` | شارات الموقف + outliers بالاسم والوضع. |
| `/groups/[id]` | المواعيد، الطلاب، إضافة طالب بالمجموعة محفوظة، تعديل/أرشفة. |
| `/groups/new`, `/groups/[id]/edit` | محرر المواعيد (يوم + وقت + مدة) بإضافة/حذف. |
| `/dues` | إجمالي، قائمة المستحقة مرتبة بالمبلغ then الاسم، «قربوا يستحقوا»، تسجيل دفعة، نسخ رسالة/واتساب. |
| `/stats` | month stepper، 4 بطاقات، chart دخل 6 شهور، chart حضور لكل مجموعة، 3 قوائم (أكتر غياب/أبطأ دفع/أعلى دخل). |
| `/settings` | لغة، غياب يتحسب (تأكيد + reconcile)، عدد حصص الدورة 2–8 (تأكيد + reconcile)، العملة، بداية الأسبوع، الحساب، تنبيه backup. |
| `/dev` | أدوات تجريبية (فقط لو `NODE_ENV !== production`). |
| `/dev/style` | معرض التصميم (ألوان/بطاقات/StatusChips/CountRing). |
| `/offline` | صفحة fallback للـ service worker. |

---

## 8) موديل البيانات في Firestore

كل حاجة تحت `users/{uid}` — القواعد تسمح للمالك فقط:

```
users/{uid}                       { settings, createdAt, updatedAt }
users/{uid}/groups/{groupId}      { name, subject?, slots[], active, createdAt, updatedAt }
users/{uid}/students/{studentId}  { name, phone?, groupId, pricePerSession,
                                    startedAtKey, active, note?,
                                    initialCounted, initialPaid,
                                    sessionsCounted, sessionsPaid, createdAt, updatedAt }
users/{uid}/sessions/{sessionId}  { groupId, groupName, dateKey, time, type, status, note? }
users/{uid}/attendance/{id}       { sessionId, studentId, groupId, dateKey, status }
users/{uid}/payments/{id}         { studentId, dateKey, amount, sessionsCovered, note? }
users/{uid}/meta/aiUsage          { dateKey, count }   <- حد المساعد اليومي
```

**ملاحظات مهمة:**
- `groupId` في مستند الحضور **بيفضل زي ما هو بعد نقل الطالب**، عشان إحصائيات المجموعات التاريخية تفضل صحيحة.
- `sessionsCounted/Paid` مجرد cache للـ AI/التصدير؛ الواجهة بتحسب الرصيد من المستندات المصدرية كل مرة.
- `firestore.rules` تسمح `read, write` لو `request.auth.uid == uid` فقط، والباقي `false`.

---

## 9) محرك العدادات (قلب التطبيق)

```
counted = initialCounted
        + لكل attendance:
            لازم session.status == "completed"
            و session.dateKey >= student.startedAtKey
            و (present OR (absent AND absenceCounts))
            + dedupe على (sessionId::studentId)
paid    = initialPaid + كل payment.sessionsCovered
balance = counted - paid
cyclePosition = counted == 0 ? 0 : ((counted-1) % L) + 1
```

- `L = settings.cycleLength` (افتراضي 4).
- `balance >= L` → `DUE` و`amountDue = floor(balance/L) × L × price`.
- `balance < 0` → `PAID_AHEAD`.
- عند حفظ حصة بيتم تحديث الـ cache **في نفس الـ batch**، وعند أي قراءة بيتم reconcile تاني (self-heal).

---

## 10) توليد الحصص

- الأفق: من النهاردة **14 يوم** (شامل النهاردة).
- لكل مجموعة نشطة ولكل slot: لو `weekday` اليوم مطابق → ID = `groupId_yyyyMMdd`.
- موجود؟ ما نعملش حاجة (حتى لو ملغية — لا نبعثها تاني).
- موجود ومجدول ومستقبلي ووقته اتغير؟ نحدّث `time` بس.
- الإضافية `_2`, `_3`... المولّد ما بيلمسهاش أبدًا.
- مُحفّزات: provider ready / focus / visibility / online / حفظ مجموعة.

---

## 11) الحضور والدفعات — ليه batch واحد؟

عشان الموبايل يكتب offline ويقفل التاب والمزامنة تحصل مرة واحدة. لو عملنا Transaction كان هيحتاج نت ومكناش بنضمن الحفظ.

- `saveLesson`: attendance docs + session `completed` + student counters → **writeBatch واحد**، وبرجّع `newlyDue` عشان الـ result sheet تعرض «ن طالب وصلوا للدفعة».
- `setAttendanceStatus`: تعديل حصة محفوظة (غاب بدل حاضر مثلًا) →حديث row واحد + إعادة حساب.
- إلغاء حصة ليها حضور → العداداتustral ترجع لوراء في نفس الـ batch.
- الدفعات: `record/update/remove` كل واحد batch مع عداد الطالب. التسجيل بيعرض معاينة حيّة للرصيد الجديد، وتحذير غيرمانع لو المبلغ مش مساوي `sessionsCovered × price`.

---

## 12) المساعد الذكي (AI)

### الـ route: `POST /api/ai/assistant`
- `runtime = nodejs`, `maxDuration = 300`, `dynamic = force-dynamic`.
- التوكن بيتحقق بـ `jose` (مش `firebase-admin/auth` — انظر §6.2).
- Body بـ zod: `text <= 1000` أو `audio <= 2MB` + `locale` + `clientToday`.
- حد يومي لكل uid في `meta/aiUsage`، افتراضي 100.
- **Provider ladder**: `gemini-3.5-flash-lite` (الأساسي الأسرع) → `gemini-3.5-flash` → Groq (`openai/gpt-oss-120b`) لو `GROQ_API_KEY` موجود.
- الحالة (`loadState`) بيتقرأ **مرة واحدة** ويتبع لكل الأدوات → توفير 10–30 ثانية.
- `GET /api/ai/assistant` endpoint للفحص: بيرجع booleans للمفاتيح + حالة Firestore (بدون أي سر).

### الأدوات (11)
**قراءة فقط (7):** `get_dues`, `get_almost_due`, `get_absences`, `get_income`, `get_group_status`, `get_student_summary`, `get_today_overview`.
**اقتراحات (4، ما بيكتبوش حاجة):** `propose_attendance`, `propose_payment`, `propose_cancel_session`, `draft_parent_reminder`.

### في الواجهة `AiSheet`
اقتراحات جاهزة، إدخال نص، ميكروفون (`SpeechRecognition ar-EG` أولًا، أو MediaRecorder → base64)، typing indicator، بطاقة اقتراح بـ **تنفيذ/تعديل/إلغاء**. التنفيذ بيستدعي الـ repository العادي وبعدين result sheet زي اليدوي. حالة offline / 401 / 429 / model error كل واحدة ليها رسالة هادية في i18n.

**مهم:** مفيش أداة لإضافة طالب — لو طلبت «ضيف طالب» هيحصلك رد إنه مش متاح. الإضافة تتم من شاشة الطلاب فيها validation.

### الخصوصية
أرقام التليفونات بتت.shiel من كل tool result قبل ما تتبعت للموديل. اللوجات فيها بس أسماء الأخطاء، مش بيانات الطلاب.

---

## 13) PWA

- `manifest.ts`: اسم عربي، `display: standalone`, `theme/background #F3EDE3`, أيقونات 192/512 + maskable في `public/icons/`.
- `layout.tsx` فيه metadata للـ manifest و apple-touch-icon و viewport-fit=cover.
- `src/app/sw.ts` + `@serwist/next` → `public/sw.js` (اتولد في الـ build، مش متCommit).
  - Precache لشل التطبيق وصفحة `/offline`.
  - `NetworkOnly` لـ `/api/*` وكل نطاقات Firebase/Google.
  - cross-origin باقي الحاجات NetworkFirst.
- `PwaPrompts`: install sheet بعد أول حصة محفوظة (مش أول زيارة) + toast «نسخة جديدة» عند تحديث الـ build.
- `npm run build` لازم يكون `--webpack` لأن @serwist/next مش بيشتغل مع Turbopack.
- أيقونات `public/icons/*.png` اتولدت بـ PIL، مش generated image.

---

## 14) الاختبارات

| النوع | العدد | بيفحص إيه |
|---|---|---|
| `test/domain/*` | ~65 | التاريخ، IDs، الفلوس، العدادات، الرصيد، المجموعات، الحصص، الإحصائيات، validation، سيناريوهات الفلوس، خصوصية الـ AI |
| `test/ai/*` | ~5 | zod schema للطلب/الرد + redaction التليفونات |
| `test/emulator/rules.test.ts` | 4 | المالك يقرأ/يكتب، مالك تاني ممنوع، مجهول ممنوع |
| `test/integration/*` | 16 | CRUD، توليد الحصص، الحضور والعدادات، الدفعات، وroute الـ AI بموديل وهمي |

`npm run test:emulator` بيشغّل الإيموليترات تلقائيًا. محتاج JDK 21+ (משום firebase-tools يرفض Java 17).

---

## 15) النشر والتشغيل

1. Firebase: Firestore API مفعّل + database في `me-central1` + Auth Email/Password + authorized domain للـ Vercel + rules/indexes منشورة.
2. Vercel env (Production):
   ```
   NEXT_PUBLIC_FIREBASE_API_KEY / AUTH_DOMAIN / PROJECT_ID / MESSAGING_SENDER_ID / APP_ID
   NEXT_PUBLIC_USE_EMULATORS=0
   FIREBASE_SERVICE_ACCOUNT=<base64 سطر واحد>
   GEMINI_API_KEY / GEMINI_MODEL=gemini-3.5-flash-lite
   AI_DAILY_LIMIT=100 / AI_ENABLED=true
   GROQ_API_KEY? / GROQ_MODEL=openai/gpt-oss-120b
   ```
3. `vercel --prod` (أو Push لـ GitHub والـ Vercel يعمل Deploy تلقائي).
4. `GET /api/ai/assistant` المفروض يرجع 200 مع `geminiKey:true, serviceAccount:true, firestore:"ok"`.

---

## 16) متغيرات البيئة (ملخص)

| المتغير | منين | ليه |
|---|---|---|
| `NEXT_PUBLIC_FIREBASE_*` | Firebase Console → Project settings → Web app | عميل Firebase |
| `NEXT_PUBLIC_USE_EMULATORS` | يدوي | 1 = إيموليتر + memory cache |
| `FIREBASE_SERVICE_ACCOUNT` | Service accounts → Generate key → base64 | Admin SDK على السيرفر |
| `GEMINI_API_KEY` | Google AI Studio | الموديل الأساسي |
| `GEMINI_MODEL` | ثابت | `gemini-3.5-flash-lite` |
| `AI_DAILY_LIMIT` / `AI_ENABLED` | ثابت | حد/مفتاح المساعد |
| `GROQ_API_KEY` / `GROQ_MODEL` | console.groq.com/keys | fallback اختياري |

---

## 17) اللي لسه مستني / حدود معروفة

- **Spike A** (offline على تليفون حقيقي) و**Spike B** (audio MIME على Android/iOS) لسه التقارير لازم تتكتب في `docs/spikes.md`.
- **Real-week trial** 7 أيام قبل وضع `v1.0`.
- تصدير JSON كامل للبيانات مؤجل لـ v1.1.
- مفيش Google sign-in في v1 — Email/Password بس.
- المساعد مش بيضيف طلاب (انظر §12).
- المجاني بتاع Gemini بيعستخدم الرسائل لتحسين المنتجات — البيانات المبعتة أسماء ومبالغ فقط، التليفونات متشالة.
- Free tier limits بتتغير؛ لو كتير من الأسئلة، اربط billing أو استخدم Groq.

---

## 18) استكشاف الأخطاء الشائعة

| العَرَض | السبب | الحل |
|---|---|---|
| `API_KEY_INVALID` في المتصفح فقط | `NEXT_PUBLIC_*` اتحفظ في build قديم | غيّر env + **Redeploy** |
| `500` من `/api/ai/assistant` | كان `firebase-admin/auth` → `jwks-rsa/jose` | اتصلح بـ `jose` dynamic import |
| `504 FUNCTION_INVOCATION_TIMEOUT` | maxDuration كان 60 | رفعناه لـ 300 |
| `CONFIGURATION_NOT_FOUND` | Auth مش متفعّل في المشروع | Firebase Console → Authentication → Get started + Email/Password |
| `PERMISSION_DENIED` من Firestore | الـ API مفعّل بس الـ database مش موجودة | Firestore Database → Create database |
| عدادات غلط بعد تعديل قديم | cache قديم | `/dev` → Recompute (أو reconcile تلقائي على snapshot) |
| خطأ `undefined` في `updateDoc` | patch فيه `undefined` | `definedFields()` في `cleanPatch.ts` |

---

## 19) خريطة ملفات سريعة

| الملف | وظيفته في سطر |
|---|---|
| `src/app/layout.tsx` | Cairo font + I18n + AiConfig + Auth + PwaPrompts |
| `src/app/globals.css` | tokens اللون (beige/sage) + `.glass-card` + safe-area + reduced-motion |
| `src/app/manifest.ts` | PWA manifest |
| `src/app/sw.ts` | Serwist worker |
| `src/i18n/ar.ts` | مصدر كل كلمة عربية |
| `src/i18n/en.ts` | ترجمة إجبارية بنفس الشكل (TS يرفض أي key ناقص) |
| `src/data/paths.ts` | كل مسارات Firestore |
| `src/data/mappers.ts` | Timestamp → ms وتحويل docs لأنواع domain |
| `src/data/ai/tools.ts` | الأدوات + تنفيذها على state محمّل |
| `src/data/ai/providers.ts` | Gemini و Groq runners |
| `src/data/ai/schema.ts` | zod + parse الرد |
| `src/components/ui/*` | مكتبة القطع المشتركة |
| `src/components/features/*` | النماذج والبطاقات والمساعد |
| `firebase.json` / `firestore.rules` / `firestore.indexes.json` | إعداداتFirestore والصلاحيات |
| `vitest.config.mts` / `vitest.emulator.mts` | اختبارات الوحدة / الإيموليتر |
| `docs/release-checklist.md` | خطوات النشر والتجربة الحقيقية |
| `docs/spikes.md` | فحص الـ offline والصوت |
| `docs/manual-tests.md` | قوائم الفحص اليدوي لكل stage |
| `docs/versions.md` | الإصدارات المثبّتة وملاحظاتها |

---

**الخلاصة:** الواجهة بتقرأ من provider واحد، والكتابة كلها services بـ batch، والمنطق كله pure في `domain/`، والذكاء الاصطناعي مقفول بعدد أدوات معروف ومش بيكتب من غير تأكيد. لو غيّرت حاجة في مكان، غالبًا فيه test أو type بيقولك.
````

### `docs/manual-tests.md`  ·  125 lines

````markdown
# Manual test checklist

Run on a phone, in the installed PWA where possible. Tick per stage; add the
date and device.

## Stage 0 — foundation

- [ ] App opens: Arabic RTL, Cairo font, warm background, frosted cards.
- [ ] Sign up with a new email → onboarding → Today.
- [ ] Sign out from Settings → back to sign-in. Sign in again.
- [ ] Kill and reopen: still signed in.
- [ ] Airplane mode + reload after a prior visit: app opens, offline bar shows.
- [ ] Bottom bar navigates all four tabs; More opens Groups, Dues, Settings.
- [ ] Language toggle in Settings switches to English LTR and back.
- [ ] No horizontal overflow at 360 px width; labels not clipped in Arabic.
- [ ] `/dev` tools visible in development only.

## Stage 10 — PWA install + release

- [ ] `npm run build` (webpack) generates `public/sw.js`; `/manifest.webmanifest` has the Arabic name and 192/512/maskable icons.
- [ ] Install from Chrome Android and Safari iOS; standalone launch has no browser chrome and respects safe areas.
- [ ] Offline launch after one online visit: shell opens, `/offline` fallback shows for uncached navigations.
- [ ] Deploy a second build → the "نسخة جديدة" toast appears → refresh runs the new build.
- [ ] Install prompt appears only after the first saved lesson, and stays dismissed after "بعدين".
- [ ] Lighthouse mobile: installable, performance ≥ 90, no console errors in production.
- [ ] Settings shows the backup note; real-week trial (7 days) tracked in `docs/release-checklist.md`.

## Stage 9 — AI assistant

- [ ] With a real `GEMINI_API_KEY`: ask "مين عليه دفعات؟" → the answer matches Dues exactly.
- [ ] Ask "قدّ إيه المحصّل الشهر ده؟" → matches Stats.
- [ ] Two students with the same name → the assistant asks "أي أحمد؟" with candidate chips, no guess.
- [ ] Request an action → a proposal card appears; nothing is saved until **تنفيذ** is tapped.
- [ ] Confirm an attendance/payment proposal → the normal result sheet appears and counters update.
- [ ] Duplicate the story of a student's phone number: no phone digits appear in the assistant payload or answer.
- [ ] Caps: 1000-char question and 2 MB audio are rejected; the daily limit returns the calm limit message.
- [ ] Voice: Android Chrome (`audio/webm;codecs=opus`) and iOS Safari (`audio/mp4`) — fill `docs/spikes.md`; the text fallback works where audio is rejected.
- [ ] Offline: mic and send are disabled with the offline copy; 401/429/model errors render their own messages.
- [ ] `AI_ENABLED=false` removes the assistant button (and the route still rejects unauthenticated calls).

## Stage 8 — statistics

- [ ] Month stepper moves through Cairo months; months with no data show the honest empty state.
- [ ] Collected = sum of that month's payments; Outstanding = Dues total (both match a hand ledger).
- [ ] Expected = Σ `price × cycleLength` for active students (capacity, not cash collected).
- [ ] Attendance rate = present / attendance docs of completed lessons in the month.
- [ ] Six-month income chart and per-group attendance chart render at 360 px with Latin digits, Arabic month names and flat colours.
- [ ] Top absences / slowest payers / top groups lists match the seeded dataset.

## Stage 7 — Today dashboard, groups, settings

- [ ] Today greeting + Cairo date, lesson cards with group position badge, due card and quick actions.
- [ ] Reminder card lists only students whose next counted session is the 4th; paid-ahead and due students never appear.
- [ ] Due count and total on Today match the Dues screen exactly.
- [ ] Group list badge shows the modal position; outliers show `name 2/4`; group detail links straight to "add student" with the group preselected.
- [ ] Settings: toggle "absence counts" → confirm → everyone is recomputed; change cycle length (2–8) → confirm → balances and badges update.
- [ ] Currency label, week start and language persist after reload; language flips `dir` to LTR/RTL.

## Stage 6 — payments, student detail, dues

- [ ] Dues total equals the sum of the rows; due list sorted by amount then name.
- [ ] Payment sheet prefills `L × price`; changing the amount shows the non-blocking mismatch warning and a live balance preview.
- [ ] Paid at session 3 → PAID_AHEAD; at 4 → SETTLED; at 8 with one payment → 200 ج.م due.
- [ ] Edit amount → counters and balance unchanged; delete → due again immediately.
- [ ] A payment covering 2 cycles settles both; deleting it restores the older balance.
- [ ] Student detail shows cycles on attendance rows and edit/delete on every payment.
- [ ] WhatsApp button copies the reminder text and opens `wa.me` (no phone → still copies).
- [ ] Offline: record payment → preview correct, write queued, one sync on reconnect.

## Stage 5 — attendance + counter engine

- [ ] Open a lesson: all students default to present; tapping a row toggles to "غايب" (tint + icon + haptic where supported).
- [ ] Save a lesson with one absence: result sheet shows the count and links newly-due students; counters update instantly.
- [ ] Edit a saved lesson: toggle a row, save → "اتحدّث الحضور والعدادات", counters recompute.
- [ ] Leave with unsaved changes → confirmation sheet; reload → browser warning.
- [ ] Cancel a lesson that had attendance → counters drop back to their prior values.
- [ ] `/dev` → **Corrupt counters** → the provider's self-heal restores them on the next snapshot; the manual **Recompute** button then reports `0` because there is nothing left to fix.
- [ ] Offline matrix: save with 2 absences → kill tab → reopen offline → data there → reconnect → one sync, no duplicates.

## Stage 4 — session generation + schedule

- [ ] Add a group with real slots → 14-day schedule appears (day view + week view).
- [ ] Run `/dev` → **Generate lessons** three times → each run reports `0` writes; no duplicates.
- [ ] Edit a slot's time → future scheduled lesson moves to the new time; today's lesson and cancelled lessons stay untouched.
- [ ] Add two extras on one day → labelled `_2`, `_3` (UI shows "إضافية"); generator leaves them alone.
- [ ] Cancel a lesson → struck through, shown as "ملغية" everywhere; it stays cancelled after a reload and a regeneration.
- [ ] Date stepper: previous/next day and "today" shortcut use Cairo dates.
- [ ] Week view shows all 14 days including empty ones with honest empty copy.
- [ ] Cancel a lesson that had attendance → computed counters drop immediately.

## Stage 3 — groups & students CRUD

- [ ] Create 5 groups / 12 students; reload → all still there.
- [ ] Student form advanced section keeps `initialCounted` / `initialPaid`; the detail screen shows the computed `حصة N من M`.
- [ ] Slots editor: add/remove slots, weekday chips + `HH:mm` + minutes; validation messages are Arabic.
- [ ] Move a student to another group → list/detail show the new group; attendance history and counters unchanged.
- [ ] Archive a student/group → gone from active lists, still reachable via the Archived filter and direct URL.
- [ ] Restore → back in active lists, counters unchanged.
- [ ] Search by name; all four student filters work.
- [ ] Phone actions open dialer / WhatsApp.
- [ ] Bottom-sheet confirmations: Escape, backdrop and cancel all close them; focus stays inside.
- [ ] Create a student offline → queued write visible immediately, one sync on reconnect.

## Stage 1 — domain core (no UI)

- [ ] `npm test` → 88 passing.
- [ ] `npm run typecheck` → no errors.
- [ ] `npm run test:emulator` → 20 passing (4 rules + 3 CRUD + 3 sessions + 4 attendance + 3 payments + 3 AI route).

## Offline matrix (repeat at Stages 2, 5, 6, 10)

1. [ ] Warm cache: Today, Schedule, Students, Dues.
2. [ ] Airplane mode; reload → all render from cache.
3. [ ] Save a lesson with 2 absences → instant UI update, result sheet correct.
4. [ ] Record a payment offline → preview correct.
5. [ ] Kill tab, reopen offline → both writes visible.
6. [ ] Reconnect → one sync, no duplicates, counters match computed.
7. [ ] Second device/browser sees the same data after sync.

## Regression checks per stage

- [ ] Counters match a hand calculation for a seeded student.
- [ ] No `runTransaction` in the codebase.
- [ ] No screen imports `firebase/firestore`.
- [ ] No user-facing string outside `src/i18n`.
````

### `docs/release-checklist.md`  ·  88 lines

````markdown
# Release checklist (v1.0)

## Live verification — done 2026-11-25

- Cloud Firestore API enabled; `(default)` database created in **`me-central1`
  (Doha)** — the closest region to Egypt. Admin SDK reads succeed.
- Firebase Auth initialized, Email/Password enabled, web API key allows
  `localhost`. Real sign-up works, which also confirms the published Firestore
  rules allow an owner to write their own `users/{uid}` tree.
- Real end-to-end run on project `tutororganizer`: sign-up → group → student
  (6 carried sessions, 50/session) → Dues `200 ج.م` → assistant answered
  "مين عليه دفعات؟" with exactly that amount → assistant proposed a payment,
  **nothing was saved until "تنفيذ"** → after confirm, payment record appeared
  and Dues became `0 ج.م` on reload.
- All three temporary `verify-*` users and their Firestore trees were deleted;
  the project has 0 users.
- Gemini 3.x tool-loop fixes: function responses are sent with role `user`, and
  the model's raw function-call parts (including `thought_signature`) are
  replayed on the next round.
- Speed/quota pass (2026-11-26): `GEMINI_MODEL=gemini-3.5-flash-lite` is primary
  (~15 RPM / 500 RPD free vs 5/20 on Flash), alternate Gemini is fallback, and
  hydrated Firestore state is loaded **once per request**. A live question with
  a due student answered `200` in ~10 s (was 19–46 s). Groq is an optional
  text-only fallback: add `GROQ_API_KEY` + `GROQ_MODEL=openai/gpt-oss-120b` on
  Vercel to enable it (30 RPM / 1,000 RPD free).

## 1. Firebase project (do this before any real sign-in)

- [x] **Enable the Cloud Firestore API** for the project (done; see live verification above).
- [ ] Authentication → Sign-in method → **Email/Password** enabled.
- [ ] Authentication → Settings → Authorized domains: add your Vercel domain
      (and `localhost` for local testing).
- [ ] Web app config copied into `.env.local` (`NEXT_PUBLIC_FIREBASE_*`).
- [ ] Service account JSON base64 → `FIREBASE_SERVICE_ACCOUNT` (server only).

## 2. AI

- [ ] `GEMINI_API_KEY` set (rotated after being shared in chat).
- [ ] `GEMINI_MODEL=gemini-3.5-flash` — `gemini-2.5-flash` returned 404 for this
      API key; `gemini-flash-latest` returned 503.
- [ ] `AI_DAILY_LIMIT=100`, `AI_ENABLED=true`.

## 3. Build & local PWA check

- [ ] `npm run build` (webpack + Serwist) passes; `public/sw.js` is generated.
- [ ] `npm start`; `http://localhost:3000/manifest.webmanifest` returns Arabic
      name/icons; `/sw.js` returns 200; `/offline` renders.
- [ ] Chrome → Application → Manifest: "Installable"; service worker active.
- [ ] Lighthouse mobile: PWA installable pass, performance ≥ 90.

## 4. Vercel

`vercel` CLI is not installed on this machine — install it (`npm i -g vercel`)
and log in yourself, then:

```bash
vercel link
vercel env add NEXT_PUBLIC_FIREBASE_API_KEY production
vercel env add NEXT_PUBLIC_FIREBASE_AUTH_DOMAIN production
vercel env add NEXT_PUBLIC_FIREBASE_PROJECT_ID production
vercel env add NEXT_PUBLIC_FIREBASE_MESSAGING_SENDER_ID production
vercel env add NEXT_PUBLIC_FIREBASE_APP_ID production
vercel env add FIREBASE_SERVICE_ACCOUNT production
vercel env add GEMINI_API_KEY production
vercel env add GEMINI_MODEL production
vercel env add AI_DAILY_LIMIT production
vercel env add AI_ENABLED production
vercel --prod
```

- [ ] No `NEXT_PUBLIC_USE_EMULATORS=1` in production.
- [ ] Production sign-up → add a group → add a student → save a lesson → record
      a payment → ask the assistant one question.
- [ ] Install from Android Chrome and iOS Safari; standalone launch has no browser chrome.
- [ ] Verify the update toast after a second deploy.

## 5. Real-week trial

- [ ] 7 consecutive days: every lesson recorded in the app, notebook only as a cross-check.
- [ ] One unaided lesson recording by the tutor, timed (< 30 s).
- [ ] Offline matrix from `docs/manual-tests.md` passes on the phone.
- [ ] Tag `v1.0` when the trial is clean.

## 6. Backup note (shown in Settings / README)

Data lives in **your Firebase account under `users/{uid}`** — clearing the
browser or reinstalling the PWA does not delete it. Do not delete the Firebase
project or the auth user. A full JSON export is planned for v1.1.
````

### `docs/spikes.md`  ·  85 lines

````markdown
# Stage 0 spikes

Two assumptions must be proven before later stages rely on them. Fill in a
verdict for each platform you test. Do not mark the stage done without them.

Dev-only helpers: `/dev` (bottom bar → **المزيد / More** → Dev tools) seeds a
group, two students, one completed session for today and two attendance rows,
and can toggle an absence, recompute counters and clear the data again.

---

## Spike A — Firestore offline + batched writes (PWA)

Device(s): ____________________  Browser: ____________________

1. [ ] Sign in with email/password (a real project, **not** the emulator —
       emulator mode deliberately runs on `memoryLocalCache`).
2. [ ] `/dev` → **Seed demo data**. Confirm counts: students 2, groups 1, sessions 1, attendance 2.
3. [ ] Open Today, Schedule, Students, Dues once (warms the cache).
4. [ ] Turn on airplane mode. Reload the tab.
       → App shell loads, data still visible, offline bar appears.
5. [ ] `/dev` → **Toggle first student's absence** while offline.
       → The attendance row flips, and after ~1 s the counters follow (the dev
         screen reconciles counters from hydrated state).
6. [ ] Kill the tab, reopen while still offline.
       → The write is still there (queued in IndexedDB).
7. [ ] Reconnect.
       → Offline bar disappears, exactly one sync, no duplicate attendance rows.
8. [ ] Cold profile: new browser profile, offline, open the app.
       → Honest "first load needs a connection" behaviour (no fake zeros).

**Verdict:** ______________________________________________

Notes / copy changes needed: ______________________________

---

## Spike B — Gemini audio MIME

The question: does Gemini accept the audio container that each phone actually
records, without a conversion step?

Helper (no app route, no `firebase-admin` needed):

```bash
# record ~3 s of Egyptian Arabic, save the blob to a file, then:
node scripts/gemini-audio-probe.mjs clip.webm "audio/webm;codecs=opus"
node scripts/gemini-audio-probe.mjs clip.m4a  "audio/mp4"
```

It reads `GEMINI_API_KEY` / `GEMINI_MODEL` from the environment or `.env.local`,
posts `inlineData` to `v1beta/models/{model}:generateContent`, and prints the
HTTP status plus the transcription (exit code 0 = accepted).

| Platform | `MediaRecorder` type | Verdict | Notes |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| Android Chrome | `audio/webm;codecs=opus` | pending | |
| iOS Safari 17+ | `audio/mp4` | pending | |
| Desktop Chrome (fallback dev) | `audio/webm;codecs=opus` | pending | |

**Fallback ladder** if a container is rejected (decided **now**, used in Stage 9):

1. Try the same container with the bare MIME type (`audio/webm`, `audio/mp4`).
2. Web Speech API `ar-EG` on the client → send **text** to the AI route
   (server still caps usage; no audio leaves the phone).
3. Client-side conversion (WAV/MP4) — last resort, only if 1 and 2 both fail.

In the shipped route (`POST /api/ai/assistant`), a rejected audio MIME returns
`422 { error: "audioUnsupported" }`; the sheet shows the calm fallback copy and
the user types instead. Phone numbers are stripped from every tool result before
it reaches the model.

**Verdict:** ______________________________________________

Notes / chosen path: ______________________________________

---

## Stage 0 environment notes

- Firestore emulator needs **JDK 21+** (`firebase-tools` refuses Java 17).
  Verified locally with Temurin 21 JRE:
  `JAVA_HOME=/path/to/jre21 npm run test:emulator` → 4/4 rules tests pass.
- Emulator mode (`NEXT_PUBLIC_USE_EMULATORS=1`) uses `memoryLocalCache`, so
  offline behaviour must be verified on a real project.
````

````markdown
# Pinned versions

Recorded after scaffolding. Do not chase upgrades mid-build.

| Package | Version |
| --- | --- |
| next | 16.4.6 |
| react / react-dom | 19.3.0 |
| typescript | 5.x (create-next-app pin) |
| tailwindcss | 4.3.3 |
| @tailwindcss/turbopack | 4.3.3 |
| firebase | 13.4.0 |
| firebase-admin | 14.5.1 |
| @firebase/rules-unit-testing | 6.0.0 |
| @google/genai | 3.6.1 |
| @serwist/next / serwist | 9.5.15 (not wired yet — Stage 10) |
| recharts | 3.11.1 (Stage 8) |
| lucide-react | 1.79.0 |
| zod | 4.6.7 (Stage 9) |
| vitest | 5.1.1 |
| @types/node | 24.x |

Runtime: Node 24 (local), Next requires ≥ 20.9.

## Notes

- `@types/node` was raised from 20 to 24 to satisfy Vitest 5's peer range.
- Next 16 uses Turbopack for `dev`, but `build` uses **webpack** so `@serwist/next` can generate `public/sw.js`. `postcss.config.mjs` + `@tailwindcss/postcss` provide Tailwind v4 for the webpack build.
- `next.config.ts` deliberately omits `cacheComponents` / `partialPrefetching`: the authenticated app is client-rendered and must not be prerendered with stale data.
- `GEMINI_MODEL=gemini-3.5-flash` is the working model for the current key; `gemini-2.5-flash` returned 404 and `gemini-flash-latest` returned 503.
- `tsconfig.target` is **ES2020** (raised from the scaffold's ES2017) because `sessionId.ts` uses named regex capture groups.
- **Firestore emulator requires JDK 21+.** `firebase-tools` rejects Java 17 with
  "no longer supports Java version before 21". Verified with Temurin 21 JRE
  (`/tmp/opencode/jre21/...` locally); run
  `JAVA_HOME=<jre21> npm run test:emulator` if the system JDK is older.
- `firebase emulators` writes `firestore-debug.log` / `.firebase/` — both are git-ignored.
````

### `firestore.rules`  ·  23 lines

````text
rules_version = '2';

service cloud.firestore {
  match /databases/{database}/documents {
    function isOwner(uid) {
      return request.auth != null && request.auth.uid == uid;
    }

    // A tutor can only ever see their own tree.
    match /users/{uid} {
      allow read, write: if isOwner(uid);

      match /{collection}/{docId} {
        allow read, write: if isOwner(uid);
      }
    }

    // Everything else is denied.
    match /{document=**} {
      allow read, write: if false;
    }
  }
}
````

### `masterPlan.md`  ·  74 lines

````markdown
You are building "Tutor Manager (مدير المدرس)", a mobile-first Offline-First Web PWA for ONE private tutor (approx. 9–15 students, 4–7 groups) using Next.js (App Router, TypeScript), Tailwind CSS, Firebase (Auth + Firestore), and Vercel.

Read all rules below carefully and follow them strictly without adding out-of-scope features.

### 1. Core Tech Stack & Architecture
- Framework: Next.js (App Router) + TypeScript + Tailwind CSS.
- PWA: Configured with web manifest (`display: standalone`, Arabic name, icons) and service worker (`@serwist/next`) for offline shell loading.
- Database & Auth: Firebase Web SDK (`firebase/auth` with Google + Email/Password; `firebase/firestore` initialized with `persistentLocalCache` and `persistentMultipleTabManager` for offline persistence).
- Testing: Vitest for unit tests. Use Firebase Local Emulators for integration testing — do NOT build separate in-memory fake repositories.
- Charts & Icons: `recharts` + `lucide-react`.
- Fonts & i18n: `Cairo` via `next/font/google` with `tabular-nums` for all money/counters. Arabic RTL (`dir="rtl"`) is default; English LTR toggle in Settings. Zero hardcoded user-facing strings in components; keep all copy in `src/i18n/ar.ts` and `src/i18n/en.ts`.
- Strict Layering: UI components NEVER import `firebase/firestore` directly. All reads/writes go through `src/data/repositories/` and `src/data/services/`.

### 2. Core Business Logic (Source of Truth — Pure TypeScript)
Implement in `src/domain/balanceCalculator.ts` (zero React/Firebase imports) with full Vitest unit tests BEFORE building any UI:
1. Default cycle length `L = 4` (configurable in Settings). Money is stored as integers (whole EGP).
2. Counters belong to the student, not the group (late joiners start at 0; moving groups preserves counters).
3. `initialCounted` and `initialPaid` are stored on each student for migrating existing mid-cycle students. Never recompute from 0 without adding them.
4. Counted sessions = `initialCounted` + count of attendance docs where:
   - the parent session has `status === 'completed'` (cancelled/scheduled sessions NEVER count),
   - `session.dateKey >= student.startedAtKey`,
   - attendance is `'present'`, OR (`'absent'` AND `settings.absenceCounts === true`).
5. Paid sessions = `initialPaid` + sum of `payment.sessionsCovered` (default 4 per payment).
6. `balance = sessionsCounted - sessionsPaid`:
   - `balance >= L` → DUE (`dueCycles = Math.floor(balance / L)`, `amountDue = dueCycles * L * pricePerSession`)
   - `0 < balance < L` → IN_PROGRESS
   - `balance === 0` → SETTLED
   - `balance < 0` → PAID_AHEAD (`abs(balance)` sessions; must NEVER appear as unpaid at session 4)
7. `cyclePosition = sessionsCounted === 0 ? 0 : ((sessionsCounted - 1) % L) + 1` (e.g., "3 of 4", based on counted sessions, not payments).
8. `groupPosition` = mode of `cyclePosition` among active students in the group with `sessionsCounted > 0` (tie → higher). Flag students whose position differs (e.g., "أحمد 2/4").
9. Single Write Path: ONLY `src/data/services/counterService.ts` is allowed to write cached `sessionsCounted` and `sessionsPaid` on student docs (using `writeBatch`). Every mutation affecting attendance, sessions, payments, or settings calls `CounterService.recompute(studentIds)`.
10. Date Handling: Every session, attendance, and payment document stores a local `dateKey` string (`"yyyy-MM-dd"`). All grouping, deterministic session IDs (`{groupId}_{yyyyMMdd}` / `_{n}` for extras), and range filters use `dateKey` to prevent Egypt DST / UTC midnight bugs.

### 3. Data Model (Firestore under `users/{uid}`)
- `users/{uid}`: `{ settings: { absenceCounts: true, cycleLength: 4, language: 'ar', currency: 'EGP', weekStartsOn: 6 } }`
- `users/{uid}/groups/{groupId}`: `{ name, subject?, slots: [{ weekday: 0-6, time: 'HH:mm', durationMin }], active, createdAt }`
- `users/{uid}/students/{studentId}`: `{ name, phone?, groupId, pricePerSession, startedAtKey, active, note?, initialCounted, initialPaid, sessionsCounted, sessionsPaid }`
- `users/{uid}/sessions/{groupId_yyyyMMdd[_n]}`: `{ groupId, groupName, dateKey, time, type: 'regular'|'extra', status: 'scheduled'|'completed'|'cancelled', note? }`
- `users/{uid}/attendance/{sessionId_studentId}`: `{ sessionId, studentId, groupId, dateKey, status: 'present'|'absent' }`
- `users/{uid}/payments/{paymentId}`: `{ studentId, dateKey, amount, sessionsCovered, note? }`
Security rules: allow read/write ONLY when `request.auth != null && request.auth.uid == uid`.

### 4. Design System (Strict — Warm Minimalist Glassmorphism)
- Mood: Calm, warm stationery aesthetic. Mobile-first (`max-w-md mx-auto` shell on desktop, full viewport on mobile).
- HARD RULES: NO CSS gradients, NO purple/blue AI glow, NO neon, NO glowing orbs.
- Background: flat `#F3EDE3` with 2–3 fixed solid blurred circles (`#E6DBC9`, `#CFDCD3`, `#EBD5C7` at 45–55% opacity, `blur-3xl`, `pointer-events-none`) behind the page so frosted cards have depth.
- Glass Cards: `bg-white/55 backdrop-blur-[18px] border border-white/70 rounded-[20px] shadow-[0_8px_24px_rgba(60,50,40,0.08)]`.
- Tokens:
  - Text: `#2E2B27` | Muted: `#7A7468`
  - Primary (Sage): `#6B8F7B` | Primary Dark: `#4F7261` | Accent (Clay): `#C58B6D`
  - Success: `#6B8F7B` | Warning (Almost due): `#D8A24A` | Danger (Absent/Due): `#C45B52` | Info (Paid ahead): `#6F8FA6`
  - Status chips: 15% opacity tint fill + full-color text + icon (never rely on color alone).
- Touch targets: min `48px` height. Motion: `200ms ease-out`, subtle `active:scale-[0.98]`.
- Every list screen MUST ship with Loading, Empty, and Error states from day one.

### 5. Screens & UX Rules
Navigation: Sticky bottom bar with 4 tabs (`Today`, `Schedule`, `Students`, `Stats`) + `More` drawer/menu (`Groups`, `Dues`, `Settings`), plus a floating subtle sage AI button.
- Attendance screen (`/attendance/[sessionId]`): Everyone is PRESENT by default. Single tap toggles ABSENT (soft red tint + icon). Show cycle position (`2/4`) and payment chip per student. Recording a full lesson must take under 30 seconds.
- Today dashboard (`/`): Greeting + Arabic date, Today's lessons (with group position badge), "Reaching session 4 today" reminder card (excluding paid-ahead students), "Due now" summary card, quick actions.
- Payments bottom sheet: Prefills expected amount (`dueCycles * L * pricePerSession` or 1 cycle), shows live "after this payment" balance preview from `BalanceCalculator`, warns (never blocks) if amount differs.
- WhatsApp reminders: Draft polite Arabic text and open `https://wa.me/...` — NEVER send messages automatically.

### 6. AI Assistant (`/api/ai/assistant` on Vercel — Free Tier)
- Backend: Next.js Route Handler calling Gemini API (`@google/genai`, model from `GEMINI_MODEL` env var, key in `GEMINI_API_KEY` server-only).
- Auth: Verify Firebase ID Token via `firebase-admin` on every request; scope all Firestore reads strictly to `decodedToken.uid`. Strip phone numbers before sending context to Gemini.
- Tool Architecture: Gemini understands Egyptian Arabic text AND audio blobs (recorded via browser `MediaRecorder` and sent as multipart/base64).
  - Read tools (`get_dues`, `get_almost_due`, `get_absences`, `get_income`, `get_group_status`, `get_student_summary`, `get_today_overview`) query Firestore via Admin SDK and return exact JSON — the model NEVER does arithmetic from memory.
  - Action tools (`propose_attendance`, `propose_payment`, `propose_cancel_session`, `draft_parent_reminder`) NEVER write to Firestore. They return a structured proposal; the UI renders a `ConfirmCard` (Confirm / Edit / Cancel) and executes writes through normal client repositories only after the tutor taps Confirm.
  - Ambiguity: If a name matches multiple students (e.g., two "Ahmed"s), return `needs_clarification` with candidate chips. Never guess.

### 7. Working Rules for This Session
- Work strictly ONE stage at a time.
- After finishing each stage, stop and give me: (1) what was built, (2) exact commands to run tests/dev server, and (3) a short manual test checklist on mobile/browser before moving to the next stage.
- Do NOT add parent accounts, online payments, homework/grades, multi-tutor features, or dark mode in v1.
````

### `next.config.ts`  ·  25 lines

````typescript
import withSerwistInit from "@serwist/next";
import type { NextConfig } from "next";

const nextConfig: NextConfig = {
  reactStrictMode: true,
  // The app is a fully client-side authenticated PWA (Firestore + Auth on the
  // client), so route-level prerendering/caching is intentionally disabled.
  turbopack: {
    rules: {
      "*.css": {
        loaders: ["@tailwindcss/turbopack"],
        as: "*.css",
      },
    },
  },
};

const withSerwist = withSerwistInit({
  swSrc: "src/app/sw.ts",
  swDest: "public/sw.js",
  // Turbopack dev doesn't support the plugin; the production build uses webpack.
  disable: process.env.NODE_ENV !== "production",
});

export default withSerwist(nextConfig);
````
