### `scripts/gemini-audio-probe.mjs`  ·  107 lines

````javascript
#!/usr/bin/env node
/**
 * Spike B helper — can Gemini read a short Arabic voice clip with the MIME type
 * the browser really produces?
 *
 *   node scripts/gemini-audio-probe.mjs <file> <mimeType> [prompt]
 *
 * Examples (record 3 s in the browser, save the blob to a file):
 *   node scripts/gemini-audio-probe.mjs clip.webm "audio/webm;codecs=opus"
 *   node scripts/gemini-audio-probe.mjs clip.m4a  "audio/mp4"
 *
 * Reads GEMINI_API_KEY from the environment (or from .env.local).
 * Exit code 0 = accepted, 1 = rejected/error (prints the API message).
 */
import { readFileSync } from "node:fs";

function loadEnvLocal() {
  try {
    const raw = readFileSync(new URL("../.env.local", import.meta.url), "utf8");
    for (const line of raw.split("\n")) {
      const match = /^([A-Z0-9_]+)=(.*)$/.exec(line.trim());
      if (match && !process.env[match[1]]) {
        process.env[match[1]] = match[2].replace(/^["']|["']$/g, "");
      }
    }
  } catch {
    // No .env.local: rely on the real environment.
  }
}

async function main() {
  const [file, mimeType, prompt] = process.argv.slice(2);
  if (!file || !mimeType) {
    console.error(
      'usage: node scripts/gemini-audio-probe.mjs <file> <mimeType> ["prompt"]',
    );
    process.exit(2);
  }

  loadEnvLocal();

  const apiKey = process.env.GEMINI_API_KEY;
  if (!apiKey) {
    console.error("GEMINI_API_KEY is not set (env or .env.local).");
    process.exit(2);
  }

  const model = process.env.GEMINI_MODEL || "gemini-3.5-flash-lite";
  const data = readFileSync(file).toString("base64");

  const body = {
    contents: [
      {
        parts: [
          {
            text:
              prompt ||
              "اكتب اللي قاله في التسجيل بالحرف، وحدد اللغة واللهجة.",
          },
          { inlineData: { mimeType, data } },
        ],
      },
    ],
  };

  const started = Date.now();
  const response = await fetch(
    `https://generativelanguage.googleapis.com/v1beta/models/${model}:generateContent`,
    {
      method: "POST",
      headers: {
        "content-type": "application/json",
        "x-goog-api-key": apiKey,
      },
      body: JSON.stringify(body),
    },
  );

  const text = await response.text();
  const elapsed = Date.now() - started;

  console.log(`model=${model} mimeType=${mimeType} status=${response.status} ${elapsed}ms`);

  let parsed;
  try {
    parsed = JSON.parse(text);
  } catch {
    console.log(text.slice(0, 800));
    process.exit(response.ok ? 0 : 1);
  }

  if (!response.ok) {
    console.log(JSON.stringify(parsed, null, 2).slice(0, 1600));
    process.exit(1);
  }

  const answer = parsed?.candidates?.[0]?.content?.parts
    ?.map((part) => part.text)
    .filter(Boolean)
    .join("\n");
  console.log(answer ? `\n${answer}` : JSON.stringify(parsed, null, 2).slice(0, 1600));
}

main().catch((error) => {
  console.error(error);
  process.exit(1);
});
````

### `src/app/(app)/attendance/[sessionId]/page.tsx`  ·  391 lines

````tsx
"use client";

import Link from "next/link";
import { useParams, useRouter } from "next/navigation";
import { useEffect, useMemo, useState } from "react";
import {
  ArrowRight,
  CalendarDays,
  Check,
  CheckCircle2,
  Loader2,
  UserRound,
  X,
} from "lucide-react";
import { BalanceChip } from "@/components/features/BalanceChip";
import { BigPrimaryButton } from "@/components/ui/BigPrimaryButton";
import { ConfirmCard } from "@/components/ui/ConfirmCard";
import { CounterRing } from "@/components/ui/CounterRing";
import { EmptyState } from "@/components/ui/EmptyState";
import { Field, inputClass } from "@/components/ui/Field";
import { GlassCard } from "@/components/ui/GlassCard";
import { PageHeader } from "@/components/ui/Headers";
import { NumberText } from "@/components/ui/NumberText";
import { Sheet } from "@/components/ui/Sheet";
import { StatusChip } from "@/components/ui/StatusChip";
import { useTutorData } from "@/data/provider/hooks";
import {
  saveLesson,
  setAttendanceStatus,
  type LessonResult,
} from "@/data/services/attendanceService";
import { formatArabicDate, formatTime } from "@/domain/dateKey";
import { attendanceId } from "@/domain/sessionId";
import type { Attendance, AttendanceStatus } from "@/domain/types";
import { format } from "@/i18n";
import { useI18n } from "@/i18n/I18nProvider";
import { vibrate } from "@/lib/vibrate";

export default function AttendancePage() {
  const { t, locale } = useI18n();
  const router = useRouter();
  const params = useParams();
  const raw = params.sessionId;
  const sessionId = Array.isArray(raw) ? raw[0] : raw;

  const { students, attendance, payments, sessionsById, balances, settings, uid } =
    useTutorData();
  const session = sessionsById.get(sessionId ?? "");

  const [absentIds, setAbsentIds] = useState<Set<string>>(
    () =>
      new Set(
        attendance
          .filter((row) => row.sessionId === sessionId && row.status === "absent")
          .map((row) => row.studentId),
      ),
  );
  const [note, setNote] = useState("");
  const [busy, setBusy] = useState(false);
  const [result, setResult] = useState<LessonResult | null>(null);
  const [message, setMessage] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [leaveOpen, setLeaveOpen] = useState(false);

  const roster = useMemo(
    () =>
      session
        ? students
            .filter(
              (student) =>
                student.groupId === session.groupId &&
                student.active &&
                student.startedAtKey <= session.dateKey,
            )
            .sort((a, b) => a.name.localeCompare(b.name, "ar"))
        : [],
    [students, session],
  );

  const changedRows = useMemo(() => {
    const changes = new Map<string, AttendanceStatus>();
    for (const student of roster) {
      const row: Attendance | undefined = attendance.find(
        (item) => item.sessionId === session?.id && item.studentId === student.id,
      );
      const current: AttendanceStatus = absentIds.has(student.id)
        ? "absent"
        : "present";
      if (current !== (row?.status ?? "present")) {
        changes.set(student.id, current);
      }
    }
    return changes;
  }, [roster, attendance, absentIds, session?.id]);

  const dirty = changedRows.size > 0 || note.trim().length > 0;

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!dirty) return;
    const warn = (event: BeforeUnloadEvent) => {
      event.preventDefault();
      event.returnValue = "";
    };
    window.addEventListener("beforeunload", warn);
    return () => window.removeEventListener("beforeunload", warn);
  }, [dirty]);

  function toggle(studentId: string) {
    setAbsentIds((current) => {
      const next = new Set(current);
      if (next.has(studentId)) {
        next.delete(studentId);
      } else {
        next.add(studentId);
        vibrate();
      }
      return next;
    });
  }

  async function handleSave() {
    if (!uid || !session || busy || roster.length === 0) return;
    setBusy(true);
    setMessage(null);
    const source = { students, sessionsById, attendance, payments, settings };

    try {
      if (session.status === "completed") {
        for (const [studentId, status] of changedRows) {
          await setAttendanceStatus(uid, {
            attendanceId: attendanceId(session.id, studentId),
            sessionId: session.id,
            studentId,
            groupId: session.groupId,
            dateKey: session.dateKey,
            status,
            source,
          });
        }
        setResult({ countedStudents: roster.length, newlyDue: [] });
      } else {
        setResult(
          await saveLesson(uid, {
            sessionId: session.id,
            absentIds: [...absentIds],
            note,
            source,
          }),
        );
        setNote("");
      }
    } catch {
      setMessage(t.forms.saveFailed);
    } finally {
      setBusy(false);
    }
  }

  if (!session) {
    return (
      <div className="flex flex-col gap-4">
        <Link
          href="/schedule"
          className="flex min-h-11 items-center gap-2 text-[13px] text-muted"
        >
          <ArrowRight size={16} aria-hidden />
          {t.schedule.title}
        </Link>
        <EmptyState
          icon={<CalendarDays size={22} aria-hidden />}
          title={t.common.empty}
        />
      </div>
    );
  }

  const presentCount = roster.length - absentIds.size;

  return (
    <div className="flex flex-col gap-5 pb-16">
      <div>
        <button
          type="button"
          onClick={() => (dirty ? setLeaveOpen(true) : router.push("/schedule"))}
          className="mb-2 flex min-h-8 items-center gap-1 text-[13px] text-muted"
        >
          <ArrowRight size={15} aria-hidden />
          {t.schedule.title}
        </button>
        <PageHeader
          title={session.groupName}
          subtitle={`${formatArabicDate(session.dateKey, {
            weekday: "long",
            day: "numeric",
            month: "long",
          })} · ${formatTime(session.time, locale)}`}
          action={
            <StatusChip
              tone={
                session.status === "completed"
                  ? "success"
                  : session.status === "cancelled"
                    ? "danger"
                    : "neutral"
              }
            >
              {session.status === "completed"
                ? t.schedule.completed
                : session.status === "cancelled"
                  ? t.schedule.cancelled
                  : t.schedule.scheduled}
            </StatusChip>
          }
        />
      </div>

      {session.status === "completed" ? (
        <GlassCard className="flex items-center gap-2 p-3 text-[12.5px] text-muted">
          <CheckCircle2 size={15} className="shrink-0 text-info" aria-hidden />
          {t.attendance.alreadyCompleted}
        </GlassCard>
      ) : null}

      {session.note ? (
        <GlassCard className="p-3 text-[12.5px] text-muted">{session.note}</GlassCard>
      ) : null}

      {roster.length === 0 ? (
        <EmptyState
          icon={<UserRound size={22} aria-hidden />}
          title={t.attendance.noStudents}
        />
      ) : (
        <ul className="flex flex-col gap-2">
          {roster.map((student) => {
            const absent = absentIds.has(student.id);
            const balance = balances.get(student.id);
            return (
              <li key={student.id}>
                <GlassCard
                  className={`flex items-center gap-3 px-3.5 py-3 transition-colors ${
                    absent ? "bg-danger/[0.08]" : ""
                  }`}
                >
                  <CounterRing
                    position={balance?.cyclePosition ?? 0}
                    length={settings.cycleLength}
                  />
                  <div className="min-w-0 flex-1">
                    <p className="truncate text-[14.5px] font-medium">
                      {student.name}
                    </p>
                    <div className="mt-1 flex items-center gap-2">
                      <NumberText className="text-[12px] text-muted">
                        {balance?.cyclePosition ?? 0}/{settings.cycleLength}
                      </NumberText>
                      {balance ? (
                        <BalanceChip
                          balance={balance}
                          t={t}
                          cycleLength={settings.cycleLength}
                        />
                      ) : null}
                    </div>
                  </div>
                  <button
                    type="button"
                    aria-label={student.name}
                    aria-pressed={absent}
                    onClick={() => toggle(student.id)}
                    className={`press flex min-h-11 min-w-[76px] items-center justify-center gap-1 rounded-btn px-3 text-[13px] font-semibold ${
                      absent
                        ? "bg-danger text-white"
                        : "bg-primary/15 text-primary"
                    }`}
                  >
                    {absent ? (
                      <X size={15} aria-hidden />
                    ) : (
                      <Check size={15} aria-hidden />
                    )}
                    {absent ? t.attendance.absent : t.attendance.present}
                  </button>
                </GlassCard>
              </li>
            );
          })}
        </ul>
      )}

      <GlassCard className="flex flex-col gap-1.5 p-4">
        <Field label={t.attendance.note} hint={t.common.optional}>
          <textarea
            rows={2}
            className={`${inputClass} min-h-16 py-2`}
            value={note}
            onChange={(event) => setNote(event.target.value)}
          />
        </Field>
      </GlassCard>

      {message ? (
        <p role="alert" className="text-[12.5px] text-danger">
          {message}
        </p>
      ) : null}

      {roster.length > 0 ? (
        <div className="fixed inset-x-0 bottom-14 z-30 mx-auto w-full max-w-md px-4">
          <GlassCard className="flex items-center gap-3 p-2">
            <div className="shrink-0 px-1 text-center">
              <p className="text-[11px] text-muted">{t.today.studentsCount}</p>
              <p className="text-[15px] font-semibold">
                <NumberText>{presentCount}</NumberText>
                <span className="text-muted">/{roster.length}</span>
              </p>
            </div>
            <div className="flex-1">
              <BigPrimaryButton
                onClick={() => void handleSave()}
                disabled={busy || !dirty}
              >
                {busy ? (
                  <Loader2 size={16} className="animate-spin" aria-hidden />
                ) : null}
                {session.status === "completed"
                  ? t.attendance.updateLesson
                  : t.attendance.saveLesson}
              </BigPrimaryButton>
            </div>
          </GlassCard>
        </div>
      ) : null}

      <Sheet
        open={result !== null}
        onClose={() => setResult(null)}
        title={t.attendance.savedTitle}
      >
        <div className="flex flex-col gap-3">
          <p className="text-[13.5px] leading-6 text-muted">
            {result && result.newlyDue.length > 0
              ? format(t.attendance.newlyDue, { n: result.newlyDue.length })
              : t.attendance.savedBody}
          </p>
          {result?.newlyDue.length ? (
            <ul className="flex flex-col gap-2">
              {result.newlyDue.map((id) => {
                const student = students.find((item) => item.id === id);
                if (!student) return null;
                return (
                  <li key={id}>
                    <Link
                      href={`/students/${id}`}
                      className="press flex min-h-12 items-center justify-between gap-2 rounded-btn bg-white/55 px-3"
                    >
                      <span className="truncate text-[14px] font-medium">
                        {student.name}
                      </span>
                      <span className="text-[12.5px] font-medium text-primary-dark">
                        {t.attendance.recordPaymentNow}
                      </span>
                    </Link>
                  </li>
                );
              })}
            </ul>
          ) : null}
          <BigPrimaryButton onClick={() => setResult(null)}>
            {t.common.close}
          </BigPrimaryButton>
        </div>
      </Sheet>

      <Sheet
        open={leaveOpen}
        onClose={() => setLeaveOpen(false)}
        title={t.attendance.unsavedTitle}
      >
        <ConfirmCard
          title={t.attendance.unsavedTitle}
          body={t.attendance.unsavedBody}
          confirmLabel={t.attendance.discard}
          cancelLabel={t.attendance.keepEditing}
          tone="danger"
          onCancel={() => setLeaveOpen(false)}
          onConfirm={() => router.push("/schedule")}
        />
      </Sheet>
    </div>
  );
}
````

### `src/app/(app)/dev/page.tsx`  ·  233 lines

````tsx
"use client";

import { useRef, useState, useEffect } from "react";
import Link from "next/link";
import {
  Database,
  Loader2,
  Palette,
  RefreshCcw,
  Trash2,
  UserX,
  CalendarDays,
  Bug,
} from "lucide-react";
import { BigPrimaryButton } from "@/components/ui/BigPrimaryButton";
import { GlassCard } from "@/components/ui/GlassCard";
import { PageHeader } from "@/components/ui/Headers";
import { NumberText } from "@/components/ui/NumberText";
import {
  clearDemoData,
  corruptCounters,
  seedDemoData,
  setAttendanceStatus,
} from "@/data/services/devSeedService";
import { reconcileCounters } from "@/data/services/counterService";
import { ensureGenerated } from "@/data/services/sessionGenerator";
import { useTutorData } from "@/data/provider/hooks";
import { todayKey } from "@/domain/dateKey";
import { attendanceId } from "@/domain/sessionId";
import { useI18n } from "@/i18n/I18nProvider";

export default function DevPage() {
  const { t } = useI18n();
  const data = useTutorData();
  const [busy, setBusy] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [message, setMessage] = useState<string | null>(null);

  const dataRef = useRef(data);
  useEffect(() => {
    dataRef.current = data;
  }, [data]);

  const uid = data.uid;

  async function run(label: string, action: () => Promise<unknown>) {
    if (!uid || busy) return;
    setBusy(label);
    setMessage(null);
    try {
      const result = await action();
      setMessage(
        typeof result === "number" ? `✓ ${label} (${result})` : `✓ ${label}`,
      );
    } catch (error) {
      setMessage(
        `✕ ${label}: ${error instanceof Error ? error.message : "error"}`,
      );
    } finally {
      setBusy(null);
    }
  }

  async function toggleAbsence() {
    if (!uid) return;
    const session = data.sessions.find((item) => item.status === "completed");
    const student = session
      ? data.students.find((item) => item.groupId === session.groupId)
      : undefined;
    if (!session || !student) {
      setMessage("✕ no completed session/student — seed data first");
      return;
    }

    const existing = data.attendance.find(
      (row) => row.sessionId === session.id && row.studentId === student.id,
    );
    const nextStatus = existing?.status === "absent" ? "present" : "absent";
    const id = existing?.id ?? attendanceId(session.id, student.id);

    await setAttendanceStatus({
      uid,
      attendanceId: id,
      sessionId: session.id,
      studentId: student.id,
      groupId: session.groupId,
      dateKey: session.dateKey,
      status: nextStatus,
    });

    // Let the snapshot land (works offline via latency compensation), then
    // reconcile the cached counters from the hydrated state.
    window.setTimeout(() => {
      void reconcileCounters(uid, dataRef.current);
    }, 900);
  }

  if (process.env.NODE_ENV === "production") {
    return null;
  }

  return (
    <div className="flex flex-col gap-4">
      <PageHeader title={t.dev.title} subtitle={t.dev.body} />

      <GlassCard className="flex flex-col gap-2 p-4 text-[13px]">
        <Row label={t.dev.users} value={data.students.length} />
        <Row label={t.dev.groups} value={data.groups.length} />
        <Row label={t.dev.sessions} value={data.sessions.length} />
        <Row label={t.dev.attendance} value={data.attendance.length} />
        <Row label={t.dev.payments} value={data.payments.length} />
        <div className="mt-1 flex flex-wrap gap-2 text-[12px] text-muted">
          <span className="rounded-full bg-white/60 px-2 py-1">
            {t.dev.online}: {data.online ? "✓" : "✕"}
          </span>
          <span className="rounded-full bg-white/60 px-2 py-1">
            {t.dev.cache}: {data.fromCache ? "✓" : "✕"}
          </span>
          <span className="rounded-full bg-white/60 px-2 py-1">
            {t.dev.state}: {data.status}
          </span>
        </div>
      </GlassCard>

      <div className="flex flex-col gap-2">
        <BigPrimaryButton
          onClick={() => void run(t.dev.seed, () => seedDemoData(uid!))}
          disabled={!uid || busy !== null}
        >
          {busy === t.dev.seed ? (
            <Loader2 size={16} className="animate-spin" aria-hidden />
          ) : (
            <Database size={16} aria-hidden />
          )}
          {t.dev.seed}
        </BigPrimaryButton>

        <BigPrimaryButton
          variant="ghost"
          onClick={() => void run(t.dev.toggleAbsence, toggleAbsence)}
          disabled={!uid || busy !== null}
        >
          <UserX size={16} aria-hidden />
          {t.dev.toggleAbsence}
        </BigPrimaryButton>

        <BigPrimaryButton
          variant="ghost"
          onClick={() =>
            void run(t.dev.recompute, () => reconcileCounters(uid!, data))
          }
          disabled={!uid || busy !== null}
        >
          <RefreshCcw size={16} aria-hidden />
          {t.dev.recompute}
        </BigPrimaryButton>

        <BigPrimaryButton
          variant="ghost"
          onClick={() =>
            void run(t.dev.regenerate, () =>
              ensureGenerated(uid!, {
                groups: data.groups,
                sessions: data.sessions,
                today: todayKey(),
              }),
            )
          }
          disabled={!uid || busy !== null}
        >
          <CalendarDays size={16} aria-hidden />
          {t.dev.regenerate}
        </BigPrimaryButton>

        <BigPrimaryButton
          variant="ghost"
          onClick={() =>
            void run(t.dev.corrupt, () =>
              corruptCounters(uid!, data.students[0].id),
            )
          }
          disabled={!uid || data.students.length === 0 || busy !== null}
        >
          <Bug size={16} aria-hidden />
          {t.dev.corrupt}
        </BigPrimaryButton>

        <BigPrimaryButton
          variant="ghost"
          onClick={() =>
            void run(t.dev.clear, () =>
              clearDemoData(uid!, {
                groupIds: data.groups.map((group) => group.id),
                studentIds: data.students.map((student) => student.id),
                sessionIds: data.sessions.map((session) => session.id),
                attendanceIds: data.attendance.map((row) => row.id),
                paymentIds: data.payments.map((payment) => payment.id),
              }),
            )
          }
          disabled={!uid || busy !== null}
        >
          <Trash2 size={16} aria-hidden />
          {t.dev.clear}
        </BigPrimaryButton>

        <Link
          href="/dev/style"
          className="press flex min-h-12 w-full items-center justify-center gap-2 rounded-btn border border-white/70 bg-white/60 text-[15px] font-semibold text-ink"
        >
          <Palette size={16} aria-hidden />
          {t.dev.styleGallery}
        </Link>
      </div>

      {message ? (
        <GlassCard className="text-[12.5px] text-muted">
          <p>{message}</p>
        </GlassCard>
      ) : null}
    </div>
  );
}

function Row({ label, value }: { label: string; value: number }) {
  return (
    <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
      <span className="text-muted">{label}</span>
      <span className="font-semibold">
        <NumberText>{value}</NumberText>
      </span>
    </div>
  );
}
````

````tsx
"use client";

import Link from "next/link";
import { ArrowRight } from "lucide-react";
import { BigPrimaryButton } from "@/components/ui/BigPrimaryButton";
import { CounterRing } from "@/components/ui/CounterRing";
import { EmptyState } from "@/components/ui/EmptyState";
import { GlassCard } from "@/components/ui/GlassCard";
import { PageHeader, SectionHeader } from "@/components/ui/Headers";
import { NumberText } from "@/components/ui/NumberText";
import { StatusChip } from "@/components/ui/StatusChip";
import { formatMoney } from "@/domain/money";
import { useI18n } from "@/i18n/I18nProvider";

const swatches = [
  { name: "canvas", className: "bg-canvas" },
  { name: "sand", className: "bg-sand" },
  { name: "sage-soft", className: "bg-sage-soft" },
  { name: "clay-soft", className: "bg-clay-soft" },
  { name: "primary", className: "bg-primary" },
  { name: "primary-dark", className: "bg-primary-dark" },
  { name: "accent", className: "bg-accent" },
  { name: "ink", className: "bg-ink" },
  { name: "muted", className: "bg-muted" },
  { name: "success", className: "bg-success" },
  { name: "warning", className: "bg-warning" },
  { name: "danger", className: "bg-danger" },
  { name: "info", className: "bg-info" },
];

export default function StyleGalleryPage() {
  const { t } = useI18n();

  return (
    <div className="flex flex-col gap-5">
      <div>
        <Link
          href="/dev"
          className="mb-2 flex min-h-8 items-center gap-1 text-[13px] text-muted"
        >
          <ArrowRight size={15} aria-hidden />
          {t.dev.title}
        </Link>
        <PageHeader
          title={t.dev.styleGallery}
          subtitle={t.dev.styleGalleryBody}
        />
      </div>

      <section>
        <SectionHeader title={t.dev.styleGallery} />
        <GlassCard className="grid grid-cols-4 gap-2 p-3">
          {swatches.map((swatch) => (
            <div key={swatch.name} className="flex flex-col items-center gap-1">
              <span
                className={`h-10 w-full rounded-btn border border-white/60 ${swatch.className}`}
              />
              <span className="text-[10px] text-muted">{swatch.name}</span>
            </div>
          ))}
        </GlassCard>
      </section>

      <section>
        <SectionHeader title={t.schedule.title} />
        <GlassCard className="flex flex-col gap-2 p-4">
          <p className="text-[15px] font-semibold">{t.common.appName}</p>
          <p className="text-[13px] text-muted">{t.common.appTagline}</p>
          <p className="text-[13px]">
            {t.today.dueAmount}:{" "}
            <NumberText className="font-semibold">
              {formatMoney(1250, t.common.currency)}
            </NumberText>
          </p>
        </GlassCard>
      </section>

      <section>
        <SectionHeader title={t.attendance.present} />
        <GlassCard className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2 p-3">
          <StatusChip tone="success">{t.attendance.present}</StatusChip>
          <StatusChip tone="danger">{t.attendance.absent}</StatusChip>
          <StatusChip tone="warning">{t.schedule.scheduled}</StatusChip>
          <StatusChip tone="info">{t.schedule.extra}</StatusChip>
          <StatusChip tone="neutral">{t.groups.archived}</StatusChip>
        </GlassCard>
      </section>

      <section>
        <SectionHeader title={t.groups.title} />
        <GlassCard className="flex items-center justify-around gap-3 p-4">
          <CounterRing position={0} length={4} />
          <CounterRing position={3} length={4} />
          <CounterRing position={4} length={4} size={56} />
          <CounterRing position={6} length={8} size={56} />
        </GlassCard>
      </section>

      <section className="flex flex-col gap-2">
        <BigPrimaryButton>{t.common.save}</BigPrimaryButton>
        <BigPrimaryButton variant="ghost">{t.common.cancel}</BigPrimaryButton>
      </section>

      <EmptyState
        icon={<ArrowRight size={20} aria-hidden />}
        title={t.common.empty}
        body={t.common.errorBody}
      />
    </div>
  );
}
````

### `src/app/(app)/dues/page.tsx`  ·  176 lines

````tsx
"use client";

import Link from "next/link";
import { useState } from "react";
import { CheckCircle2, MessageCircle, Wallet } from "lucide-react";
import { PaymentSheet } from "@/components/features/PaymentSheet";
import { EmptyState } from "@/components/ui/EmptyState";
import { GlassCard } from "@/components/ui/GlassCard";
import { PageHeader, SectionHeader } from "@/components/ui/Headers";
import { NumberText } from "@/components/ui/NumberText";
import { StatusChip } from "@/components/ui/StatusChip";
import { useDueStudents, useTutorData } from "@/data/provider/hooks";
import { formatMoney } from "@/domain/money";
import type { Student, StudentBalance } from "@/domain/types";
import { format } from "@/i18n";
import { useI18n } from "@/i18n/I18nProvider";

type PayTarget = { student: Student; balance: StudentBalance };

export default function DuesPage() {
  const { t } = useI18n();
  const { groups, settings } = useTutorData();
  const { due, almostDue, total } = useDueStudents();

  const [payTarget, setPayTarget] = useState<PayTarget | null>(null);
  const [notice, setNotice] = useState<string | null>(null);

  const groupName = (groupId: string) =>
    groups.find((group) => group.id === groupId)?.name ?? "—";

  function reminder(target: PayTarget) {
    const text = format(t.dues.reminderMessage, {
      name: target.student.name,
      amount: formatMoney(target.balance.amountDue, t.common.currency),
    });
    void navigator.clipboard?.writeText(text).catch(() => {});
    setNotice(t.dues.copied);
    const phone = target.student.phone?.replace(/[^\d]/g, "") ?? "";
    window.open(
      `https://wa.me/${phone}?text=${encodeURIComponent(text)}`,
      "_blank",
      "noreferrer",
    );
  }

  return (
    <div className="flex flex-col gap-5">
      <PageHeader title={t.dues.title} />

      <GlassCard className="flex items-center gap-3 p-4">
        <span className="flex h-11 w-11 items-center justify-center rounded-full bg-danger/10 text-danger">
          <Wallet size={18} aria-hidden />
        </span>
        <div>
          <p className="text-[12.5px] text-muted">{t.dues.total}</p>
          <p className="text-[20px] font-bold">
            <NumberText>{formatMoney(total, t.common.currency)}</NumberText>
          </p>
        </div>
      </GlassCard>

      {notice ? (
        <p role="status" className="text-[12.5px] text-primary-dark">
          {notice}
        </p>
      ) : null}

      {due.length === 0 ? (
        <EmptyState
          icon={<CheckCircle2 size={22} aria-hidden />}
          title={t.dues.noDues}
          body={t.dues.noDuesBody}
        />
      ) : (
        <section>
          <SectionHeader title={t.dues.title} />
          <ul className="flex flex-col gap-2">
            {due.map((target) => (
              <li key={target.student.id}>
                <GlassCard className="flex flex-col gap-2 p-3.5">
                  <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
                    <Link
                      href={`/students/${target.student.id}`}
                      className="min-w-0 flex-1"
                    >
                      <p className="truncate text-[14.5px] font-semibold">
                        {target.student.name}
                      </p>
                      <p className="text-[12px] text-muted">
                        {groupName(target.student.groupId)}
                        <span className="mx-1" aria-hidden>
                          ·
                        </span>
                        <NumberText>{target.balance.balance}</NumberText>{" "}
                        {t.dues.sessionsSincePayment}
                      </p>
                    </Link>
                    <StatusChip tone="danger">
                      <NumberText>
                        {formatMoney(target.balance.amountDue, t.common.currency)}
                      </NumberText>
                    </StatusChip>
                  </div>
                  <div className="flex gap-2">
                    <button
                      type="button"
                      onClick={() => setPayTarget(target)}
                      className="press flex min-h-10 flex-1 items-center justify-center gap-1.5 rounded-btn bg-primary text-[12.5px] font-semibold text-white"
                    >
                      <Wallet size={14} aria-hidden />
                      {t.dues.record}
                    </button>
                    <button
                      type="button"
                      onClick={() => reminder(target)}
                      className="press flex min-h-10 flex-1 items-center justify-center gap-1.5 rounded-btn border border-white/70 bg-white/60 text-[12.5px] font-medium"
                    >
                      <MessageCircle size={14} className="text-primary" aria-hidden />
                      {t.dues.whatsapp}
                    </button>
                  </div>
                </GlassCard>
              </li>
            ))}
          </ul>
        </section>
      )}

      {almostDue.length > 0 ? (
        <section>
          <SectionHeader title={t.dues.almostDue} />
          <ul className="flex flex-col gap-2">
            {almostDue.map((target) => (
              <li key={target.student.id}>
                <GlassCard className="flex items-center gap-3 p-3.5">
                  <Link
                    href={`/students/${target.student.id}`}
                    className="min-w-0 flex-1"
                  >
                    <p className="truncate text-[14px] font-medium">
                      {target.student.name}
                    </p>
                    <p className="text-[12px] text-muted">
                      {groupName(target.student.groupId)}
                    </p>
                  </Link>
                  <StatusChip tone="warning">
                    <NumberText>{target.balance.balance}</NumberText>/
                    <NumberText>{settings.cycleLength}</NumberText>
                  </StatusChip>
                  <button
                    type="button"
                    onClick={() => setPayTarget(target)}
                    aria-label={t.dues.record}
                    className="press flex h-10 w-10 shrink-0 items-center justify-center rounded-full bg-primary text-white"
                  >
                    <Wallet size={16} aria-hidden />
                  </button>
                </GlassCard>
              </li>
            ))}
          </ul>
        </section>
      ) : null}

      {payTarget ? (
        <PaymentSheet
          key={payTarget.student.id}
          open
          onClose={() => setPayTarget(null)}
          student={payTarget.student}
        />
      ) : null}
    </div>
  );
}
````

````tsx
"use client";

import { useParams } from "next/navigation";
import { GroupForm } from "@/components/features/GroupForm";
import { EmptyState } from "@/components/ui/EmptyState";
import { FolderOpen } from "lucide-react";
import { useTutorData } from "@/data/provider/hooks";
import { useI18n } from "@/i18n/I18nProvider";

export default function EditGroupPage() {
  const params = useParams();
  const raw = params.groupId;
  const groupId = Array.isArray(raw) ? raw[0] : raw;
  const { groups } = useTutorData();
  const { t } = useI18n();

  const group = groups.find((item) => item.id === groupId);
  if (!group) {
    return (
      <EmptyState
        icon={<FolderOpen size={22} aria-hidden />}
        title={t.common.empty}
      />
    );
  }

  return <GroupForm group={group} />;
}
````

````tsx
"use client";

import Link from "next/link";
import { useParams } from "next/navigation";
import { useState } from "react";
import {
  Archive,
  ArchiveRestore,
  ArrowRight,
  Clock,
  FolderOpen,
  Pencil,
  UserRound,
} from "lucide-react";
import { BalanceChip } from "@/components/features/BalanceChip";
import { ConfirmCard } from "@/components/ui/ConfirmCard";
import { CounterRing } from "@/components/ui/CounterRing";
import { EmptyState } from "@/components/ui/EmptyState";
import { GlassCard } from "@/components/ui/GlassCard";
import { PageHeader, SectionHeader } from "@/components/ui/Headers";
import { NumberText } from "@/components/ui/NumberText";
import { Sheet } from "@/components/ui/Sheet";
import { StatusChip } from "@/components/ui/StatusChip";
import { useTutorData } from "@/data/provider/hooks";
import { setGroupActive } from "@/data/services/groupService";
import { useI18n } from "@/i18n/I18nProvider";
import { weekdayLabel } from "@/lib/weekdays";

export default function GroupDetailPage() {
  const { t } = useI18n();
  const params = useParams();
  const raw = params.groupId;
  const groupId = Array.isArray(raw) ? raw[0] : raw;

  const { groups, students, balances, settings, uid } = useTutorData();
  const [confirmOpen, setConfirmOpen] = useState(false);
  const [busy, setBusy] = useState(false);
  const [actionError, setActionError] = useState(false);
  const group = groups.find((item) => item.id === groupId);
  const groupStudents = students
    .filter((student) => student.groupId === groupId && student.active)
    .sort((a, b) => a.name.localeCompare(b.name, "ar"));

  if (!group) {
    return (
      <div className="flex flex-col gap-4">
        <Link
          href="/groups"
          className="flex min-h-11 items-center gap-2 text-[13px] text-muted"
        >
          <ArrowRight size={16} aria-hidden />
          {t.groups.title}
        </Link>
        <EmptyState
          icon={<FolderOpen size={22} aria-hidden />}
          title={t.common.empty}
        />
      </div>
    );
  }

  return (
    <div className="flex flex-col gap-5">
      <div>
        <Link
          href="/groups"
          className="mb-2 flex min-h-8 items-center gap-1 text-[13px] text-muted"
        >
          <ArrowRight size={15} aria-hidden />
          {t.groups.title}
        </Link>
        <PageHeader
          title={group.name}
          subtitle={group.subject}
          action={
            !group.active ? (
              <StatusChip tone="neutral">{t.groups.archived}</StatusChip>
            ) : undefined
          }
        />
      </div>

      <GlassCard className="flex flex-col gap-2 p-4">
        <p className="text-[13px] font-medium text-muted">{t.groups.slots}</p>
        {group.slots.length === 0 ? (
          <p className="text-[13px] text-muted">{t.common.empty}</p>
        ) : (
          <ul className="flex flex-col gap-2">
            {group.slots.map((slot, index) => (
              <li
                key={`${slot.weekday}-${slot.time}-${index}`}
                className="flex items-center gap-2 text-[14px]"
              >
                <Clock size={15} className="text-primary" aria-hidden />
                {weekdayLabel(t, slot.weekday)}
                <span className="text-muted">·</span>
                <NumberText>{slot.time}</NumberText>
                <span className="text-muted">·</span>
                <NumberText>{slot.durationMin}</NumberText>
                <span className="text-[12px] text-muted">
                  {t.groups.minutesShort}
                </span>
              </li>
            ))}
          </ul>
        )}
      </GlassCard>

      <div className="flex gap-2">
        <Link
          href={`/groups/${group.id}/edit`}
          className="press flex min-h-12 flex-1 items-center justify-center gap-2 rounded-btn border border-white/70 bg-white/60 text-[14px] font-medium"
        >
          <Pencil size={16} className="text-primary" aria-hidden />
          {t.forms.editAction}
        </Link>
        <button
          type="button"
          onClick={() => setConfirmOpen(true)}
          className="press flex min-h-12 flex-1 items-center justify-center gap-2 rounded-btn border border-white/70 bg-white/60 text-[14px] font-medium"
        >
          {group.active ? (
            <Archive size={16} className="text-danger" aria-hidden />
          ) : (
            <ArchiveRestore size={16} className="text-primary" aria-hidden />
          )}
          {group.active ? t.forms.archiveAction : t.forms.unarchiveAction}
        </button>
      </div>

      <Sheet
        open={confirmOpen}
        onClose={() => setConfirmOpen(false)}
        title={group.active ? t.groups.archive : t.forms.unarchiveAction}
      >
        <ConfirmCard
          title={
            group.active
              ? t.forms.confirmArchiveGroupTitle
              : t.forms.confirmUnarchiveTitle
          }
          body={
            group.active
              ? t.forms.confirmArchiveGroupBody
              : t.forms.confirmUnarchiveBody
          }
          confirmLabel={
            group.active ? t.forms.archiveAction : t.forms.unarchiveAction
          }
          cancelLabel={t.common.cancel}
          tone={group.active ? "danger" : "primary"}
          busy={busy}
          onCancel={() => setConfirmOpen(false)}
          onConfirm={() => {
            if (!uid) return;
            setBusy(true);
            setActionError(false);
            void setGroupActive(uid, group.id, !group.active)
              .then(() => setConfirmOpen(false))
              .catch(() => setActionError(true))
              .finally(() => setBusy(false));
          }}
        />
      </Sheet>

      {actionError ? (
        <p role="alert" className="text-[12.5px] text-danger">
          {t.forms.saveFailed}
        </p>
      ) : null}

      <section>
        <SectionHeader
          title={t.students.title}
          action={
            <Link
              href={`/students/new?groupId=${group.id}`}
              className="press flex min-h-9 items-center gap-1 rounded-full bg-primary px-3 text-[12.5px] font-semibold text-white"
            >
              <UserRound size={14} aria-hidden />
              {t.students.addStudent}
            </Link>
          }
        />
        {groupStudents.length === 0 ? (
          <EmptyState
            icon={<UserRound size={20} aria-hidden />}
            title={t.students.noStudents}
          />
        ) : (
          <ul className="flex flex-col gap-2">
            {groupStudents.map((student) => {
              const balance = balances.get(student.id);
              return (
                <li key={student.id}>
                  <Link href={`/students/${student.id}`} className="press block">
                    <GlassCard className="flex items-center gap-3 px-3.5 py-3">
                      <CounterRing
                        position={balance?.cyclePosition ?? 0}
                        length={settings.cycleLength}
                      />
                      <p className="min-w-0 flex-1 truncate text-[14px] font-medium">
                        {student.name}
                      </p>
                      {balance ? (
                        <BalanceChip
                          balance={balance}
                          t={t}
                          cycleLength={settings.cycleLength}
                        />
                      ) : null}
                    </GlassCard>
                  </Link>
                </li>
              );
            })}
          </ul>
        )}
      </section>
    </div>
  );
}
````

````tsx
"use client";

import Link from "next/link";
import { FolderOpen, Plus, Users } from "lucide-react";
import { EmptyState } from "@/components/ui/EmptyState";
import { GlassCard } from "@/components/ui/GlassCard";
import { PageHeader } from "@/components/ui/Headers";
import { NumberText } from "@/components/ui/NumberText";
import { StatusChip } from "@/components/ui/StatusChip";
import { useTutorData } from "@/data/provider/hooks";
import { computeGroupStatus } from "@/domain/groupPosition";
import { useI18n } from "@/i18n/I18nProvider";
import { weekdayLabel } from "@/lib/weekdays";

export default function GroupsPage() {
  const { t } = useI18n();
  const { groups, students, balances, settings } = useTutorData();

  const sorted = [...groups].sort(
    (a, b) => Number(b.active) - Number(a.active) || a.name.localeCompare(b.name, "ar"),
  );

  return (
    <div className="flex flex-col gap-4">
      <PageHeader
        title={t.groups.title}
        subtitle={`${groups.filter((group) => group.active).length} ${t.students.active}`}
        action={
          <Link
            href="/groups/new"
            className="press flex min-h-9 items-center gap-1 rounded-full bg-primary px-3 text-[12.5px] font-semibold text-white"
          >
            <Plus size={15} aria-hidden />
            {t.groups.addGroup}
          </Link>
        }
      />

      {sorted.length === 0 ? (
        <EmptyState
          icon={<FolderOpen size={22} aria-hidden />}
          title={t.groups.noGroups}
          body={t.groups.noGroupsBody}
        />
      ) : (
        <ul className="flex flex-col gap-3">
          {sorted.map((group) => {
            const groupStudents = students.filter(
              (student) => student.groupId === group.id && student.active,
            );
            const status = computeGroupStatus(groupStudents, balances);
            return (
              <li key={group.id}>
                <Link href={`/groups/${group.id}`} className="press block">
                  <GlassCard className="flex flex-col gap-2 p-4">
                    <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
                      <span className="flex h-9 w-9 items-center justify-center rounded-full bg-primary/10 text-primary">
                        <FolderOpen size={16} aria-hidden />
                      </span>
                      <p className="flex-1 truncate text-[15px] font-semibold">
                        {group.name}
                      </p>
                      {!group.active ? (
                        <StatusChip tone="neutral">{t.groups.archived}</StatusChip>
                      ) : null}
                      {status.position > 0 ? (
                        <StatusChip tone="info">
                          <NumberText>
                            {status.position}
                          </NumberText>
                          /<NumberText>{settings.cycleLength}</NumberText>
                        </StatusChip>
                      ) : null}
                    </div>

                    <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-x-3 gap-y-1 text-[12.5px] text-muted">
                      <span className="inline-flex items-center gap-1">
                        <Users size={13} aria-hidden />
                        <NumberText>{groupStudents.length}</NumberText>{" "}
                        {t.groups.studentsCount}
                      </span>
                      {group.slots.map((slot, index) => (
                        <span key={`${slot.weekday}-${slot.time}-${index}`}>
                          {weekdayLabel(t, slot.weekday)} ·{" "}
                          <NumberText>{slot.time}</NumberText>
                        </span>
                      ))}
                    </div>

                    {status.outliers.length > 0 ? (
                      <p className="text-[12px] text-warning">
                        {t.groups.outliers}:{" "}
                        {status.outliers
                          .map(
                            (outlier) =>
                              `${outlier.name} ${outlier.position}/${settings.cycleLength}`,
                          )
                          .join(" · ")}
                      </p>
                    ) : null}
                  </GlassCard>
                </Link>
              </li>
            );
          })}
        </ul>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}
````

````tsx
"use client";

import { useEffect, type ReactNode } from "react";
import { useRouter } from "next/navigation";
import { BookOpen } from "lucide-react";
import { Backdrop } from "@/components/ui/Backdrop";
import { BottomBar } from "@/components/ui/BottomBar";
import { ErrorState } from "@/components/ui/ErrorState";
import { FloatingAiButton } from "@/components/ui/FloatingAiButton";
import { GlassCard } from "@/components/ui/GlassCard";
import { OfflineBar } from "@/components/ui/OfflineBar";
import { SkeletonList } from "@/components/ui/Skeleton";
import { useAuth } from "@/data/auth/AuthProvider";
import { useTutorData } from "@/data/provider/hooks";
import { TutorDataProvider } from "@/data/provider/TutorDataProvider";
import { useI18n } from "@/i18n/I18nProvider";

function BootScreen({ label }: { label?: string }) {
  return (
    <div className="relative mx-auto flex min-h-dvh w-full max-w-md flex-col justify-center gap-4 px-5">
      <Backdrop />
      <div className="flex flex-col items-center gap-3 text-center">
        <div className="flex h-14 w-14 items-center justify-center rounded-card bg-primary text-white">
          <BookOpen size={26} aria-hidden />
        </div>
        <p className="text-[13px] text-muted">{label}</p>
      </div>
      <SkeletonList rows={2} />
    </div>
  );
}

function Shell({ children }: { children: ReactNode }) {
  const { t } = useI18n();
  const data = useTutorData();

  return (
    <div className="relative mx-auto flex min-h-dvh w-full max-w-md flex-col">
      <Backdrop />
      <main className="safe-top flex-1 px-4 pt-4 pb-28">
        <OfflineBar offline={!data.online} />
        {data.status === "loading" ? (
          <SkeletonList rows={4} />
        ) : data.status === "error" ? (
          <ErrorState
            title={t.errors.loadFailed}
            body={t.errors.loadFailedHint}
            retryLabel={t.common.retry}
            onRetry={() => window.location.reload()}
          />
        ) : (
          children
        )}
      </main>
      <FloatingAiButton />
      <BottomBar />
    </div>
  );
}

export default function AppLayout({ children }: { children: ReactNode }) {
  const { t } = useI18n();
  const { user, loading, configured } = useAuth();
  const router = useRouter();

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!loading && configured && !user) {
      router.replace("/sign-in");
    }
  }, [loading, configured, user, router]);

  if (!configured) {
    return (
      <div className="relative mx-auto flex min-h-dvh w-full max-w-md flex-col justify-center px-5">
        <Backdrop />
        <GlassCard className="p-5">
          <h1 className="text-[20px] font-bold">{t.auth.missingConfigTitle}</h1>
          <p className="mt-2 text-[13.5px] leading-6 text-muted">
            {t.auth.missingConfigBody}
          </p>
        </GlassCard>
      </div>
    );
  }

  if (loading || !user) {
    return <BootScreen label={t.common.loading} />;
  }

  return (
    <TutorDataProvider>
      <Shell>{children}</Shell>
    </TutorDataProvider>
  );
}
````

````tsx
"use client";

import Link from "next/link";
import { useMemo } from "react";
import {
  CalendarDays,
  ChevronLeft,
  Clock,
  Users,
  Wallet,
} from "lucide-react";
import { BalanceChip } from "@/components/features/BalanceChip";
import { EmptyState } from "@/components/ui/EmptyState";
import { GlassCard } from "@/components/ui/GlassCard";
import { PageHeader, SectionHeader } from "@/components/ui/Headers";
import { NumberText } from "@/components/ui/NumberText";
import { StatusChip } from "@/components/ui/StatusChip";
import { useToday, useTutorData } from "@/data/provider/hooks";
import { formatArabicDate, formatTime, todayKey } from "@/domain/dateKey";
import { computeGroupStatus } from "@/domain/groupPosition";
import { formatMoney } from "@/domain/money";
import { format } from "@/i18n";
import { useI18n } from "@/i18n/I18nProvider";
import { useMounted } from "@/lib/useMounted";

export default function TodayPage() {
  const { t, locale } = useI18n();
  const { students, groups, settings, balances } = useTutorData();
  const { sessions: todaysSessions, reminder, dueCount, dueTotal } = useToday();

  const mounted = useMounted();
  const hour = new Date().getHours();
  const greeting =
    hour < 12
      ? t.today.greetingMorning
      : hour < 18
        ? t.today.greetingAfternoon
        : t.today.greetingEvening;

  // Clock-dependent copy is only rendered after hydration (no mismatch, no
  // setState inside an effect).
  const header = mounted
    ? { greeting, date: formatArabicDate(todayKey()) }
    : { greeting: t.today.title, date: "" };

  const activeStudents = students.filter((student) => student.active);
  const statuses = useMemo(
    () =>
      new Map(
        groups.map((group) => [
          group.id,
          computeGroupStatus(
            students.filter((student) => student.groupId === group.id),
            balances,
          ),
        ]),
      ),
    [groups, students, balances],
  );

  const groupName = (groupId: string) =>
    groups.find((group) => group.id === groupId)?.name ?? "—";

  return (
    <div className="flex flex-col gap-5">
      <PageHeader
        title={header?.greeting ?? t.today.title}
        subtitle={header?.date}
      />

      {activeStudents.length === 0 ? (
        <EmptyState
          icon={<Users size={22} aria-hidden />}
          title={t.today.firstRunTitle}
          body={t.today.firstRunBody}
          action={
            <Link
              href="/groups"
              className="press flex min-h-12 items-center justify-center rounded-btn bg-primary px-4 text-[15px] font-semibold text-white"
            >
              {t.today.goToGroups}
            </Link>
          }
        />
      ) : (
        <>
          <section>
            <SectionHeader
              title={t.today.lessonsTitle}
              action={
                <Link
                  href="/schedule"
                  className="text-[13px] font-medium text-primary-dark"
                >
                  {t.nav.schedule}
                </Link>
              }
            />
            {todaysSessions.length === 0 ? (
              <EmptyState
                icon={<CalendarDays size={22} aria-hidden />}
                title={t.today.noLessons}
                body={t.today.noLessonsBody}
              />
            ) : (
              <ul className="flex flex-col gap-3">
                {todaysSessions.map((session) => {
                  const count = students.filter(
                    (student) =>
                      student.active && student.groupId === session.groupId,
                  ).length;
                  const tone =
                    session.status === "completed"
                      ? "success"
                      : session.status === "cancelled"
                        ? "danger"
                        : "neutral";
                  const label =
                    session.status === "completed"
                      ? t.schedule.completed
                      : session.status === "cancelled"
                        ? t.schedule.cancelled
                        : t.schedule.scheduled;

                  return (
                    <li key={session.id}>
                      <Link
                        href={`/attendance/${session.id}`}
                        className="press block"
                      >
                        <GlassCard className="flex items-center gap-3 p-4">
                          <div className="flex h-11 w-11 items-center justify-center rounded-full bg-primary/10 text-primary">
                            <Clock size={18} aria-hidden />
                          </div>
                          <div className="min-w-0 flex-1">
                            <p className="truncate text-[15px] font-semibold">
                              {session.groupName}
                            </p>
                            <p className="mt-0.5 flex items-center gap-2 text-[12.5px] text-muted">
                              <NumberText>
                                {formatTime(session.time, locale)}
                              </NumberText>
                              <span aria-hidden>·</span>
                              <span>
                                <NumberText>{count}</NumberText>{" "}
                                {t.today.studentsCount}
                              </span>
                            </p>
                          </div>
                          {statuses.get(session.groupId)?.position ? (
                            <StatusChip tone="info">
                              {format(t.students.sessionOf, {
                                position: statuses.get(session.groupId)?.position ?? 0,
                                length: settings.cycleLength,
                              })}
                            </StatusChip>
                          ) : null}
                          <StatusChip tone={tone}>{label}</StatusChip>
                          <ChevronLeft
                            size={16}
                            className="text-muted rtl:rotate-0 ltr:rotate-180"
                            aria-hidden
                          />
                        </GlassCard>
                      </Link>
                    </li>
                  );
                })}
              </ul>
            )}
          </section>

          {reminder.length > 0 ? (
            <section>
              <SectionHeader title={t.today.reminderTitle} />
              <GlassCard className="flex flex-col gap-3 p-4">
                <p className="text-[12.5px] text-muted">{t.today.reminderBody}</p>
                <ul className="flex flex-col gap-2">
                  {reminder.map(({ studentId, balance }) => {
                    const student = students.find((item) => item.id === studentId);
                    if (!student) return null;
                    return (
                      <li
                        key={student.id}
                        className="flex items-center justify-between gap-2"
                      >
                        <Link
                          href={`/students/${student.id}`}
                          className="min-w-0 truncate text-[14px] font-medium"
                        >
                          {student.name}
                          <span className="ms-2 text-[12px] text-muted">
                            {groupName(student.groupId)}
                          </span>
                        </Link>
                        <BalanceChip
                          balance={balance}
                          t={t}
                          cycleLength={settings.cycleLength}
                        />
                      </li>
                    );
                  })}
                </ul>
              </GlassCard>
            </section>
          ) : null}

          <section>
            <SectionHeader title={t.today.dueTitle} />
            <Link href="/dues" className="press block">
              <GlassCard className="flex items-center gap-3 p-4">
                <div className="flex h-11 w-11 items-center justify-center rounded-full bg-danger/10 text-danger">
                  <Wallet size={18} aria-hidden />
                </div>
                <div className="flex-1">
                  <p className="text-[15px] font-semibold">
                    <NumberText>{dueCount}</NumberText> {t.today.dueBody}
                  </p>
                  <p className="mt-0.5 text-[12.5px] text-muted">
                    {t.today.dueAmount}:{" "}
                    <NumberText>
                      {formatMoney(dueTotal, t.common.currency)}
                    </NumberText>
                  </p>
                </div>
                <ChevronLeft
                  size={16}
                  className="text-muted rtl:rotate-0 ltr:rotate-180"
                  aria-hidden
                />
              </GlassCard>
            </Link>
          </section>

          <section>
            <SectionHeader title={t.today.quickActions} />
            <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-3">
              <Link
                href="/schedule"
                className="press flex min-h-12 items-center justify-center gap-2 rounded-btn border border-white/70 bg-white/60 text-[13px] font-medium"
              >
                <CalendarDays size={16} className="text-primary" aria-hidden />
                {t.today.quickAddExtra}
              </Link>
              <Link
                href="/dues"
                className="press flex min-h-12 items-center justify-center gap-2 rounded-btn border border-white/70 bg-white/60 text-[13px] font-medium"
              >
                <Wallet size={16} className="text-primary" aria-hidden />
                {t.today.quickRecordPayment}
              </Link>
            </div>
          </section>
        </>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}
````
