````tsx
"use client";

import Link from "next/link";
import { useMemo, useState } from "react";
import { CalendarDays, Clock, Plus } from "lucide-react";
import { BigPrimaryButton } from "@/components/ui/BigPrimaryButton";
import { ConfirmCard } from "@/components/ui/ConfirmCard";
import { DateStepper } from "@/components/ui/DateStepper";
import { EmptyState } from "@/components/ui/EmptyState";
import { Field, inputClass } from "@/components/ui/Field";
import { GlassCard } from "@/components/ui/GlassCard";
import { PageHeader } from "@/components/ui/Headers";
import { NumberText } from "@/components/ui/NumberText";
import { Segmented } from "@/components/ui/Segmented";
import { Sheet } from "@/components/ui/Sheet";
import { StatusChip } from "@/components/ui/StatusChip";
import { useTutorData } from "@/data/provider/hooks";
import {
  addExtraSession,
  cancelSession,
} from "@/data/services/sessionService";
import { formatArabicDate, formatTime, nextDateKeys, todayKey } from "@/domain/dateKey";
import { GENERATION_HORIZON_DAYS } from "@/domain/sessionGenerator";
import type { Session } from "@/domain/types";
import { useI18n } from "@/i18n/I18nProvider";
import { groupColor } from "@/lib/groupColor";

type View = "day" | "week";

export default function SchedulePage() {
  const { t, locale } = useI18n();
  const { sessions, groups, uid } = useTutorData();

  const [view, setView] = useState<View>("day");
  const [selected, setSelected] = useState(() => todayKey());
  const [extraOpen, setExtraOpen] = useState(false);
  const [cancelTarget, setCancelTarget] = useState<Session | null>(null);
  const [extraGroupId, setExtraGroupId] = useState("");
  const [extraDate, setExtraDate] = useState(() => todayKey());
  const [extraTime, setExtraTime] = useState("17:00");
  const [cancelNote, setCancelNote] = useState("");
  const [busy, setBusy] = useState(false);
  const [message, setMessage] = useState<string | null>(null);

  const horizon = useMemo(
    () => nextDateKeys(todayKey(), GENERATION_HORIZON_DAYS),
    [],
  );

  const statusFor = (session: Session) => {
    if (session.status === "completed")
      return { tone: "success" as const, label: t.schedule.completed };
    if (session.status === "cancelled")
      return { tone: "danger" as const, label: t.schedule.cancelled };
    return { tone: "neutral" as const, label: t.schedule.scheduled };
  };

  function sessionRow(session: Session) {
    const status = statusFor(session);
    return (
      <li key={session.id}>
        <GlassCard className="flex items-center gap-3 p-3.5">
          <Link href={`/attendance/${session.id}`} className="press flex min-w-0 flex-1 items-center gap-3">
            <span
              className={`h-9 w-1.5 shrink-0 rounded-full ${groupColor(session.groupId)}`}
              aria-hidden
            />
            <span className="min-w-0 flex-1">
              <span
                className={`block truncate text-[14.5px] font-semibold ${
                  session.status === "cancelled" ? "line-through text-muted" : ""
                }`}
              >
                {session.groupName}
              </span>
              <span className="mt-0.5 block text-[12px] text-muted">
                <NumberText>{formatTime(session.time, locale)}</NumberText>
                {session.type === "extra" ? ` · ${t.schedule.extra}` : ""}
              </span>
            </span>
          </Link>
          <StatusChip tone={status.tone}>{status.label}</StatusChip>
          {session.status === "scheduled" ? (
            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={() => setCancelTarget(session)}
              className="press shrink-0 rounded-full px-2 py-1 text-[12px] text-danger"
            >
              {t.schedule.cancelSession}
            </button>
          ) : null}
        </GlassCard>
      </li>
    );
  }

  const daySessions = sessions
    .filter((session) => session.dateKey === selected)
    .sort((a, b) => a.time.localeCompare(b.time));

  async function saveExtra() {
    const group = groups.find((item) => item.id === extraGroupId);
    if (!uid || !group || busy) return;
    setBusy(true);
    setMessage(null);
    try {
      await addExtraSession(uid, {
        groupId: group.id,
        groupName: group.name,
        dateKey: extraDate,
        time: extraTime,
        existingIds: sessions.map((session) => session.id),
      });
      setExtraOpen(false);
      setMessage(t.schedule.extraSaved);
    } catch {
      setMessage(t.forms.saveFailed);
    } finally {
      setBusy(false);
    }
  }

  async function confirmCancel() {
    if (!uid || !cancelTarget || busy) return;
    setBusy(true);
    setMessage(null);
    try {
      await cancelSession(uid, cancelTarget.id, cancelNote);
      setCancelTarget(null);
      setCancelNote("");
    } catch {
      setMessage(t.forms.saveFailed);
    } finally {
      setBusy(false);
    }
  }

  return (
    <div className="flex flex-col gap-5">
      <PageHeader
        title={t.schedule.title}
        subtitle={t.schedule.upcoming}
        action={
          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={() => {
              setExtraGroupId(groups.find((group) => group.active)?.id ?? "");
              setExtraOpen(true);
            }}
            className="press flex h-10 w-10 items-center justify-center rounded-full bg-primary text-white"
            aria-label={t.schedule.addExtra}
          >
            <Plus size={19} aria-hidden />
          </button>
        }
      />

      <Segmented
        label={t.schedule.title}
        value={view}
        options={[
          { value: "day", label: t.schedule.day },
          { value: "week", label: t.schedule.week },
        ]}
        onChange={setView}
      />

      {message ? (
        <p role="status" className="text-[12.5px] text-primary-dark">
          {message}
        </p>
      ) : null}

      {view === "day" ? (
        <section className="flex flex-col gap-3">
          <DateStepper value={selected} onChange={setSelected} />
          {daySessions.length === 0 ? (
            <EmptyState
              icon={<CalendarDays size={22} aria-hidden />}
              title={t.schedule.noLessons}
            />
          ) : (
            <ul className="flex flex-col gap-2">
              {daySessions.map(sessionRow)}
            </ul>
          )}
        </section>
      ) : (
        <div className="flex flex-col gap-4">
          {horizon.map((dateKey) => {
            const rows = sessions
              .filter((session) => session.dateKey === dateKey)
              .sort((a, b) => a.time.localeCompare(b.time));
            return (
              <section key={dateKey}>
                <h2 className="mb-1.5 px-1 text-[14px] font-semibold">
                  {formatArabicDate(dateKey, {
                    weekday: "long",
                    day: "numeric",
                    month: "long",
                  })}
                </h2>
                {rows.length === 0 ? (
                  <p className="px-2 text-[12.5px] text-muted">
                    {t.schedule.noLessons}
                  </p>
                ) : (
                  <ul className="flex flex-col gap-2">{rows.map(sessionRow)}</ul>
                )}
              </section>
            );
          })}
        </div>
      )}

      <Sheet
        open={extraOpen}
        onClose={() => setExtraOpen(false)}
        title={t.schedule.addExtraTitle}
      >
        <div className="flex flex-col gap-3">
          {groups.filter((group) => group.active).length === 0 ? (
            <p className="text-[13px] text-muted">{t.schedule.noGroupsForExtra}</p>
          ) : (
            <>
              <Field label={t.students.group}>
                <select
                  className={inputClass}
                  value={extraGroupId}
                  onChange={(event) => setExtraGroupId(event.target.value)}
                >
                  <option value="">{t.forms.selectGroup}</option>
                  {groups
                    .filter((group) => group.active)
                    .map((group) => (
                      <option key={group.id} value={group.id}>
                        {group.name}
                      </option>
                    ))}
                </select>
              </Field>
              <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-3">
                <Field label={t.students.startDate}>
                  <input
                    type="date"
                    dir="ltr"
                    className={`${inputClass} tabular-nums`}
                    value={extraDate}
                    onChange={(event) => setExtraDate(event.target.value)}
                  />
                </Field>
                <Field label={t.groups.time}>
                  <input
                    type="time"
                    dir="ltr"
                    className={`${inputClass} tabular-nums`}
                    value={extraTime}
                    onChange={(event) => setExtraTime(event.target.value)}
                  />
                </Field>
              </div>
              <BigPrimaryButton
                onClick={() => void saveExtra()}
                disabled={busy || !extraGroupId || !extraDate || !extraTime}
              >
                <Clock size={16} aria-hidden />
                {t.common.save}
              </BigPrimaryButton>
            </>
          )}
        </div>
      </Sheet>

      <Sheet
        open={cancelTarget !== null}
        onClose={() => {
          setCancelTarget(null);
          setCancelNote("");
        }}
        title={t.schedule.cancelSession}
      >
        <div className="flex flex-col gap-3">
          <ConfirmCard
            title={t.schedule.cancelTitle}
            body={t.schedule.cancelBody}
            confirmLabel={t.schedule.cancelSession}
            cancelLabel={t.common.cancel}
            tone="danger"
            busy={busy}
            onCancel={() => setCancelTarget(null)}
            onConfirm={() => void confirmCancel()}
          />
          <Field
            label={t.attendance.note}
            hint={t.schedule.cancelNoteHint}
          >
            <textarea
              rows={2}
              className={`${inputClass} min-h-16 py-2`}
              value={cancelNote}
              onChange={(event) => setCancelNote(event.target.value)}
            />
          </Field>
        </div>
      </Sheet>
    </div>
  );
}
````

### `src/app/(app)/settings/page.tsx`  ·  275 lines

````tsx
"use client";

import { useState } from "react";
import { useRouter } from "next/navigation";
import { LogOut, Settings2 } from "lucide-react";
import { BigPrimaryButton } from "@/components/ui/BigPrimaryButton";
import { ConfirmCard } from "@/components/ui/ConfirmCard";
import { Field, inputClass } from "@/components/ui/Field";
import { GlassCard } from "@/components/ui/GlassCard";
import { PageHeader } from "@/components/ui/Headers";
import { NumberText } from "@/components/ui/NumberText";
import { Sheet } from "@/components/ui/Sheet";
import { useAuth } from "@/data/auth/AuthProvider";
import { reconcileCounters } from "@/data/services/counterService";
import { saveSettings } from "@/data/services/settingsService";
import { useTutorData } from "@/data/provider/hooks";
import { normalizeCount } from "@/domain/validation";
import type { Weekday } from "@/domain/types";
import { useI18n } from "@/i18n/I18nProvider";
import type { Locale } from "@/i18n";
import { weekdayOptions } from "@/lib/weekdays";

type Pending =
  | { type: "absence"; value: boolean }
  | { type: "cycle"; value: number }
  | null;

export default function SettingsPage() {
  const { t, locale, setLocale } = useI18n();
  const { settings, uid, students, sessionsById, attendance, payments } =
    useTutorData();
  const { user, signOut } = useAuth();
  const router = useRouter();

  const [cycleDraft, setCycleDraft] = useState(String(settings.cycleLength));
  const [currencyDraft, setCurrencyDraft] = useState(settings.currencyLabel);
  const [pending, setPending] = useState<Pending>(null);
  const [busy, setBusy] = useState(false);
  const [notice, setNotice] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [signingOut, setSigningOut] = useState(false);

  const source = { students, sessionsById, attendance, payments, settings };

  async function persist(next: typeof settings, reconcile = false) {
    if (!uid) return;
    setBusy(true);
    setNotice(null);
    setError(null);
    try {
      await saveSettings(uid, next);
      if (reconcile) {
        await reconcileCounters(uid, { ...source, settings: next });
      }
      setNotice(t.settings.saved);
    } catch {
      setError(t.forms.saveFailed);
    } finally {
      setBusy(false);
    }
  }

  async function changeLanguage(next: Locale) {
    setLocale(next);
    await persist({ ...settings, language: next });
  }

  function requestCycle() {
    const parsed = normalizeCount(cycleDraft);
    if (!parsed.ok || parsed.value < 2 || parsed.value > 8) {
      setError(t.settings.invalidCycleLength);
      return;
    }
    setError(null);
    setPending({ type: "cycle", value: parsed.value });
  }

  async function confirmPending() {
    if (!pending) return;
    if (pending.type === "absence") {
      await persist({ ...settings, absenceCounts: pending.value }, true);
    } else {
      await persist({ ...settings, cycleLength: pending.value }, true);
      setCycleDraft(String(pending.value));
    }
    setPending(null);
  }

  async function handleSignOut() {
    if (!window.confirm(t.auth.signOutConfirm)) return;
    setSigningOut(true);
    await signOut();
    router.replace("/sign-in");
  }

  return (
    <div className="flex flex-col gap-5">
      <PageHeader title={t.settings.title} />

      {notice ? (
        <p role="status" className="text-[12.5px] text-primary-dark">
          {notice}
        </p>
      ) : null}
      {error ? (
        <p role="alert" className="text-[12.5px] text-danger">
          {error}
        </p>
      ) : null}

      <GlassCard className="flex flex-col gap-3 p-4">
        <p className="flex items-center gap-2 text-[13px] font-medium text-muted">
          <Settings2 size={15} aria-hidden />
          {t.settings.language}
        </p>
        <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-2">
          {(["ar", "en"] as const).map((value) => (
            <button
              key={value}
              type="button"
              onClick={() => void changeLanguage(value)}
              className={
                locale === value
                  ? "press min-h-11 rounded-btn bg-primary text-[14px] font-semibold text-white"
                  : "press min-h-11 rounded-btn border border-white/70 bg-white/60 text-[14px]"
              }
            >
              {value === "ar" ? t.settings.arabic : t.settings.english}
            </button>
          ))}
        </div>
      </GlassCard>

      <GlassCard className="flex flex-col gap-3 p-4">
        <div className="flex items-center justify-between gap-3">
          <div>
            <p className="text-[14px] font-medium">{t.settings.absenceCounts}</p>
            <p className="mt-0.5 text-[12px] leading-5 text-muted">
              {t.settings.absenceCountsHint}
            </p>
          </div>
          <input
            type="checkbox"
            role="switch"
            checked={settings.absenceCounts}
            onChange={(event) =>
              setPending({ type: "absence", value: event.target.checked })
            }
            className="h-6 w-6 shrink-0 accent-[#6B8F7B]"
            aria-label={t.settings.absenceCounts}
          />
        </div>
      </GlassCard>

      <GlassCard className="flex flex-col gap-3 p-4">
        <Field label={t.settings.cycleLength} hint={t.settings.cycleLengthHint}>
          <div className="flex gap-2">
            <input
              type="number"
              inputMode="numeric"
              dir="ltr"
              min={2}
              max={8}
              step={1}
              className={`${inputClass} tabular-nums`}
              value={cycleDraft}
              onChange={(event) => setCycleDraft(event.target.value)}
            />
            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={requestCycle}
              disabled={busy || cycleDraft === String(settings.cycleLength)}
              className="press shrink-0 rounded-btn bg-primary px-4 text-[13px] font-semibold text-white disabled:opacity-50"
            >
              {t.common.save}
            </button>
          </div>
        </Field>
        <p className="text-[12px] leading-5 text-muted">
          <NumberText>{settings.cycleLength}</NumberText>
          {" · "}
          {t.settings.cycleHistoryWarning}
        </p>
      </GlassCard>

      <GlassCard className="flex flex-col gap-3 p-4">
        <Field label={t.settings.currencyLabel}>
          <div className="flex gap-2">
            <input
              className={inputClass}
              value={currencyDraft}
              onChange={(event) => setCurrencyDraft(event.target.value)}
              maxLength={8}
            />
            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={() =>
                void persist({ ...settings, currencyLabel: currencyDraft.trim() })
              }
              disabled={busy || !currencyDraft.trim() || currencyDraft.trim() === settings.currencyLabel}
              className="press shrink-0 rounded-btn bg-primary px-4 text-[13px] font-semibold text-white disabled:opacity-50"
            >
              {t.common.save}
            </button>
          </div>
        </Field>

        <Field label={t.settings.weekStartsOn}>
          <select
            className={inputClass}
            value={String(settings.weekStartsOn)}
            onChange={(event) =>
              void persist({
                ...settings,
                weekStartsOn: Number(event.target.value) as Weekday,
              })
            }
          >
            {weekdayOptions(t).map((option) => (
              <option key={option.value} value={option.value}>
                {option.label}
              </option>
            ))}
          </select>
        </Field>
      </GlassCard>

      <GlassCard className="flex flex-col gap-3 p-4">
        <p className="text-[13px] font-medium text-muted">{t.settings.account}</p>
        <p dir="ltr" className="truncate text-[14px]">
          {user?.email ?? "—"}
        </p>
        <BigPrimaryButton
          variant="ghost"
          onClick={() => void handleSignOut()}
          disabled={signingOut}
        >
          <LogOut size={16} aria-hidden />
          {t.nav.signOut}
        </BigPrimaryButton>
      </GlassCard>

      <GlassCard className="p-4">
        <p className="text-[13px] font-medium">{t.settings.about}</p>
        <p className="mt-1 text-[12.5px] leading-5 text-muted">
          {t.settings.aboutBody}
        </p>
        <p className="mt-2 text-[12px] leading-5 text-muted">
          {t.settings.backupNote}
        </p>
      </GlassCard>

      <Sheet
        open={pending !== null}
        onClose={() => setPending(null)}
        title={t.settings.recomputeConfirmTitle}
      >
        <ConfirmCard
          title={t.settings.recomputeConfirmTitle}
          body={
            pending?.type === "cycle"
              ? `${t.settings.cycleHistoryWarning} ${t.settings.recomputeConfirmBody}`
              : t.settings.recomputeConfirmBody
          }
          confirmLabel={t.common.confirm}
          cancelLabel={t.common.cancel}
          tone="primary"
          busy={busy}
          onCancel={() => setPending(null)}
          onConfirm={() => void confirmPending()}
        />
      </Sheet>
    </div>
  );
}
````

````tsx
"use client";

import { useMemo, useState } from "react";
import {
  Bar,
  BarChart,
  CartesianGrid,
  ResponsiveContainer,
  Tooltip,
  XAxis,
  YAxis,
} from "recharts";
import {
  BarChart3,
  CalendarCheck2,
  ChevronLeft,
  ChevronRight,
  Users,
  Wallet,
} from "lucide-react";
import { EmptyState } from "@/components/ui/EmptyState";
import { GlassCard } from "@/components/ui/GlassCard";
import { PageHeader, SectionHeader } from "@/components/ui/Headers";
import { NumberText } from "@/components/ui/NumberText";
import { useDueStudents, useTutorData } from "@/data/provider/hooks";
import { todayKey } from "@/domain/dateKey";
import { formatMoney } from "@/domain/money";
import {
  attendanceByGroup,
  attendanceRate,
  collectedInMonth,
  expectedForMonth,
  incomeByMonth,
  monthKeyOf,
  shiftMonthKey,
  slowestPayers,
  topAbsences,
  topGroupsByIncome,
} from "@/domain/stats";
import { formatArabicDate } from "@/domain/dateKey";
import { useI18n } from "@/i18n/I18nProvider";

function monthLabel(key: string, long: boolean): string {
  const [year, month] = key.split("-").map(Number);
  return new Intl.DateTimeFormat("ar-EG-u-nu-latn", {
    timeZone: "UTC",
    month: long ? "long" : "short",
    ...(long ? { year: "numeric" } : {}),
  }).format(new Date(Date.UTC(year, month - 1, 1)));
}

export default function StatsPage() {
  const { t } = useI18n();
  const { students, groups, payments, attendance, sessionsById, balances, settings } =
    useTutorData();
  const { total: outstanding } = useDueStudents();

  const [monthKey, setMonthKey] = useState(() => monthKeyOf(todayKey()));

  const stats = useMemo(() => {
    return {
      collected: collectedInMonth(payments, monthKey),
      expected: expectedForMonth(students, settings),
      rate: attendanceRate(attendance, sessionsById, monthKey),
      income: incomeByMonth(payments, monthKey, 6).map((row) => ({
        ...row,
        label: monthLabel(row.monthKey, false),
      })),
      byGroup: attendanceByGroup(attendance, sessionsById, groups, monthKey),
      absences: topAbsences(attendance, sessionsById, students, monthKey),
      slow: slowestPayers(students, balances, payments),
      topGroups: topGroupsByIncome(payments, students, groups, monthKey),
    };
  }, [
    monthKey,
    payments,
    students,
    settings,
    attendance,
    sessionsById,
    groups,
    balances,
  ]);

  const hasData =
    stats.collected > 0 ||
    stats.byGroup.some((row) => row.total > 0) ||
    stats.absences.length > 0;

  const tick = { fontSize: 10, fill: "#7a7468" };

  return (
    <div className="flex flex-col gap-5">
      <PageHeader title={t.stats.title} subtitle={monthLabel(monthKey, true)} />

      <div className="glass-card flex items-center justify-between gap-2 p-2">
        <button
          type="button"
          aria-label={t.stats.prevMonth}
          onClick={() => setMonthKey(shiftMonthKey(monthKey, -1))}
          className="press flex h-11 w-11 items-center justify-center rounded-btn text-muted hover:bg-white/60"
        >
          <ChevronRight size={20} className="rtl:rotate-180" aria-hidden />
        </button>
        <p className="text-[15px] font-semibold">{monthLabel(monthKey, true)}</p>
        <button
          type="button"
          aria-label={t.stats.nextMonth}
          onClick={() => setMonthKey(shiftMonthKey(monthKey, 1))}
          className="press flex h-11 w-11 items-center justify-center rounded-btn text-muted hover:bg-white/60"
        >
          <ChevronLeft size={20} className="rtl:rotate-180" aria-hidden />
        </button>
      </div>

      <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-3">
        <GlassCard className="flex flex-col gap-1 p-4">
          <span className="flex items-center gap-1.5 text-[12px] text-muted">
            <Wallet size={14} aria-hidden /> {t.stats.collected}
          </span>
          <span className="text-[19px] font-bold">
            <NumberText>
              {formatMoney(stats.collected, t.common.currency)}
            </NumberText>
          </span>
        </GlassCard>
        <GlassCard className="flex flex-col gap-1 p-4">
          <span className="flex items-center gap-1.5 text-[12px] text-muted">
            <BarChart3 size={14} aria-hidden /> {t.stats.outstanding}
          </span>
          <span className="text-[19px] font-bold">
            <NumberText>{formatMoney(outstanding, t.common.currency)}</NumberText>
          </span>
        </GlassCard>
        <GlassCard className="flex flex-col gap-1 p-4">
          <span className="flex items-center gap-1.5 text-[12px] text-muted">
            <Users size={14} aria-hidden /> {t.stats.expected}
          </span>
          <span className="text-[19px] font-bold">
            <NumberText>
              {formatMoney(stats.expected, t.common.currency)}
            </NumberText>
          </span>
        </GlassCard>
        <GlassCard className="flex flex-col gap-1 p-4">
          <span className="flex items-center gap-1.5 text-[12px] text-muted">
            <CalendarCheck2 size={14} aria-hidden /> {t.stats.attendanceRate}
          </span>
          <span className="text-[19px] font-bold">
            <NumberText>{stats.rate}</NumberText>%
          </span>
        </GlassCard>
      </div>

      {!hasData ? (
        <EmptyState
          icon={<BarChart3 size={22} aria-hidden />}
          title={t.stats.noData}
        />
      ) : (
        <>
          <section>
            <SectionHeader title={t.stats.incomeChart} />
            <GlassCard className="p-3">
              <div className="h-[180px] w-full" dir="ltr">
                <ResponsiveContainer width="100%" height="100%">
                  <BarChart data={stats.income} margin={{ top: 8, right: 4, left: -18, bottom: 0 }}>
                    <CartesianGrid strokeDasharray="3 3" vertical={false} stroke="#E6DBC9" />
                    <XAxis dataKey="label" reversed tick={tick} axisLine={false} tickLine={false} />
                    <YAxis tick={tick} axisLine={false} tickLine={false} width={48} />
                    <Tooltip
                      formatter={(value) => [
                        `${Number(value)} ${t.common.currency}`,
                        t.stats.collected,
                      ]}
                    />
                    <Bar dataKey="amount" fill="#6B8F7B" radius={[6, 6, 0, 0]} />
                  </BarChart>
                </ResponsiveContainer>
              </div>
            </GlassCard>
          </section>

          <section>
            <SectionHeader title={t.stats.attendanceByGroup} />
            <GlassCard className="p-3">
              <div className="h-[170px] w-full" dir="ltr">
                <ResponsiveContainer width="100%" height="100%">
                  <BarChart
                    data={stats.byGroup}
                    layout="vertical"
                    margin={{ top: 8, right: 12, left: 8, bottom: 0 }}
                  >
                    <CartesianGrid strokeDasharray="3 3" horizontal={false} stroke="#E6DBC9" />
                    <XAxis type="number" domain={[0, 100]} tick={tick} axisLine={false} tickLine={false} />
                    <YAxis
                      type="category"
                      dataKey="groupName"
                      reversed
                      tick={tick}
                      axisLine={false}
                      tickLine={false}
                      width={82}
                    />
                    <Tooltip
                      formatter={(value) => [
                        `${Number(value)}%`,
                        t.stats.attendanceRate,
                      ]}
                    />
                    <Bar dataKey="rate" fill="#C58B6D" radius={[0, 6, 6, 0]} />
                  </BarChart>
                </ResponsiveContainer>
              </div>
            </GlassCard>
          </section>

          <section>
            <SectionHeader title={t.stats.topAbsences} />
            {stats.absences.length === 0 ? (
              <EmptyState icon={<BarChart3 size={22} aria-hidden />} title={t.stats.noData} />
            ) : (
              <ul className="flex flex-col gap-2">
                {stats.absences.map((row) => (
                  <li
                    key={row.studentId}
                    className="glass-card flex items-center justify-between px-3.5 py-3"
                  >
                    <span className="text-[14px]">{row.name}</span>
                    <span className="text-[13px] text-muted">
                      <NumberText>{row.absences}</NumberText> {t.attendance.absent}
                    </span>
                  </li>
                ))}
              </ul>
            )}
          </section>

          <section>
            <SectionHeader title={t.stats.slowPayers} />
            {stats.slow.length === 0 ? (
              <EmptyState icon={<Wallet size={22} aria-hidden />} title={t.stats.noData} />
            ) : (
              <ul className="flex flex-col gap-2">
                {stats.slow.map((row) => (
                  <li
                    key={row.studentId}
                    className="glass-card flex items-center justify-between gap-2 px-3.5 py-3"
                  >
                    <div className="min-w-0">
                      <p className="truncate text-[14px] font-medium">{row.name}</p>
                      <p className="text-[12px] text-muted">
                        {row.lastPaymentKey
                          ? formatArabicDate(row.lastPaymentKey, {
                              day: "numeric",
                              month: "short",
                            })
                          : t.stats.neverPaid}
                      </p>
                    </div>
                    <span className="text-[13px] font-semibold">
                      <NumberText>
                        {formatMoney(row.amountDue, t.common.currency)}
                      </NumberText>
                    </span>
                  </li>
                ))}
              </ul>
            )}
          </section>

          <section>
            <SectionHeader title={t.stats.topGroups} />
            {stats.topGroups.length === 0 ? (
              <EmptyState icon={<BarChart3 size={22} aria-hidden />} title={t.stats.noData} />
            ) : (
              <ul className="flex flex-col gap-2">
                {stats.topGroups.map((row) => (
                  <li
                    key={row.groupId}
                    className="glass-card flex items-center justify-between px-3.5 py-3"
                  >
                    <span className="text-[14px]">{row.groupName}</span>
                    <span className="text-[13px] font-semibold">
                      <NumberText>
                        {formatMoney(row.amount, t.common.currency)}
                      </NumberText>
                    </span>
                  </li>
                ))}
              </ul>
            )}
          </section>
        </>
      )}

    </div>
  );
}
````

````tsx
"use client";

import { useParams } from "next/navigation";
import { UserRound } from "lucide-react";
import { StudentForm } from "@/components/features/StudentForm";
import { EmptyState } from "@/components/ui/EmptyState";
import { useTutorData } from "@/data/provider/hooks";
import { useI18n } from "@/i18n/I18nProvider";

export default function EditStudentPage() {
  const params = useParams();
  const raw = params.studentId;
  const studentId = Array.isArray(raw) ? raw[0] : raw;
  const { students } = useTutorData();
  const { t } = useI18n();

  const student = students.find((item) => item.id === studentId);
  if (!student) {
    return (
      <EmptyState
        icon={<UserRound size={22} aria-hidden />}
        title={t.common.empty}
      />
    );
  }

  return <StudentForm student={student} />;
}
````

````tsx
"use client";

import Link from "next/link";
import { useParams } from "next/navigation";
import { useState } from "react";
import {
  Archive,
  ArchiveRestore,
  ArrowRight,
  CalendarDays,
  FolderOpen,
  MoveRight,
  Pencil,
  Phone,
  Trash2,
  UserRound,
  Wallet,
} from "lucide-react";
import { BalanceChip } from "@/components/features/BalanceChip";
import { PaymentSheet } from "@/components/features/PaymentSheet";
import { ConfirmCard } from "@/components/ui/ConfirmCard";
import { CounterRing } from "@/components/ui/CounterRing";
import { EmptyState } from "@/components/ui/EmptyState";
import { GlassCard } from "@/components/ui/GlassCard";
import { PageHeader, SectionHeader } from "@/components/ui/Headers";
import { NumberText } from "@/components/ui/NumberText";
import { Sheet } from "@/components/ui/Sheet";
import { StatusChip } from "@/components/ui/StatusChip";
import { useTutorData } from "@/data/provider/hooks";
import {
  moveStudentAndReconcile,
  setStudentActiveAndReconcile,
} from "@/data/services/enrollmentService";
import { formatArabicDate } from "@/domain/dateKey";
import { formatMoney } from "@/domain/money";
import type { Payment } from "@/domain/types";
import { format } from "@/i18n";
import { useI18n } from "@/i18n/I18nProvider";

export default function StudentDetailPage() {
  const { t } = useI18n();
  const params = useParams();
  const raw = params.studentId;
  const studentId = Array.isArray(raw) ? raw[0] : raw;

  const { students, groups, payments, attendance, sessionsById, balances, settings, uid } =
    useTutorData();

  const [archiveOpen, setArchiveOpen] = useState(false);
  const [moveOpen, setMoveOpen] = useState(false);
  const [pendingGroupId, setPendingGroupId] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [busy, setBusy] = useState(false);
  const [actionError, setActionError] = useState(false);
  const [paymentOpen, setPaymentOpen] = useState(false);
  const [editingPayment, setEditingPayment] = useState<Payment | null>(null);
  const [paymentStartDelete, setPaymentStartDelete] = useState(false);

  const student = students.find((item) => item.id === studentId);
  const group = groups.find((item) => item.id === student?.groupId);
  const balance = student ? balances.get(student.id) : undefined;

  if (!student) {
    return (
      <div className="flex flex-col gap-4">
        <Link
          href="/students"
          className="flex min-h-11 items-center gap-2 text-[13px] text-muted"
        >
          <ArrowRight size={16} aria-hidden />
          {t.students.title}
        </Link>
        <EmptyState
          icon={<UserRound size={22} aria-hidden />}
          title={t.common.empty}
        />
      </div>
    );
  }

  const studentPayments = payments
    .filter((payment) => payment.studentId === student.id)
    .sort((a, b) => b.dateKey.localeCompare(a.dateKey));

  const studentAttendance = attendance
    .filter((row) => row.studentId === student.id)
    .sort((a, b) => b.dateKey.localeCompare(a.dateKey));

  const cycleByRow = new Map<string, number>();
  let countedSoFar = student.initialCounted;
  for (const row of [...studentAttendance].reverse()) {
    const counts =
      row.status === "present" || (row.status === "absent" && settings.absenceCounts);
    if (!counts) continue;
    countedSoFar += 1;
    cycleByRow.set(row.id, Math.floor((countedSoFar - 1) / settings.cycleLength) + 1);
  }

  const phoneDigits = student.phone?.replace(/[^\d+]/g, "") ?? "";

  return (
    <div className="flex flex-col gap-5">
      <div>
        <Link
          href="/students"
          className="mb-2 flex min-h-8 items-center gap-1 text-[13px] text-muted"
        >
          <ArrowRight size={15} aria-hidden />
          {t.students.title}
        </Link>
        <PageHeader
          title={student.name}
          subtitle={group?.name}
          action={
            balance ? (
              <BalanceChip
                balance={balance}
                t={t}
                cycleLength={settings.cycleLength}
              />
            ) : undefined
          }
        />
      </div>

      <GlassCard className="flex items-center gap-4 p-4">
        <CounterRing
          position={balance?.cyclePosition ?? 0}
          length={settings.cycleLength}
          size={64}
        />
        <div className="flex flex-1 flex-col gap-1 text-[13px]">
          <span className="text-muted">
            {t.students.price}:{" "}
            <NumberText className="font-semibold text-ink">
              {formatMoney(student.pricePerSession, t.common.currency)}
            </NumberText>
          </span>
          <span className="text-muted">
            {t.students.startDate}:{" "}
            <NumberText className="font-semibold text-ink">
              {formatArabicDate(student.startedAtKey, {
                day: "numeric",
                month: "short",
                year: "numeric",
              })}
            </NumberText>
          </span>
          {balance ? (
            <span className="text-muted">
              {t.students.sessionOf
                .replace("{position}", String(balance.cyclePosition))
                .replace("{length}", String(settings.cycleLength))}
            </span>
          ) : null}
        </div>
      </GlassCard>

      {student.phone ? (
        <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-3">
          <a
            href={`tel:${phoneDigits}`}
            className="press flex min-h-12 items-center justify-center gap-2 rounded-btn border border-white/70 bg-white/60 text-[14px] font-medium"
          >
            <Phone size={16} className="text-primary" aria-hidden />
            {t.students.phone}
          </a>
          <a
            href={`https://wa.me/${phoneDigits.replace(/^\+/, "")}`}
            target="_blank"
            rel="noreferrer"
            className="press flex min-h-12 items-center justify-center gap-2 rounded-btn border border-white/70 bg-white/60 text-[14px] font-medium"
          >
            <Wallet size={16} className="text-primary" aria-hidden />
            {t.dues.whatsapp}
          </a>
        </div>
      ) : null}

      <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-2">
        <button
          type="button"
          onClick={() => {
            setEditingPayment(null);
            setPaymentStartDelete(false);
            setPaymentOpen(true);
          }}
          className="press flex min-h-11 items-center justify-center gap-1.5 rounded-btn bg-primary text-[12.5px] font-semibold text-white"
        >
          <Wallet size={15} aria-hidden />
          {t.dues.record}
        </button>
        <Link
          href={`/students/${student.id}/edit`}
          className="press flex min-h-11 items-center justify-center gap-1.5 rounded-btn border border-white/70 bg-white/60 text-[12.5px] font-medium"
        >
          <Pencil size={15} className="text-primary" aria-hidden />
          {t.forms.editAction}
        </Link>
        <button
          type="button"
          onClick={() => setMoveOpen(true)}
          className="press flex min-h-11 items-center justify-center gap-1.5 rounded-btn border border-white/70 bg-white/60 text-[12.5px] font-medium"
        >
          <MoveRight size={15} className="text-primary" aria-hidden />
          {t.forms.moveAction}
        </button>
        <button
          type="button"
          onClick={() => setArchiveOpen(true)}
          className="press flex min-h-11 items-center justify-center gap-1.5 rounded-btn border border-white/70 bg-white/60 text-[12.5px] font-medium"
        >
          {student.active ? (
            <Archive size={15} className="text-danger" aria-hidden />
          ) : (
            <ArchiveRestore size={15} className="text-primary" aria-hidden />
          )}
          {student.active ? t.forms.archiveAction : t.forms.unarchiveAction}
        </button>
      </div>

      {actionError ? (
        <p role="alert" className="text-[12.5px] text-danger">
          {t.forms.saveFailed}
        </p>
      ) : null}

      <Sheet
        open={archiveOpen}
        onClose={() => setArchiveOpen(false)}
        title={student.active ? t.students.archive : t.students.unarchive}
      >
        <ConfirmCard
          title={
            student.active
              ? t.forms.confirmArchiveStudentTitle
              : t.forms.confirmUnarchiveTitle
          }
          body={
            student.active
              ? t.forms.confirmArchiveStudentBody
              : t.forms.confirmUnarchiveBody
          }
          confirmLabel={
            student.active ? t.forms.archiveAction : t.forms.unarchiveAction
          }
          cancelLabel={t.common.cancel}
          tone={student.active ? "danger" : "primary"}
          busy={busy}
          onCancel={() => setArchiveOpen(false)}
          onConfirm={() => {
            if (!uid) return;
            setBusy(true);
            setActionError(false);
            void setStudentActiveAndReconcile(uid, student.id, !student.active, {
              students,
              sessionsById,
              attendance,
              payments,
              settings,
            })
              .then(() => setArchiveOpen(false))
              .catch(() => setActionError(true))
              .finally(() => setBusy(false));
          }}
        />
      </Sheet>

      <Sheet
        open={moveOpen}
        onClose={() => setMoveOpen(false)}
        title={t.students.moveGroup}
      >
        {groups.filter((group) => group.active && group.id !== student.groupId)
          .length === 0 ? (
          <p className="text-[13px] text-muted">{t.forms.noActiveGroups}</p>
        ) : (
          <ul className="flex flex-col gap-1">
            {groups
              .filter((group) => group.active && group.id !== student.groupId)
              .map((group) => (
                <li key={group.id}>
                  <button
                    type="button"
                    onClick={() => {
                      setMoveOpen(false);
                      setPendingGroupId(group.id);
                    }}
                    className="press flex min-h-12 w-full items-center gap-2 rounded-btn px-3 text-[14px] hover:bg-white/50"
                  >
                    <FolderOpen size={18} className="text-primary" aria-hidden />
                    {group.name}
                  </button>
                </li>
              ))}
          </ul>
        )}
      </Sheet>

      <Sheet
        open={pendingGroupId !== null}
        onClose={() => setPendingGroupId(null)}
        title={t.students.moveGroup}
      >
        <ConfirmCard
          title={t.forms.confirmMoveTitle}
          body={t.forms.confirmMoveBody}
          confirmLabel={t.forms.moveAction}
          cancelLabel={t.common.cancel}
          tone="primary"
          busy={busy}
          onCancel={() => setPendingGroupId(null)}
          onConfirm={() => {
            if (!uid || !pendingGroupId) return;
            setBusy(true);
            setActionError(false);
            void moveStudentAndReconcile(uid, student.id, pendingGroupId, {
              students,
              sessionsById,
              attendance,
              payments,
              settings,
            })
              .then(() => setPendingGroupId(null))
              .catch(() => setActionError(true))
              .finally(() => setBusy(false));
          }}
        />
      </Sheet>

      <section>
        <SectionHeader title={t.students.attendanceHistory} />
        {studentAttendance.length === 0 ? (
          <EmptyState
            icon={<CalendarDays size={20} aria-hidden />}
            title={t.students.noHistory}
          />
        ) : (
          <ul className="flex flex-col gap-2">
            {studentAttendance.slice(0, 12).map((row) => {
              const session = sessionsById.get(row.sessionId);
              return (
                <li
                  key={row.id}
                  className="glass-card flex items-center justify-between gap-2 px-3.5 py-3"
                >
                  <div className="min-w-0">
                    <p className="truncate text-[14px] font-medium">
                      {session?.groupName ?? "—"}
                    </p>
                    <p className="text-[12px] text-muted">
                      <NumberText>
                        {formatArabicDate(row.dateKey, {
                          day: "numeric",
                          month: "short",
                        })}
                      </NumberText>
                      </p>
                      {cycleByRow.has(row.id) ? (
                        <StatusChip tone="neutral" className="mt-1">
                          {format(t.students.cycleNumber, {
                            n: cycleByRow.get(row.id) ?? 0,
                          })}
                        </StatusChip>
                      ) : null}
                    </div>
                    <StatusChip
                      tone={row.status === "present" ? "success" : "danger"}
                    >
                    {row.status === "present"
                      ? t.attendance.present
                      : t.attendance.absent}
                  </StatusChip>
                </li>
              );
            })}
          </ul>
        )}
      </section>

      <section>
        <SectionHeader title={t.students.paymentsHistory} />
        {studentPayments.length === 0 ? (
          <EmptyState
            icon={<Wallet size={20} aria-hidden />}
            title={t.students.noHistory}
          />
        ) : (
          <ul className="flex flex-col gap-2">
            {studentPayments.map((payment) => (
              <li
                key={payment.id}
                className="glass-card flex items-center justify-between gap-2 px-3.5 py-3"
              >
                <div>
                  <p className="text-[14px] font-semibold">
                    <NumberText>
                      {formatMoney(payment.amount, t.common.currency)}
                    </NumberText>
                  </p>
                  <p className="text-[12px] text-muted">
                    <NumberText>
                      {formatArabicDate(payment.dateKey, {
                        day: "numeric",
                        month: "short",
                      })}
                    </NumberText>{" "}
                    · <NumberText>{payment.sessionsCovered}</NumberText>{" "}
                    {t.today.sessionsToday}
                  </p>
                </div>
                <div className="flex shrink-0 items-center gap-0.5">
                  <button
                    type="button"
                    aria-label={t.common.edit}
                    onClick={() => {
                      setEditingPayment(payment);
                      setPaymentStartDelete(false);
                      setPaymentOpen(true);
                    }}
                    className="press rounded-full p-2 text-muted hover:text-primary"
                  >
                    <Pencil size={15} aria-hidden />
                  </button>
                  <button
                    type="button"
                    aria-label={t.common.delete}
                    onClick={() => {
                      setEditingPayment(payment);
                      setPaymentStartDelete(true);
                      setPaymentOpen(true);
                    }}
                    className="press rounded-full p-2 text-muted hover:text-danger"
                  >
                    <Trash2 size={15} aria-hidden />
                  </button>
                </div>
              </li>
            ))}
          </ul>
        )}
      </section>

      {paymentOpen ? (
        <PaymentSheet
          key={`${editingPayment?.id ?? "new"}-${paymentStartDelete ? "delete" : "edit"}`}
          open
          onClose={() => setPaymentOpen(false)}
          student={student}
          payment={editingPayment ?? undefined}
          initialDelete={paymentStartDelete}
        />
      ) : null}
    </div>
  );
}
````

````tsx
"use client";

import Link from "next/link";
import { useMemo, useState } from "react";
import { Plus, Search, Users } from "lucide-react";
import { BalanceChip } from "@/components/features/BalanceChip";
import { CounterRing } from "@/components/ui/CounterRing";
import { EmptyState } from "@/components/ui/EmptyState";
import { GlassCard } from "@/components/ui/GlassCard";
import { PageHeader } from "@/components/ui/Headers";
import { useTutorData } from "@/data/provider/hooks";
import { cn } from "@/lib/cn";
import { useI18n } from "@/i18n/I18nProvider";

type Filter = "all" | "due" | "paidAhead" | "archived";

export default function StudentsPage() {
  const { t } = useI18n();
  const { students, groups, balances, settings } = useTutorData();
  const [query, setQuery] = useState("");
  const [filter, setFilter] = useState<Filter>("all");

  const rows = useMemo(() => {
    const normalized = query.trim().toLowerCase();
    return students
      .filter((student) => {
        if (filter === "archived") return !student.active;
        if (!student.active) return false;
        const balance = balances.get(student.id);
        if (filter === "due") return balance?.state === "DUE";
        if (filter === "paidAhead") return balance?.state === "PAID_AHEAD";
        return true;
      })
      .filter((student) =>
        normalized ? student.name.toLowerCase().includes(normalized) : true,
      )
      .sort((a, b) => a.name.localeCompare(b.name, "ar"));
  }, [students, balances, filter, query]);

  const filters: Array<{ key: Filter; label: string }> = [
    { key: "all", label: t.students.filterAll },
    { key: "due", label: t.students.filterDue },
    { key: "paidAhead", label: t.students.filterPaidAhead },
    { key: "archived", label: t.students.filterArchived },
  ];

  const groupName = (groupId: string) =>
    groups.find((group) => group.id === groupId)?.name ?? "—";

  return (
    <div className="flex flex-col gap-4">
      <PageHeader
        title={t.students.title}
        subtitle={`${students.filter((student) => student.active).length} ${t.students.active}`}
        action={
          <Link
            href="/students/new"
            className="press flex min-h-9 items-center gap-1 rounded-full bg-primary px-3 text-[12.5px] font-semibold text-white"
          >
            <Plus size={15} aria-hidden />
            {t.students.addStudent}
          </Link>
        }
      />

      <div className="glass-card flex items-center gap-2 px-3 py-2">
        <Search size={18} className="text-muted" aria-hidden />
        <input
          value={query}
          onChange={(event) => setQuery(event.target.value)}
          placeholder={t.students.searchPlaceholder}
          className="min-h-9 w-full bg-transparent text-[14px] outline-none placeholder:text-muted/80"
        />
      </div>

      <div className="no-scrollbar -mx-1 flex gap-2 overflow-x-auto px-1">
        {filters.map((item) => (
          <button
            key={item.key}
            type="button"
            onClick={() => setFilter(item.key)}
            className={cn(
              "press shrink-0 rounded-full px-3 py-1.5 text-[12.5px] font-medium",
              filter === item.key
                ? "bg-primary text-white"
                : "bg-white/55 text-muted",
            )}
          >
            {item.label}
          </button>
        ))}
      </div>

      {rows.length === 0 ? (
        <EmptyState
          icon={<Users size={22} aria-hidden />}
          title={query ? t.students.noResults : t.students.noStudents}
          body={query ? undefined : t.students.noStudentsBody}
        />
      ) : (
        <ul className="flex flex-col gap-3">
          {rows.map((student) => {
            const balance = balances.get(student.id);
            return (
              <li key={student.id}>
                <Link href={`/students/${student.id}`} className="press block">
                  <GlassCard className="flex items-center gap-3 p-3.5">
                    <CounterRing
                      position={balance?.cyclePosition ?? 0}
                      length={settings.cycleLength}
                    />
                    <div className="min-w-0 flex-1">
                      <p className="truncate text-[15px] font-semibold">
                        {student.name}
                      </p>
                      <p className="mt-0.5 truncate text-[12.5px] text-muted">
                        {groupName(student.groupId)}
                        {!student.active ? ` · ${t.students.archived}` : ""}
                      </p>
                    </div>
                    {balance ? (
                      <BalanceChip
                        balance={balance}
                        t={t}
                        cycleLength={settings.cycleLength}
                      />
                    ) : null}
                  </GlassCard>
                </Link>
              </li>
            );
          })}
        </ul>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}
````

### `src/app/(auth)/sign-in/page.tsx`  ·  141 lines

````tsx
"use client";

import { useEffect, useState } from "react";
import { useRouter } from "next/navigation";
import { BookOpen, Eye, EyeOff, Loader2 } from "lucide-react";
import { BigPrimaryButton } from "@/components/ui/BigPrimaryButton";
import { GlassCard } from "@/components/ui/GlassCard";
import { useAuth, type AuthErrorKey } from "@/data/auth/AuthProvider";
import { useI18n } from "@/i18n/I18nProvider";

export default function SignInPage() {
  const { t } = useI18n();
  const { user, loading, configured, signIn, signUp } = useAuth();
  const router = useRouter();

  const [mode, setMode] = useState<"signin" | "signup">("signin");
  const [email, setEmail] = useState("");
  const [password, setPassword] = useState("");
  const [showPassword, setShowPassword] = useState(false);
  const [busy, setBusy] = useState(false);
  const [errorKey, setErrorKey] = useState<AuthErrorKey | null>(null);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!loading && user) router.replace("/");
  }, [loading, user, router]);

  if (!configured) {
    return (
      <GlassCard className="p-5">
        <h1 className="text-[20px] font-bold">{t.auth.missingConfigTitle}</h1>
        <p className="mt-2 text-[13.5px] leading-6 text-muted">
          {t.auth.missingConfigBody}
        </p>
      </GlassCard>
    );
  }

  async function handleSubmit(event: React.FormEvent<HTMLFormElement>) {
    event.preventDefault();
    setBusy(true);
    setErrorKey(null);
    const result =
      mode === "signin"
        ? await signIn(email, password)
        : await signUp(email, password);
    setBusy(false);
    if (!result.ok) {
      setErrorKey(result.errorKey);
      return;
    }
    router.replace("/");
  }

  return (
    <div className="flex flex-col gap-5">
      <div className="flex flex-col items-center gap-2 text-center">
        <div className="flex h-14 w-14 items-center justify-center rounded-card bg-primary text-white shadow-lg">
          <BookOpen size={26} aria-hidden />
        </div>
        <h1 className="text-[24px] font-bold">{t.common.appName}</h1>
        <p className="text-[13px] text-muted">{t.auth.signInSubtitle}</p>
      </div>

      <GlassCard className="p-5">
        <form className="flex flex-col gap-3" onSubmit={handleSubmit}>
          <label className="flex flex-col gap-1.5">
            <span className="text-[13px] font-medium text-muted">
              {t.auth.email}
            </span>
            <input
              type="email"
              inputMode="email"
              autoComplete="email"
              required
              dir="ltr"
              value={email}
              onChange={(event) => setEmail(event.target.value)}
              className="min-h-12 rounded-btn border border-white/80 bg-white/70 px-3 text-[15px] outline-none focus:border-primary/60"
            />
          </label>

          <label className="flex flex-col gap-1.5">
            <span className="text-[13px] font-medium text-muted">
              {t.auth.password}
            </span>
            <div className="relative">
              <input
                type={showPassword ? "text" : "password"}
                autoComplete={
                  mode === "signin" ? "current-password" : "new-password"
                }
                required
                minLength={6}
                dir="ltr"
                value={password}
                onChange={(event) => setPassword(event.target.value)}
                className="min-h-12 w-full rounded-btn border border-white/80 bg-white/70 px-3 pe-11 text-[15px] outline-none focus:border-primary/60"
              />
              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={() => setShowPassword((value) => !value)}
                aria-label={t.auth.password}
                className="absolute inset-y-0 end-0 flex w-11 items-center justify-center text-muted"
              >
                {showPassword ? (
                  <EyeOff size={18} aria-hidden />
                ) : (
                  <Eye size={18} aria-hidden />
                )}
              </button>
            </div>
          </label>

          {errorKey ? (
            <p role="alert" className="rounded-btn bg-danger/10 px-3 py-2 text-[13px] text-danger">
              {t.auth[errorKey]}
            </p>
          ) : null}

          <BigPrimaryButton type="submit" disabled={busy || loading}>
            {busy ? (
              <Loader2 size={18} className="animate-spin" aria-hidden />
            ) : null}
            {mode === "signin" ? t.auth.signIn : t.auth.signUp}
          </BigPrimaryButton>
        </form>

        <button
          type="button"
          onClick={() => {
            setMode(mode === "signin" ? "signup" : "signin");
            setErrorKey(null);
          }}
          className="mt-4 w-full text-center text-[13px] text-primary-dark underline-offset-4 hover:underline"
        >
          {mode === "signin" ? t.auth.createAccount : t.auth.haveAccount}
        </button>
      </GlassCard>
    </div>
  );
}
````

### `src/app/api/ai/assistant/route.ts`  ·  165 lines

````typescript
import { NextResponse, type NextRequest } from "next/server";
import { getAdmin, verifyTutorToken } from "@/data/firebase/admin";
import {
  AssistantBodySchema,
  type AssistantReply,
} from "@/data/ai/schema";
import {
  runGemini,
  runGroq,
  systemInstruction,
  type RunInput,
} from "@/data/ai/providers";
import {
  ClarificationNeeded,
  loadState,
  type ToolContext,
} from "@/data/ai/tools";

export const runtime = "nodejs";
export const maxDuration = 300;
export const dynamic = "force-dynamic";

const PRIMARY_MODEL = process.env.GEMINI_MODEL || "gemini-3.5-flash-lite";
const FALLBACK_GEMINI = PRIMARY_MODEL.includes("lite")
  ? "gemini-3.5-flash"
  : "gemini-3.5-flash-lite";

/** Config probe: booleans only, no secret values. */
export async function GET() {
  let firestore = "unknown";
  try {
    await getAdmin().db.doc("users/healthcheck-probe").get();
    firestore = "ok";
  } catch (error) {
    firestore = String(
      (error as { code?: string })?.code ?? (error as Error)?.name ?? "error",
    );
  }

  return NextResponse.json({
    geminiKey: Boolean(process.env.GEMINI_API_KEY),
    groqKey: Boolean(process.env.GROQ_API_KEY),
    serviceAccount: Boolean(process.env.FIREBASE_SERVICE_ACCOUNT),
    model: PRIMARY_MODEL,
    groqModel: process.env.GROQ_MODEL || "openai/gpt-oss-120b",
    dailyLimit: process.env.AI_DAILY_LIMIT ?? null,
    enabled: process.env.AI_ENABLED !== "false",
    firestore,
  });
}

export async function POST(request: NextRequest) {
  // 1) Authenticate the tutor with the real Firebase ID token.
  const token = /^Bearer\s+(.+)$/i.exec(
    request.headers.get("authorization") ?? "",
  )?.[1];
  if (!token) {
    return NextResponse.json({ error: "authRequired" }, { status: 401 });
  }

  let uid: string;
  try {
    uid = (await verifyTutorToken(token)).uid;
  } catch (error) {
    console.warn("ai_auth_error", (error as Error)?.name ?? "Error");
    return NextResponse.json({ error: "authRequired" }, { status: 401 });
  }

  // 2) Validate body.
  const parsedBody = AssistantBodySchema.safeParse(
    await request.json().catch(() => null),
  );
  if (!parsedBody.success) {
    return NextResponse.json({ error: "invalidRequest" }, { status: 400 });
  }
  const body = parsedBody.data;

  // 3) Daily cap, per tutor and Cairo day.
  const limit = Math.max(1, Number(process.env.AI_DAILY_LIMIT ?? 100) || 100);
  try {
    const usageRef = getAdmin().db.doc(`users/${uid}/meta/aiUsage`);
    const usage = await usageRef.get();
    const usageData = usage.data() as
      | { dateKey?: string; count?: number }
      | undefined;
    const count =
      usageData?.dateKey === body.clientToday
        ? Number(usageData?.count ?? 0)
        : 0;
    if (count >= limit) {
      return NextResponse.json({ error: "limitReached" }, { status: 429 });
    }
    await usageRef.set(
      {
        dateKey: body.clientToday,
        count: count + 1,
        updatedAt: new Date().toISOString(),
      },
      { merge: true },
    );
  } catch (error) {
    console.warn(
      "ai_usage_error",
      (error as Error)?.name ?? "Error",
      String((error as { code?: string })?.code ?? ""),
    );
    return NextResponse.json({ error: "usageError" }, { status: 503 });
  }

  if (!process.env.GEMINI_API_KEY && !process.env.GROQ_API_KEY) {
    return NextResponse.json({ error: "aiUnconfigured" }, { status: 503 });
  }

  // 4) One hydrated snapshot for every tool call in this request.
  let state;
  try {
    state = await loadState(uid);
  } catch (error) {
    console.warn("ai_state_error", (error as Error)?.name ?? "Error");
    return NextResponse.json({ error: "usageError" }, { status: 503 });
  }

  const ctx: ToolContext = { uid, clientToday: body.clientToday };
  const input: RunInput = {
    locale: body.locale,
    ...(body.text ? { text: body.text } : {}),
    ...(body.audio ? { audio: body.audio } : {}),
    ctx,
    state,
    system: systemInstruction(body.locale),
  };

  // 5) Provider ladder: fast Gemini → other Gemini → Groq (text only).
  const attempts: Array<() => Promise<AssistantReply>> = [];
  if (process.env.GEMINI_API_KEY) {
    attempts.push(() => runGemini(input, PRIMARY_MODEL));
    attempts.push(() => runGemini(input, FALLBACK_GEMINI));
  }
  if (process.env.GROQ_API_KEY && (body.text || !body.audio)) {
    attempts.push(() => runGroq(input));
  }

  let reply: AssistantReply | null = null;
  for (const attempt of attempts) {
    try {
      reply = await attempt();
      break;
    } catch (error) {
      if (error instanceof ClarificationNeeded) {
        return NextResponse.json({
          answer:
            body.locale === "ar" ? "أي طالب تقصد؟" : "Which student do you mean?",
          needsClarification: { candidates: error.candidates },
        });
      }
      // Log only the error class — never prompts, tokens or student data.
      console.warn("ai_provider_error", (error as Error)?.name ?? "Error");
    }
  }

  if (!reply) {
    return NextResponse.json({ error: "modelError" }, { status: 502 });
  }
  return NextResponse.json(reply);
}
````

````tsx
"use client";

import { useEffect } from "react";
import { ErrorState } from "@/components/ui/ErrorState";
import { useI18n } from "@/i18n/I18nProvider";

export default function GlobalError({
  error,
  reset,
}: {
  error: Error & { digest?: string };
  reset: () => void;
}) {
  const { t } = useI18n();

  useEffect(() => {
    console.error(error);
  }, [error]);

  return (
    <div className="mx-auto flex min-h-dvh w-full max-w-md items-center justify-center px-5">
      <ErrorState
        title={t.common.errorTitle}
        body={t.common.errorBody}
        retryLabel={t.common.retry}
        onRetry={reset}
      />
    </div>
  );
}
````

````css
@import "tailwindcss";

@theme {
  --color-canvas: #f3ede3;
  --color-ink: #2e2b27;
  --color-muted: #7a7468;

  --color-primary: #6b8f7b;
  --color-primary-dark: #4f7261;
  --color-accent: #c58b6d;

  --color-success: #6b8f7b;
  --color-warning: #d8a24a;
  --color-danger: #c45b52;
  --color-info: #6f8fa6;

  --color-sand: #e6dbc9;
  --color-sage-soft: #cfdcd3;
  --color-clay-soft: #ebd5c7;

  --radius-card: 20px;
  --radius-btn: 14px;

  --shadow-glass: 0 8px 24px rgb(60 50 40 / 0.08);

  --font-sans: var(--font-cairo), ui-sans-serif, system-ui, -apple-system,
    "Segoe UI", sans-serif;
}

@layer base {
  html {
    -webkit-text-size-adjust: 100%;
  }

  body {
    background-color: var(--color-canvas);
    color: var(--color-ink);
    font-family: var(--font-sans);
    min-height: 100dvh;
    -webkit-tap-highlight-color: transparent;
  }

  ::selection {
    background-color: color-mix(in srgb, var(--color-primary) 25%, transparent);
  }

  :focus-visible {
    outline: 2px solid var(--color-primary);
    outline-offset: 2px;
  }
}

@layer components {
  /* Frosted stationery card. The only glass recipe in the app. */
  .glass-card {
    background-color: rgb(255 255 255 / 0.55);
    backdrop-filter: blur(18px);
    -webkit-backdrop-filter: blur(18px);
    border: 1px solid rgb(255 255 255 / 0.7);
    border-radius: var(--radius-card);
    box-shadow: var(--shadow-glass);
  }

  .safe-bottom {
    padding-bottom: max(env(safe-area-inset-bottom), 0px);
  }

  .safe-top {
    padding-top: max(env(safe-area-inset-top), 0px);
  }

  .press {
    transition:
      transform 200ms ease-out,
      opacity 200ms ease-out,
      background-color 200ms ease-out;
  }

  .press:active {
    transform: scale(0.98);
  }

  .no-scrollbar {
    scrollbar-width: none;
  }

  .no-scrollbar::-webkit-scrollbar {
    display: none;
  }
}

@media (prefers-reduced-motion: reduce) {
  .press:active {
    transform: none;
  }

  *,
  *::before,
  *::after {
    animation-duration: 0.01ms !important;
    transition-duration: 0.01ms !important;
  }
}
````

### `src/app/sw.ts`  ·  37 lines

````typescript
import { NetworkOnly, Serwist } from "serwist";
import { defaultCache } from "@serwist/next/worker";

interface SwScope {
  __SW_MANIFEST?: Array<{ url: string; revision: string | null }>;
}

const scope = self as unknown as SwScope;

const serwist = new Serwist({
  precacheEntries: scope.__SW_MANIFEST ?? [],
  skipWaiting: true,
  clientsClaim: true,
  navigationPreload: true,
  runtimeCaching: [
    ...defaultCache,
    {
      matcher: ({ url }) => url.pathname.startsWith("/api/"),
      handler: new NetworkOnly(),
    },
    {
      matcher: ({ url }) =>
        /googleapis|gstatic|firebase|identitytoolkit/.test(url.hostname),
      handler: new NetworkOnly(),
    },
  ],
  fallbacks: {
    entries: [
      {
        url: "/offline",
        matcher: ({ request }) => request.mode === "navigate",
      },
    ],
  },
});

serwist.addEventListeners();
````
