### `src/components/features/AiSheet.tsx`  ·  432 lines

````tsx
"use client";

import { useState } from "react";
import { Loader2, Mic, Send, SquarePen } from "lucide-react";
import { ConfirmCard } from "@/components/ui/ConfirmCard";
import { GlassCard } from "@/components/ui/GlassCard";
import { Sheet } from "@/components/ui/Sheet";
import { StatusChip } from "@/components/ui/StatusChip";
import { useAuth } from "@/data/auth/AuthProvider";
import { useTutorData } from "@/data/provider/hooks";
import { saveLesson } from "@/data/services/attendanceService";
import { recordPayment } from "@/data/services/paymentService";
import { cancelSession } from "@/data/services/sessionService";
import { todayKey } from "@/domain/dateKey";
import { format } from "@/i18n";
import { useI18n } from "@/i18n/I18nProvider";

type Message = { role: "user" | "assistant"; text: string };
type Proposal = { type: string; params: Record<string, unknown> };
type Candidate = { id: string; name: string; groupName: string };

interface SpeechLike {
  lang: string;
  interimResults: boolean;
  onresult: ((event: { results: ArrayLike<ArrayLike<{ transcript: string }>> }) => void) | null;
  onerror: (() => void) | null;
  onend: (() => void) | null;
  start: () => void;
}

function toBase64(buffer: ArrayBuffer): string {
  const bytes = new Uint8Array(buffer);
  let text = "";
  for (let i = 0; i < bytes.length; i += 8192) {
    text += String.fromCharCode(...bytes.subarray(i, i + 8192));
  }
  return btoa(text);
}

export function AiSheet({ open, onClose }: { open: boolean; onClose: () => void }) {
  const { t, locale } = useI18n();
  const { getIdToken } = useAuth();
  const {
    uid,
    online,
    students,
    sessionsById,
    attendance,
    payments,
    settings,
  } = useTutorData();

  const [messages, setMessages] = useState<Message[]>([]);
  const [input, setInput] = useState("");
  const [busy, setBusy] = useState(false);
  const [listening, setListening] = useState(false);
  const [errorKey, setErrorKey] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [proposals, setProposals] = useState<Proposal[]>([]);
  const [candidates, setCandidates] = useState<Candidate[]>([]);
  const [editing, setEditing] = useState<number | null>(null);
  const [editText, setEditText] = useState("");
  const [result, setResult] = useState<string | null>(null);

  const source = { students, sessionsById, attendance, payments, settings };

  function mapError(status: number, code?: string): string {
    if (status === 401) return "auth";
    if (status === 429) return "limit";
    if (status === 422 || code === "audioUnsupported") return "audio";
    if (code === "invalidRequest") return "generic";
    // 500/502/503/504 (including a Vercel function error) are server-side,
    // not "no connection" — only a thrown fetch is a network failure.
    return "model";
  }

  async function ask(text: string, audio?: { base64: string; mimeType: string }) {
    if (busy || !online) return;
    setBusy(true);
    setErrorKey(null);
    setResult(null);
    setProposals([]);
    setCandidates([]);
    if (text) setMessages((current) => [...current, { role: "user", text }]);

    try {
      const token = await getIdToken();
      if (!token) {
        setErrorKey("auth");
        return;
      }
      const response = await fetch("/api/ai/assistant", {
        method: "POST",
        headers: {
          authorization: `Bearer ${token}`,
          "content-type": "application/json",
        },
        body: JSON.stringify({
          ...(text ? { text } : {}),
          ...(audio ? { audio } : {}),
          locale,
          clientToday: todayKey(),
        }),
      });
      const data = (await response.json().catch(() => ({}))) as {
        answer?: string;
        proposals?: Proposal[];
        needsClarification?: { candidates: Candidate[] };
        error?: string;
      };

      if (!response.ok) {
        setErrorKey(mapError(response.status, data.error));
        return;
      }

      setMessages((current) => [
        ...current,
        { role: "assistant", text: data.answer ?? "" },
      ]);
      setProposals(data.proposals ?? []);
      setCandidates(data.needsClarification?.candidates ?? []);
    } catch {
      setErrorKey("network");
    } finally {
      setBusy(false);
    }
  }

  function startSpeech() {
    type SpeechWindow = Window & {
      SpeechRecognition?: new () => SpeechLike;
      webkitSpeechRecognition?: new () => SpeechLike;
    };
    const scope = window as SpeechWindow;
    const Recognizer = scope.SpeechRecognition ?? scope.webkitSpeechRecognition;

    if (Recognizer) {
      const recognition = new Recognizer();
      recognition.lang = "ar-EG";
      recognition.interimResults = false;
      recognition.onresult = (event) => {
        setInput(event.results[0][0].transcript);
        setListening(false);
      };
      recognition.onerror = () => setListening(false);
      recognition.onend = () => setListening(false);
      recognition.start();
      setListening(true);
      return;
    }

    void (async () => {
      try {
        if (!navigator.mediaDevices?.getUserMedia || typeof MediaRecorder === "undefined") {
          setErrorKey("audio");
          return;
        }
        const stream = await navigator.mediaDevices.getUserMedia({ audio: true });
        const mime = ["audio/webm;codecs=opus", "audio/mp4", "audio/webm"].find(
          (candidate) => MediaRecorder.isTypeSupported(candidate),
        );
        const recorder = new MediaRecorder(
          stream,
          mime ? { mimeType: mime } : undefined,
        );
        const chunks: Blob[] = [];
        recorder.ondataavailable = (event) => chunks.push(event.data);
        recorder.onstop = () => {
          const blob = new Blob(chunks, { type: recorder.mimeType || mime || "audio/webm" });
          void blob.arrayBuffer().then((buffer) => {
            void ask("", { base64: toBase64(buffer), mimeType: blob.type });
          });
          stream.getTracks().forEach((track) => track.stop());
          setListening(false);
        };
        recorder.start();
        setListening(true);
        window.setTimeout(() => recorder.stop(), 3000);
      } catch {
        setListening(false);
        setErrorKey("audio");
      }
    })();
  }

  async function runProposal(proposal: Proposal, params: Record<string, unknown>) {
    if (!uid) return;
    setBusy(true);
    setErrorKey(null);
    try {
      if (proposal.type === "attendance") {
        const resultFromSave = await saveLesson(uid, {
          sessionId: String(params.sessionId),
          absentIds: Array.isArray(params.absentIds)
            ? params.absentIds.map(String)
            : [],
          source,
        });
        setResult(
          resultFromSave.newlyDue.length > 0
            ? format(t.attendance.newlyDue, { n: resultFromSave.newlyDue.length })
            : t.attendance.savedBody,
        );
      } else if (proposal.type === "payment") {
        await recordPayment(
          uid,
          {
            studentId: String(params.studentId),
            amount: Number(params.amount),
            sessionsCovered: Number(params.sessionsCovered ?? settings.cycleLength),
            dateKey: String(params.dateKey ?? todayKey()),
            ...(typeof params.note === "string" ? { note: params.note } : {}),
          },
          source,
        );
        setResult(t.ai.resultBody);
      } else if (proposal.type === "cancelSession") {
        await cancelSession(uid, String(params.sessionId), undefined, source);
        setResult(t.ai.resultBody);
      } else if (proposal.type === "reminder") {
        const text = String(params.text ?? "");
        void navigator.clipboard?.writeText(text).catch(() => {});
        const student = students.find((item) => item.id === params.studentId);
        const phone = student?.phone?.replace(/[^\d]/g, "") ?? "";
        window.open(
          `https://wa.me/${phone}?text=${encodeURIComponent(text)}`,
          "_blank",
          "noreferrer",
        );
        setResult(t.dues.copied);
      }
      setProposals((current) => current.filter((_, index) => index !== editing));
      setEditing(null);
    } catch {
      setErrorKey("generic");
    } finally {
      setBusy(false);
    }
  }

  const suggestions = [
    t.ai.suggestion1,
    t.ai.suggestion2,
    t.ai.suggestion3,
    t.ai.suggestion4,
  ];

  return (
    <Sheet open={open} onClose={onClose} title={t.ai.title}>
      <div className="flex flex-col gap-3">
        <p className="text-[12.5px] text-muted">{t.ai.subtitle}</p>

        <div className="no-scrollbar flex max-h-64 flex-col gap-2 overflow-y-auto">
          {messages.length === 0 ? (
            <div className="no-scrollbar flex gap-2 overflow-x-auto pb-1">
              {suggestions.map((suggestion) => (
                <button
                  key={suggestion}
                  type="button"
                  onClick={() => void ask(suggestion)}
                  disabled={!online || busy}
                  className="press shrink-0 rounded-full bg-white/60 px-3 py-2 text-[12px] disabled:opacity-50"
                >
                  {suggestion}
                </button>
              ))}
            </div>
          ) : (
            messages.map((message, index) => (
              <GlassCard
                key={index}
                className={`max-w-[90%] p-3 text-[13.5px] leading-6 ${
                  message.role === "user" ? "self-end bg-primary/15" : "self-start"
                }`}
              >
                {message.text}
              </GlassCard>
            ))
          )}

          {candidates.length > 0 ? (
            <GlassCard className="self-start p-3">
              <p className="mb-2 text-[13px] font-semibold">{t.ai.clarifyTitle}</p>
              <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-2">
                {candidates.map((candidate) => (
                  <button
                    key={candidate.id}
                    type="button"
                    onClick={() => void ask(`${candidate.name} — ${candidate.groupName}`)}
                    className="press rounded-full bg-white/60 px-3 py-1.5 text-[12px]"
                  >
                    {candidate.name} · {candidate.groupName}
                  </button>
                ))}
              </div>
            </GlassCard>
          ) : null}

          {proposals.map((proposal, index) => (
            <GlassCard key={index} className="self-start p-3">
              <p className="mb-1 text-[13px] font-semibold">{t.ai.proposalTitle}</p>
              <p className="mb-2 text-[12px] text-muted">{t.ai.proposalExplain}</p>
              <p dir="ltr" className="mb-2 text-[12px]">{proposal.type}</p>
              {editing === index ? (
                <div className="flex flex-col gap-2">
                  <textarea
                    rows={4}
                    dir="ltr"
                    className="w-full rounded-btn border border-white/80 bg-white/70 p-2 text-[11px] outline-none"
                    value={editText}
                    onChange={(event) => setEditText(event.target.value)}
                  />
                  <p className="text-[11px] text-muted">{t.ai.editHint}</p>
                  <button
                    type="button"
                    onClick={() => {
                      try {
                        const parsed = JSON.parse(editText) as Record<string, unknown>;
                        setProposals((current) =>
                          current.map((item, i) =>
                            i === index ? { ...item, params: parsed } : item,
                          ),
                        );
                        setEditing(null);
                      } catch {
                        setErrorKey("generic");
                      }
                    }}
                    className="press min-h-10 rounded-btn bg-primary text-[12px] font-semibold text-white"
                  >
                    {t.common.save}
                  </button>
                </div>
              ) : (
                <ConfirmCard
                  title={proposal.type}
                  confirmLabel={t.ai.confirm}
                  cancelLabel={t.ai.cancel}
                  tone="primary"
                  busy={busy}
                  onCancel={() =>
                    setProposals((current) => current.filter((_, i) => i !== index))
                  }
                  onConfirm={() => void runProposal(proposal, proposal.params)}
                />
              )}
              {editing !== index ? (
                <button
                  type="button"
                  onClick={() => {
                    setEditing(index);
                    setEditText(JSON.stringify(proposal.params, null, 2));
                  }}
                  className="press mt-1 flex items-center gap-1 text-[12px] text-primary-dark"
                >
                  <SquarePen size={13} aria-hidden />
                  {t.ai.edit}
                </button>
              ) : null}
            </GlassCard>
          ))}
        </div>

        {busy ? (
          <p className="flex items-center gap-2 text-[12.5px] text-muted">
            <Loader2 size={14} className="animate-spin" aria-hidden />
            {t.ai.thinking}
          </p>
        ) : null}
        {errorKey ? (
          <p role="alert" className="text-[12.5px] text-danger">
            {t.ai.errors[errorKey as keyof typeof t.ai.errors]}
          </p>
        ) : null}
        {result ? (
          <div className="flex items-center justify-between gap-2 rounded-btn bg-success/10 px-3 py-2">
            <StatusChip tone="success">{result}</StatusChip>
            <button type="button" onClick={() => setResult(null)} className="text-[12px] text-muted">
              {t.common.close}
            </button>
          </div>
        ) : null}

        {!online ? <p className="text-[12.5px] text-warning">{t.ai.offline}</p> : null}

        <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
          <input
            className="min-h-11 flex-1 rounded-btn border border-white/80 bg-white/70 px-3 text-[14px] outline-none focus:border-primary/60"
            placeholder={t.ai.placeholder}
            value={input}
            onChange={(event) => setInput(event.target.value)}
            onKeyDown={(event) => {
              if (event.key === "Enter" && input.trim()) {
                const text = input.trim();
                setInput("");
                void ask(text);
              }
            }}
          />
          <button
            type="button"
            aria-label={t.ai.mic}
            disabled={!online || busy}
            onClick={startSpeech}
            className={`press flex h-11 w-11 shrink-0 items-center justify-center rounded-btn ${
              listening ? "bg-danger text-white" : "bg-white/60 text-primary"
            } disabled:opacity-50`}
          >
            <Mic size={18} aria-hidden />
          </button>
          <button
            type="button"
            aria-label={t.ai.send}
            disabled={!online || busy || !input.trim()}
            onClick={() => {
              const text = input.trim();
              setInput("");
              void ask(text);
            }}
            className="press flex h-11 w-11 shrink-0 items-center justify-center rounded-btn bg-primary text-white disabled:opacity-50"
          >
            <Send size={17} aria-hidden />
          </button>
        </div>

        {listening ? (
          <p className="text-[12px] text-primary-dark">{t.ai.listening}</p>
        ) : null}
      </div>
    </Sheet>
  );
}
````

### `src/components/features/BalanceChip.tsx`  ·  48 lines

````tsx
"use client";

import { AlertCircle, CheckCircle2, Clock, Coins } from "lucide-react";
import { StatusChip } from "@/components/ui/StatusChip";
import { formatMoney } from "@/domain/money";
import type { StudentBalance } from "@/domain/types";
import { format, type Dict } from "@/i18n";

export function BalanceChip({
  balance,
  t,
  cycleLength,
}: {
  balance: StudentBalance;
  t: Dict;
  cycleLength: number;
}) {
  switch (balance.state) {
    case "DUE":
      return (
        <StatusChip tone="danger" icon={<AlertCircle size={12} aria-hidden />}>
          {format(t.students.amountDueChip, {
            amount: formatMoney(balance.amountDue, t.common.currency),
          })}
        </StatusChip>
      );
    case "PAID_AHEAD":
      return (
        <StatusChip tone="info" icon={<Coins size={12} aria-hidden />}>
          {format(t.students.paidAhead, { n: balance.paidAheadBy })}
        </StatusChip>
      );
    case "IN_PROGRESS":
      return (
        <StatusChip tone="warning" icon={<Clock size={12} aria-hidden />}>
          {format(t.students.remainingSessions, {
            n: Math.max(cycleLength - balance.balance, 0),
          })}
        </StatusChip>
      );
    default:
      return (
        <StatusChip tone="success" icon={<CheckCircle2 size={12} aria-hidden />}>
          {t.students.settled}
        </StatusChip>
      );
  }
}
````

````tsx
"use client";

import { useState } from "react";
import { useRouter } from "next/navigation";
import { Loader2, Plus, Trash2 } from "lucide-react";
import { BigPrimaryButton } from "@/components/ui/BigPrimaryButton";
import { Field, inputClass } from "@/components/ui/Field";
import { GlassCard } from "@/components/ui/GlassCard";
import { PageHeader } from "@/components/ui/Headers";
import { Segmented } from "@/components/ui/Segmented";
import { createGroup, updateGroup } from "@/data/services/groupService";
import { useTutorData } from "@/data/provider/hooks";
import type { Group } from "@/domain/types";
import {
  normalizeName,
  normalizeSlot,
  type ValidationErrorKey,
} from "@/domain/validation";
import { useI18n } from "@/i18n/I18nProvider";
import { weekdayOptions } from "@/lib/weekdays";

type SlotDraft = { weekday: string; time: string; durationMin: string };
type ErrorKey = ValidationErrorKey | "saveFailed";

const emptySlot = (): SlotDraft => ({
  weekday: "6",
  time: "17:00",
  durationMin: "60",
});

export function GroupForm({ group }: { group?: Group }) {
  const { t } = useI18n();
  const router = useRouter();
  const { uid } = useTutorData();

  const [name, setName] = useState(group?.name ?? "");
  const [subject, setSubject] = useState(group?.subject ?? "");
  const [slots, setSlots] = useState<SlotDraft[]>(
    group?.slots.length
      ? group.slots.map((slot) => ({
          weekday: String(slot.weekday),
          time: slot.time,
          durationMin: String(slot.durationMin),
        }))
      : [emptySlot()],
  );
  const [error, setError] = useState<ErrorKey | null>(null);
  const [busy, setBusy] = useState(false);

  const weekdays = weekdayOptions(t).map((option) => ({
    value: String(option.value),
    label: option.label,
  }));

  function updateSlot(index: number, patch: Partial<SlotDraft>) {
    setSlots((current) =>
      current.map((slot, i) => (i === index ? { ...slot, ...patch } : slot)),
    );
  }

  async function handleSubmit(event: React.FormEvent<HTMLFormElement>) {
    event.preventDefault();
    if (!uid || busy) return;
    setError(null);

    const title = normalizeName(name);
    if (!title.ok) return setError(title.error);
    if (slots.length === 0) return setError("noSlots");

    const normalized = [];
    for (const slot of slots) {
      const result = normalizeSlot({
        weekday: Number(slot.weekday),
        time: slot.time,
        durationMin: slot.durationMin,
      });
      if (!result.ok) return setError(result.error);
      normalized.push(result.value);
    }

    setBusy(true);
    try {
      let id: string;
      if (group) {
        await updateGroup(uid, group.id, {
          name: title.value,
          subject: subject.trim() || undefined,
          slots: normalized,
        });
        id = group.id;
      } else {
        id = await createGroup(uid, {
          name: title.value,
          subject: subject.trim() || undefined,
          slots: normalized,
        });
      }
      router.push(`/groups/${id}`);
    } catch {
      setError("saveFailed");
      setBusy(false);
    }
  }

  return (
    <form onSubmit={handleSubmit} className="flex flex-col gap-5">
      <PageHeader
        title={group ? t.forms.editGroup : t.forms.newGroup}
        subtitle={t.forms.noSlotsHint}
      />

      <GlassCard className="flex flex-col gap-4 p-4">
        <Field
          label={t.groups.name}
          error={error === "nameRequired" || error === "nameTooShort" ? t.forms[error] : undefined}
        >
          <input
            id="group-name"
            className={inputClass}
            value={name}
            onChange={(event) => setName(event.target.value)}
            required
          />
        </Field>

        <Field label={t.groups.subject} hint={t.common.optional}>
          <input
            id="group-subject"
            className={inputClass}
            value={subject}
            onChange={(event) => setSubject(event.target.value)}
          />
        </Field>
      </GlassCard>

      <section className="flex flex-col gap-2">
        <h2 className="px-1 text-[17px] font-semibold">{t.groups.slots}</h2>

        {slots.map((slot, index) => (
          <GlassCard key={index} className="flex flex-col gap-3 p-4">
            <div className="flex items-center justify-between gap-2">
              <p className="text-[13px] font-medium text-muted">
                {t.groups.slotNumber.replace("{n}", String(index + 1))}
              </p>
              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={() => setSlots((current) => current.filter((_, i) => i !== index))}
                aria-label={t.forms.removeSlot}
                className="press rounded-full p-2 text-muted hover:text-danger"
              >
                <Trash2 size={17} aria-hidden />
              </button>
            </div>

            <div className="flex flex-col gap-1.5">
              <span className="text-[13px] font-medium text-muted">
                {t.groups.weekday}
              </span>
              <Segmented
                label={t.groups.weekday}
                value={slot.weekday}
                options={weekdays}
                onChange={(value) => updateSlot(index, { weekday: value })}
              />
            </div>

            <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-3">
              <Field label={t.groups.time}>
                <input
                  type="time"
                  dir="ltr"
                  className={inputClass}
                  value={slot.time}
                  onChange={(event) => updateSlot(index, { time: event.target.value })}
                  required
                />
              </Field>
              <Field label={t.groups.duration}>
                <input
                  type="number"
                  inputMode="numeric"
                  dir="ltr"
                  min={15}
                  max={180}
                  step={5}
                  className={`${inputClass} tabular-nums`}
                  value={slot.durationMin}
                  onChange={(event) =>
                    updateSlot(index, { durationMin: event.target.value })
                  }
                  required
                />
              </Field>
            </div>
          </GlassCard>
        ))}

        <button
          type="button"
          onClick={() => setSlots((current) => [...current, emptySlot()])}
          className="press flex min-h-12 items-center justify-center gap-2 rounded-btn border border-white/70 bg-white/50 text-[14px] font-medium"
        >
          <Plus size={17} aria-hidden />
          {t.groups.addSlot}
        </button>
      </section>

      {error && error !== "nameRequired" && error !== "nameTooShort" ? (
        <p role="alert" className="rounded-btn bg-danger/10 px-3 py-2 text-[13px] text-danger">
          {t.forms[error]}
        </p>
      ) : null}

      <div className="flex gap-2">
        <div className="flex-1">
          <BigPrimaryButton variant="ghost" onClick={() => router.back()}>
            {t.common.cancel}
          </BigPrimaryButton>
        </div>
        <div className="flex-[2]">
          <BigPrimaryButton type="submit" disabled={busy || !uid}>
            {busy ? <Loader2 size={17} className="animate-spin" aria-hidden /> : null}
            {busy ? t.forms.saving : t.common.save}
          </BigPrimaryButton>
        </div>
      </div>
    </form>
  );
}
````

### `src/components/features/PaymentSheet.tsx`  ·  239 lines

````tsx
"use client";

import { useState } from "react";
import { Loader2, Trash2 } from "lucide-react";
import { BalanceChip } from "@/components/features/BalanceChip";
import { BigPrimaryButton } from "@/components/ui/BigPrimaryButton";
import { ConfirmCard } from "@/components/ui/ConfirmCard";
import { Field, inputClass } from "@/components/ui/Field";
import { GlassCard } from "@/components/ui/GlassCard";
import { Sheet } from "@/components/ui/Sheet";
import { useTutorData } from "@/data/provider/hooks";
import { recordPayment, removePayment, updatePayment } from "@/data/services/paymentService";
import { computeStudentBalance } from "@/domain/balanceCalculator";
import { todayKey } from "@/domain/dateKey";
import { expectedCycleAmount, formatMoney } from "@/domain/money";
import type { Payment, Student } from "@/domain/types";
import {
  normalizeCount,
  normalizeDateKey,
  normalizePrice,
} from "@/domain/validation";
import { useI18n } from "@/i18n/I18nProvider";

type ErrorKey = "invalidAmount" | "invalidCount" | "invalidDate" | "saveFailed";

export function PaymentSheet({
  open,
  onClose,
  student,
  payment,
  initialDelete = false,
}: {
  open: boolean;
  onClose: () => void;
  student: Student;
  payment?: Payment;
  initialDelete?: boolean;
}) {
  const { t } = useI18n();
  const { uid, balances, settings, students, sessionsById, attendance, payments } =
    useTutorData();

  const [amount, setAmount] = useState(
    String(payment?.amount ?? expectedCycleAmount(student.pricePerSession, settings.cycleLength)),
  );
  const [covered, setCovered] = useState(
    String(payment?.sessionsCovered ?? settings.cycleLength),
  );
  const [dateKey, setDateKey] = useState(payment?.dateKey ?? todayKey());
  const [note, setNote] = useState(payment?.note ?? "");
  const [error, setError] = useState<ErrorKey | null>(null);
  const [busy, setBusy] = useState(false);
  const [deleting, setDeleting] = useState(initialDelete);

  const current = balances.get(student.id);
  const rawCovered = Number(covered);
  const safeCovered =
    Number.isFinite(rawCovered) && rawCovered >= 1 ? Math.round(rawCovered) : 1;
  const extraCovered = safeCovered - (payment?.sessionsCovered ?? 0);
  const preview = computeStudentBalance(
    student,
    {
      counted: current?.counted ?? 0,
      paid: Math.max(0, (current?.paid ?? 0) + extraCovered),
    },
    settings,
  );

  const source = { students, sessionsById, attendance, payments, settings };

  async function handleSave() {
    if (!uid || busy) return;
    setError(null);

    const fee = normalizePrice(amount);
    if (!fee.ok || fee.value === 0) return setError("invalidAmount");
    const sessions = normalizeCount(covered);
    if (!sessions.ok || sessions.value === 0) return setError("invalidCount");
    const day = normalizeDateKey(dateKey);
    if (!day.ok) return setError("invalidDate");

    setBusy(true);
    try {
      if (payment) {
        await updatePayment(
          uid,
          payment.id,
          {
            amount: fee.value,
            sessionsCovered: sessions.value,
            dateKey: day.value,
            note: note.trim() || undefined,
          },
          source,
        );
      } else {
        await recordPayment(
          uid,
          {
            studentId: student.id,
            amount: fee.value,
            sessionsCovered: sessions.value,
            dateKey: day.value,
            note: note.trim() || undefined,
          },
          source,
        );
      }
      onClose();
    } catch {
      setError("saveFailed");
      setBusy(false);
    }
  }

  async function handleDelete() {
    if (!uid || !payment || busy) return;
    setBusy(true);
    try {
      await removePayment(uid, payment.id, source);
      onClose();
    } catch {
      setError("saveFailed");
      setBusy(false);
    }
  }

  if (!open) return null;

  return (
    <Sheet
      open={open}
      onClose={() => (deleting ? setDeleting(false) : onClose())}
      title={payment ? `${t.common.edit} · ${t.dues.record}` : t.dues.record}
    >
      {deleting ? (
        <ConfirmCard
          title={t.forms.confirmDeletePaymentTitle}
          body={t.forms.confirmDeletePaymentBody}
          confirmLabel={t.common.delete}
          cancelLabel={t.common.cancel}
          tone="danger"
          busy={busy}
          onCancel={() => setDeleting(false)}
          onConfirm={() => void handleDelete()}
        />
      ) : (
        <div className="flex flex-col gap-3">
          <GlassCard className="flex items-center justify-between gap-3 p-3">
            <div>
              <p className="text-[14px] font-semibold">{student.name}</p>
              <p className="text-[12px] text-muted">
                {t.students.price}: {formatMoney(student.pricePerSession, t.common.currency)}
              </p>
            </div>
            <BalanceChip balance={preview} t={t} cycleLength={settings.cycleLength} />
          </GlassCard>

          <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-3">
            <Field
              label={t.dues.amountDue}
              error={error === "invalidAmount" ? t.forms.invalidAmount : undefined}
            >
              <input
                type="number"
                inputMode="numeric"
                dir="ltr"
                min={1}
                step={1}
                className={`${inputClass} tabular-nums`}
                value={amount}
                onChange={(event) => setAmount(event.target.value)}
              />
            </Field>
            <Field
              label={t.students.paymentSessions}
              error={error === "invalidCount" ? t.forms.invalidCount : undefined}
            >
              <input
                type="number"
                inputMode="numeric"
                dir="ltr"
                min={1}
                step={1}
                className={`${inputClass} tabular-nums`}
                value={covered}
                onChange={(event) => setCovered(event.target.value)}
              />
            </Field>
          </div>

          <Field label={t.students.startDate} error={error === "invalidDate" ? t.forms.invalidDate : undefined}>
            <input
              type="date"
              dir="ltr"
              className={`${inputClass} tabular-nums`}
              value={dateKey}
              onChange={(event) => setDateKey(event.target.value)}
            />
          </Field>

          <Field label={t.students.note} hint={t.common.optional}>
            <input
              className={inputClass}
              value={note}
              onChange={(event) => setNote(event.target.value)}
            />
          </Field>

          {expectedCycleAmount(student.pricePerSession, safeCovered) !== Number(amount) ? (
            <p className="text-[12px] leading-5 text-warning">{t.forms.paymentMismatch}</p>
          ) : null}

          {error === "saveFailed" ? (
            <p role="alert" className="text-[12.5px] text-danger">
              {t.forms.saveFailed}
            </p>
          ) : null}

          <BigPrimaryButton onClick={() => void handleSave()} disabled={busy}>
            {busy ? <Loader2 size={16} className="animate-spin" aria-hidden /> : null}
            {t.common.save}
          </BigPrimaryButton>

          {payment ? (
            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={() => setDeleting(true)}
              className="press flex min-h-11 items-center justify-center gap-2 rounded-btn text-[13px] text-danger"
            >
              <Trash2 size={15} aria-hidden />
              {t.common.delete}
            </button>
          ) : null}
        </div>
      )}
    </Sheet>
  );
}
````

### `src/components/features/PwaPrompts.tsx`  ·  116 lines

````tsx
"use client";

import { useEffect, useState } from "react";
import { Download, RefreshCw } from "lucide-react";
import { BigPrimaryButton } from "@/components/ui/BigPrimaryButton";
import { GlassCard } from "@/components/ui/GlassCard";
import { Sheet } from "@/components/ui/Sheet";
import { useI18n } from "@/i18n/I18nProvider";

interface InstallPromptEvent extends Event {
  prompt: () => Promise<void>;
  userChoice: Promise<{ outcome: string }>;
}

const DISMISSED_KEY = "tm.install.dismissed";

export function PwaPrompts() {
  const { t } = useI18n();
  const [installEvent, setInstallEvent] = useState<InstallPromptEvent | null>(null);
  const [installOpen, setInstallOpen] = useState(false);
  const [updateReady, setUpdateReady] = useState(false);

  useEffect(() => {
    const onBeforeInstall = (event: Event) => {
      event.preventDefault();
      setInstallEvent(event as InstallPromptEvent);
    };
    const onLessonSaved = () => {
      try {
        if (localStorage.getItem(DISMISSED_KEY) === "1") return;
      } catch {
        // Private mode: just show the prompt.
      }
      setInstallOpen(true);
    };

    window.addEventListener("beforeinstallprompt", onBeforeInstall);
    window.addEventListener("tm:lesson-saved", onLessonSaved);

    if ("serviceWorker" in navigator) {
      void navigator.serviceWorker.ready
        .then((registration) => {
          registration.addEventListener("updatefound", () => {
            const installing = registration.installing;
            installing?.addEventListener("statechange", () => {
              if (
                installing.state === "installed" &&
                navigator.serviceWorker.controller
              ) {
                setUpdateReady(true);
              }
            });
          });
        })
        .catch(() => {});
    }

    return () => {
      window.removeEventListener("beforeinstallprompt", onBeforeInstall);
      window.removeEventListener("tm:lesson-saved", onLessonSaved);
    };
  }, []);

  function dismiss() {
    try {
      localStorage.setItem(DISMISSED_KEY, "1");
    } catch {
      // Ignore storage failures.
    }
    setInstallOpen(false);
  }

  return (
    <>
      <Sheet
        open={installOpen && installEvent !== null}
        onClose={dismiss}
        title={t.pwa.installTitle}
      >
        <div className="flex flex-col gap-3">
          <p className="text-[13.5px] leading-6 text-muted">{t.pwa.installBody}</p>
          <BigPrimaryButton
            onClick={() => {
              void installEvent?.prompt().finally(dismiss);
            }}
          >
            <Download size={16} aria-hidden />
            {t.pwa.install}
          </BigPrimaryButton>
          <BigPrimaryButton variant="ghost" onClick={dismiss}>
            {t.pwa.later}
          </BigPrimaryButton>
        </div>
      </Sheet>

      {updateReady ? (
        <div className="fixed inset-x-0 top-3 z-40 mx-auto w-full max-w-md px-4">
          <GlassCard className="flex items-center gap-3 p-3">
            <RefreshCw size={17} className="shrink-0 text-primary" aria-hidden />
            <div className="min-w-0 flex-1">
              <p className="text-[13.5px] font-semibold">{t.pwa.updateTitle}</p>
              <p className="text-[12px] text-muted">{t.pwa.updateBody}</p>
            </div>
            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={() => window.location.reload()}
              className="press shrink-0 rounded-full bg-primary px-3 py-2 text-[12px] font-semibold text-white"
            >
              {t.pwa.update}
            </button>
          </GlassCard>
        </div>
      ) : null}
    </>
  );
}
````

### `src/components/features/StudentForm.tsx`  ·  272 lines

````tsx
"use client";

import { useState } from "react";
import { useRouter } from "next/navigation";
import { ChevronDown, Loader2 } from "lucide-react";
import { BigPrimaryButton } from "@/components/ui/BigPrimaryButton";
import { Field, inputClass } from "@/components/ui/Field";
import { GlassCard } from "@/components/ui/GlassCard";
import { PageHeader } from "@/components/ui/Headers";
import {
  enrollStudent,
  updateStudentAndReconcile,
} from "@/data/services/enrollmentService";
import { useTutorData } from "@/data/provider/hooks";
import { todayKey } from "@/domain/dateKey";
import type { Student } from "@/domain/types";
import {
  normalizeCount,
  normalizeDateKey,
  normalizeName,
  normalizePhone,
  normalizePrice,
  type ValidationErrorKey,
} from "@/domain/validation";
import { useI18n } from "@/i18n/I18nProvider";

type ErrorKey = ValidationErrorKey | "groupRequired" | "saveFailed";

export function StudentForm({ student }: { student?: Student }) {
  const { t } = useI18n();
  const router = useRouter();
  const { uid, groups, students, sessionsById, attendance, payments, settings } =
    useTutorData();

  const [name, setName] = useState(student?.name ?? "");
  const [groupId, setGroupId] = useState(() => {
    if (typeof window === "undefined") return "";
    return new URLSearchParams(window.location.search).get("groupId") ?? "";
  });
  const [price, setPrice] = useState(String(student?.pricePerSession ?? 50));
  const [startedAtKey, setStartedAtKey] = useState(student?.startedAtKey ?? todayKey());
  const [phone, setPhone] = useState(student?.phone ?? "");
  const [note, setNote] = useState(student?.note ?? "");
  const [initialCounted, setInitialCounted] = useState(
    String(student?.initialCounted ?? 0),
  );
  const [initialPaid, setInitialPaid] = useState(String(student?.initialPaid ?? 0));
  const [error, setError] = useState<ErrorKey | null>(null);
  const [busy, setBusy] = useState(false);

  // An archived student's group may itself be archived: keep it selectable so
  // editing does not silently move them to another group.
  const groupOptions = groups.filter(
    (group) => group.active || group.id === student?.groupId,
  );

  async function handleSubmit(event: React.FormEvent<HTMLFormElement>) {
    event.preventDefault();
    if (!uid || busy) return;
    setError(null);

    const title = normalizeName(name);
    if (!title.ok) return setError(title.error);
    if (!groupId) return setError("groupRequired");

    const fee = normalizePrice(price);
    if (!fee.ok) return setError(fee.error);
    const start = normalizeDateKey(startedAtKey);
    if (!start.ok) return setError(start.error);
    const parentPhone = normalizePhone(phone);
    if (!parentPhone.ok) return setError(parentPhone.error);
    const carriedCounted = normalizeCount(initialCounted);
    if (!carriedCounted.ok) return setError(carriedCounted.error);
    const carriedPaid = normalizeCount(initialPaid);
    if (!carriedPaid.ok) return setError(carriedPaid.error);

    const source = { students, sessionsById, attendance, payments, settings };
    const values = {
      name: title.value,
      groupId,
      pricePerSession: fee.value,
      startedAtKey: start.value,
      phone: parentPhone.value,
      note: note.trim() || undefined,
      initialCounted: carriedCounted.value,
      initialPaid: carriedPaid.value,
    };

    setBusy(true);
    try {
      let id: string;
      if (student) {
        await updateStudentAndReconcile(uid, student.id, values, source);
        id = student.id;
      } else {
        id = await enrollStudent(uid, values, source);
      }
      router.push(`/students/${id}`);
    } catch {
      setError("saveFailed");
      setBusy(false);
    }
  }

  return (
    <form onSubmit={handleSubmit} className="flex flex-col gap-5">
      <PageHeader title={student ? t.forms.editStudent : t.forms.newStudent} />

      <GlassCard className="flex flex-col gap-4 p-4">
        <Field
          label={t.students.title}
          error={error === "nameRequired" || error === "nameTooShort" ? t.forms[error] : undefined}
        >
          <input
            id="student-name"
            className={inputClass}
            value={name}
            onChange={(event) => setName(event.target.value)}
            required
          />
        </Field>

        <Field
          label={t.students.group}
          error={error === "groupRequired" ? t.forms.groupRequired : undefined}
        >
          <select
            id="student-group"
            className={inputClass}
            value={groupId}
            onChange={(event) => setGroupId(event.target.value)}
            required
          >
            <option value="">{t.forms.selectGroup}</option>
            {groupOptions.map((group) => (
              <option key={group.id} value={group.id}>
                {group.name}
              </option>
            ))}
          </select>
        </Field>

        {groupOptions.length === 0 ? (
          <p className="text-[12.5px] text-warning">{t.forms.noActiveGroups}</p>
        ) : null}

        <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-3">
          <Field
            label={t.students.price}
            error={error === "invalidPrice" ? t.forms.invalidPrice : undefined}
          >
            <input
              type="number"
              inputMode="numeric"
              dir="ltr"
              min={0}
              step={1}
              className={`${inputClass} tabular-nums`}
              value={price}
              onChange={(event) => setPrice(event.target.value)}
              required
            />
          </Field>

          <Field
            label={t.students.startDate}
            error={error === "invalidDate" ? t.forms.invalidDate : undefined}
          >
            <input
              type="date"
              dir="ltr"
              className={`${inputClass} tabular-nums`}
              value={startedAtKey}
              onChange={(event) => setStartedAtKey(event.target.value)}
              required
            />
          </Field>
        </div>

        <Field
          label={t.students.phone}
          hint={t.common.optional}
          error={error === "invalidPhone" ? t.forms.invalidPhone : undefined}
        >
          <input
            type="tel"
            dir="ltr"
            inputMode="tel"
            className={`${inputClass} tabular-nums`}
            value={phone}
            onChange={(event) => setPhone(event.target.value)}
          />
        </Field>

        <Field label={t.students.note} hint={t.common.optional}>
          <textarea
            rows={3}
            className={`${inputClass} min-h-20 py-2`}
            value={note}
            onChange={(event) => setNote(event.target.value)}
          />
        </Field>
      </GlassCard>

      <details className="glass-card group p-4">
        <summary className="flex cursor-pointer list-none items-center justify-between text-[14px] font-semibold">
          {t.forms.advanced}
          <ChevronDown
            size={18}
            className="text-muted transition-transform group-open:rotate-180"
            aria-hidden
          />
        </summary>
        <p className="mt-1 text-[12px] leading-5 text-muted">
          {t.forms.advancedHint}
        </p>
        <div className="mt-3 grid grid-cols-2 gap-3">
          <Field
            label={t.students.initialCounted}
            error={error === "invalidCount" ? t.forms.invalidCount : undefined}
          >
            <input
              type="number"
              inputMode="numeric"
              dir="ltr"
              min={0}
              step={1}
              className={`${inputClass} tabular-nums`}
              value={initialCounted}
              onChange={(event) => setInitialCounted(event.target.value)}
            />
          </Field>
          <Field
            label={t.students.initialPaid}
            error={error === "invalidCount" ? t.forms.invalidCount : undefined}
          >
            <input
              type="number"
              inputMode="numeric"
              dir="ltr"
              min={0}
              step={1}
              className={`${inputClass} tabular-nums`}
              value={initialPaid}
              onChange={(event) => setInitialPaid(event.target.value)}
            />
          </Field>
        </div>
      </details>

      {error === "saveFailed" ? (
        <p role="alert" className="rounded-btn bg-danger/10 px-3 py-2 text-[13px] text-danger">
          {t.forms.saveFailed}
        </p>
      ) : null}

      <div className="flex gap-2">
        <div className="flex-1">
          <BigPrimaryButton variant="ghost" onClick={() => router.back()}>
            {t.common.cancel}
          </BigPrimaryButton>
        </div>
        <div className="flex-[2]">
          <BigPrimaryButton type="submit" disabled={busy || !uid}>
            {busy ? <Loader2 size={17} className="animate-spin" aria-hidden /> : null}
            {busy ? t.forms.saving : t.common.save}
          </BigPrimaryButton>
        </div>
      </div>
    </form>
  );
}
````

````tsx
"use client";

import Link from "next/link";
import { usePathname } from "next/navigation";
import { useState } from "react";
import {
  BarChart3,
  CalendarDays,
  FolderOpen,
  MoreHorizontal,
  Settings as SettingsIcon,
  Sun,
  Users,
  Wallet,
  Wrench,
} from "lucide-react";
import { cn } from "@/lib/cn";
import { useI18n } from "@/i18n/I18nProvider";
import { Sheet } from "./Sheet";

const tabs = [
  { href: "/", key: "today", Icon: Sun },
  { href: "/schedule", key: "schedule", Icon: CalendarDays },
  { href: "/students", key: "students", Icon: Users },
  { href: "/stats", key: "stats", Icon: BarChart3 },
] as const;

export function BottomBar() {
  const pathname = usePathname();
  const { t } = useI18n();
  const [moreOpen, setMoreOpen] = useState(false);

  const moreLinks = [
    { href: "/groups", label: t.nav.groups, Icon: FolderOpen },
    { href: "/dues", label: t.nav.dues, Icon: Wallet },
    { href: "/settings", label: t.nav.settings, Icon: SettingsIcon },
    ...(process.env.NODE_ENV !== "production"
      ? [{ href: "/dev", label: t.nav.dev, Icon: Wrench }]
      : []),
  ];

  return (
    <>
      <nav
        aria-label={t.nav.more}
        className="safe-bottom fixed inset-x-0 bottom-0 z-40 mx-auto w-full max-w-md border-t border-white/60 bg-white/70 backdrop-blur-[18px]"
      >
        <ul className="grid grid-cols-5">
          {tabs.map(({ href, key, Icon }) => {
            const active =
              href === "/" ? pathname === "/" : pathname.startsWith(href);
            return (
              <li key={href}>
                <Link
                  href={href}
                  aria-current={active ? "page" : undefined}
                  className={cn(
                    "press flex min-h-14 flex-col items-center justify-center gap-0.5 text-[11px]",
                    active ? "text-primary" : "text-muted",
                  )}
                >
                  <Icon size={20} strokeWidth={active ? 2.4 : 2} aria-hidden />
                  {t.nav[key]}
                </Link>
              </li>
            );
          })}
          <li>
            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={() => setMoreOpen(true)}
              className="press flex min-h-14 w-full flex-col items-center justify-center gap-0.5 text-[11px] text-muted"
            >
              <MoreHorizontal size={20} aria-hidden />
              {t.nav.more}
            </button>
          </li>
        </ul>
      </nav>

      <Sheet
        open={moreOpen}
        onClose={() => setMoreOpen(false)}
        title={t.nav.more}
      >
        <ul className="flex flex-col gap-1">
          {moreLinks.map(({ href, label, Icon }) => (
            <li key={href}>
              <Link
                href={href}
                onClick={() => setMoreOpen(false)}
                className="press flex min-h-12 items-center gap-3 rounded-btn px-3 text-[15px] hover:bg-white/50"
              >
                <Icon size={20} className="text-primary" aria-hidden />
                {label}
              </Link>
            </li>
          ))}
        </ul>
      </Sheet>
    </>
  );
}
````

import type { ReactNode } from "react";
import { AlertTriangle } from "lucide-react";
import { BigPrimaryButton } from "./BigPrimaryButton";

/** Inline confirmation — used inside a Sheet for destructive actions. */
export function ConfirmCard({
  title,
  body,
  confirmLabel,
  cancelLabel,
  tone = "danger",
  busy,
  onConfirm,
  onCancel,
}: {
  title: string;
  body?: string;
  confirmLabel: string;
  cancelLabel: string;
  tone?: "danger" | "primary";
  busy?: boolean;
  onConfirm: () => void;
  onCancel: () => void;
}): ReactNode {
  return (
    <div className="flex flex-col gap-3">
      <div className="flex items-start gap-2">
        <span
          className={`mt-0.5 flex h-8 w-8 shrink-0 items-center justify-center rounded-full ${
            tone === "danger" ? "bg-danger/15 text-danger" : "bg-primary/15 text-primary"
          }`}
        >
          <AlertTriangle size={16} aria-hidden />
        </span>
        <div>
          <p className="text-[15px] font-semibold">{title}</p>
          {body ? <p className="mt-1 text-[13px] leading-5 text-muted">{body}</p> : null}
        </div>
      </div>

      <div className="flex gap-2">
        <div className="flex-1">
          <BigPrimaryButton variant="ghost" onClick={onCancel} disabled={busy}>
            {cancelLabel}
          </BigPrimaryButton>
        </div>
        <div className="flex-1">
          <BigPrimaryButton
            onClick={onConfirm}
            disabled={busy}
            className={
              tone === "danger"
                ? "bg-danger shadow-[0_8px_20px_rgba(196,91,82,0.2)]"
                : undefined
            }
          >
            {confirmLabel}
          </BigPrimaryButton>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

### `src/components/ui/CounterRing.tsx`  ·  57 lines

````tsx
export function CounterRing({
  position,
  length,
  size = 44,
}: {
  position: number;
  length: number;
  size?: number;
}) {
  const stroke = 4;
  const radius = (size - stroke) / 2;
  const circumference = 2 * Math.PI * radius;
  const ratio = length > 0 ? Math.min(Math.max(position / length, 0), 1) : 0;

  return (
    <div
      className="relative inline-flex shrink-0 items-center justify-center"
      style={{ width: size, height: size }}
    >
      <svg
        width={size}
        height={size}
        viewBox={`0 0 ${size} ${size}`}
        className="-rotate-90"
        aria-hidden
      >
        <circle
          cx={size / 2}
          cy={size / 2}
          r={radius}
          fill="none"
          strokeWidth={stroke}
          className="text-sand"
          stroke="currentColor"
        />
        <circle
          cx={size / 2}
          cy={size / 2}
          r={radius}
          fill="none"
          strokeWidth={stroke}
          strokeLinecap="round"
          stroke="currentColor"
          className="text-primary transition-[stroke-dashoffset] duration-200 ease-out"
          strokeDasharray={circumference}
          strokeDashoffset={circumference * (1 - ratio)}
        />
      </svg>
      <span className="absolute text-[11px] font-semibold tabular-nums">
        {position}/{length}
      </span>
      <span className="sr-only">
        {position} / {length}
      </span>
    </div>
  );
}
````

### `src/components/ui/DateStepper.tsx`  ·  61 lines

````tsx
"use client";

import { ChevronLeft, ChevronRight } from "lucide-react";
import { addDaysKey, formatArabicDate, todayKey } from "@/domain/dateKey";
import { NumberText } from "./NumberText";
import { useI18n } from "@/i18n/I18nProvider";

/** Cairo-aware day navigation. The "today" shortcut appears off the current day. */
export function DateStepper({
  value,
  onChange,
}: {
  value: string;
  onChange: (value: string) => void;
}) {
  const { t } = useI18n();
  const isToday = value === todayKey();

  return (
    <div className="glass-card flex items-center justify-between gap-2 p-2">
      <button
        type="button"
        aria-label={t.schedule.prevDay}
        onClick={() => onChange(addDaysKey(value, -1))}
        className="press flex h-11 w-11 items-center justify-center rounded-btn text-muted hover:bg-white/60"
      >
        <ChevronRight size={20} className="rtl:rotate-180" aria-hidden />
      </button>

      <div className="flex flex-col items-center">
        <p className="text-[15px] font-semibold">
          <NumberText>
            {formatArabicDate(value, {
              weekday: "long",
              day: "numeric",
              month: "long",
            })}
          </NumberText>
        </p>
        {!isToday ? (
          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={() => onChange(todayKey())}
            className="text-[12px] font-medium text-primary-dark"
          >
            {t.schedule.today}
          </button>
        ) : null}
      </div>

      <button
        type="button"
        aria-label={t.schedule.nextDay}
        onClick={() => onChange(addDaysKey(value, 1))}
        className="press flex h-11 w-11 items-center justify-center rounded-btn text-muted hover:bg-white/60"
      >
        <ChevronLeft size={20} className="rtl:rotate-180" aria-hidden />
      </button>
    </div>
  );
}
````

"use client";

import { useState } from "react";
import { usePathname } from "next/navigation";
import { Sparkles } from "lucide-react";
import { useAiEnabled } from "@/components/features/AiConfig";
import { AiSheet } from "@/components/features/AiSheet";
import { useI18n } from "@/i18n/I18nProvider";

export function FloatingAiButton() {
  const { t } = useI18n();
  const enabled = useAiEnabled();
  const pathname = usePathname();
  const [open, setOpen] = useState(false);

  // The attendance save bar owns this spot while a lesson is being recorded.
  if (!enabled || pathname.startsWith("/attendance/")) return null;

  return (
    <>
      <div className="pointer-events-none fixed inset-x-0 bottom-32 z-30 mx-auto flex max-w-md justify-end px-4">
        <button
          type="button"
          onClick={() => setOpen(true)}
          aria-label={t.ai.title}
          className="press pointer-events-auto flex h-12 w-12 items-center justify-center rounded-full bg-primary text-white shadow-[0_8px_20px_rgba(107,143,123,0.3)]"
        >
          <Sparkles size={20} aria-hidden />
        </button>
      </div>
      <AiSheet open={open} onClose={() => setOpen(false)} />
    </>
  );
}
````

export function Segmented<T extends string>({
  value,
  options,
  onChange,
  label,
}: {
  value: T;
  options: ReadonlyArray<{ value: T; label: string }>;
  onChange: (value: T) => void;

          key={option.value}

            "press shrink-0 rounded-full px-3 py-1.5 text-[12.5px] font-medium",
            value === option.value
              ? "bg-primary text-white"
              : "bg-white/55 text-muted",

        >
          {option.label}
        </button>
      ))}

  );

### `src/components/ui/Sheet.tsx`  ·  98 lines

````tsx
"use client";

import { useEffect, useRef, type ReactNode } from "react";
import { useI18n } from "@/i18n/I18nProvider";

const FOCUSABLE =
  'button:not([disabled]), [href], input:not([disabled]), select:not([disabled]), textarea:not([disabled]), [tabindex]:not([tabindex="-1"])';

export function Sheet({
  open,
  onClose,
  title,
  children,
}: {
  open: boolean;
  onClose: () => void;
  title: string;
  children: ReactNode;
}) {
  const dialogRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null);
  const onCloseRef = useRef(onClose);
  const { t } = useI18n();

  useEffect(() => {
    onCloseRef.current = onClose;
  }, [onClose]);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!open) return;

    const previousFocus = document.activeElement as HTMLElement | null;
    const focusables = () =>
      Array.from(dialogRef.current?.querySelectorAll<HTMLElement>(FOCUSABLE) ?? []);

    focusables()[0]?.focus();

    const onKey = (event: KeyboardEvent) => {
      if (event.key === "Escape") {
        onCloseRef.current();
        return;
      }
      if (event.key !== "Tab") return;
      const items = focusables();
      if (items.length === 0) return;
      const first = items[0];
      const last = items[items.length - 1];
      if (event.shiftKey && document.activeElement === first) {
        event.preventDefault();
        last.focus();
      } else if (!event.shiftKey && document.activeElement === last) {
        event.preventDefault();
        first.focus();
      }
    };

    document.addEventListener("keydown", onKey);
    document.body.style.overflow = "hidden";
    return () => {
      document.removeEventListener("keydown", onKey);
      document.body.style.overflow = "";
      previousFocus?.focus?.();
    };
  }, [open]);

  if (!open) return null;

  return (
    <div className="fixed inset-0 z-50 flex items-end justify-center">
      <button
        type="button"
        aria-label={title}
        onClick={() => onCloseRef.current()}
        className="absolute inset-0 bg-ink/25"
      />
      <div
        ref={dialogRef}
        role="dialog"
        aria-modal="true"
        aria-label={title}
        className="glass-card safe-bottom relative max-h-[85dvh] w-full max-w-md overflow-y-auto rounded-b-none p-4"
      >
        <div className="mx-auto mb-3 h-1 w-10 rounded-full bg-ink/15" />
        <div className="mb-3 flex items-center justify-between gap-3">
          <h2 className="text-[17px] font-semibold">{title}</h2>
          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={() => onCloseRef.current()}
            className="rounded-full px-3 py-1 text-[13px] text-muted"
            aria-label={t.common.close}
          >
            ✕
          </button>
        </div>
        {children}
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}
````

import type { ReactNode } from "react";
import { cn } from "@/lib/cn";

export type ChipTone = "neutral" | "success" | "warning" | "danger" | "info";

const tones: Record<ChipTone, string> = {
  neutral: "bg-ink/[0.06] text-muted",
  success: "bg-success/15 text-success",
  warning: "bg-warning/15 text-warning",
  danger: "bg-danger/15 text-danger",
  info: "bg-info/15 text-info",
};

/** Status is never colour-only: callers pass an icon or a word. */
export function StatusChip({
  tone = "neutral",
  icon,
  children,
  className,
}: {
  tone?: ChipTone;
  icon?: ReactNode;
  children: ReactNode;
  className?: string;
}) {
  return (
    <span
      className={cn(
        "inline-flex items-center gap-1 rounded-full px-2.5 py-1 text-[12.5px] leading-none font-medium",
        tones[tone],
        className,
      )}
    >
      {icon ? <span aria-hidden>{icon}</span> : null}
      {children}
    </span>
  );
}

### `src/data/ai/providers.ts`  ·  256 lines

````typescript
import { GoogleGenAI, type Content, type Part } from "@google/genai";
import { parseAssistantReply, type AssistantReply } from "./schema";
import {
  AI_TOOLS,
  ClarificationNeeded,
  executeTool,
  type State,
  type ToolContext,
} from "./tools";

export interface RunInput {
  locale: "ar" | "en";
  text?: string;
  audio?: { base64: string; mimeType: string };
  ctx: ToolContext;
  state: State;
  system: string;
}

export function systemInstruction(locale: "ar" | "en"): string {
  const arabic = `
أنت مساعد مدير مدرس خاص. أجب بالعربية المصرية الواضحة والمختصرة.
قواعد لا تخرقها:
1) كل رقم في إجابتك يجب أن يأتي من نتيجة أداة (tool) — لا تخمن أبدًا.
2) إذا سُئلت عن مبلغ أو طالب أو حصة، استدعِ الأداة المناسبة أولًا.
3) لا تنفّذ أي تعديل بنفسك. استخدم أدوات propose_* فقط لإرجاع مقترح، ثم يؤكد المستخدم في الواجهة.
4) إذا كان الاسم مكررًا أو غير واضح، اطلب التوضيح ولا تختر طالبًا.
5) لا تذكر أرقام تليفونات، ولا تدّعي أنك حفظت شيئًا.
6) أرجع القيمة بصيغة JSON فقط: {"answer":"...", "proposals":[{"type":"...","params":{...}}]}.
7) إذا لم تكن هناك مقترحات، أرجع proposals كقائمة فارغة أو احذفها.
`;
  const english = `
You are a private tutor's assistant. Answer briefly in the same language as the question.
Every number must come from a tool result — never guess. Use propose_* tools for
actions; never claim something was saved. Ask for clarification on ambiguous names.
Reply with JSON only: {"answer":"...", "proposals":[]}.
`;
  return locale === "ar" ? arabic : english;
}

/** Gemini (primary). Keeps raw model parts so thought_signature survives. */
export async function runGemini(
  input: RunInput,
  model: string,
): Promise<AssistantReply> {
  const apiKey = process.env.GEMINI_API_KEY;
  if (!apiKey) throw new Error("GEMINI_NOT_CONFIGURED");

  const parts: Part[] = [];
  if (input.text) parts.push({ text: input.text });
  if (input.audio) {
    parts.push({
      inlineData: {
        mimeType: input.audio.mimeType,
        data: input.audio.base64,
      },
    });
  }
  const contents: Content[] = [{ role: "user", parts }];

  const ai = new GoogleGenAI({ apiKey });

  for (let round = 0; round < 3; round += 1) {
    const response = await ai.models.generateContent({
      model,
      contents,
      config: {
        systemInstruction: input.system,
        tools: [{ functionDeclarations: AI_TOOLS }],
        temperature: 0.1,
        maxOutputTokens: 2048,
      },
    });

    const calls = response.functionCalls ?? [];
    if (calls.length === 0) {
      return parseAssistantReply(response.text ?? "", input.locale);
    }

    contents.push(
      response.candidates?.[0]?.content ?? {
        role: "model",
        parts: calls.map((call) => ({
          functionCall: { name: call.name ?? "", args: call.args ?? {} },
        })),
      },
    );

    const outcomes: Array<{ name: string; output: unknown }> = [];
    for (const call of calls) {
      try {
        const output = await executeTool(
          call.name ?? "",
          (call.args ?? {}) as Record<string, unknown>,
          input.ctx,
          input.state,
        );
        outcomes.push({ name: call.name ?? "", output });
      } catch (error) {
        if (error instanceof ClarificationNeeded) throw error;
        console.warn("ai_tool_error", call.name, (error as Error)?.name ?? "Error");
        outcomes.push({
          name: call.name ?? "",
          output: { error: (error as Error)?.name ?? "TOOL_ERROR" },
        });
      }
    }

    contents.push({
      role: "user",
      parts: outcomes.map((outcome) => ({
        functionResponse: {
          name: outcome.name,
          response: { output: outcome.output },
        },
      })),
    });
  }

  return parseAssistantReply("", input.locale);
}

type GroqMessage = {
  role: "system" | "user" | "assistant" | "tool";
  content: string | null;
  tool_calls?: Array<{
    id: string;
    type: "function";
    function: { name: string; arguments: string };
  }>;
  tool_call_id?: string;
};

function groqTools() {
  return AI_TOOLS.map((tool) => ({
    type: "function",
    function: {
      name: tool.name ?? "",
      description: tool.description ?? "",
      parameters: (tool.parametersJsonSchema ?? {
        type: "object",
        properties: {},
      }) as Record<string, unknown>,
    },
  }));
}

/**
 * Groq fallback. Text-only (no inline audio); uses the OpenAI-compatible API
 * with the same read/propose tools as Gemini.
 */
export async function runGroq(input: RunInput): Promise<AssistantReply> {
  const apiKey = process.env.GROQ_API_KEY;
  if (!apiKey) throw new Error("GROQ_NOT_CONFIGURED");

  const model = process.env.GROQ_MODEL || "openai/gpt-oss-120b";
  const messages: GroqMessage[] = [
    { role: "system", content: input.system },
    {
      role: "user",
      content: input.text || "اكتب سؤالك بدل التسجيل الصوتي.",
    },
  ];

  for (let round = 0; round < 3; round += 1) {
    const response = await fetch(
      "https://api.groq.com/openai/v1/chat/completions",
      {
        method: "POST",
        headers: {
          authorization: `Bearer ${apiKey}`,
          "content-type": "application/json",
        },
        body: JSON.stringify({
          model,
          messages,
          tools: groqTools(),
          tool_choice: "auto",
          temperature: 0.1,
          max_completion_tokens: 2048,
        }),
      },
    );

    if (!response.ok) {
      throw new Error(`GROQ_${response.status}`);
    }

    const body = (await response.json()) as {
      choices?: Array<{
        message?: {
          content?: string | null;
          tool_calls?: Array<{
            id: string;
            type: string;
            function: { name: string; arguments: string };
          }>;
        };
      }>;
    };
    const message = body.choices?.[0]?.message;
    if (!message) throw new Error("GROQ_EMPTY_RESPONSE");

    const calls = message.tool_calls ?? [];
    if (calls.length === 0) {
      return parseAssistantReply(message.content ?? "", input.locale);
    }

    messages.push({
      role: "assistant",
      content: message.content ?? null,
      tool_calls: calls.map((call) => ({
        id: call.id,
        type: "function",
        function: {
          name: call.function.name,
          arguments: call.function.arguments,
        },
      })),
    });

    for (const call of calls) {
      let args: Record<string, unknown> = {};
      try {
        args = JSON.parse(call.function.arguments || "{}") as Record<
          string,
          unknown
        >;
      } catch {
        // Keep empty args; the tool validator will report the problem to the model.
      }

      let output: unknown;
      try {
        output = await executeTool(
          call.function.name,
          args,
          input.ctx,
          input.state,
        );
      } catch (error) {
        if (error instanceof ClarificationNeeded) throw error;
        console.warn("ai_tool_error", call.function.name, (error as Error)?.name ?? "Error");
        output = { error: (error as Error)?.name ?? "TOOL_ERROR" };
      }

      messages.push({
        role: "tool",
        tool_call_id: call.id,
        content: JSON.stringify(output),
      });
    }
  }

  return parseAssistantReply("", input.locale);
}
````

### `src/data/ai/schema.ts`  ·  51 lines

````typescript
import { z } from "zod";

export const AssistantBodySchema = z
  .object({
    text: z.string().trim().min(1).max(1000).optional(),
    audio: z
      .object({
        base64: z.string().min(1).max(2_800_000), // ≤ 2 MB decoded
        mimeType: z.string().min(1).max(100),
      })
      .optional(),
    locale: z.enum(["ar", "en"]),
    clientToday: z.string().regex(/^\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}$/),
  })
  .refine((body) => Boolean(body.text || body.audio), {
    message: "text_or_audio_required",
  });

export const AssistantReplySchema = z.object({
  answer: z.string().min(1),
  proposals: z
    .array(
      z.object({
        type: z.string(),
        params: z.record(z.string(), z.unknown()),
      }),
    )
    .optional(),
});

export type AssistantReply = z.infer<typeof AssistantReplySchema>;

/** Defensive parse: a model that ignores the JSON contract still gets an answer. */
export function parseAssistantReply(
  text: string,
  locale: "ar" | "en",
): AssistantReply {
  const cleaned = text
    .trim()
    .replace(/^```(?:json)?\s*/i, "")
    .replace(/\s*```$/, "");
  try {
    return AssistantReplySchema.parse(JSON.parse(cleaned));
  } catch {
    return {
      answer:
        cleaned ||
        (locale === "ar" ? "مش قادر أجاوب دلوقتي." : "I can't answer right now."),
    };
  }
}
````

### `src/data/ai/tools.ts`  ·  446 lines

````typescript
import { getAdmin } from "@/data/firebase/admin";
import {
  attendancePath,
  groupsPath,
  paymentsPath,
  sessionsPath,
  studentsPath,
  userPath,
} from "@/data/paths";
import {
  mapAttendance,
  mapGroup,
  mapPayment,
  mapSession,
  mapSettings,
  mapStudent,
} from "@/data/mappers";
import { needsReminder, computeStudentBalance } from "@/domain/balanceCalculator";
import { computeCounters } from "@/domain/counters";
import { expectedCycleAmount } from "@/domain/money";
import { computeGroupStatus } from "@/domain/groupPosition";
import {
  collectedInMonth,
  incomeByMonth,
  monthKeyOf,
} from "@/domain/stats";
import type {
  Counters,
  DateKey,
  Group,
  Session,
  Student,
  StudentBalance,
  TutorSettings,
} from "@/domain/types";
import { redactStrings } from "@/domain/aiSanitize";
import type { FunctionDeclaration } from "@google/genai";

/** Raised when a name matches several students; the model must ask, not guess. */
export class ClarificationNeeded extends Error {
  constructor(
    public readonly candidates: Array<{ id: string; name: string; groupName: string }>,
  ) {
    super("NEEDS_CLARIFICATION");
  }
}

export interface ToolContext {
  uid: string;
  clientToday: DateKey;
}

export interface State {
  settings: TutorSettings;
  groups: Group[];
  students: Student[];
  sessionsById: Map<string, Session>;
  attendance: ReturnType<typeof mapAttendance>[];
  payments: ReturnType<typeof mapPayment>[];
  counters: Map<string, Counters>;
  balances: Map<string, StudentBalance>;
}

export async function loadState(uid: string): Promise<State> {
  const { db } = getAdmin();
  const [user, groups, students, sessions, attendance, payments] =
    await Promise.all([
      db.doc(userPath(uid)).get(),
      db.collection(groupsPath(uid)).get(),
      db.collection(studentsPath(uid)).get(),
      db.collection(sessionsPath(uid)).get(),
      db.collection(attendancePath(uid)).get(),
      db.collection(paymentsPath(uid)).get(),
    ]);

  const settings = mapSettings(user.data()?.settings);
  const groupList = groups.docs.map((item) => mapGroup(item.id, item.data()));
  const studentList = students.docs.map((item) => mapStudent(item.id, item.data()));
  const sessionMap = new Map(
    sessions.docs.map((item) => {
      const session = mapSession(item.id, item.data());
      return [session.id, session];
    }),
  );
  const attendanceList = attendance.docs.map((item) =>
    mapAttendance(item.id, item.data()),
  );
  const paymentList = payments.docs.map((item) => mapPayment(item.id, item.data()));

  const counters = computeCounters({
    students: studentList,
    sessionsById: sessionMap,
    attendance: attendanceList,
    payments: paymentList,
    settings,
  });
  const balances = new Map<string, StudentBalance>();
  for (const student of studentList) {
    balances.set(
      student.id,
      computeStudentBalance(
        student,
        counters.get(student.id) ?? { counted: 0, paid: 0 },
        settings,
      ),
    );
  }

  return {
    settings,
    groups: groupList,
    students: studentList,
    sessionsById: sessionMap,
    attendance: attendanceList,
    payments: paymentList,
    counters,
    balances,
  };
}

function resolveStudent(state: State, raw: unknown): Student {
  const name = String(raw ?? "").trim().toLowerCase();
  if (!name) throw new Error("STUDENT_NAME_REQUIRED");
  const byId = state.students.find((student) => student.id === name);
  if (byId) return byId;

  const exact = state.students.filter(
    (student) => student.name.trim().toLowerCase() === name,
  );
  const matches = exact.length
    ? exact
    : state.students.filter((student) =>
        student.name.toLowerCase().includes(name),
      );
  if (matches.length === 0) throw new Error("STUDENT_NOT_FOUND");
  if (matches.length > 1) {
    throw new ClarificationNeeded(
      matches.map((student) => ({
        id: student.id,
        name: student.name,
        groupName:
          state.groups.find((group) => group.id === student.groupId)?.name ?? "—",
      })),
    );
  }
  return matches[0];
}

function groupName(state: State, groupId: string): string {
  return state.groups.find((group) => group.id === groupId)?.name ?? "—";
}

function studentRow(state: State, student: Student) {
  const balance = state.balances.get(student.id);
  return {
    id: student.id,
    name: student.name,
    groupName: groupName(state, student.groupId),
    counted: balance?.counted ?? 0,
    paid: balance?.paid ?? 0,
    balance: balance?.balance ?? 0,
    state: balance?.state ?? "SETTLED",
    amountDue: balance?.amountDue ?? 0,
  };
}

async function execute(
  name: string,
  args: Record<string, unknown>,
  ctx: ToolContext,
  state: State,
): Promise<unknown> {
  switch (name) {
    case "get_dues": {
      const rows = state.students
        .filter((student) => student.active)
        .map((student) => studentRow(state, student))
        .filter((row) => row.state === "DUE")
        .sort((a, b) => b.amountDue - a.amountDue || a.name.localeCompare(b.name, "ar"));
      return { rows, total: rows.reduce((sum, row) => sum + row.amountDue, 0) };
    }

    case "get_almost_due": {
      const rows = state.students
        .filter((student) => student.active)
        .map((student) => ({
          ...studentRow(state, student),
          counted: state.counters.get(student.id)?.counted ?? 0,
        }))
        .filter((row) =>
          needsReminder(
            { counted: row.counted, state: row.state as StudentBalance["state"] },
            state.settings.cycleLength,
          ),
        );
      return { rows };
    }

    case "get_absences": {
      const monthKey = typeof args.monthKey === "string" ? args.monthKey : undefined;
      const target = args.studentName ? resolveStudent(state, args.studentName) : null;
      const rows = state.attendance
        .filter((row) => row.status === "absent")
        .filter((row) => !target || row.studentId === target.id)
        .filter((row) => {
          const session = state.sessionsById.get(row.sessionId);
          return (
            session?.status === "completed" &&
            (!monthKey || row.dateKey.startsWith(monthKey))
          );
        })
        .map((row) => ({
          dateKey: row.dateKey,
          studentName:
            state.students.find((student) => student.id === row.studentId)?.name ?? "—",
          groupName: groupName(state, row.groupId),
        }));
      return { total: rows.length, rows: rows.slice(0, 40) };
    }

    case "get_income": {
      const monthKey =
        typeof args.monthKey === "string" ? args.monthKey : monthKeyOf(ctx.clientToday);
      return {
        monthKey,
        collected: collectedInMonth(state.payments, monthKey),
        expectedOneCycle: state.students
          .filter((student) => student.active)
          .reduce(
            (sum, student) =>
              sum + expectedCycleAmount(student.pricePerSession, state.settings.cycleLength),
            0,
          ),
        series: incomeByMonth(state.payments, monthKey, 6),
      };
    }

    case "get_group_status": {
      return {
        groups: state.groups.map((group) => {
          const status = computeGroupStatus(
            state.students.filter((student) => student.groupId === group.id),
            state.balances,
          );
          return { id: group.id, name: group.name, ...status };
        }),
      };
    }

    case "get_student_summary": {
      const student = resolveStudent(state, args.studentName ?? args.studentId);
      return {
        ...studentRow(state, student),
        initialCounted: student.initialCounted,
        initialPaid: student.initialPaid,
      };
    }

    case "get_today_overview": {
      const sessions = [...state.sessionsById.values()]
        .filter((session) => session.dateKey === ctx.clientToday)
        .sort((a, b) => a.time.localeCompare(b.time))
        .map((session) => ({
          id: session.id,
          time: session.time,
          groupName: session.groupName,
          status: session.status,
        }));
      return {
        dateKey: ctx.clientToday,
        sessions,
        dueTotal: [...state.balances.values()].reduce(
          (sum, balance) => sum + Math.max(0, balance.amountDue),
          0,
        ),
        reminderCount: state.students.filter((student) =>
          needsReminder(
            {
              counted: state.counters.get(student.id)?.counted ?? 0,
              state: state.balances.get(student.id)?.state ?? "SETTLED",
            },
            state.settings.cycleLength,
          ),
        ).length,
      };
    }

    case "propose_attendance": {
      const sessionId = String(args.sessionId ?? "");
      const session = state.sessionsById.get(sessionId);
      if (!session) throw new Error("SESSION_NOT_FOUND");
      const absentIds = Array.isArray(args.absentStudentIds)
        ? args.absentStudentIds.map(String)
        : [];
      const valid = new Set(
        state.students
          .filter(
            (student) =>
              student.groupId === session.groupId &&
              student.active &&
              student.startedAtKey <= session.dateKey,
          )
          .map((student) => student.id),
      );
      if (absentIds.some((id) => !valid.has(id))) throw new Error("STUDENT_NOT_IN_LESSON");
      return {
        type: "attendance",
        params: { sessionId, absentIds, dateKey: session.dateKey },
      };
    }

    case "propose_payment": {
      const student = resolveStudent(state, args.studentName ?? args.studentId);
      const amount = Number(args.amount);
      if (!Number.isInteger(amount) || amount <= 0) throw new Error("INVALID_AMOUNT");
      const sessionsCovered = Number(
        args.sessionsCovered ?? state.settings.cycleLength,
      );
      if (!Number.isInteger(sessionsCovered) || sessionsCovered < 1) {
        throw new Error("INVALID_SESSIONS");
      }
      return {
        type: "payment",
        params: {
          studentId: student.id,
          amount,
          sessionsCovered,
          dateKey: typeof args.dateKey === "string" ? args.dateKey : ctx.clientToday,
          ...(typeof args.note === "string" ? { note: args.note } : {}),
        },
      };
    }

    case "propose_cancel_session": {
      const sessionId = String(args.sessionId ?? "");
      if (!state.sessionsById.has(sessionId)) throw new Error("SESSION_NOT_FOUND");
      return { type: "cancelSession", params: { sessionId } };
    }

    case "draft_parent_reminder": {
      const student = resolveStudent(state, args.studentName ?? args.studentId);
      const balance = state.balances.get(student.id);
      const text = `السلام عليكم ${student.name}، عندك ${balance?.amountDue ?? 0} مستحق. لو تحب نرتب الدفعة كلمني.`;
      return { type: "reminder", params: { studentId: student.id, text } };
    }

    default:
      throw new Error(`UNKNOWN_TOOL: ${name}`);
  }
}

export async function executeTool(
  name: string,
  args: Record<string, unknown>,
  ctx: ToolContext,
  preloaded?: State,
): Promise<unknown> {
  // One hydrated snapshot per request: a question that calls three tools must
  // not re-read six Firestore collections three times.
  const state = preloaded ?? (await loadState(ctx.uid));
  return redactStrings(await execute(name, args, ctx, state));
}

const object = (properties: Record<string, unknown>, required: string[] = []) => ({
  type: "object",
  properties,
  additionalProperties: false,
  ...(required.length ? { required } : {}),
});

export const AI_TOOLS: FunctionDeclaration[] = [
  {
    name: "get_dues",
    description: "Students who currently owe money, with exact amounts. No arguments.",
    parametersJsonSchema: object({}),
  },
  {
