### `src/data/auth/AuthProvider.tsx`  ·  135 lines

````tsx
"use client";

import {
  createContext,
  useCallback,
  useContext,
  useEffect,
  useMemo,
  useState,
  type ReactNode,
} from "react";
import {
  createUserWithEmailAndPassword,
  onAuthStateChanged,
  signInWithEmailAndPassword,
  signOut as firebaseSignOut,
  type User,
} from "firebase/auth";
import { getFirebase, isFirebaseConfigured } from "@/data/firebase/client";

export type AuthErrorKey =
  | "wrongPassword"
  | "invalidEmail"
  | "weakPassword"
  | "emailInUse"
  | "tooManyAttempts"
  | "networkError"
  | "genericError";

type SignInResult = { ok: true } | { ok: false; errorKey: AuthErrorKey };

type AuthContextValue = {
  user: User | null;
  loading: boolean;
  configured: boolean;
  signIn: (email: string, password: string) => Promise<SignInResult>;
  signUp: (email: string, password: string) => Promise<SignInResult>;
  signOut: () => Promise<void>;
  getIdToken: () => Promise<string | null>;
};

const AuthContext = createContext<AuthContextValue | null>(null);

function mapAuthError(error: unknown): AuthErrorKey {
  const code =
    typeof error === "object" && error !== null && "code" in error
      ? String((error as { code: unknown }).code)
      : "";

  switch (code) {
    case "auth/invalid-credential":
    case "auth/wrong-password":
    case "auth/user-not-found":
      return "wrongPassword";
    case "auth/invalid-email":
      return "invalidEmail";
    case "auth/weak-password":
      return "weakPassword";
    case "auth/email-already-in-use":
      return "emailInUse";
    case "auth/too-many-requests":
      return "tooManyAttempts";
    case "auth/network-request-failed":
      return "networkError";
    default:
      return "genericError";
  }
}

export function AuthProvider({ children }: { children: ReactNode }) {
  const configured = isFirebaseConfigured();
  const [user, setUser] = useState<User | null>(null);
  // Without a Firebase config there is no listener to wait for.
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(configured);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!configured) return;

    const { auth } = getFirebase();
    const unsubscribe = onAuthStateChanged(
      auth,
      (nextUser) => {
        setUser(nextUser);
        setLoading(false);
      },
      () => setLoading(false),
    );

    return unsubscribe;
  }, [configured]);

  const signIn = useCallback(async (email: string, password: string) => {
    try {
      const { auth } = getFirebase();
      await signInWithEmailAndPassword(auth, email.trim(), password);
      return { ok: true } as const;
    } catch (error) {
      return { ok: false, errorKey: mapAuthError(error) } as const;
    }
  }, []);

  const signUp = useCallback(async (email: string, password: string) => {
    try {
      const { auth } = getFirebase();
      await createUserWithEmailAndPassword(auth, email.trim(), password);
      return { ok: true } as const;
    } catch (error) {
      return { ok: false, errorKey: mapAuthError(error) } as const;
    }
  }, []);

  const signOut = useCallback(async () => {
    const { auth } = getFirebase();
    await firebaseSignOut(auth);
  }, []);

  const getIdToken = useCallback(async () => {
    if (!configured) return null;
    const { auth } = getFirebase();
    return (await auth.currentUser?.getIdToken()) ?? null;
  }, [configured]);

  const value = useMemo<AuthContextValue>(
    () => ({ user, loading, configured, signIn, signUp, signOut, getIdToken }),
    [user, loading, configured, signIn, signUp, signOut, getIdToken],
  );

  return <AuthContext.Provider value={value}>{children}</AuthContext.Provider>;
}

export function useAuth(): AuthContextValue {
  const ctx = useContext(AuthContext);
  if (!ctx) throw new Error("useAuth must be used inside <AuthProvider>");
  return ctx;
}
````

### `src/data/firebase/admin.ts`  ·  102 lines

````typescript
import {
  cert,
  getApp,
  getApps,
  initializeApp,
  type App,
  type ServiceAccount,
} from "firebase-admin/app";
import { getFirestore, type Firestore } from "firebase-admin/firestore";

let cached: { app: App; db: Firestore } | null = null;

/**
 * Server-only singleton for Firestore. We deliberately do NOT import
 * `firebase-admin/auth`: on Vercel it pulls `jwks-rsa` → `jose` (ESM) through a
 * CommonJS require and crashes the whole route at module load. ID tokens are
 * verified with `jose` via dynamic import instead (see verifyTutorToken).
 */
export function getAdmin(): { app: App; db: Firestore } {
  if (cached) return cached;

  const app = getApps().length > 0 ? getApp() : initializeAdmin();
  cached = { app, db: getFirestore(app) };
  return cached;
}

function initializeAdmin(): App {
  const raw = process.env.FIREBASE_SERVICE_ACCOUNT;
  if (raw) {
    const serviceAccount = JSON.parse(
      Buffer.from(raw, "base64").toString("utf8"),
    ) as { project_id?: string };
    return initializeApp({
      credential: cert(serviceAccount as ServiceAccount),
      projectId: serviceAccount.project_id,
    });
  }

  const projectId =
    process.env.GOOGLE_CLOUD_PROJECT ??
    process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_FIREBASE_PROJECT_ID ??
    "demo-tutor-manager";
  return initializeApp({ projectId });
}

/** Project id used both for the Admin app and for token verification. */
export function adminProjectId(): string {
  const raw = process.env.FIREBASE_SERVICE_ACCOUNT;
  if (raw) {
    try {
      const decoded = JSON.parse(Buffer.from(raw, "base64").toString("utf8")) as {
        project_id?: string;
      };
      if (decoded.project_id) return decoded.project_id;
    } catch {
      // Fall through to the public project id.
    }
  }
  return (
    process.env.GOOGLE_CLOUD_PROJECT ??
    process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_FIREBASE_PROJECT_ID ??
    "demo-tutor-manager"
  );
}

/**
 * Verify a Firebase ID token without `firebase-admin/auth`.
 * `jose` is ESM-only, so it is loaded with a dynamic import (safe on Node 18–26).
 */
export async function verifyTutorToken(token: string): Promise<{ uid: string }> {
  const projectId = adminProjectId();

  // Emulator tokens are unsigned; the audience still identifies the project.
  if (process.env.FIREBASE_AUTH_EMULATOR_HOST) {
    const payloadPart = token.split(".")[1];
    if (!payloadPart) throw new Error("INVALID_TOKEN");
    const payload = JSON.parse(
      Buffer.from(payloadPart, "base64url").toString("utf8"),
    ) as { user_id?: string; sub?: string; aud?: string };
    const uid = payload.user_id ?? payload.sub;
    if (!uid || (payload.aud && payload.aud !== projectId)) {
      throw new Error("INVALID_TOKEN");
    }
    return { uid };
  }

  const { createRemoteJWKSet, jwtVerify } = await import("jose");
  const jwks = createRemoteJWKSet(
    new URL(
      "https://www.googleapis.com/service_accounts/v1/jwk/securetoken@system.gserviceaccount.com",
    ),
  );
  const { payload } = await jwtVerify(token, jwks, {
    issuer: `https://securetoken.google.com/${projectId}`,
    audience: projectId,
    algorithms: ["RS256"],
  });

  const uid = String(payload.user_id ?? payload.sub ?? "");
  if (!uid) throw new Error("INVALID_TOKEN");
  return { uid };
}
````

### `src/data/firebase/client.ts`  ·  86 lines

````typescript
import { getApp, getApps, initializeApp, type FirebaseApp } from "firebase/app";
import {
  browserLocalPersistence,
  connectAuthEmulator,
  getAuth,
  setPersistence,
  type Auth,
} from "firebase/auth";
import {
  connectFirestoreEmulator,
  getFirestore,
  initializeFirestore,
  memoryLocalCache,
  persistentLocalCache,
  persistentMultipleTabManager,
  type Firestore,
} from "firebase/firestore";

const firebaseConfig = {
  apiKey: process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_FIREBASE_API_KEY,
  authDomain: process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_FIREBASE_AUTH_DOMAIN,
  projectId: process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_FIREBASE_PROJECT_ID,
  appId: process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_FIREBASE_APP_ID,
  messagingSenderId: process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_FIREBASE_MESSAGING_SENDER_ID,
};

export const USING_EMULATORS = process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_USE_EMULATORS === "1";

export function isFirebaseConfigured(): boolean {
  return Boolean(
    firebaseConfig.apiKey &&
      firebaseConfig.projectId &&
      firebaseConfig.appId &&
      firebaseConfig.authDomain,
  );
}

let app: FirebaseApp | undefined;
let auth: Auth | undefined;
let db: Firestore | undefined;

/**
 * Lazy singleton. Lazy so `next build` can prerender the shell without env vars,
 * and so the production build never initialises Firebase on the server.
 */
export function getFirebase(): { app: FirebaseApp; auth: Auth; db: Firestore } {
  if (!isFirebaseConfigured()) {
    throw new Error("FIREBASE_NOT_CONFIGURED");
  }

  if (!app) {
    app = getApps().length > 0 ? getApp() : initializeApp(firebaseConfig);
  }

  if (!auth) {
    auth = getAuth(app);
    void setPersistence(auth, browserLocalPersistence).catch(() => {
      // Private mode can block persistence; the session then lives in memory.
    });
    if (USING_EMULATORS) {
      connectAuthEmulator(auth, "http://127.0.0.1:9099", {
        disableWarnings: true,
      });
    }
  }

  if (!db) {
    try {
      db = initializeFirestore(app, {
        localCache: USING_EMULATORS
          ? memoryLocalCache()
          : persistentLocalCache({
              tabManager: persistentMultipleTabManager(),
            }),
      });
    } catch {
      // Hot reload can re-run this module while the app instance survives.
      db = getFirestore(app);
    }
    if (USING_EMULATORS) {
      connectFirestoreEmulator(db, "127.0.0.1", 8080);
    }
  }

  return { app, auth, db };
}
````

### `src/data/mappers.ts`  ·  151 lines

````typescript
import { DEFAULT_SETTINGS } from "@/domain/types";
import type {
  Attendance,
  Group,
  Payment,
  Session,
  Slot,
  Student,
  TutorSettings,
  Weekday,
} from "@/domain/types";

type Raw = Record<string, unknown>;

function str(value: unknown, fallback = ""): string {
  return typeof value === "string" ? value : fallback;
}

function num(value: unknown, fallback = 0): number {
  return typeof value === "number" && Number.isFinite(value) ? value : fallback;
}

function bool(value: unknown, fallback = false): boolean {
  return typeof value === "boolean" ? value : fallback;
}

function ms(value: unknown): number | undefined {
  if (
    typeof value === "object" &&
    value !== null &&
    "toMillis" in value &&
    typeof (value as { toMillis: unknown }).toMillis === "function"
  ) {
    return (value as { toMillis: () => number }).toMillis();
  }
  return typeof value === "number" ? value : undefined;
}

function isWeekday(value: unknown): value is Weekday {
  return (
    typeof value === "number" &&
    Number.isInteger(value) &&
    value >= 0 &&
    value <= 6
  );
}

function toSlots(value: unknown): Slot[] {
  if (!Array.isArray(value)) return [];
  return value.flatMap((item) => {
    if (typeof item !== "object" || item === null) return [];
    const raw = item as Raw;
    if (!isWeekday(raw.weekday)) return [];
    return [
      {
        weekday: raw.weekday,
        time: str(raw.time, "17:00"),
        durationMin: num(raw.durationMin, 60),
      },
    ];
  });
}

export function mapSettings(value: unknown): TutorSettings {
  const raw = (typeof value === "object" && value !== null ? value : {}) as Raw;
  return {
    absenceCounts: bool(raw.absenceCounts, DEFAULT_SETTINGS.absenceCounts),
    cycleLength: Math.max(1, Math.round(num(raw.cycleLength, DEFAULT_SETTINGS.cycleLength))),
    language: raw.language === "en" ? "en" : "ar",
    currency: str(raw.currency, DEFAULT_SETTINGS.currency),
    currencyLabel: str(raw.currencyLabel, DEFAULT_SETTINGS.currencyLabel),
    weekStartsOn: isWeekday(raw.weekStartsOn)
      ? raw.weekStartsOn
      : DEFAULT_SETTINGS.weekStartsOn,
  };
}

export function mapGroup(id: string, value: Raw): Group {
  return {
    id,
    name: str(value.name, "—"),
    subject: typeof value.subject === "string" ? value.subject : undefined,
    slots: toSlots(value.slots),
    active: bool(value.active, true),
    createdAtMs: ms(value.createdAt),
    updatedAtMs: ms(value.updatedAt),
  };
}

export function mapStudent(id: string, value: Raw): Student {
  return {
    id,
    name: str(value.name, "—"),
    phone: typeof value.phone === "string" ? value.phone : undefined,
    groupId: str(value.groupId),
    pricePerSession: Math.max(0, Math.round(num(value.pricePerSession, 0))),
    startedAtKey: str(value.startedAtKey, "1970-01-01"),
    active: bool(value.active, true),
    note: typeof value.note === "string" ? value.note : undefined,
    initialCounted: Math.max(0, Math.round(num(value.initialCounted, 0))),
    initialPaid: Math.max(0, Math.round(num(value.initialPaid, 0))),
    sessionsCounted: Math.max(0, Math.round(num(value.sessionsCounted, 0))),
    sessionsPaid: Math.max(0, Math.round(num(value.sessionsPaid, 0))),
    createdAtMs: ms(value.createdAt),
    updatedAtMs: ms(value.updatedAt),
  };
}

export function mapSession(id: string, value: Raw): Session {
  const status =
    value.status === "completed" || value.status === "cancelled"
      ? value.status
      : "scheduled";
  return {
    id,
    groupId: str(value.groupId),
    groupName: str(value.groupName, "—"),
    dateKey: str(value.dateKey, "1970-01-01"),
    time: str(value.time, "17:00"),
    type: value.type === "extra" ? "extra" : "regular",
    status,
    note: typeof value.note === "string" ? value.note : undefined,
    createdAtMs: ms(value.createdAt),
    updatedAtMs: ms(value.updatedAt),
  };
}

export function mapAttendance(id: string, value: Raw): Attendance {
  return {
    id,
    sessionId: str(value.sessionId),
    studentId: str(value.studentId),
    groupId: str(value.groupId),
    dateKey: str(value.dateKey, "1970-01-01"),
    status: value.status === "absent" ? "absent" : "present",
    updatedAtMs: ms(value.updatedAt),
  };
}

export function mapPayment(id: string, value: Raw): Payment {
  return {
    id,
    studentId: str(value.studentId),
    dateKey: str(value.dateKey, "1970-01-01"),
    amount: Math.max(0, Math.round(num(value.amount, 0))),
    sessionsCovered: Math.max(0, Math.round(num(value.sessionsCovered, 0))),
    note: typeof value.note === "string" ? value.note : undefined,
    createdAtMs: ms(value.createdAt),
    updatedAtMs: ms(value.updatedAt),
  };
}
````

### `src/data/provider/TutorDataProvider.tsx`  ·  335 lines

````tsx
"use client";

import {
  createContext,
  useEffect,
  useMemo,
  useRef,
  useState,
  type ReactNode,
} from "react";
import { collection, doc, onSnapshot } from "firebase/firestore";
import { useAuth } from "@/data/auth/AuthProvider";
import {
  attendancePath,
  groupsPath,
  paymentsPath,
  sessionsPath,
  studentsPath,
  userPath,
} from "@/data/paths";
import { getFirebase } from "@/data/firebase/client";
import {
  mapAttendance,
  mapGroup,
  mapPayment,
  mapSession,
  mapSettings,
  mapStudent,
} from "@/data/mappers";
import { createDefaults } from "@/data/services/settingsService";
import { reconcileCounters } from "@/data/services/counterService";
import { ensureGenerated } from "@/data/services/sessionGenerator";
import { todayKey } from "@/domain/dateKey";
import { computeCounters } from "@/domain/counters";
import { computeStudentBalance } from "@/domain/balanceCalculator";
import {
  DEFAULT_SETTINGS,
  type Attendance,
  type Group,
  type Payment,
  type Session,
  type Student,
  type StudentBalance,
  type TutorSettings,
} from "@/domain/types";

type Source =
  | "settings"
  | "groups"
  | "students"
  | "sessions"
  | "attendance"
  | "payments";

const SOURCES: Source[] = [
  "settings",
  "groups",
  "students",
  "sessions",
  "attendance",
  "payments",
];

const emptyFlags = (): Record<Source, boolean> => ({
  settings: false,
  groups: false,
  students: false,
  sessions: false,
  attendance: false,
  payments: false,
});

export type TutorData = {
  status: "loading" | "ready" | "error";
  error: string | null;
  fromCache: boolean;
  online: boolean;
  uid: string | null;
  settings: TutorSettings;
  groups: Group[];
  students: Student[];
  sessions: Session[];
  attendance: Attendance[];
  payments: Payment[];
  sessionsById: ReadonlyMap<string, Session>;
  balances: ReadonlyMap<string, StudentBalance>;
};

const TutorDataContext = createContext<TutorData | null>(null);

export { TutorDataContext };

/**
 * The single hydrated source of truth for the signed-in tutor.
 *
 * Six listeners cover six small collections (≈15 students, ≤ 1k docs/year), so
 * every screen and the counter engine can work from memory — including offline,
 * where Firestore replays its IndexedDB cache.
 */
export function TutorDataProvider({ children }: { children: ReactNode }) {
  const { user, configured } = useAuth();
  const uid = user?.uid ?? null;

  const [settings, setSettings] = useState<TutorSettings>(DEFAULT_SETTINGS);
  const [groups, setGroups] = useState<Group[]>([]);
  const [students, setStudents] = useState<Student[]>([]);
  const [sessions, setSessions] = useState<Session[]>([]);
  const [attendance, setAttendance] = useState<Attendance[]>([]);
  const [payments, setPayments] = useState<Payment[]>([]);

  const [loaded, setLoaded] = useState<Record<Source, boolean>>(emptyFlags);
  const [cache, setCache] = useState<Record<Source, boolean>>(emptyFlags);
  const [settingsMissing, setSettingsMissing] = useState(false);
  const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [online, setOnline] = useState(true);

  const bootstrappedFor = useRef<string | null>(null);

  useEffect(() => {
    const update = () => setOnline(window.navigator.onLine);
    update();
    window.addEventListener("online", update);
    window.addEventListener("offline", update);
    return () => {
      window.removeEventListener("online", update);
      window.removeEventListener("offline", update);
    };
  }, []);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!uid || !configured) return;

    const { db } = getFirebase();

    const markLoaded = (source: Source, fromCache: boolean) => {
      setLoaded((prev) => (prev[source] ? prev : { ...prev, [source]: true }));
      setCache((prev) =>
        prev[source] === fromCache ? prev : { ...prev, [source]: fromCache },
      );
    };

    const onError = (err: unknown) => {
      setError(err instanceof Error ? err.message : "load-failed");
    };

    const unsubscribers = [
      onSnapshot(
        doc(db, userPath(uid)),
        (snap) => {
          const data = snap.data() as Record<string, unknown> | undefined;
          const hasSettings = Boolean(data?.settings);
          setSettingsMissing(!hasSettings);
          setSettings(
            hasSettings ? mapSettings(data?.settings) : DEFAULT_SETTINGS,
          );
          markLoaded("settings", snap.metadata.fromCache);
        },
        onError,
      ),
      onSnapshot(
        collection(db, groupsPath(uid)),
        (snap) => {
          setGroups(snap.docs.map((item) => mapGroup(item.id, item.data())));
          markLoaded("groups", snap.metadata.fromCache);
        },
        onError,
      ),
      onSnapshot(
        collection(db, studentsPath(uid)),
        (snap) => {
          setStudents(snap.docs.map((item) => mapStudent(item.id, item.data())));
          markLoaded("students", snap.metadata.fromCache);
        },
        onError,
      ),
      onSnapshot(
        collection(db, sessionsPath(uid)),
        (snap) => {
          setSessions(snap.docs.map((item) => mapSession(item.id, item.data())));
          markLoaded("sessions", snap.metadata.fromCache);
        },
        onError,
      ),
      onSnapshot(
        collection(db, attendancePath(uid)),
        (snap) => {
          setAttendance(
            snap.docs.map((item) => mapAttendance(item.id, item.data())),
          );
          markLoaded("attendance", snap.metadata.fromCache);
        },
        onError,
      ),
      onSnapshot(
        collection(db, paymentsPath(uid)),
        (snap) => {
          setPayments(snap.docs.map((item) => mapPayment(item.id, item.data())));
          markLoaded("payments", snap.metadata.fromCache);
        },
        onError,
      ),
    ];

    return () => {
      for (const unsubscribe of unsubscribers) unsubscribe();
    };
  }, [uid, configured]);

  // First-run bootstrap: create settings defaults exactly once per account.
  useEffect(() => {
    if (!uid || !settingsMissing) return;
    if (bootstrappedFor.current === uid) return;
    bootstrappedFor.current = uid;
    void createDefaults(uid).catch(() => {
      bootstrappedFor.current = null;
    });
  }, [uid, settingsMissing]);

  const sessionsById = useMemo(
    () => new Map(sessions.map((session) => [session.id, session])),
    [sessions],
  );

  const balances = useMemo(() => {
    const counters = computeCounters({
      students,
      sessionsById,
      attendance,
      payments,
      settings,
    });
    const result = new Map<string, StudentBalance>();
    for (const student of students) {
      result.set(
        student.id,
        computeStudentBalance(
          student,
          counters.get(student.id) ?? { counted: 0, paid: 0 },
          settings,
        ),
      );
    }
    return result;
  }, [students, sessionsById, attendance, payments, settings]);

  const fromCache = SOURCES.some((source) => cache[source]);

  const status: TutorData["status"] = error
    ? "error"
    : SOURCES.every((source) => loaded[source])
      ? "ready"
      : "loading";

  // Materialise the rolling horizon whenever hydrated data or connectivity
  // changes. planGeneration is idempotent, so a no-op run writes nothing and
  // cannot loop with its own snapshot.
  useEffect(() => {
    if (!uid || status !== "ready") return;

    const run = () => {
      void ensureGenerated(uid, { groups, sessions, today: todayKey() }).catch(
        () => {
          // Retried on the next snapshot / focus / online event.
        },
      );
    };

    run();
    const onVisible = () => {
      if (document.visibilityState === "visible") run();
    };
    window.addEventListener("focus", run);
    window.addEventListener("online", run);
    document.addEventListener("visibilitychange", onVisible);
    return () => {
      window.removeEventListener("focus", run);
      window.removeEventListener("online", run);
      document.removeEventListener("visibilitychange", onVisible);
    };
  }, [uid, status, groups, sessions]);

  // Self-heal cached counters from the source documents. Idempotent: a clean
  // account produces no writes, so this never loops with its own snapshot.
  useEffect(() => {
    if (!uid || status !== "ready") return;
    void reconcileCounters(uid, {
      students,
      sessionsById,
      attendance,
      payments,
      settings,
    }).catch(() => {
      // Retried on the next snapshot / reconnect.
    });
  }, [uid, status, students, sessionsById, attendance, payments, settings]);

  const value = useMemo<TutorData>(
    () => ({
      status,
      error,
      fromCache,
      online,
      uid,
      settings,
      groups,
      students,
      sessions,
      attendance,
      payments,
      sessionsById,
      balances,
    }),
    [
      status,
      error,
      fromCache,
      online,
      uid,
      settings,
      groups,
      students,
      sessions,
      attendance,
      payments,
      sessionsById,
      balances,
    ],
  );

  return (
    <TutorDataContext.Provider value={value}>
      {children}
    </TutorDataContext.Provider>
  );
}
````

### `src/data/provider/hooks.ts`  ·  100 lines

````typescript
"use client";

import { useContext, useMemo } from "react";
import {
  TutorDataContext,
  type TutorData,
} from "@/data/provider/TutorDataProvider";
import { needsReminder } from "@/domain/balanceCalculator";
import { computeGroupStatus } from "@/domain/groupPosition";
import { todayKey } from "@/domain/dateKey";
import type { GroupStatus, StudentBalance } from "@/domain/types";

export function useTutorData(): TutorData {
  const ctx = useContext(TutorDataContext);
  if (!ctx) {
    throw new Error("useTutorData must be used inside <TutorDataProvider>");
  }
  return ctx;
}

export function useStudentBalance(
  studentId: string | undefined,
): StudentBalance | undefined {
  const { balances } = useTutorData();
  return studentId ? balances.get(studentId) : undefined;
}

export function useGroupStatus(groupId: string | undefined): GroupStatus {
  const { students, balances } = useTutorData();
  return useMemo(() => {
    if (!groupId) return { position: 0, outliers: [], activeCount: 0 };
    return computeGroupStatus(
      students.filter((student) => student.groupId === groupId),
      balances,
    );
  }, [groupId, students, balances]);
}

export function useDueStudents() {
  const { students, balances, settings } = useTutorData();
  return useMemo(() => {
    const rows = students
      .filter((student) => student.active)
      .map((student) => ({
        student,
        balance: balances.get(student.id),
      }))
      .filter(
        (row): row is { student: (typeof students)[number]; balance: StudentBalance } =>
          Boolean(row.balance),
      );

    const due = rows
      .filter((row) => row.balance.state === "DUE")
      .sort(
        (a, b) =>
          b.balance.amountDue - a.balance.amountDue ||
          a.student.name.localeCompare(b.student.name, "ar"),
      );

    const almostDue = rows.filter(
      (row) =>
        row.balance.state === "IN_PROGRESS" &&
        row.balance.balance === settings.cycleLength - 1,
    );

    return {
      due,
      almostDue,
      total: due.reduce((sum, row) => sum + row.balance.amountDue, 0),
    };
  }, [students, balances, settings.cycleLength]);
}

export function useTodaySessions() {
  const { sessions } = useTutorData();
  return useMemo(() => {
    const key = todayKey();
    return sessions
      .filter((session) => session.dateKey === key)
      .sort((a, b) => a.time.localeCompare(b.time));
  }, [sessions]);
}

/** Everything the Today screen needs, computed once from hydrated state. */
export function useToday() {
  const { settings, balances } = useTutorData();
  const sessions = useTodaySessions();
  const { due, total } = useDueStudents();

  const reminder = useMemo(
    () =>
      [...balances.entries()]
        .filter(([, balance]) => needsReminder(balance, settings.cycleLength))
        .map(([studentId, balance]) => ({ studentId, balance })),
    [balances, settings.cycleLength],
  );

  return { sessions, reminder, dueCount: due.length, dueTotal: total };
}
````

### `src/data/services/attendanceService.ts`  ·  180 lines

````typescript
import { doc, serverTimestamp, writeBatch, type Firestore } from "firebase/firestore";
import { getFirebase } from "@/data/firebase/client";
import {
  attendanceDocPath,
  sessionDocPath,
  studentDocPath,
} from "@/data/paths";
import { computeStudentBalance } from "@/domain/balanceCalculator";
import { computeCounters } from "@/domain/counters";
import { attendanceId } from "@/domain/sessionId";
import type {
  Attendance,
  AttendanceStatus,
  SessionStatus,
} from "@/domain/types";
import type { CounterSource } from "./counterService";

/** What the counters would be once a lesson's attendance is written. */
function withLesson(
  source: CounterSource,
  sessionId: string,
  rows: readonly Attendance[],
  status: SessionStatus,
): CounterSource {
  const sessionsById = new Map(source.sessionsById);
  const session = sessionsById.get(sessionId);
  if (session) sessionsById.set(sessionId, { ...session, status });

  return {
    ...source,
    sessionsById,
    attendance: [
      ...source.attendance.filter((row) => row.sessionId !== sessionId),
      ...rows,
    ],
  };
}

function persistCounters(
  batch: ReturnType<typeof writeBatch>,
  db: Firestore,
  uid: string,
  students: CounterSource["students"],
  counters: ReturnType<typeof computeCounters>,
): void {
  for (const student of students) {
    const next = counters.get(student.id);
    if (!next) continue;
    batch.update(doc(db, studentDocPath(uid, student.id)), {
      sessionsCounted: next.counted,
      sessionsPaid: next.paid,
      updatedAt: serverTimestamp(),
    });
  }
}

export interface SaveLessonInput {
  sessionId: string;
  absentIds: readonly string[];
  note?: string;
  source: CounterSource;
}

export interface LessonResult {
  countedStudents: number;
  newlyDue: string[];
}

/**
 * The heart of the app: attendance docs, session completion and cached
 * counters in ONE offline-safe batch. No transactions (they need network).
 */
export async function saveLesson(
  uid: string,
  input: SaveLessonInput,
): Promise<LessonResult> {
  const session = input.source.sessionsById.get(input.sessionId);
  if (!session) throw new Error("SESSION_NOT_FOUND");

  const absent = new Set(input.absentIds);
  const roster = input.source.students.filter(
    (student) =>
      student.active &&
      student.groupId === session.groupId &&
      student.startedAtKey <= session.dateKey,
  );

  const rows: Attendance[] = roster.map((student) => ({
    id: attendanceId(session.id, student.id),
    sessionId: session.id,
    studentId: student.id,
    groupId: session.groupId,
    dateKey: session.dateKey,
    status: absent.has(student.id) ? "absent" : "present",
  }));

  const before = computeCounters(input.source);
  const after = computeCounters(
    withLesson(input.source, session.id, rows, "completed"),
  );

  const newlyDue = roster
    .filter((student) => {
      const prior = computeStudentBalance(
        student,
        before.get(student.id) ?? { counted: 0, paid: 0 },
        input.source.settings,
      );
      const next = computeStudentBalance(
        student,
        after.get(student.id) ?? { counted: 0, paid: 0 },
        input.source.settings,
      );
      return prior.state !== "DUE" && next.state === "DUE";
    })
    .map((student) => student.id);

  const { db } = getFirebase();
  const batch = writeBatch(db);

  for (const row of rows) {
    batch.set(doc(db, attendanceDocPath(uid, row.id)), {
      ...row,
      updatedAt: serverTimestamp(),
    });
  }
  batch.update(doc(db, sessionDocPath(uid, session.id)), {
    status: "completed",
    ...(input.note?.trim() ? { note: input.note.trim() } : {}),
    updatedAt: serverTimestamp(),
  });
  persistCounters(batch, db, uid, input.source.students, after);

  await batch.commit();
  if (typeof window !== "undefined") {
    window.dispatchEvent(new Event("tm:lesson-saved"));
  }
  return { countedStudents: roster.length, newlyDue };
}

export interface SetStatusInput {
  attendanceId: string;
  sessionId: string;
  studentId: string;
  groupId: string;
  dateKey: string;
  status: AttendanceStatus;
  source: CounterSource;
}

/** Edit one row of an already completed lesson, then recompute the counters. */
export async function setAttendanceStatus(
  uid: string,
  input: SetStatusInput,
): Promise<void> {
  const { db } = getFirebase();
  const updated: Attendance = {
    id: input.attendanceId,
    sessionId: input.sessionId,
    studentId: input.studentId,
    groupId: input.groupId,
    dateKey: input.dateKey,
    status: input.status,
  };
  const rows = [
    ...input.source.attendance.filter((row) => row.id !== input.attendanceId),
    updated,
  ];
  const counters = computeCounters(
    withLesson(input.source, input.sessionId, rows, "completed"),
  );

  const batch = writeBatch(db);
  batch.set(doc(db, attendanceDocPath(uid, input.attendanceId)), {
    ...updated,
    updatedAt: serverTimestamp(),
  });
  persistCounters(batch, db, uid, input.source.students, counters);
  await batch.commit();
}
````

````typescript
/** Firestore rejects `undefined` field values in `updateDoc` patches. */
export function definedFields<T extends Record<string, unknown>>(
  patch: T,
): Partial<T> {
  return Object.fromEntries(
    Object.entries(patch).filter(([, value]) => value !== undefined),
  ) as Partial<T>;
}
````

### `src/data/services/counterService.ts`  ·  89 lines

````typescript
import { doc, serverTimestamp, writeBatch } from "firebase/firestore";
import { getFirebase } from "@/data/firebase/client";
import { studentDocPath } from "@/data/paths";
import { computeCounters } from "@/domain/counters";
import type {
  Attendance,
  Counters,
  Payment,
  Session,
  Student,
  TutorSettings,
} from "@/domain/types";

export interface CounterSource {
  students: readonly Student[];
  sessionsById: ReadonlyMap<string, Session>;
  attendance: readonly Attendance[];
  payments: readonly Payment[];
  settings: Pick<TutorSettings, "absenceCounts" | "cycleLength">;
}

/**
 * The ONLY writer of `students.sessionsCounted` / `students.sessionsPaid`.
 *
 * Uses a batched write (offline-safe, queued in IndexedDB). Never transactions.
 */
export async function persistCounters(
  uid: string,
  students: readonly Student[],
  counters: ReadonlyMap<string, Counters>,
): Promise<number> {
  const { db } = getFirebase();
  const batch = writeBatch(db);
  let changed = 0;

  for (const student of students) {
    const next = counters.get(student.id);
    if (!next) continue;
    if (
      next.counted === student.sessionsCounted &&
      next.paid === student.sessionsPaid
    ) {
      continue;
    }
    batch.update(doc(db, studentDocPath(uid, student.id)), {
      sessionsCounted: next.counted,
      sessionsPaid: next.paid,
      updatedAt: serverTimestamp(),
    });
    changed += 1;
  }

  if (changed > 0) {
    await batch.commit();
  }
  return changed;
}

/** Recompute every student from source documents and persist the deltas. */
export async function reconcileCounters(
  uid: string,
  source: CounterSource,
): Promise<number> {
  const counters = computeCounters({
    students: source.students,
    sessionsById: source.sessionsById,
    attendance: source.attendance,
    payments: source.payments,
    settings: source.settings,
  });
  return persistCounters(uid, source.students, counters);
}

/** Recompute a single student (e.g. after a payment). */
export async function reconcileStudent(
  uid: string,
  studentId: string,
  source: CounterSource,
): Promise<number> {
  const counters = computeCounters({
    students: source.students,
    sessionsById: source.sessionsById,
    attendance: source.attendance,
    payments: source.payments,
    settings: source.settings,
    studentIds: [studentId],
  });
  return persistCounters(uid, source.students, counters);
}
````

### `src/data/services/devSeedService.ts`  ·  156 lines

````typescript
import {
  collection,
  doc,
  serverTimestamp,
  setDoc,
  updateDoc,
  writeBatch,
} from "firebase/firestore";
import { getFirebase } from "@/data/firebase/client";
import {
  attendanceDocPath,
  attendancePath,
  groupsPath,
  paymentsPath,
  sessionDocPath,
  sessionsPath,
  studentDocPath,
  studentsPath,
} from "@/data/paths";
import { todayKey, weekdayOfKey } from "@/domain/dateKey";
import { attendanceId, regularSessionId } from "@/domain/sessionId";
import type { AttendanceStatus } from "@/domain/types";

/**
 * Dev-only fixtures for the offline spike (Stage 0). Never imported by app
 * screens outside `/dev`, which is excluded from production builds.
 */
export async function seedDemoData(
  uid: string,
): Promise<{ groupId: string; sessionId: string }> {
  const { db } = getFirebase();
  const batch = writeBatch(db);

  const groupRef = doc(collection(db, groupsPath(uid)));
  const firstStudentRef = doc(collection(db, studentsPath(uid)));
  const secondStudentRef = doc(collection(db, studentsPath(uid)));

  const dateKey = todayKey();
  const sessionId = regularSessionId(groupRef.id, dateKey);
  const sessionRef = doc(db, sessionDocPath(uid, sessionId));

  batch.set(groupRef, {
    name: "مجموعة تجريبية",
    slots: [{ weekday: weekdayOfKey(dateKey), time: "17:00", durationMin: 60 }],
    active: true,
    createdAt: serverTimestamp(),
    updatedAt: serverTimestamp(),
  });

  const studentSeeds = [
    { ref: firstStudentRef, name: "أحمد (تجريبي)" },
    { ref: secondStudentRef, name: "محمود (تجريبي)" },
  ];

  for (const { ref, name } of studentSeeds) {
    batch.set(ref, {
      name,
      groupId: groupRef.id,
      pricePerSession: 50,
      startedAtKey: dateKey,
      active: true,
      initialCounted: 0,
      initialPaid: 0,
      // The seeded lesson is completed and both are present.
      sessionsCounted: 1,
      sessionsPaid: 0,
      createdAt: serverTimestamp(),
      updatedAt: serverTimestamp(),
    });

    const id = attendanceId(sessionId, ref.id);
    batch.set(doc(db, attendanceDocPath(uid, id)), {
      sessionId,
      studentId: ref.id,
      groupId: groupRef.id,
      dateKey,
      status: "present",
      updatedAt: serverTimestamp(),
    });
  }

  batch.set(sessionRef, {
    groupId: groupRef.id,
    groupName: "مجموعة تجريبية",
    dateKey,
    time: "17:00",
    type: "regular",
    status: "completed",
    createdAt: serverTimestamp(),
    updatedAt: serverTimestamp(),
  });

  await batch.commit();
  return { groupId: groupRef.id, sessionId };
}

/** Deliberately break a cached counter so "Recompute counters" can be tested. */
export async function corruptCounters(
  uid: string,
  studentId: string,
): Promise<void> {
  const { db } = getFirebase();
  await updateDoc(doc(db, studentDocPath(uid, studentId)), {
    sessionsCounted: 999,
    sessionsPaid: 999,
    updatedAt: serverTimestamp(),
  });
}

export async function setAttendanceStatus(input: {
  uid: string;
  attendanceId: string;
  sessionId: string;
  studentId: string;
  groupId: string;
  dateKey: string;
  status: AttendanceStatus;
}): Promise<void> {
  const { db } = getFirebase();
  await setDoc(
    doc(db, attendanceDocPath(input.uid, input.attendanceId)),
    {
      sessionId: input.sessionId,
      studentId: input.studentId,
      groupId: input.groupId,
      dateKey: input.dateKey,
      status: input.status,
      updatedAt: serverTimestamp(),
    },
    { merge: true },
  );
}

export interface ClearDemoInput {
  groupIds: string[];
  studentIds: string[];
  sessionIds: string[];
  attendanceIds: string[];
  paymentIds: string[];
}

export async function clearDemoData(
  uid: string,
  input: ClearDemoInput,
): Promise<void> {
  const { db } = getFirebase();
  const batch = writeBatch(db);

  for (const id of input.groupIds) batch.delete(doc(db, `${groupsPath(uid)}/${id}`));
  for (const id of input.studentIds) batch.delete(doc(db, studentDocPath(uid, id)));
  for (const id of input.sessionIds) batch.delete(doc(db, `${sessionsPath(uid)}/${id}`));
  for (const id of input.attendanceIds) batch.delete(doc(db, `${attendancePath(uid)}/${id}`));
  for (const id of input.paymentIds) batch.delete(doc(db, `${paymentsPath(uid)}/${id}`));

  await batch.commit();
}
````

### `src/data/services/enrollmentService.ts`  ·  104 lines

````typescript
import { todayKey } from "@/domain/dateKey";
import type { Student } from "@/domain/types";
import { reconcileStudent, type CounterSource } from "./counterService";
import {
  createStudent,
  moveStudentToGroup,
  setStudentActive,
  updateStudent,
  type CreateStudentInput,
} from "./studentService";

/**
 * Student writes plus the counter reconciliation they require.
 *
 * `counterService` remains the only writer of the cached counters; these
 * helpers pass it the hydrated source (plus the pending change) so the cache
 * is never trusted as an input.
 */

type StudentPatch = Partial<CreateStudentInput> & { active?: boolean };

function withPendingStudent(
  source: CounterSource,
  studentId: string,
  patch: StudentPatch,
): CounterSource {
  return {
    ...source,
    students: source.students.map((student) =>
      student.id === studentId ? { ...student, ...patch } : student,
    ),
  };
}

export async function enrollStudent(
  uid: string,
  input: CreateStudentInput,
  source: CounterSource,
): Promise<string> {
  const id = await createStudent(uid, input);
  const created: Student = {
    id,
    name: input.name.trim(),
    groupId: input.groupId,
    pricePerSession: input.pricePerSession,
    startedAtKey: input.startedAtKey ?? todayKey(),
    active: true,
    initialCounted: input.initialCounted ?? 0,
    initialPaid: input.initialPaid ?? 0,
    sessionsCounted: 0,
    sessionsPaid: 0,
  };
  await reconcileStudent(uid, id, {
    ...source,
    students: [...source.students, created],
  });
  return id;
}

export async function updateStudentAndReconcile(
  uid: string,
  studentId: string,
  patch: StudentPatch,
  source: CounterSource,
): Promise<void> {
  await updateStudent(uid, studentId, patch);
  await reconcileStudent(
    uid,
    studentId,
    withPendingStudent(source, studentId, patch),
  );
}

/**
 * Moving does NOT touch attendance documents: they keep the group they were
 * recorded under, so historical group statistics stay truthful.
 */
export async function moveStudentAndReconcile(
  uid: string,
  studentId: string,
  groupId: string,
  source: CounterSource,
): Promise<void> {
  await moveStudentToGroup(uid, studentId, groupId);
  await reconcileStudent(
    uid,
    studentId,
    withPendingStudent(source, studentId, { groupId }),
  );
}

export async function setStudentActiveAndReconcile(
  uid: string,
  studentId: string,
  active: boolean,
  source: CounterSource,
): Promise<void> {
  await setStudentActive(uid, studentId, active);
  await reconcileStudent(
    uid,
    studentId,
    withPendingStudent(source, studentId, { active }),
  );
}
````

### `src/data/services/groupService.ts`  ·  55 lines

````typescript
import {
  addDoc,
  collection,
  doc,
  serverTimestamp,
  updateDoc,
} from "firebase/firestore";
import { definedFields } from "./cleanPatch";
import { getFirebase } from "@/data/firebase/client";
import { groupDocPath, groupsPath } from "@/data/paths";
import type { Slot } from "@/domain/types";

export interface CreateGroupInput {
  name: string;
  subject?: string;
  slots: Slot[];
}

export async function createGroup(
  uid: string,
  input: CreateGroupInput,
): Promise<string> {
  const { db } = getFirebase();
  const ref = await addDoc(collection(db, groupsPath(uid)), {
    name: input.name.trim(),
    ...(input.subject?.trim() ? { subject: input.subject.trim() } : {}),
    slots: input.slots,
    active: true,
    createdAt: serverTimestamp(),
    updatedAt: serverTimestamp(),
  });
  return ref.id;
}

export async function updateGroup(
  uid: string,
  groupId: string,
  patch: Partial<Pick<CreateGroupInput, "name" | "subject" | "slots">> & {
    active?: boolean;
  },
): Promise<void> {
  const { db } = getFirebase();
  await updateDoc(doc(db, groupDocPath(uid, groupId)), {
    ...definedFields(patch as Record<string, unknown>),
    updatedAt: serverTimestamp(),
  });
}

export async function setGroupActive(
  uid: string,
  groupId: string,
  active: boolean,
): Promise<void> {
  await updateGroup(uid, groupId, { active });
}
````

### `src/data/services/paymentService.ts`  ·  125 lines

````typescript
import {
  collection,
  doc,
  serverTimestamp,
  writeBatch,
  type Firestore,
} from "firebase/firestore";
import { getFirebase } from "@/data/firebase/client";
import {
  paymentDocPath,
  paymentsPath,
  studentDocPath,
} from "@/data/paths";
import { computeCounters } from "@/domain/counters";
import type { Payment } from "@/domain/types";
import type { CounterSource } from "./counterService";
import { definedFields } from "./cleanPatch";

export interface RecordPaymentInput {
  studentId: string;
  dateKey: string;
  amount: number;
  sessionsCovered: number;
  note?: string;
}

function validate(input: RecordPaymentInput): void {
  if (!Number.isInteger(input.amount) || input.amount <= 0) {
    throw new RangeError("amount must be a positive whole number");
  }
  if (!Number.isInteger(input.sessionsCovered) || input.sessionsCovered < 1) {
    throw new RangeError("sessionsCovered must be a whole number >= 1");
  }
}

function prospectivePayments(
  source: CounterSource,
  change: { kind: "add" | "update" | "remove"; id: string; payment?: Payment },
): Payment[] {
  const existing = source.payments.filter((payment) => payment.id !== change.id);
  if (change.kind === "remove" || !change.payment) return existing;
  return [...existing, change.payment];
}

function persist(
  batch: ReturnType<typeof writeBatch>,
  db: Firestore,
  uid: string,
  source: CounterSource,
  payments: readonly Payment[],
): void {
  const counters = computeCounters({ ...source, payments });
  for (const student of source.students) {
    const next = counters.get(student.id);
    if (!next) continue;
    batch.update(doc(db, studentDocPath(uid, student.id)), {
      sessionsCounted: next.counted,
      sessionsPaid: next.paid,
      updatedAt: serverTimestamp(),
    });
  }
}

/** Payment doc + counter persist in one offline-safe batch. */
export async function recordPayment(
  uid: string,
  input: RecordPaymentInput,
  source: CounterSource,
): Promise<string> {
  validate(input);
  const { db } = getFirebase();
  const ref = doc(collection(db, paymentsPath(uid)));
  const payment: Payment = {
    id: ref.id,
    studentId: input.studentId,
    dateKey: input.dateKey,
    amount: input.amount,
    sessionsCovered: input.sessionsCovered,
    ...(input.note?.trim() ? { note: input.note.trim() } : {}),
  };

  const batch = writeBatch(db);
  batch.set(ref, { ...payment, createdAt: serverTimestamp(), updatedAt: serverTimestamp() });
  persist(batch, db, uid, source, prospectivePayments(source, { kind: "add", id: ref.id, payment }));
  await batch.commit();
  return ref.id;
}

export async function updatePayment(
  uid: string,
  paymentId: string,
  patch: Partial<RecordPaymentInput>,
  source: CounterSource,
): Promise<void> {
  const current = source.payments.find((payment) => payment.id === paymentId);
  if (!current) throw new Error("PAYMENT_NOT_FOUND");
  const next: Payment = { ...current, ...patch };
  validate({
    studentId: next.studentId,
    dateKey: next.dateKey,
    amount: next.amount,
    sessionsCovered: next.sessionsCovered,
  });

  const { db } = getFirebase();
  const batch = writeBatch(db);
  batch.update(doc(db, paymentDocPath(uid, paymentId)), {
    ...definedFields(patch as Record<string, unknown>),
    updatedAt: serverTimestamp(),
  });
  persist(batch, db, uid, source, prospectivePayments(source, { kind: "update", id: paymentId, payment: next }));
  await batch.commit();
}

export async function removePayment(
  uid: string,
  paymentId: string,
  source: CounterSource,
): Promise<void> {
  const { db } = getFirebase();
  const batch = writeBatch(db);
  batch.delete(doc(db, paymentDocPath(uid, paymentId)));
  persist(batch, db, uid, source, prospectivePayments(source, { kind: "remove", id: paymentId }));
  await batch.commit();
}
````

### `src/data/services/sessionGenerator.ts`  ·  55 lines

````typescript
import { doc, serverTimestamp, writeBatch } from "firebase/firestore";
import { getFirebase } from "@/data/firebase/client";
import { sessionDocPath } from "@/data/paths";
import { todayKey } from "@/domain/dateKey";
import {
  planGeneration,
  type GenerationInput,
} from "@/domain/sessionGenerator";
import type { DateKey } from "@/domain/types";

/** At most one generation batch per tutor at a time (focus + snapshot triggers). */
const running = new Map<string, Promise<number>>();

export interface EnsureInput extends Omit<GenerationInput, "today"> {
  today?: DateKey;
}

export function ensureGenerated(uid: string, state: EnsureInput): Promise<number> {
  const inFlight = running.get(uid);
  if (inFlight) return inFlight;

  const task = run(uid, state).finally(() => running.delete(uid));
  running.set(uid, task);
  return task;
}

async function run(uid: string, state: EnsureInput): Promise<number> {
  const changes = planGeneration({
    groups: state.groups,
    sessions: state.sessions,
    today: state.today ?? todayKey(),
  });
  if (changes.length === 0) return 0;

  const { db } = getFirebase();
  const batch = writeBatch(db);

  for (const change of changes) {
    if (change.kind === "create") {
      batch.set(doc(db, sessionDocPath(uid, change.session.id)), {
        ...change.session,
        createdAt: serverTimestamp(),
        updatedAt: serverTimestamp(),
      });
    } else {
      batch.update(doc(db, sessionDocPath(uid, change.id)), {
        time: change.time,
        updatedAt: serverTimestamp(),
      });
    }
  }

  await batch.commit();
  return changes.length;
}
````

````typescript
import {
  deleteDoc,
  doc,
  serverTimestamp,
  setDoc,
  updateDoc,
  writeBatch,
} from "firebase/firestore";
import { getFirebase } from "@/data/firebase/client";
import { sessionDocPath, studentDocPath } from "@/data/paths";
import { computeCounters } from "@/domain/counters";
import {
  extraSessionId,
  nextExtraSessionNumber,
} from "@/domain/sessionId";
import type { DateKey } from "@/domain/types";
import type { CounterSource } from "./counterService";

/**
 * Cancelling a lesson must drop the counters it contributed. With a hydrated
 * source it also persists that change in the same batch as the status.
 */
export async function cancelSession(
  uid: string,
  sessionId: string,
  note?: string,
  source?: CounterSource,
): Promise<void> {
  const { db } = getFirebase();
  const batch = writeBatch(db);

  batch.update(doc(db, sessionDocPath(uid, sessionId)), {
    status: "cancelled",
    ...(note?.trim() ? { note: note.trim() } : {}),
    updatedAt: serverTimestamp(),
  });

  if (source) {
    const sessionsById = new Map(source.sessionsById);
    const session = sessionsById.get(sessionId);
    if (session) sessionsById.set(sessionId, { ...session, status: "cancelled" });
    const counters = computeCounters({ ...source, sessionsById });
    for (const student of source.students) {
      const next = counters.get(student.id);
      if (!next) continue;
      batch.update(doc(db, studentDocPath(uid, student.id)), {
        sessionsCounted: next.counted,
        sessionsPaid: next.paid,
        updatedAt: serverTimestamp(),
      });
    }
  }

  await batch.commit();
}

export async function updateSessionNote(
  uid: string,
  sessionId: string,
  note: string,
): Promise<void> {
  const { db } = getFirebase();
  await updateDoc(doc(db, sessionDocPath(uid, sessionId)), {
    note: note.trim() || null,
    updatedAt: serverTimestamp(),
  });
}

export async function updateSessionTime(
  uid: string,
  sessionId: string,
  time: string,
): Promise<void> {
  const { db } = getFirebase();
  await updateDoc(doc(db, sessionDocPath(uid, sessionId)), {
    time,
    updatedAt: serverTimestamp(),
  });
}

/** Deterministic id + smallest-free number => re-adds never duplicate. */
export async function addExtraSession(
  uid: string,
  input: {
    groupId: string;
    groupName: string;
    dateKey: DateKey;
    time: string;
    existingIds: Iterable<string>;
  },
): Promise<string> {
  const { db } = getFirebase();
  const id = extraSessionId(
    input.groupId,
    input.dateKey,
    nextExtraSessionNumber(input.groupId, input.dateKey, input.existingIds),
  );

  await setDoc(doc(db, sessionDocPath(uid, id)), {
    id,
    groupId: input.groupId,
    groupName: input.groupName,
    dateKey: input.dateKey,
    time: input.time,
    type: "extra",
    status: "scheduled",
    createdAt: serverTimestamp(),
    updatedAt: serverTimestamp(),
  });
  return id;
}

/** Only extra lessons may be deleted; regular ones are cancelled instead. */
export async function deleteExtraSession(
  uid: string,
  sessionId: string,
): Promise<void> {
  const { db } = getFirebase();
  await deleteDoc(doc(db, sessionDocPath(uid, sessionId)));
}
````

````typescript
import {
  doc,
  getDoc,
  serverTimestamp,
  setDoc,
  updateDoc,
} from "firebase/firestore";
import { getFirebase } from "@/data/firebase/client";
import { userPath } from "@/data/paths";
import { DEFAULT_SETTINGS, type TutorSettings } from "@/domain/types";

/** Creates the settings document only when it does not exist yet. */
export async function createDefaults(uid: string): Promise<boolean> {
  const { db } = getFirebase();
  const ref = doc(db, userPath(uid));
  const snapshot = await getDoc(ref);
  const data = snapshot.data() as { settings?: unknown } | undefined;
  if (data?.settings) return false;

  await setDoc(
    ref,
    {
      settings: DEFAULT_SETTINGS,
      createdAt: serverTimestamp(),
      updatedAt: serverTimestamp(),
    },
    { merge: true },
  );
  return true;
}

export async function saveSettings(
  uid: string,
  settings: TutorSettings,
): Promise<void> {
  const { db } = getFirebase();
  await updateDoc(doc(db, userPath(uid)), {
    settings,
    updatedAt: serverTimestamp(),
  });
}
````

### `src/domain/aiSanitize.ts`  ·  29 lines

````typescript
/** Never send parent phone numbers (or any long digit run) to the model. */
export function redactPhones(value: unknown): string {
  if (value === null || value === undefined) return "";
  return String(value)
    .replace(/(?:\+?\d[\d\s().-]{6,}\d)/g, (match) => {
      const digits = match.replace(/\D/g, "");
      // Dates and Firestore ids are digit runs too — only real phone shapes
      // get redacted: '+' prefix, leading 0, or at least 10 digits.
      if (match.includes("+") || digits.startsWith("0") || digits.length >= 10) {
        return "[number]";
      }
      return match;
    })
    .trim();
}

/** Plain object with every string value redacted; ids are kept. */
export function redactStrings<T>(value: T): T {
  if (Array.isArray(value)) return value.map(redactStrings) as T;
  if (value && typeof value === "object") {
    return Object.fromEntries(
      Object.entries(value).map(([key, item]) => [
        key,
        typeof item === "string" ? redactPhones(item) : redactStrings(item),
      ]),
    ) as T;
  }
  return value;
}
````

### `src/domain/balanceCalculator.ts`  ·  91 lines

````typescript
import {
  assertInteger,
  assertNonNegativeInteger,
  expectedCycleAmount,
} from "./money";
import type { Counters, Student, StudentBalance, TutorSettings } from "./types";

export interface BalanceInput {
  counted: number;
  paid: number;
  pricePerSession: number;
  cycleLength: number;
}

/**
 * Balance rules (source of truth):
 *
 *   balance = counted - paid
 *   balance >= L  -> DUE (dueCycles = floor(balance / L))
 *   0 < balance < L -> IN_PROGRESS
 *   balance === 0 -> SETTLED
 *   balance < 0   -> PAID_AHEAD by abs(balance)
 *
 * `cyclePosition` depends only on counted sessions, never on payments.
 */
export function calculateBalance(input: BalanceInput): StudentBalance {
  const { counted, paid, pricePerSession } = input;

  assertNonNegativeInteger(counted, "counted");
  assertNonNegativeInteger(paid, "paid");
  assertNonNegativeInteger(pricePerSession, "pricePerSession");
  assertInteger(input.cycleLength, "cycleLength");
  if (input.cycleLength < 1) {
    throw new RangeError("cycleLength must be >= 1");
  }

  const cycleLength = input.cycleLength;
  const balance = counted - paid;

  const state =
    balance >= cycleLength
      ? "DUE"
      : balance > 0
        ? "IN_PROGRESS"
        : balance === 0
          ? "SETTLED"
          : "PAID_AHEAD";

  const dueCycles =
    balance >= cycleLength ? Math.floor(balance / cycleLength) : 0;

  return {
    counted,
    paid,
    balance,
    cyclePosition:
      counted === 0 ? 0 : ((counted - 1) % cycleLength) + 1,
    state,
    dueCycles,
    amountDue: dueCycles * expectedCycleAmount(pricePerSession, cycleLength),
    paidAheadBy: balance < 0 ? Math.abs(balance) : 0,
  };
}

/** Calculate a student's live balance from derived counters + settings. */
export function computeStudentBalance(
  student: Pick<Student, "pricePerSession">,
  counters: Counters,
  settings: Pick<TutorSettings, "cycleLength">,
): StudentBalance {
  return calculateBalance({
    counted: counters.counted,
    paid: counters.paid,
    pricePerSession: student.pricePerSession,
    cycleLength: settings.cycleLength,
  });
}

export function isDue(balance: Pick<StudentBalance, "state">): boolean {
  return balance.state === "DUE";
}

/** The reminder card: next counted session is the 4th (or 8th, 12th, …). */
export function needsReminder(
  balance: Pick<StudentBalance, "state" | "counted">,
  cycleLength: number,
): boolean {
  if (balance.counted === 0) return false;
  if (balance.state === "PAID_AHEAD" || balance.state === "DUE") return false;
  return balance.counted % cycleLength === cycleLength - 1;
}
````

### `src/domain/counters.ts`  ·  88 lines

````typescript
import type {
  Attendance,
  Counters,
  Payment,
  Session,
  Student,
  TutorSettings,
} from "./types";

export interface ComputeCountersInput {
  students: readonly Student[];
  sessionsById: ReadonlyMap<string, Session>;
  attendance: readonly Attendance[];
  payments: readonly Payment[];
  settings: Pick<TutorSettings, "absenceCounts" | "cycleLength">;
  /** Optional subset of student ids to compute. */
  studentIds?: Iterable<string>;
}

/**
 * The single derivation of a student's counters.
 *
 * `counted = initialCounted + sum(attendance) where`
 *   - the parent session is `completed` (scheduled/cancelled never count),
 *   - `session.dateKey >= student.startedAtKey`,
 *   - attendance is present, or absent and `settings.absenceCounts`.
 * `paid = initialPaid + sum(payment.sessionsCovered)`.
 *
 * Pure and deterministic: no cached state is trusted.
 */
export function computeCounters(
  input: ComputeCountersInput,
): Map<string, Counters> {
  const { students, sessionsById, attendance, payments, settings } = input;
  const studentsById = new Map(students.map((student) => [student.id, student]));
  const targets = input.studentIds ? new Set(input.studentIds) : null;

  const result = new Map<string, Counters>();
  for (const student of students) {
    if (targets && !targets.has(student.id)) continue;
    result.set(student.id, {
      counted: student.initialCounted,
      paid: student.initialPaid,
    });
  }

  const seen = new Set<string>();
  for (const row of attendance) {
    if (!result.has(row.studentId)) continue;
    const student = studentsById.get(row.studentId);
    if (!student) continue;

    const session = sessionsById.get(row.sessionId);
    if (!session || session.status !== "completed") continue;
    if (session.dateKey < student.startedAtKey) continue;

    // Defensive: deterministic ids make duplicates impossible, but a money
    // counter must never double count malformed data either.
    const dedupeKey = `${row.sessionId}::${row.studentId}`;
    if (seen.has(dedupeKey)) continue;
    seen.add(dedupeKey);

    const counts =
      row.status === "present" ||
      (row.status === "absent" && settings.absenceCounts);
    if (counts) {
      const counters = result.get(row.studentId);
      if (counters) counters.counted += 1;
    }
  }

  for (const payment of payments) {
    const counters = result.get(payment.studentId);
    if (!counters) continue;
    counters.paid += payment.sessionsCovered;
  }

  return result;
}

/** Convenience for a single student (used by detail screens and tests). */
export function countersForStudent(
  studentId: string,
  input: Omit<ComputeCountersInput, "studentIds">,
): Counters {
  const all = computeCounters({ ...input, studentIds: [studentId] });
  return all.get(studentId) ?? { counted: 0, paid: 0 };
}
````

### `src/domain/dateKey.ts`  ·  157 lines

````typescript
import type { DateKey, Weekday } from "./types";

/**
 * Single source of truth for calendar identity.
 *
 * The tutor lives in Cairo, so "today" must be the Cairo day even when the
 * device is in another timezone or the browser clock is near UTC midnight.
 * `yyyy-MM-dd` keys are lexicographically sortable and DST-proof.
 */
export const APP_TIMEZONE = "Africa/Cairo";

export const MS_PER_DAY = 86_400_000;

const DATE_KEY_RE = /^(\d{4})-(\d{2})-(\d{2})$/;

const keyFormatter = new Intl.DateTimeFormat("en-CA", {
  timeZone: APP_TIMEZONE,
  year: "numeric",
  month: "2-digit",
  day: "2-digit",
});

function pad2(value: number): string {
  return value < 10 ? `0${value}` : String(value);
}

/** Cairo calendar day of an instant, e.g. `2026-11-24`. */
export function dateKeyOf(date: Date): DateKey {
  const parts = keyFormatter.formatToParts(date);
  const year = parts.find((p) => p.type === "year")?.value;
  const month = parts.find((p) => p.type === "month")?.value;
  const day = parts.find((p) => p.type === "day")?.value;
  if (!year || !month || !day) {
    throw new Error("Could not format date key");
  }
  return `${year}-${month}-${day}`;
}

export function todayKey(now: Date = new Date()): DateKey {
  return dateKeyOf(now);
}

export function isValidDateKey(value: unknown): value is DateKey {
  if (typeof value !== "string") return false;
  const match = DATE_KEY_RE.exec(value);
  if (!match) return false;
  const [, y, m, d] = match;
  const year = Number(y);
  const month = Number(m);
  const day = Number(d);
  if (month < 1 || month > 12 || day < 1 || day > 31) return false;
  // Round-trip through UTC catches e.g. 2026-02-30.
  const utc = new Date(Date.UTC(year, month - 1, day));
  return (
    utc.getUTCFullYear() === year &&
    utc.getUTCMonth() === month - 1 &&
    utc.getUTCDate() === day
  );
}

export function dateKeyFromParts(
  year: number,
  month: number,
  day: number,
): DateKey {
  const key = `${year}-${pad2(month)}-${pad2(day)}`;
  if (!isValidDateKey(key)) {
    throw new RangeError(`Invalid date: ${year}-${month}-${day}`);
  }
  return key;
}

export function parseDateKey(key: DateKey): {
  year: number;
  month: number;
  day: number;
} {
  const match = DATE_KEY_RE.exec(key);
  if (!match || !isValidDateKey(key)) {
    throw new RangeError(`Invalid date key: ${key}`);
  }
  return { year: Number(match[1]), month: Number(match[2]), day: Number(match[3]) };
}

function utcMsOfKey(key: DateKey): number {
  const { year, month, day } = parseDateKey(key);
  return Date.UTC(year, month - 1, day);
}

function keyFromUtcMs(ms: number): DateKey {
  const date = new Date(ms);
  return `${date.getUTCFullYear()}-${pad2(date.getUTCMonth() + 1)}-${pad2(
    date.getUTCDate(),
  )}`;
}

/** Shift a key by whole days without ever touching local time or DST. */
export function addDaysKey(key: DateKey, days: number): DateKey {
  if (!Number.isInteger(days)) throw new RangeError("days must be an integer");
  return keyFromUtcMs(utcMsOfKey(key) + days * MS_PER_DAY);
}

/** 0 = Sunday … 6 = Saturday, matching `Date.getDay()`. */
export function weekdayOfKey(key: DateKey): Weekday {
  return new Date(utcMsOfKey(key)).getUTCDay() as Weekday;
}

export function nextDateKeys(fromKey: DateKey, count: number): DateKey[] {
  if (!Number.isInteger(count) || count < 0) {
    throw new RangeError("count must be a non-negative integer");
  }
  return Array.from({ length: count }, (_, index) => addDaysKey(fromKey, index));
}

export function compareDateKeys(a: DateKey, b: DateKey): number {
  return a < b ? -1 : a > b ? 1 : 0;
}

export function isKeyInRange(
  key: DateKey,
  fromKey: DateKey,
  toKey: DateKey,
): boolean {
  return key >= fromKey && key <= toKey;
}

export function daysBetweenKeys(fromKey: DateKey, toKey: DateKey): number {
  return Math.round((utcMsOfKey(toKey) - utcMsOfKey(fromKey)) / MS_PER_DAY);
}

/** Human date in Arabic (Latin digits per the product decision). */
export function formatArabicDate(
  key: DateKey,
  options: Intl.DateTimeFormatOptions = {
    weekday: "long",
    day: "numeric",
    month: "long",
  },
): string {
  const { year, month, day } = parseDateKey(key);
  // Noon UTC keeps the Cairo rendering on the intended day for any offset.
  const instant = new Date(Date.UTC(year, month - 1, day, 12, 0, 0));
  return new Intl.DateTimeFormat("ar-EG-u-nu-latn", {
    timeZone: APP_TIMEZONE,
    ...options,
  }).format(instant);
}

export function formatTime(time: string, locale: "ar" | "en" = "ar"): string {
  const match = /^(\d{2}):(\d{2})$/.exec(time);
  if (!match) return time;
  const hour = Number(match[1]);
  const minute = Number(match[2]);
  const suffix = hour < 12 ? (locale === "ar" ? "ص" : "AM") : locale === "ar" ? "م" : "PM";
  const display = hour % 12 === 0 ? 12 : hour % 12;
  return `${display}:${pad2(minute)} ${suffix}`;
}
````

### `src/domain/groupPosition.ts`  ·  52 lines

````typescript
import type { GroupOutlier, GroupStatus, Student, StudentBalance } from "./types";

/**
 * Group position = the most common `cyclePosition` among the active students of
 * the group who already have counted sessions. A tie resolves to the higher
 * position. Students who differ are flagged as outliers.
 */
export function computeGroupStatus(
  students: readonly Student[],
  balances: ReadonlyMap<string, Pick<StudentBalance, "cyclePosition" | "counted">>,
): GroupStatus {
  const eligible: Array<{ student: Student; position: number }> = [];

  for (const student of students) {
    if (!student.active) continue;
    const balance = balances.get(student.id);
    if (!balance || balance.counted <= 0) continue;
    eligible.push({ student, position: balance.cyclePosition });
  }

  if (eligible.length === 0) {
    return { position: 0, outliers: [], activeCount: 0 };
  }

  const frequencies = new Map<number, number>();
  for (const { position } of eligible) {
    frequencies.set(position, (frequencies.get(position) ?? 0) + 1);
  }

  let position = 0;
  let best = -1;
  for (const [candidate, count] of frequencies) {
    if (count > best || (count === best && candidate > position)) {
      position = candidate;
      best = count;
    }
  }

  const outliers: GroupOutlier[] = eligible
    .filter((entry) => entry.position !== position)
    .map(({ student, position: studentPosition }) => ({
      studentId: student.id,
      name: student.name,
      position: studentPosition,
    }));

  return {
    position,
    outliers,
    activeCount: students.filter((student) => student.active).length,
  };
}
````

````typescript
/**
 * Money is always whole currency units (EGP). No floats anywhere in the app.
 */

export function assertInteger(value: number, name = "value"): number {
  if (!Number.isInteger(value)) {
    throw new RangeError(`${name} must be an integer, received ${value}`);
  }
  return value;
}

export function assertNonNegativeInteger(value: number, name = "value"): number {
  assertInteger(value, name);
  if (value < 0) {
    throw new RangeError(`${name} must be >= 0, received ${value}`);
  }
  return value;
}

export function expectedCycleAmount(
  pricePerSession: number,
  cycleLength: number,
): number {
  assertNonNegativeInteger(pricePerSession, "pricePerSession");
  assertInteger(cycleLength, "cycleLength");
  if (cycleLength < 1) {
    throw new RangeError("cycleLength must be >= 1");
  }
  return pricePerSession * cycleLength;
}

export function formatMoney(amount: number, label: string): string {
  assertInteger(amount, "amount");
  return `${amount.toLocaleString("en-US")} ${label}`;
}

export function formatEGP(amount: number): string {
  return formatMoney(amount, "ج.م");
}

/** Renders digits with Latin numerals regardless of the UI language, per D10. */
export function formatCount(value: number): string {
  assertInteger(value, "value");
  return value.toLocaleString("en-US");
}
````

````typescript
import { nextDateKeys, weekdayOfKey } from "./dateKey";
import { regularSessionId } from "./sessionId";
import type { DateKey, Group, Session } from "./types";

/**
 * Number of Cairo days materialised ahead of the tutor, including today.
 * The schedule view uses the same horizon.
 */
export const GENERATION_HORIZON_DAYS = 14;

export interface GenerationInput {
  groups: readonly Group[];
  sessions: readonly Session[];
  today: DateKey;
}

export type GenerationChange =
  | { kind: "create"; session: Session }
  | { kind: "updateTime"; id: string; time: string };

/**
 * Pure planner. Existing documents are never resurrected or duplicated: the
 * deterministic id is the uniqueness constraint. Only a *future, scheduled*
 * regular lesson follows a slot time edit.
 *
 * Extras (`_2`, `_3`, …) are deliberately outside the generator's scope.
 */
export function planGeneration(input: GenerationInput): GenerationChange[] {
  const { groups, sessions, today } = input;
  const byId = new Map(sessions.map((session) => [session.id, session]));
  const dates = nextDateKeys(today, GENERATION_HORIZON_DAYS);
  const changes: GenerationChange[] = [];
  const seen = new Set<string>();

  for (const group of groups) {
    if (!group.active) continue;

    for (const slot of group.slots) {
      for (const dateKey of dates) {
        if (weekdayOfKey(dateKey) !== slot.weekday) continue;

        const id = regularSessionId(group.id, dateKey);
        if (seen.has(id)) continue;
        seen.add(id);

        const existing = byId.get(id);
        if (!existing) {
          changes.push({
            kind: "create",
            session: {
              id,
              groupId: group.id,
              groupName: group.name,
              dateKey,
              time: slot.time,
              type: "regular",
              status: "scheduled",
            },
          });
          continue;
        }

        // Cancelled and completed lessons are historical truth: never rewrite
        // them, and never bring a cancelled one back.
        if (
          existing.status === "scheduled" &&
          existing.dateKey > today &&
          existing.time !== slot.time
        ) {
          changes.push({ kind: "updateTime", id, time: slot.time });
        }
      }
    }
  }

  return changes;
}
````

### `src/domain/sessionId.ts`  ·  91 lines

````typescript
import { isValidDateKey, parseDateKey } from "./dateKey";
import type { DateKey } from "./types";

/**
 * Deterministic document ids.
 *
 * Doubling as a natural uniqueness constraint means offline retries and
 * generator re-runs can never create duplicates.
 */

const SESSION_ID_RE = /^(?<groupId>.+)_(?<day>\d{8})(?:_(?<extra>\d+))?$/;

export function compactDateKey(dateKey: DateKey): string {
  if (!isValidDateKey(dateKey)) {
    throw new RangeError(`Invalid date key: ${dateKey}`);
  }
  return dateKey.replace(/-/g, "");
}

export function regularSessionId(groupId: string, dateKey: DateKey): string {
  if (groupId.length === 0) {
    throw new RangeError("groupId is required");
  }
  return `${groupId}_${compactDateKey(dateKey)}`;
}

/** Extras are numbered from 2: `{groupId}_{yyyyMMdd}_2`, `_3`, … */
export function extraSessionId(
  groupId: string,
  dateKey: DateKey,
  extraNumber: number,
): string {
  if (!Number.isInteger(extraNumber) || extraNumber < 2) {
    throw new RangeError("extraNumber must be an integer >= 2");
  }
  return `${regularSessionId(groupId, dateKey)}_${extraNumber}`;
}

export interface ParsedSessionId {
  groupId: string;
  dateKey: DateKey;
  /** Undefined for regular sessions. */
  extraNumber?: number;
}

export function parseSessionId(id: string): ParsedSessionId | null {
  const match = SESSION_ID_RE.exec(id);
  if (!match?.groups) return null;
  const day = match.groups.day;
  const dateKey = `${day.slice(0, 4)}-${day.slice(4, 6)}-${day.slice(6, 8)}`;
  if (!isValidDateKey(dateKey)) return null;
  const extraRaw = match.groups.extra;
  const extraNumber = extraRaw ? Number(extraRaw) : undefined;
  if (extraNumber !== undefined && (!Number.isInteger(extraNumber) || extraNumber < 2)) {
    return null;
  }
  return { groupId: match.groups.groupId, dateKey, extraNumber };
}

/** `{sessionId}_{studentId}` — one attendance row per student per lesson. */
export function attendanceId(sessionId: string, studentId: string): string {
  return `${sessionId}_${studentId}`;
}

/** Next free extra number for a group on a date, given existing session ids. */
export function nextExtraSessionNumber(
  groupId: string,
  dateKey: DateKey,
  existingIds: Iterable<string>,
): number {
  let candidate = 2;
  const used = new Set<number>();
  for (const id of existingIds) {
    const parsed = parseSessionId(id);
    if (!parsed) continue;
    if (parsed.groupId !== groupId || parsed.dateKey !== dateKey) continue;
    if (parsed.extraNumber !== undefined) used.add(parsed.extraNumber);
  }
  while (used.has(candidate)) candidate += 1;
  return candidate;
}

/** Compact `yyyyMMdd` back to a date key, for UI/debug helpers. */
export function dateKeyFromCompact(compact: string): DateKey {
  if (!/^\d{8}$/.test(compact)) {
    throw new RangeError(`Invalid compact date: ${compact}`);
  }
  const key = `${compact.slice(0, 4)}-${compact.slice(4, 6)}-${compact.slice(6, 8)}`;
  parseDateKey(key);
  return key;
}
````

### `src/domain/stats.ts`  ·  238 lines

````typescript
import { expectedCycleAmount } from "./money";
import type {
  Attendance,
  Group,
  Payment,
  Session,
  Student,
  StudentBalance,
  TutorSettings,
} from "./types";

/**
 * Pure statistics. Everything is derived from source documents (or the live
 * balance map) — cached counters are never an input.
 *
 * Month keys are `yyyy-MM` in Africa/Cairo; all digit rendering uses Latin
 * numerals at the UI layer.
 */

export function monthKeyOf(dateKey: string): string {
  return dateKey.slice(0, 7);
}

export function shiftMonthKey(monthKey: string, delta: number): string {
  const [year, month] = monthKey.split("-").map(Number);
  const date = new Date(Date.UTC(year, month - 1 + delta, 1));
  return `${date.getUTCFullYear()}-${String(date.getUTCMonth() + 1).padStart(2, "0")}`;
}

export function collectedInMonth(
  payments: readonly Payment[],
  monthKey: string,
): number {
  return payments
    .filter((payment) => payment.dateKey.startsWith(monthKey))
    .reduce((sum, payment) => sum + payment.amount, 0);
}

export function outstandingNow(
  balances: ReadonlyMap<string, StudentBalance>,
): number {
  return [...balances.values()].reduce(
    (sum, balance) => sum + Math.max(0, balance.amountDue),
    0,
  );
}

/**
 * Expected income for one cycle per ACTIVE student:
 *   Σ expectedCycleAmount(pricePerSession, cycleLength)
 * It is a capacity figure, not a claim that every student paid this month.
 */
export function expectedForMonth(
  students: readonly Student[],
  settings: Pick<TutorSettings, "cycleLength">,
): number {
  return students
    .filter((student) => student.active)
    .reduce(
      (sum, student) =>
        sum + expectedCycleAmount(student.pricePerSession, settings.cycleLength),
      0,
    );
}

function completedSessionsInMonth(
  sessionsById: ReadonlyMap<string, Session>,
  monthKey: string,
): ReadonlyMap<string, Session> {
  return new Map(
    [...sessionsById.values()]
      .filter(
        (session) =>
          session.status === "completed" &&
          session.dateKey.startsWith(monthKey),
      )
      .map((session) => [session.id, session]),
  );
}

/** present / all attendance docs of completed lessons in the month. */
export function attendanceRate(
  attendance: readonly Attendance[],
  sessionsById: ReadonlyMap<string, Session>,
  monthKey: string,
): number {
  const sessions = completedSessionsInMonth(sessionsById, monthKey);
  const rows = attendance.filter((row) => sessions.has(row.sessionId));
  if (rows.length === 0) return 0;
  return Math.round((rows.filter((row) => row.status === "present").length / rows.length) * 100);
}

export interface MonthIncome {
  monthKey: string;
  amount: number;
}

export function incomeByMonth(
  payments: readonly Payment[],
  monthKey: string,
  months = 6,
): MonthIncome[] {
  return Array.from({ length: months }, (_, index) => {
    const key = shiftMonthKey(monthKey, index - (months - 1));
    return { monthKey: key, amount: collectedInMonth(payments, key) };
  });
}

export interface GroupAttendance {
  groupId: string;
  groupName: string;
  present: number;
  total: number;
  rate: number;
}

export function attendanceByGroup(
  attendance: readonly Attendance[],
  sessionsById: ReadonlyMap<string, Session>,
  groups: readonly Group[],
  monthKey: string,
): GroupAttendance[] {
  const sessions = completedSessionsInMonth(sessionsById, monthKey);
  return groups
    .filter((group) => group.active)
    .map((group) => {
      const rows = attendance.filter(
        (row) => sessions.has(row.sessionId) && row.groupId === group.id,
      );
      const present = rows.filter((row) => row.status === "present").length;
      return {
        groupId: group.id,
        groupName: group.name,
        present,
        total: rows.length,
        rate: rows.length === 0 ? 0 : Math.round((present / rows.length) * 100),
      };
    });
}

export interface AbsenceRow {
  studentId: string;
  name: string;
  absences: number;
}

export function topAbsences(
  attendance: readonly Attendance[],
  sessionsById: ReadonlyMap<string, Session>,
  students: readonly Student[],
  monthKey: string,
  limit = 5,
): AbsenceRow[] {
  const sessions = completedSessionsInMonth(sessionsById, monthKey);
  return students
    .map((student) => ({
      studentId: student.id,
      name: student.name,
      absences: attendance.filter(
        (row) =>
          row.studentId === student.id &&
          row.status === "absent" &&
          sessions.has(row.sessionId),
      ).length,
    }))
    .filter((row) => row.absences > 0)
    .sort((a, b) => b.absences - a.absences || a.name.localeCompare(b.name, "ar"))
    .slice(0, limit);
}

export interface SlowPayer {
  studentId: string;
  name: string;
  amountDue: number;
  lastPaymentKey: string | null;
}

export function slowestPayers(
  students: readonly Student[],
  balances: ReadonlyMap<string, StudentBalance>,
  payments: readonly Payment[],
  limit = 5,
): SlowPayer[] {
  return students
    .filter((student) => student.active)
    .map((student) => {
      const last = payments
        .filter((payment) => payment.studentId === student.id)
        .map((payment) => payment.dateKey)
        .sort((a, b) => b.localeCompare(a))[0];
      return {
        studentId: student.id,
        name: student.name,
        amountDue: balances.get(student.id)?.amountDue ?? 0,
        lastPaymentKey: last ?? null,
      };
    })
    .filter((row) => row.amountDue > 0)
    // No payment ever → oldest possible date → first in the list.
    .sort(
      (a, b) =>
        (a.lastPaymentKey ?? "").localeCompare(b.lastPaymentKey ?? "") ||
        b.amountDue - a.amountDue,
    )
    .slice(0, limit);
}

export interface GroupIncome {
  groupId: string;
  groupName: string;
  amount: number;
}

export function topGroupsByIncome(
  payments: readonly Payment[],
  students: readonly Student[],
  groups: readonly Group[],
  monthKey: string,
  limit = 5,
): GroupIncome[] {
  const groupByStudent = new Map(students.map((student) => [student.id, student.groupId]));
  const totals = new Map<string, number>();
  for (const payment of payments) {
    if (!payment.dateKey.startsWith(monthKey)) continue;
    const groupId = groupByStudent.get(payment.studentId);
    if (!groupId) continue;
    totals.set(groupId, (totals.get(groupId) ?? 0) + payment.amount);
  }
  return groups
    .map((group) => ({
      groupId: group.id,
      groupName: group.name,
      amount: totals.get(group.id) ?? 0,
    }))
    .filter((row) => row.amount > 0)
    .sort((a, b) => b.amount - a.amount)
    .slice(0, limit);
}
````
