````typescript
/**
 * Domain types for Tutor Manager.
 *
 * PURE MODULE: no React, Next.js, Firebase or browser imports are allowed here.
 * Firestore `Timestamp`s are mapped to epoch milliseconds at the data layer so
 * the domain stays serialisable and testable.
 */

export type Weekday = 0 | 1 | 2 | 3 | 4 | 5 | 6;

/** Local Cairo date in `yyyy-MM-dd` form. The only date identity in the app. */
export type DateKey = string;

export type Locale = "ar" | "en";

export type SessionStatus = "scheduled" | "completed" | "cancelled";
export type SessionType = "regular" | "extra";
export type AttendanceStatus = "present" | "absent";

export type BalanceState = "DUE" | "IN_PROGRESS" | "SETTLED" | "PAID_AHEAD";

export interface Slot {
  weekday: Weekday;
  /** Cairo wall-clock time, `HH:mm`. */
  time: string;
  durationMin: number;
}

export interface TutorSettings {
  absenceCounts: boolean;
  cycleLength: number;
  language: Locale;
  currency: string;
  currencyLabel: string;
  weekStartsOn: Weekday;
}

export const DEFAULT_SETTINGS: TutorSettings = {
  absenceCounts: true,
  cycleLength: 4,
  language: "ar",
  currency: "EGP",
  currencyLabel: "ج.م",
  weekStartsOn: 6,
};

export interface Group {
  id: string;
  name: string;
  subject?: string;
  slots: Slot[];
  active: boolean;
  createdAtMs?: number;
  updatedAtMs?: number;
}

export interface Student {
  id: string;
  name: string;
  /** Parent phone. Never sent to the AI provider. */
  phone?: string;
  groupId: string;
  pricePerSession: number;
  startedAtKey: DateKey;
  active: boolean;
  note?: string;
  /** Sessions carried over from before the app was used. */
  initialCounted: number;
  initialPaid: number;
  /** Cached, derived. Only `counterService` writes these. */
  sessionsCounted: number;
  sessionsPaid: number;
  createdAtMs?: number;
  updatedAtMs?: number;
}

export interface Session {
  id: string;
  groupId: string;
  groupName: string;
  dateKey: DateKey;
  time: string;
  type: SessionType;
  status: SessionStatus;
  note?: string;
  createdAtMs?: number;
  updatedAtMs?: number;
}

export interface Attendance {
  id: string;
  sessionId: string;
  studentId: string;
  /** Group at the time of the lesson (preserved across moves). */
  groupId: string;
  dateKey: DateKey;
  status: AttendanceStatus;
  updatedAtMs?: number;
}

export interface Payment {
  id: string;
  studentId: string;
  dateKey: DateKey;
  /** Whole currency units. */
  amount: number;
  sessionsCovered: number;
  note?: string;
  createdAtMs?: number;
  updatedAtMs?: number;
}

export interface Counters {
  counted: number;
  paid: number;
}

export interface StudentBalance {
  counted: number;
  paid: number;
  balance: number;
  /** 0 when the student has no counted sessions, otherwise 1..cycleLength. */
  cyclePosition: number;
  state: BalanceState;
  dueCycles: number;
  amountDue: number;
  paidAheadBy: number;
}

export interface GroupOutlier {
  studentId: string;
  name: string;
  position: number;
}

export interface GroupStatus {
  /** Most common position among active students with counted > 0; tie -> higher. */
  position: number;
  outliers: GroupOutlier[];
  activeCount: number;
}
````

### `src/domain/validation.ts`  ·  102 lines

````typescript
import { isValidDateKey } from "./dateKey";
import type { DateKey, Slot, Weekday } from "./types";

/**
 * Pure form validation. The UI only maps these keys through the dictionary —
 * no validation message is ever hardcoded in a screen.
 */

export type ValidationErrorKey =
  | "nameRequired"
  | "nameTooShort"
  | "invalidTime"
  | "invalidDuration"
  | "invalidPrice"
  | "invalidCount"
  | "invalidDate"
  | "invalidPhone"
  | "noSlots";

export type ValidationResult<T> =
  | { ok: true; value: T }
  | { ok: false; error: ValidationErrorKey };

const TIME_RE = /^([01]\d|2[0-3]):([0-5]\d)$/;

const fail = <T>(error: ValidationErrorKey): ValidationResult<T> => ({
  ok: false,
  error,
});

export function normalizeName(raw: string): ValidationResult<string> {
  const value = raw.trim().replace(/\s+/g, " ");
  if (!value) return fail("nameRequired");
  if (value.length < 2) return fail("nameTooShort");
  return { ok: true, value };
}

export function normalizeTime(raw: string): ValidationResult<string> {
  const value = raw.trim();
  return TIME_RE.test(value)
    ? { ok: true, value }
    : fail("invalidTime");
}

export function normalizeDuration(raw: number | string): ValidationResult<number> {
  const value = typeof raw === "number" ? raw : Number(raw.trim());
  if (!Number.isInteger(value) || value < 15 || value > 180) {
    return fail("invalidDuration");
  }
  return { ok: true, value };
}

export function normalizeSlot(raw: {
  weekday: number;
  time: string;
  durationMin: number | string;
}): ValidationResult<Slot> {
  if (!Number.isInteger(raw.weekday) || raw.weekday < 0 || raw.weekday > 6) {
    return fail("invalidDate");
  }
  const time = normalizeTime(raw.time);
  if (!time.ok) return fail(time.error);
  const duration = normalizeDuration(raw.durationMin);
  if (!duration.ok) return fail(duration.error);
  return {
    ok: true,
    value: {
      weekday: raw.weekday as Weekday,
      time: time.value,
      durationMin: duration.value,
    },
  };
}

/** Whole currency units only — never decimals, never negatives. */
export function normalizePrice(raw: number | string): ValidationResult<number> {
  const value = typeof raw === "number" ? raw : Number(raw.trim());
  if (!Number.isInteger(value) || value < 0) return fail("invalidPrice");
  return { ok: true, value };
}

export function normalizeCount(raw: number | string): ValidationResult<number> {
  const value = raw === "" ? 0 : typeof raw === "number" ? raw : Number(raw.trim());
  if (!Number.isInteger(value) || value < 0) return fail("invalidCount");
  return { ok: true, value };
}

export function normalizeDateKey(raw: string): ValidationResult<DateKey> {
  const value = raw.trim();
  return isValidDateKey(value)
    ? { ok: true, value }
    : fail("invalidDate");
}

/** Empty is allowed; a present value must have 7–15 digits. */
export function normalizePhone(raw: string): ValidationResult<string | undefined> {
  const value = raw.trim();
  if (!value) return { ok: true, value: undefined };
  const digits = value.replace(/[^\d]/g, "");
  if (digits.length < 7 || digits.length > 15) return fail("invalidPhone");
  return { ok: true, value };
}
````

### `src/i18n/I18nProvider.tsx`  ·  64 lines

````tsx
"use client";

import {
  createContext,
  useCallback,
  useContext,
  useEffect,
  useMemo,
  useSyncExternalStore,
  type ReactNode,
} from "react";
import {
  dictionaries,
  dirFor,
  type Dict,
  type Locale,
} from "./index";
import {
  getLocaleServerSnapshot,
  getLocaleSnapshot,
  setStoredLocale,
  subscribeToLocale,
} from "./localeStore";

type I18nContextValue = {
  locale: Locale;
  dir: "rtl" | "ltr";
  t: Dict;
  setLocale: (locale: Locale) => void;
};

const I18nContext = createContext<I18nContextValue | null>(null);

export function I18nProvider({ children }: { children: ReactNode }) {
  const locale = useSyncExternalStore(
    subscribeToLocale,
    getLocaleSnapshot,
    getLocaleServerSnapshot,
  );

  const dir = dirFor(locale);

  useEffect(() => {
    document.documentElement.lang = locale;
    document.documentElement.dir = dir;
  }, [locale, dir]);

  const setLocale = useCallback((next: Locale) => {
    setStoredLocale(next);
  }, []);

  const value = useMemo<I18nContextValue>(
    () => ({ locale, dir, t: dictionaries[locale], setLocale }),
    [locale, dir, setLocale],
  );

  return <I18nContext.Provider value={value}>{children}</I18nContext.Provider>;
}

export function useI18n(): I18nContextValue {
  const ctx = useContext(I18nContext);
  if (!ctx) throw new Error("useI18n must be used inside <I18nProvider>");
  return ctx;
}
````

````typescript
export type Dictionary = typeof ar;
export type DeepStringify<T> = {
  [K in keyof T]: T[K] extends string ? string : DeepStringify<T[K]>;
};
export type DictShape = DeepStringify<Dictionary>;
````

### `src/i18n/index.ts`  ·  32 lines

````typescript
import { ar, type DictShape } from "./ar";
import { en } from "./en";

export type Locale = "ar" | "en";
export type Dict = DictShape;

export const dictionaries: Record<Locale, Dict> = { ar, en };
export const DEFAULT_LOCALE: Locale = "ar";
export const LOCALE_STORAGE_KEY = "tm.locale";

export function dirFor(locale: Locale): "rtl" | "ltr" {
  return locale === "ar" ? "rtl" : "ltr";
}

export function isLocale(value: unknown): value is Locale {
  return value === "ar" || value === "en";
}

/**
 * Tiny placeholder interpolation: `format("Session {position} of {length}", {...})`.
 * Keeps the dictionaries plain objects instead of functions.
 */
export function format(
  template: string,
  params: Record<string, string | number>,
): string {
  return template.replace(/\{(\w+)\}/g, (match, key: string) =>
    key in params ? String(params[key]) : match,
  );
}

export type { DictShape };
````

### `src/i18n/localeStore.ts`  ·  63 lines

````typescript
import {
  DEFAULT_LOCALE,
  LOCALE_STORAGE_KEY,
  isLocale,
  type Locale,
} from "./index";

/**
 * Tiny external store for the UI language.
 *
 * Using `useSyncExternalStore` keeps the server snapshot stable (Arabic) and
 * swaps to the saved locale right after hydration without a setState-in-effect
 * and without a hydration mismatch.
 */

type Listener = () => void;

const listeners = new Set<Listener>();
let cached: Locale | null = null;

function readStored(): Locale {
  if (typeof window === "undefined") return DEFAULT_LOCALE;
  try {
    const saved = window.localStorage.getItem(LOCALE_STORAGE_KEY);
    return isLocale(saved) ? saved : DEFAULT_LOCALE;
  } catch {
    return DEFAULT_LOCALE;
  }
}

export function getLocaleSnapshot(): Locale {
  if (cached === null) cached = readStored();
  return cached;
}

export function getLocaleServerSnapshot(): Locale {
  return DEFAULT_LOCALE;
}

export function subscribeToLocale(listener: Listener): () => void {
  listeners.add(listener);
  const onStorage = (event: StorageEvent) => {
    if (event.key === LOCALE_STORAGE_KEY) {
      cached = readStored();
      listener();
    }
  };
  window.addEventListener("storage", onStorage);
  return () => {
    listeners.delete(listener);
    window.removeEventListener("storage", onStorage);
  };
}

export function setStoredLocale(locale: Locale): void {
  cached = locale;
  try {
    window.localStorage.setItem(LOCALE_STORAGE_KEY, locale);
  } catch {
    // Private mode: the choice still applies for this session.
  }
  for (const listener of listeners) listener();
}
````

````typescript
  ...parts: Array<string | false | null | undefined>
): string {
````

### `src/lib/groupColor.ts`  ·  16 lines

````typescript
/** Stable, non-semantic colour per group id — no colour is the only cue. */
const palette = [
  "bg-primary",
  "bg-accent",
  "bg-info",
  "bg-warning",
  "bg-danger",
] as const;

export function groupColor(groupId: string): string {
  let hash = 0;
  for (const character of groupId) {
    hash = (hash * 31 + character.charCodeAt(0)) | 0;
  }
  return palette[Math.abs(hash) % palette.length];
}
````

### `src/lib/useMounted.ts`  ·  19 lines

````typescript
"use client";

import { useSyncExternalStore } from "react";

const noopSubscribe = () => () => {};

/**
 * `false` during SSR and the hydration pass, `true` afterwards.
 *
 * Used to render clock/device-dependent text (greetings, "today") without a
 * setState-in-effect and without a hydration mismatch.
 */
export function useMounted(): boolean {
  return useSyncExternalStore(
    noopSubscribe,
    () => true,
    () => false,
  );
}
````

### `src/lib/vibrate.ts`  ·  15 lines

````typescript
/**
 * Haptics are a nicety, never a requirement: unsupported browsers (iOS Safari),
 * reduced-motion users and SSR all fall through silently.
 */
export function vibrate(pattern: number | number[] = 12): void {
  if (typeof navigator === "undefined") return;
  if (typeof navigator.vibrate !== "function") return;
  if (typeof window !== "undefined") {
    const reduced = window.matchMedia?.(
      "(prefers-reduced-motion: reduce)",
    )?.matches;
    if (reduced) return;
  }
  navigator.vibrate(pattern);
}
````

### `src/lib/weekdays.ts`  ·  20 lines

````typescript
import type { Dict } from "@/i18n";

const ORDER = [
  "sunday",
  "monday",
  "tuesday",
  "wednesday",
  "thursday",
  "friday",
  "saturday",
] as const;

export function weekdayLabel(t: Dict, weekday: number): string {
  const key = ORDER[weekday] ?? "saturday";
  return t.settings.weekdays[key];
}

export function weekdayOptions(t: Dict): Array<{ value: number; label: string }> {
  return ORDER.map((_, index) => ({ value: index, label: weekdayLabel(t, index) }));
}
````

### `test/ai/sanitize.test.ts`  ·  55 lines

````typescript
import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
import { redactPhones, redactStrings } from "@/domain/aiSanitize";
import { AI_TOOLS, ClarificationNeeded } from "@/data/ai/tools";

describe("AI privacy", () => {
  it("redacts local and international phone numbers", () => {
    expect(redactPhones("اتصل ب 01001234567")).toBe("اتصل ب [number]");
    expect(redactPhones("+20 100 123 4567")).toBe("[number]");
    expect(redactPhones("السعر 200 ج.م")).toBe("السعر 200 ج.م");
  });

  it("keeps ids but redacts every string field in a tool result", () => {
    const result = redactStrings({
      studentId: "s1_20261125",
      note: "موبيله 01123456789",
      nested: [{ phone: "01234567890", name: "أحمد" }],
      amount: 200,
    });
    expect(result.studentId).toBe("s1_20261125");
    expect(result.note).toBe("موبيله [number]");
    expect(result.nested[0]).toEqual({ phone: "[number]", name: "أحمد" });
    expect(result.amount).toBe(200);
  });
});

describe("AI tools", () => {
  it("declares all seven read tools and four proposal tools", () => {
    const names = AI_TOOLS.map((tool) => tool.name);
    expect(names).toEqual(
      expect.arrayContaining([
        "get_dues",
        "get_almost_due",
        "get_absences",
        "get_income",
        "get_group_status",
        "get_student_summary",
        "get_today_overview",
        "propose_attendance",
        "propose_payment",
        "propose_cancel_session",
        "draft_parent_reminder",
      ]),
    );
    expect(names.every((name) => name?.startsWith("get_") || name?.startsWith("propose_") || name === "draft_parent_reminder")).toBe(true);
  });

  it("carries duplicate-name candidates without guessing", () => {
    const error = new ClarificationNeeded([
      { id: "s1", name: "أحمد", groupName: "رياضيات" },
      { id: "s2", name: "أحمد", groupName: "إنجليزي" },
    ]);
    expect(error.candidates).toHaveLength(2);
    expect(error.candidates[1].groupName).toBe("إنجليزي");
  });
});
````

### `test/ai/schema.test.ts`  ·  62 lines

````typescript
import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
import {
  AssistantBodySchema,
  parseAssistantReply,
} from "@/data/ai/schema";

describe("assistant request schema", () => {
  const base = { locale: "ar", clientToday: "2026-11-25" };

  it("accepts text or audio, not neither", () => {
    expect(AssistantBodySchema.safeParse({ ...base, text: "مين عليه دفعات؟" }).success).toBe(true);
    expect(
      AssistantBodySchema.safeParse({
        ...base,
        audio: { base64: "AAAA", mimeType: "audio/webm;codecs=opus" },
      }).success,
    ).toBe(true);
    expect(AssistantBodySchema.safeParse(base).success).toBe(false);
  });

  it("enforces the 1000-character and 2 MB caps", () => {
    expect(
      AssistantBodySchema.safeParse({ ...base, text: "x".repeat(1001) }).success,
    ).toBe(false);
    expect(
      AssistantBodySchema.safeParse({
        ...base,
        audio: { base64: "A".repeat(2_800_001), mimeType: "audio/mp4" },
      }).success,
    ).toBe(false);
  });

  it("rejects malformed dates", () => {
    expect(
      AssistantBodySchema.safeParse({
        ...base,
        clientToday: "25-11-2026",
        text: "مرحبا",
      }).success,
    ).toBe(false);
  });
});

describe("assistant reply parsing", () => {
  it("parses JSON answers with proposals", () => {
    const reply = parseAssistantReply(
      '{"answer":"عليه 200 ج.م","proposals":[{"type":"payment","params":{"amount":200}}]}',
      "ar",
    );
    expect(reply.answer).toBe("عليه 200 ج.م");
    expect(reply.proposals?.[0]?.type).toBe("payment");
  });

  it("accepts fenced JSON and falls back to plain text", () => {
    expect(
      parseAssistantReply('```json\n{"answer":"تمام"}\n```', "ar").answer,
    ).toBe("تمام");
    expect(parseAssistantReply("مش قادر أفهم السؤال", "ar").answer).toBe(
      "مش قادر أفهم السؤال",
    );
  });
});
````

### `test/domain/balanceCalculator.test.ts`  ·  150 lines

````typescript
import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
import {
  calculateBalance,
  computeStudentBalance,
} from "@/domain/balanceCalculator";
import type { Payment } from "@/domain/types";

const base = { pricePerSession: 50, cycleLength: 4 };

describe("calculateBalance", () => {
  it("marks a student due after 4 counted sessions", () => {
    const balance = calculateBalance({ ...base, counted: 4, paid: 0 });

    expect(balance.state).toBe("DUE");
    expect(balance.balance).toBe(4);
    expect(balance.dueCycles).toBe(1);
    expect(balance.amountDue).toBe(200);
    expect(balance.cyclePosition).toBe(4);
  });

  it("treats an early payment as paid ahead and never as unpaid at session 4", () => {
    const atThree = calculateBalance({ ...base, counted: 3, paid: 4 });
    expect(atThree.state).toBe("PAID_AHEAD");
    expect(atThree.balance).toBe(-1);
    expect(atThree.paidAheadBy).toBe(1);
    expect(atThree.cyclePosition).toBe(3);
    expect(atThree.amountDue).toBe(0);

    const atFour = calculateBalance({ ...base, counted: 4, paid: 4 });
    expect(atFour.state).toBe("SETTLED");
    expect(atFour.balance).toBe(0);
    expect(atFour.cyclePosition).toBe(4);

    const atEight = calculateBalance({ ...base, counted: 8, paid: 4 });
    expect(atEight.state).toBe("DUE");
    expect(atEight.dueCycles).toBe(1);
    expect(atEight.amountDue).toBe(200);
  });

  it("keeps an unpaid student at one cycle when six sessions are owed", () => {
    const balance = calculateBalance({ ...base, counted: 6, paid: 0 });

    expect(balance.balance).toBe(6);
    expect(balance.dueCycles).toBe(1);
    expect(balance.amountDue).toBe(200);
    expect(balance.cyclePosition).toBe(2);

    const afterPayment = calculateBalance({ ...base, counted: 6, paid: 4 });
    expect(afterPayment.state).toBe("IN_PROGRESS");
    expect(afterPayment.balance).toBe(2);
    expect(afterPayment.cyclePosition).toBe(2);
    expect(afterPayment.amountDue).toBe(0);
  });

  it("uses the discounted per-student price", () => {
    const balance = calculateBalance({
      counted: 4,
      paid: 0,
      pricePerSession: 40,
      cycleLength: 4,
    });

    expect(balance.amountDue).toBe(160);
  });

  it("respects a cycle length of 5", () => {
    const five = calculateBalance({
      counted: 5,
      paid: 0,
      pricePerSession: 50,
      cycleLength: 5,
    });
    expect(five.cyclePosition).toBe(5);
    expect(five.state).toBe("DUE");
    expect(five.amountDue).toBe(250);

    const six = calculateBalance({
      counted: 6,
      paid: 0,
      pricePerSession: 50,
      cycleLength: 5,
    });
    expect(six.cyclePosition).toBe(1);
    expect(six.dueCycles).toBe(1);
    expect(six.amountDue).toBe(250);
  });

  it("re-prices new dues without touching past payments", () => {
    const oldPayment: Payment = {
      id: "p1",
      studentId: "s1",
      dateKey: "2026-01-05",
      amount: 200,
      sessionsCovered: 4,
    };
    const before = calculateBalance({
      counted: 4,
      paid: oldPayment.sessionsCovered,
      pricePerSession: 50,
      cycleLength: 4,
    });
    const afterPriceChange = calculateBalance({
      counted: 8,
      paid: oldPayment.sessionsCovered,
      pricePerSession: 60,
      cycleLength: 4,
    });

    expect(before.state).toBe("SETTLED");
    expect(afterPriceChange.amountDue).toBe(240);
    expect(oldPayment.amount).toBe(200);
  });

  it("counts each full cycle, not just one", () => {
    const balance = calculateBalance({ ...base, counted: 9, paid: 0 });
    expect(balance.dueCycles).toBe(2);
    expect(balance.amountDue).toBe(400);
  });

  it("returns position 0 for a student with no counted sessions", () => {
    const balance = calculateBalance({ ...base, counted: 0, paid: 0 });
    expect(balance.cyclePosition).toBe(0);
    expect(balance.state).toBe("SETTLED");
  });

  it("rejects invalid input", () => {
    expect(() =>
      calculateBalance({ ...base, counted: 1.5, paid: 0 }),
    ).toThrow(RangeError);
    expect(() =>
      calculateBalance({ ...base, counted: -1, paid: 0 }),
    ).toThrow(RangeError);
    expect(() =>
      calculateBalance({ ...base, counted: 1, paid: 0, cycleLength: 0 }),
    ).toThrow(RangeError);
    expect(() =>
      calculateBalance({ ...base, counted: 1, paid: 0, pricePerSession: 10.5 }),
    ).toThrow(RangeError);
  });

  it("computes a student balance from counters + settings", () => {
    const balance = computeStudentBalance(
      { pricePerSession: 50 },
      { counted: 3, paid: 4 },
      { cycleLength: 4 },
    );
    expect(balance.state).toBe("PAID_AHEAD");
    expect(balance.paidAheadBy).toBe(1);
  });
});
````

### `test/domain/counters.test.ts`  ·  310 lines

````typescript
import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
import { computeCounters } from "@/domain/counters";
import type { Attendance, Session } from "@/domain/types";
import {
  TEST_SETTINGS,
  makeAttendance,
  makePayment,
  makeSession,
  makeStudent,
  sessionsMap,
  weeklyDateKeys,
} from "./helpers";

function buildLessons(
  studentId: string,
  dateKeys: string[],
  overrides: Partial<Session> = {},
): { sessions: Session[]; attendance: Attendance[] } {
  const sessions = dateKeys.map((dateKey, index) =>
    makeSession({
      id: `lesson_${index + 1}`,
      dateKey,
      ...overrides,
    }),
  );
  const attendance = sessions.map((session) =>
    makeAttendance({ sessionId: session.id, studentId, dateKey: session.dateKey }),
  );
  return { sessions, attendance };
}

describe("computeCounters", () => {
  it("counts four present sessions", () => {
    const student = makeStudent({ id: "s1" });
    const keys = weeklyDateKeys("2026-01-05", 4);
    const { sessions, attendance } = buildLessons("s1", keys);

    const counters = computeCounters({
      students: [student],
      sessionsById: sessionsMap(...sessions),
      attendance,
      payments: [],
      settings: TEST_SETTINGS,
    });

    expect(counters.get("s1")).toEqual({ counted: 4, paid: 0 });
  });

  it("counts an absence when absenceCounts is true", () => {
    const student = makeStudent({ id: "s1" });
    const session = makeSession({ id: "g1_20260105" });
    const attendance = [
      makeAttendance({
        sessionId: session.id,
        studentId: "s1",
        status: "absent",
      }),
    ];

    const counters = computeCounters({
      students: [student],
      sessionsById: sessionsMap(session),
      attendance,
      payments: [],
      settings: { absenceCounts: true, cycleLength: 4 },
    });

    expect(counters.get("s1")?.counted).toBe(1);
  });

  it("does not count an absence when absenceCounts is false", () => {
    const student = makeStudent({ id: "s1" });
    const session = makeSession({ id: "g1_20260105" });
    const attendance = [
      makeAttendance({
        sessionId: session.id,
        studentId: "s1",
        status: "absent",
      }),
    ];

    const counters = computeCounters({
      students: [student],
      sessionsById: sessionsMap(session),
      attendance,
      payments: [],
      settings: { absenceCounts: false, cycleLength: 4 },
    });

    expect(counters.get("s1")?.counted).toBe(0);
  });

  it("never counts attendance of a cancelled session", () => {
    const student = makeStudent({ id: "s1" });
    const session = makeSession({ id: "g1_20260105", status: "cancelled" });
    const attendance = [
      makeAttendance({ sessionId: session.id, studentId: "s1" }),
    ];

    const counters = computeCounters({
      students: [student],
      sessionsById: sessionsMap(session),
      attendance,
      payments: [],
      settings: TEST_SETTINGS,
    });

    expect(counters.get("s1")?.counted).toBe(0);
  });

  it("never counts attendance of a scheduled (not completed) session", () => {
    const student = makeStudent({ id: "s1" });
    const session = makeSession({ id: "g1_20260105", status: "scheduled" });
    const attendance = [
      makeAttendance({ sessionId: session.id, studentId: "s1" }),
    ];

    const counters = computeCounters({
      students: [student],
      sessionsById: sessionsMap(session),
      attendance,
      payments: [],
      settings: TEST_SETTINGS,
    });

    expect(counters.get("s1")?.counted).toBe(0);
  });

  it("counts an extra session like a regular one", () => {
    const student = makeStudent({ id: "s1" });
    const session = makeSession({
      id: "g1_20260105_2",
      type: "extra",
      status: "completed",
    });
    const attendance = [
      makeAttendance({ sessionId: session.id, studentId: "s1" }),
    ];

    const counters = computeCounters({
      students: [student],
      sessionsById: sessionsMap(session),
      attendance,
      payments: [],
      settings: TEST_SETTINGS,
    });

    expect(counters.get("s1")?.counted).toBe(1);
  });

  it("recomputes upward when an old absence is edited to present", () => {
    const student = makeStudent({ id: "s1" });
    const session = makeSession({ id: "g1_20260105" });
    const absent: Attendance[] = [
      makeAttendance({
        sessionId: session.id,
        studentId: "s1",
        status: "absent",
      }),
    ];
    const present: Attendance[] = [
      makeAttendance({ sessionId: session.id, studentId: "s1" }),
    ];
    const base = {
      students: [student],
      sessionsById: sessionsMap(session),
      payments: [],
      settings: { absenceCounts: false, cycleLength: 4 },
    };

    expect(computeCounters({ ...base, attendance: absent }).get("s1")?.counted).toBe(0);
    expect(computeCounters({ ...base, attendance: present }).get("s1")?.counted).toBe(1);
  });

  it("keeps initialCounted when recomputing (imported history)", () => {
    const student = makeStudent({ id: "s1", initialCounted: 2 });
    const session = makeSession({ id: "g1_20260105" });
    const attendance = [
      makeAttendance({ sessionId: session.id, studentId: "s1" }),
    ];

    const counters = computeCounters({
      students: [student],
      sessionsById: sessionsMap(session),
      attendance,
      payments: [],
      settings: TEST_SETTINGS,
    });

    expect(counters.get("s1")).toEqual({ counted: 3, paid: 0 });
  });

  it("adds initialPaid to covered sessions", () => {
    const student = makeStudent({ id: "s1", initialPaid: 4 });
    const payment = makePayment({ id: "p1", studentId: "s1", sessionsCovered: 4 });

    const counters = computeCounters({
      students: [student],
      sessionsById: sessionsMap(),
      attendance: [],
      payments: [payment],
      settings: TEST_SETTINGS,
    });

    expect(counters.get("s1")?.paid).toBe(8);
  });

  it("keeps counters when a student moves to another group", () => {
    const dateKeys = weeklyDateKeys("2026-01-05", 3);
    const sessions = dateKeys.map((dateKey, index) =>
      makeSession({ id: `g1_lesson_${index + 1}`, dateKey }),
    );
    const attendance = sessions.map((session) =>
      makeAttendance({
        sessionId: session.id,
        studentId: "s1",
        groupId: "g1",
        dateKey: session.dateKey,
      }),
    );
    const newSession = makeSession({
      id: "g2_20260202",
      groupId: "g2",
      groupName: "Group 2",
      dateKey: "2026-02-02",
    });
    const newAttendance = makeAttendance({
      sessionId: newSession.id,
      studentId: "s1",
      groupId: "g2",
      dateKey: "2026-02-02",
    });

    const moved = makeStudent({ id: "s1", groupId: "g2" });
    const counters = computeCounters({
      students: [moved],
      sessionsById: sessionsMap(...sessions, newSession),
      attendance: [...attendance, newAttendance],
      payments: [],
      settings: TEST_SETTINGS,
    });

    expect(counters.get("s1")?.counted).toBe(4);
  });

  it("ignores attendance dated before the student started", () => {
    const student = makeStudent({ id: "s1", startedAtKey: "2026-01-12" });
    const session = makeSession({ id: "g1_20260105", dateKey: "2026-01-05" });
    const attendance = [
      makeAttendance({ sessionId: session.id, studentId: "s1" }),
    ];

    const counters = computeCounters({
      students: [student],
      sessionsById: sessionsMap(session),
      attendance,
      payments: [],
      settings: TEST_SETTINGS,
    });

    expect(counters.get("s1")?.counted).toBe(0);
  });

  it("counts a duplicate attendance doc only once", () => {
    const student = makeStudent({ id: "s1" });
    const session = makeSession({ id: "g1_20260105" });
    const row = makeAttendance({ sessionId: session.id, studentId: "s1" });

    const counters = computeCounters({
      students: [student],
      sessionsById: sessionsMap(session),
      attendance: [row, { ...row, id: "duplicate" }],
      payments: [],
      settings: TEST_SETTINGS,
    });

    expect(counters.get("s1")?.counted).toBe(1);
  });

  it("computes only the requested subset", () => {
    const s1 = makeStudent({ id: "s1" });
    const s2 = makeStudent({ id: "s2" });

    const counters = computeCounters({
      students: [s1, s2],
      sessionsById: sessionsMap(),
      attendance: [],
      payments: [makePayment({ id: "p1", studentId: "s1" })],
      settings: TEST_SETTINGS,
      studentIds: ["s1"],
    });

    expect(counters.has("s1")).toBe(true);
    expect(counters.has("s2")).toBe(false);
  });

  it("ignores attendance whose session document is missing", () => {
    const student = makeStudent({ id: "s1" });

    const counters = computeCounters({
      students: [student],
      sessionsById: sessionsMap(),
      attendance: [makeAttendance({ sessionId: "missing", studentId: "s1" })],
      payments: [],
      settings: TEST_SETTINGS,
    });

    expect(counters.get("s1")?.counted).toBe(0);
  });
});
````

### `test/domain/dateKey.test.ts`  ·  83 lines

````typescript
import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
import {
  addDaysKey,
  compareDateKeys,
  dateKeyFromParts,
  dateKeyOf,
  daysBetweenKeys,
  formatArabicDate,
  formatTime,
  isKeyInRange,
  isValidDateKey,
  nextDateKeys,
  parseDateKey,
  todayKey,
  weekdayOfKey,
} from "@/domain/dateKey";

describe("dateKey", () => {
  it("uses the Cairo day at the UTC midnight boundary", () => {
    // 21:59 UTC = 23:59 in Cairo (UTC+2 in November) -> same day.
    expect(dateKeyOf(new Date("2026-11-24T21:59:00Z"))).toBe("2026-11-24");
    // 22:30 UTC = 00:30 next day in Cairo.
    expect(dateKeyOf(new Date("2026-11-24T22:30:00Z"))).toBe("2026-11-25");
  });

  it("follows Cairo summer time (UTC+3)", () => {
    expect(dateKeyOf(new Date("2026-07-01T21:30:00Z"))).toBe("2026-07-02");
  });

  it("derives the weekday from the key, not from local time", () => {
    expect(weekdayOfKey("2026-11-24")).toBe(2); // Tuesday
    expect(weekdayOfKey("2026-11-28")).toBe(6); // Saturday
  });

  it("adds days across month and leap-year boundaries", () => {
    expect(addDaysKey("2026-11-30", 1)).toBe("2026-12-01");
    expect(addDaysKey("2026-02-28", 1)).toBe("2026-03-01");
    expect(addDaysKey("2028-02-28", 1)).toBe("2028-02-29");
    expect(addDaysKey("2026-01-01", -1)).toBe("2025-12-31");
  });

  it("keeps a stable 14-day generation window", () => {
    const keys = nextDateKeys("2026-11-24", 14);
    expect(keys).toHaveLength(14);
    expect(keys[0]).toBe("2026-11-24");
    expect(keys[13]).toBe("2026-12-07");
  });

  it("validates keys", () => {
    expect(isValidDateKey("2026-02-28")).toBe(true);
    expect(isValidDateKey("2026-02-30")).toBe(false);
    expect(isValidDateKey("2026-13-01")).toBe(false);
    expect(isValidDateKey("2026-1-01")).toBe(false);
    expect(isValidDateKey("24-11-2026")).toBe(false);
    expect(() => parseDateKey("2026-02-30")).toThrow(RangeError);
  });

  it("builds valid keys from parts", () => {
    expect(dateKeyFromParts(2026, 3, 7)).toBe("2026-03-07");
    expect(() => dateKeyFromParts(2026, 2, 30)).toThrow(RangeError);
  });

  it("compares and ranges keys", () => {
    expect(compareDateKeys("2026-01-01", "2026-01-02")).toBe(-1);
    expect(compareDateKeys("2026-01-02", "2026-01-02")).toBe(0);
    expect(isKeyInRange("2026-01-15", "2026-01-01", "2026-01-31")).toBe(true);
    expect(isKeyInRange("2026-02-01", "2026-01-01", "2026-01-31")).toBe(false);
    expect(daysBetweenKeys("2026-01-01", "2026-01-31")).toBe(30);
  });

  it("formats Arabic dates with Latin digits", () => {
    const label = formatArabicDate("2026-11-24", { day: "numeric" });
    expect(label).toContain("24");
    expect(todayKey(new Date("2026-11-24T10:00:00Z"))).toBe("2026-11-24");
  });

  it("formats wall-clock times", () => {
    expect(formatTime("17:05")).toBe("5:05 م");
    expect(formatTime("09:00", "en")).toBe("9:00 AM");
    expect(formatTime("00:30")).toBe("12:30 ص");
    expect(formatTime("bad")).toBe("bad");
  });
});
````

### `test/domain/groupPosition.test.ts`  ·  71 lines

````typescript
import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
import { computeGroupStatus } from "@/domain/groupPosition";
import type { StudentBalance } from "@/domain/types";
import { makeStudent } from "./helpers";

function balance(position: number, counted = 3): Pick<StudentBalance, "cyclePosition" | "counted"> {
  return { cyclePosition: position, counted };
}

describe("computeGroupStatus", () => {
  it("uses the group position and flags the late joiner", () => {
    const a = makeStudent({ id: "a", name: "Ahmed" });
    const b = makeStudent({ id: "b", name: "Hamada" });
    const late = makeStudent({ id: "late", name: "Omar", startedAtKey: "2026-01-15" });

    const status = computeGroupStatus(
      [a, b, late],
      new Map([
        ["a", balance(3)],
        ["b", balance(3)],
        ["late", balance(1, 1)],
      ]),
    );

    expect(status.position).toBe(3);
    expect(status.outliers).toEqual([
      { studentId: "late", name: "Omar", position: 1 },
    ]);
  });

  it("breaks a tie toward the higher position", () => {
    const a = makeStudent({ id: "a", name: "A" });
    const b = makeStudent({ id: "b", name: "B" });

    const status = computeGroupStatus(
      [a, b],
      new Map([
        ["a", balance(3)],
        ["b", balance(2)],
      ]),
    );

    expect(status.position).toBe(3);
    expect(status.outliers).toEqual([{ studentId: "b", name: "B", position: 2 }]);
  });

  it("ignores inactive students and students without counted sessions", () => {
    const active = makeStudent({ id: "active", name: "Active" });
    const inactive = makeStudent({ id: "inactive", name: "Inactive", active: false });
    const newJoin = makeStudent({ id: "new", name: "New" });

    const status = computeGroupStatus(
      [active, inactive, newJoin],
      new Map([
        ["active", balance(2)],
        ["inactive", balance(4)],
        ["new", balance(0, 0)],
      ]),
    );

    expect(status.position).toBe(2);
    expect(status.outliers).toEqual([]);
    expect(status.activeCount).toBe(2);
  });

  it("reports position 0 for an empty group", () => {
    const status = computeGroupStatus([], new Map());
    expect(status.position).toBe(0);
    expect(status.outliers).toEqual([]);
  });
});
````

### `test/domain/guard.test.ts`  ·  41 lines

````typescript
import { readdirSync, readFileSync, statSync } from "node:fs";
import { join } from "node:path";
import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest";

const DOMAIN_DIR = join(process.cwd(), "src", "domain");

const FORBIDDEN_IMPORT =
  /(?:from\s+|import\s*\(\s*|require\s*\(\s*)["'](react|react-dom|next(?:\/[^"']*)?|firebase(?:\/[^"']*)?|@firebase\/[^"']*|@google\/[^"']*|@\/data[^"']*|@\/components[^"']*|@\/app[^"']*)["']/;

function walk(dir: string): string[] {
  return readdirSync(dir).flatMap((entry) => {
    const full = join(dir, entry);
    return statSync(full).isDirectory()
      ? walk(full)
      : full.endsWith(".ts")
        ? [full]
        : [];
  });
}

describe("domain purity", () => {
  it("imports nothing from React, Next, Firebase or the UI/data layers", () => {
    const files = walk(DOMAIN_DIR);
    expect(files.length).toBeGreaterThan(0);

    const offenders = files.filter((file) =>
      FORBIDDEN_IMPORT.test(readFileSync(file, "utf8")),
    );

    expect(offenders).toEqual([]);
  });

  it("uses only relative imports inside the domain", () => {
    const files = walk(DOMAIN_DIR);
    const badSpecifier = /from\s+["']@\//;
    const offenders = files.filter((file) =>
      badSpecifier.test(readFileSync(file, "utf8")),
    );
    expect(offenders).toEqual([]);
  });
});
````

### `test/domain/helpers.ts`  ·  78 lines

````typescript
import type {
  Attendance,
  DateKey,
  Payment,
  Session,
  Student,
  TutorSettings,
} from "@/domain/types";

export const TEST_SETTINGS: Pick<TutorSettings, "absenceCounts" | "cycleLength"> =
  { absenceCounts: true, cycleLength: 4 };

export function makeStudent(
  overrides: Partial<Student> & { id: string },
): Student {
  return {
    name: overrides.id,
    groupId: "g1",
    pricePerSession: 50,
    startedAtKey: "2026-01-01",
    active: true,
    initialCounted: 0,
    initialPaid: 0,
    sessionsCounted: 0,
    sessionsPaid: 0,
    ...overrides,
  };
}

export function makeSession(
  overrides: Partial<Session> & { id: string },
): Session {
  return {
    groupId: "g1",
    groupName: "Group 1",
    dateKey: "2026-01-05",
    time: "17:00",
    type: "regular",
    status: "completed",
    ...overrides,
  };
}

export function makeAttendance(
  overrides: Partial<Attendance> & { sessionId: string; studentId: string },
): Attendance {
  return {
    id: `${overrides.sessionId}_${overrides.studentId}`,
    groupId: "g1",
    dateKey: "2026-01-05",
    status: "present",
    ...overrides,
  };
}

export function makePayment(
  overrides: Partial<Payment> & { id: string; studentId: string },
): Payment {
  return {
    dateKey: "2026-01-05",
    amount: 200,
    sessionsCovered: 4,
    ...overrides,
  };
}

export function sessionsMap(...sessions: Session[]): Map<string, Session> {
  return new Map(sessions.map((session) => [session.id, session]));
}

/** Weekly sessions starting at `fromKey`, every 7 days. */
export function weeklyDateKeys(fromKey: DateKey, count: number): DateKey[] {
  const start = Date.parse(`${fromKey}T00:00:00Z`);
  return Array.from({ length: count }, (_, index) => {
    const date = new Date(start + index * 7 * 86_400_000);
    return date.toISOString().slice(0, 10);
  });
}
````

````typescript
import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
import {
  assertInteger,
  assertNonNegativeInteger,
  expectedCycleAmount,
  formatCount,
  formatEGP,
  formatMoney,
} from "@/domain/money";

describe("money", () => {
  it("accepts integers and rejects floats", () => {
    expect(assertInteger(4)).toBe(4);
    expect(() => assertInteger(4.2)).toThrow(RangeError);
    expect(() => assertNonNegativeInteger(-1)).toThrow(RangeError);
    expect(assertNonNegativeInteger(0)).toBe(0);
  });

  it("computes the expected cycle amount", () => {
    expect(expectedCycleAmount(50, 4)).toBe(200);
    expect(expectedCycleAmount(40, 5)).toBe(200);
    expect(expectedCycleAmount(0, 4)).toBe(0);
    expect(() => expectedCycleAmount(50, 0)).toThrow(RangeError);
  });

  it("formats money with tabular-friendly Latin digits", () => {
    expect(formatEGP(200)).toBe("200 ج.م");
    expect(formatMoney(1234, "ج.م")).toBe("1,234 ج.م");
    expect(formatCount(1234)).toBe("1,234");
    expect(() => formatEGP(200.5)).toThrow(RangeError);
  });
});
````

### `test/domain/moneyScenarios.test.ts`  ·  63 lines

````typescript
import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
import { calculateBalance, needsReminder } from "@/domain/balanceCalculator";
import { expectedCycleAmount, formatMoney } from "@/domain/money";
import { DEFAULT_SETTINGS } from "@/domain/types";

const L = 4;
const PRICE = 50;
const balance = (counted: number, paid: number) =>
  calculateBalance({ counted, paid, pricePerSession: PRICE, cycleLength: L });

/** §7.1 money scenarios, reproduced through the pure calculator. */
describe("money scenarios", () => {
  it("early payer: paid ahead at session 3, settled at session 4", () => {
    const early = balance(3, 4);
    expect(early.state).toBe("PAID_AHEAD");
    expect(early.cyclePosition).toBe(3);
    expect(early.paidAheadBy).toBe(1);

    const atFour = balance(4, 4);
    expect(atFour.state).toBe("SETTLED");
    expect(atFour.balance).toBe(0);
  });

  it("after eight sessions with one payment, exactly one cycle is due", () => {
    const result = balance(8, 4);
    expect(result.state).toBe("DUE");
    expect(result.dueCycles).toBe(1);
    expect(result.amountDue).toBe(200);
    expect(result.cyclePosition).toBe(4);
  });

  it("unpaid six sessions: one cycle due, position wraps to 2", () => {
    const result = balance(6, 0);
    expect(result.state).toBe("DUE");
    expect(result.dueCycles).toBe(1);
    expect(result.amountDue).toBe(L * PRICE);
    expect(result.cyclePosition).toBe(2);
  });

  it("a discount is just a smaller payment amount", () => {
    const full = expectedCycleAmount(PRICE, L);
    const discounted = full - 40;
    expect(discounted).toBe(160);
    expect(formatMoney(discounted, DEFAULT_SETTINGS.currencyLabel)).toBe("160 ج.م");
    // Counters do not change: the student is still two sessions from due.
    expect(balance(6, 4).balance).toBe(2);
  });

  it("paying for two cycles settles both, then the next session is in progress", () => {
    const settled = balance(8, 8);
    expect(settled.state).toBe("SETTLED");
    expect(balance(9, 8).state).toBe("IN_PROGRESS");
  });

  it("reminder targets exactly students whose next counted session is the 4th", () => {
    expect(needsReminder(balance(3, 0), L)).toBe(true);
    expect(needsReminder(balance(7, 4), L)).toBe(true);
    // Paid ahead at session 3 and already due at 4 are never reminded.
    expect(needsReminder(balance(3, 4), L)).toBe(false);
    expect(needsReminder(balance(4, 0), L)).toBe(false);
    expect(needsReminder(balance(0, 0), L)).toBe(false);
  });
});
````

### `test/domain/sessionGenerator.test.ts`  ·  133 lines

````typescript
import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
import { addDaysKey, weekdayOfKey } from "@/domain/dateKey";
import {
  GENERATION_HORIZON_DAYS,
  planGeneration,
  type GenerationChange,
} from "@/domain/sessionGenerator";
import { extraSessionId, regularSessionId } from "@/domain/sessionId";
import type { Group, Session } from "@/domain/types";

const today = "2026-11-24"; // Tuesday

function group(id: string, weekday: number, time = "17:00"): Group {
  return {
    id,
    name: `Group ${id}`,
    slots: [{ weekday: weekday as Group["slots"][number]["weekday"], time, durationMin: 60 }],
    active: true,
  };
}

function createdSessions(changes: GenerationChange[]): Session[] {
  return changes.flatMap((change) =>
    change.kind === "create" ? [change.session] : [],
  );
}

describe("planGeneration", () => {
  it("materialises only matching weekdays inside the horizon", () => {
    const changes = planGeneration({
      groups: [group("g1", weekdayOfKey(today))],
      sessions: [],
      today,
    });

    const sessions = createdSessions(changes);
    expect(sessions.length).toBeGreaterThanOrEqual(2);
    expect(sessions.every((item) => item.dateKey >= today)).toBe(true);
    expect(sessions.every((item) => item.dateKey < addDaysKey(today, GENERATION_HORIZON_DAYS))).toBe(true);
    expect(sessions[0]).toMatchObject({
      id: regularSessionId("g1", today),
      groupName: "Group g1",
      status: "scheduled",
      time: "17:00",
    });
  });

  it("is idempotent: a second run produces no writes", () => {
    const input = {
      groups: [group("g1", weekdayOfKey(today))],
      sessions: [] as Session[],
      today,
    };
    const first = createdSessions(planGeneration(input));
    expect(planGeneration({ ...input, sessions: first })).toEqual([]);
  });

  it("skips archived groups and never resurrects a cancelled lesson", () => {
    const cancelled: Session = {
      id: regularSessionId("g1", today),
      groupId: "g1",
      groupName: "Group g1",
      dateKey: today,
      time: "17:00",
      type: "regular",
      status: "cancelled",
    };
    const archived = { ...group("g2", weekdayOfKey(today)), active: false };

    const changes = createdSessions(
      planGeneration({
        groups: [group("g1", weekdayOfKey(today)), archived],
        sessions: [cancelled],
        today,
      }),
    );

    expect(changes.some((session) => session.id === cancelled.id)).toBe(false);
    expect(changes.some((session) => session.groupId === "g2")).toBe(false);
    expect(changes.length).toBeGreaterThan(0);
  });

  it("updates only future scheduled lessons when a slot time changes", () => {
    const futureKey = addDaysKey(today, 7);
    const future: Session = {
      id: regularSessionId("g1", futureKey),
      groupId: "g1",
      groupName: "Group g1",
      dateKey: futureKey,
      time: "17:00",
      type: "regular",
      status: "scheduled",
    };
    const todaySession: Session = {
      ...future,
      id: regularSessionId("g1", today),
      dateKey: today,
    };

    const changes = planGeneration({
      groups: [group("g1", weekdayOfKey(today), "18:30")],
      sessions: [future, todaySession],
      today,
    });

    expect(changes).toEqual([
      { kind: "updateTime", id: future.id, time: "18:30" },
    ]);
  });

  it("leaves extra sessions alone", () => {
    const extra: Session = {
      id: extraSessionId("g1", today, 2),
      groupId: "g1",
      groupName: "Group g1",
      dateKey: today,
      time: "19:00",
      type: "extra",
      status: "scheduled",
    };
    const changes = createdSessions(
      planGeneration({
        groups: [group("g1", weekdayOfKey(today))],
        sessions: [extra],
        today,
      }),
    );
    expect(changes.map((item) => item.id)).toContain(
      regularSessionId("g1", today),
    );
    expect(changes.map((item) => item.id)).not.toContain(extra.id);
  });
});
````

### `test/domain/sessionId.test.ts`  ·  77 lines

````typescript
import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
import {
  attendanceId,
  compactDateKey,
  dateKeyFromCompact,
  extraSessionId,
  nextExtraSessionNumber,
  parseSessionId,
  regularSessionId,
} from "@/domain/sessionId";

describe("session ids", () => {
  it("is deterministic per group and date", () => {
    expect(regularSessionId("g1", "2026-11-24")).toBe("g1_20261124");
    expect(regularSessionId("g1", "2026-11-24")).toBe("g1_20261124");
  });

  it("numbers extra sessions from 2", () => {
    expect(extraSessionId("g1", "2026-11-24", 2)).toBe("g1_20261124_2");
    expect(() => extraSessionId("g1", "2026-11-24", 1)).toThrow(RangeError);
  });

  it("parses regular and extra ids", () => {
    expect(parseSessionId("g1_20261124")).toEqual({
      groupId: "g1",
      dateKey: "2026-11-24",
      extraNumber: undefined,
    });
    expect(parseSessionId("g1_20261124_3")).toEqual({
      groupId: "g1",
      dateKey: "2026-11-24",
      extraNumber: 3,
    });
  });

  it("parses group ids that contain underscores", () => {
    expect(parseSessionId("group_with_underscore_20261124")).toEqual({
      groupId: "group_with_underscore",
      dateKey: "2026-11-24",
      extraNumber: undefined,
    });
  });

  it("rejects malformed ids", () => {
    expect(parseSessionId("g1")).toBeNull();
    expect(parseSessionId("g1_20261340")).toBeNull();
    expect(parseSessionId("g1_20261124_1")).toBeNull();
  });

  it("builds stable attendance ids", () => {
    expect(attendanceId("g1_20261124", "s1")).toBe("g1_20261124_s1");
  });

  it("finds the smallest free extra number (never reuses a taken slot)", () => {
    expect(
      nextExtraSessionNumber("g1", "2026-11-24", [
        "g1_20261124",
        "g1_20261124_2",
        "g1_20261124_4",
        "g2_20261124_9",
      ]),
    ).toBe(3);
    expect(nextExtraSessionNumber("g1", "2026-11-24", ["g1_20261124"])).toBe(2);
    expect(
      nextExtraSessionNumber("g1", "2026-11-24", [
        "g1_20261124_2",
        "g1_20261124_3",
      ]),
    ).toBe(4);
  });

  it("converts between compact and dashed date keys", () => {
    expect(compactDateKey("2026-11-24")).toBe("20261124");
    expect(dateKeyFromCompact("20261124")).toBe("2026-11-24");
    expect(() => compactDateKey("24/11/2026")).toThrow(RangeError);
  });
});
````

````typescript
import type {

} from "@/domain/types";

const NOV = "2026-11";
const OCT = "2026-10";

const groups: Group[] = [
  { id: "g1", name: "رياضيات", slots: [], active: true },
  { id: "g2", name: "إنجليزي", slots: [], active: true },

  },
];

const sessions: Session[] = [
  {

    dateKey: "2026-11-02",
    time: "17:00",
    type: "regular",
    status: "completed",
  },
  {
    id: "g1_20261109",
    groupId: "g1",
    groupName: "رياضيات",
    dateKey: "2026-11-09",
    time: "17:00",
    type: "regular",
    status: "completed",
  },
  {
    id: "g2_20261103",
    groupId: "g2",
    groupName: "إنجليزي",
    dateKey: "2026-11-03",
    time: "18:00",
    type: "regular",
    status: "cancelled",
  },
  {
    id: "g1_20261005",
    groupId: "g1",
    groupName: "رياضيات",
    dateKey: "2026-10-05",
    time: "17:00",
    type: "regular",
    status: "completed",
  },
];
const sessionsById = new Map(sessions.map((session) => [session.id, session]));

const attendance: Attendance[] = [
  {
    id: "a1",
    sessionId: "g1_20261102",
    studentId: "s1",
    groupId: "g1",
    dateKey: "2026-11-02",
    status: "present",
  },
  {
    id: "a2",
    sessionId: "g1_20261102",
    studentId: "s2",
    groupId: "g1",
    dateKey: "2026-11-02",
    status: "present",
  },
  {
    id: "a3",
    sessionId: "g1_20261109",
    studentId: "s1",
    groupId: "g1",
    dateKey: "2026-11-09",
    status: "absent",
  },
  {
    id: "a4",
    sessionId: "g2_20261103",
    studentId: "s3",
    groupId: "g2",
    dateKey: "2026-11-03",
    status: "present",
  },
  {
    id: "a5",
    sessionId: "g1_20261005",
    studentId: "s1",
    groupId: "g1",
    dateKey: "2026-10-05",
    status: "present",
  },
];

const payments: Payment[] = [
  { id: "p1", studentId: "s1", dateKey: "2026-11-04", amount: 200, sessionsCovered: 4 },
  { id: "p2", studentId: "s2", dateKey: "2026-11-11", amount: 160, sessionsCovered: 4 },
  { id: "p3", studentId: "s1", dateKey: "2026-10-04", amount: 400, sessionsCovered: 8 },
];

const balances = new Map<string, StudentBalance>([
  ["s1", calculateBalance({ counted: 8, paid: 4, pricePerSession: 50, cycleLength: 4 })],
  ["s2", calculateBalance({ counted: 4, paid: 4, pricePerSession: 40, cycleLength: 4 })],
  ["s3", calculateBalance({ counted: 2, paid: 0, pricePerSession: 60, cycleLength: 4 })],
]);

describe("stats", () => {
  it("shifts month keys across year boundaries", () => {
    expect(shiftMonthKey(NOV, -1)).toBe(OCT);
    expect(shiftMonthKey("2026-01", -1)).toBe("2025-12");
  });

  it("collected matches the payment list for that month", () => {
    expect(collectedInMonth(payments, NOV)).toBe(360);
    expect(collectedInMonth(payments, OCT)).toBe(400);
    expect(collectedInMonth(payments, "2026-09")).toBe(0);
  });

  it("outstanding is the sum of live amountDue", () => {
    expect(outstandingNow(balances)).toBe(200);
  });

  it("expected is one cycle per active student", () => {
    expect(expectedForMonth(students, { cycleLength: 4 })).toBe(200 + 160 + 240);
  });

  it("attendance rate ignores cancelled lessons and other months", () => {
    // 2 present / 3 docs of completed November lessons.
    expect(attendanceRate(attendance, sessionsById, NOV)).toBe(67);
    expect(attendanceRate(attendance, sessionsById, OCT)).toBe(100);
  });

  it("six-month income ends at the selected month", () => {
    const series = incomeByMonth(payments, NOV, 6);
    expect(series).toHaveLength(6);
    expect(series[5]).toEqual({ monthKey: NOV, amount: 360 });
    expect(series[4]).toEqual({ monthKey: OCT, amount: 400 });
  });

  it("attendance by group, top absences and slowest payers", () => {
    const byGroup = attendanceByGroup(attendance, sessionsById, groups, NOV);
    expect(byGroup.find((row) => row.groupId === "g1")).toMatchObject({
      present: 2,
      total: 3,
      rate: 67,
    });
    expect(byGroup.find((row) => row.groupId === "g2")?.rate).toBe(0);
    expect(byGroup.find((row) => row.groupId === "g3")).toBeUndefined();

    expect(topAbsences(attendance, sessionsById, students, NOV)).toEqual([
      { studentId: "s1", name: "أحمد", absences: 1 },
    ]);

    expect(slowestPayers(students, balances, payments)).toEqual([
      {
        studentId: "s1",
        name: "أحمد",
        amountDue: 200,
        lastPaymentKey: "2026-11-04",
      },
    ]);
  });

  it("top groups by income adds only the month's payments", () => {
    expect(topGroupsByIncome(payments, students, groups, NOV)).toEqual([
      { groupId: "g1", groupName: "رياضيات", amount: 360 },
    ]);
  });
});
````
