### `test/domain/validation.test.ts`  ·  87 lines

````typescript
import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
import {
  normalizeCount,
  normalizeDateKey,
  normalizeDuration,
  normalizeName,
  normalizePhone,
  normalizePrice,
  normalizeSlot,
  normalizeTime,
} from "@/domain/validation";

describe("normalizeName", () => {
  it("trims and collapses whitespace", () => {
    expect(normalizeName("  Ahmed   Ali  ")).toEqual({
      ok: true,
      value: "Ahmed Ali",
    });
  });

  it("rejects empty and one-character names", () => {
    expect(normalizeName("   ")).toEqual({ ok: false, error: "nameRequired" });
    expect(normalizeName("أ")).toEqual({ ok: false, error: "nameTooShort" });
  });
});

describe("time, duration, slot", () => {
  it("accepts 24-hour wall-clock times", () => {
    expect(normalizeTime("17:00")).toEqual({ ok: true, value: "17:00" });
    expect(normalizeTime("00:05")).toEqual({ ok: true, value: "00:05" });
  });

  it("rejects impossible times and durations", () => {
    expect(normalizeTime("24:00")).toEqual({ ok: false, error: "invalidTime" });
    expect(normalizeTime("9:00")).toEqual({ ok: false, error: "invalidTime" });
    expect(normalizeDuration(0)).toEqual({ ok: false, error: "invalidDuration" });
    expect(normalizeDuration(181)).toEqual({ ok: false, error: "invalidDuration" });
    expect(normalizeDuration("60")).toEqual({ ok: true, value: 60 });
  });

  it("normalises a slot including its weekday", () => {
    expect(normalizeSlot({ weekday: 6, time: "17:00", durationMin: "60" })).toEqual({
      ok: true,
      value: { weekday: 6, time: "17:00", durationMin: 60 },
    });
    expect(normalizeSlot({ weekday: 7, time: "17:00", durationMin: 60 })).toEqual({
      ok: false,
      error: "invalidDate",
    });
  });
});

describe("money and counters", () => {
  it("accepts non-negative integers only", () => {
    expect(normalizePrice("50")).toEqual({ ok: true, value: 50 });
    expect(normalizePrice(50.5)).toEqual({ ok: false, error: "invalidPrice" });
    expect(normalizePrice(-1)).toEqual({ ok: false, error: "invalidPrice" });
  });

  it("treats an empty counter as zero", () => {
    expect(normalizeCount("")).toEqual({ ok: true, value: 0 });
    expect(normalizeCount("12")).toEqual({ ok: true, value: 12 });
    expect(normalizeCount("1.5")).toEqual({ ok: false, error: "invalidCount" });
  });
});

describe("date and phone", () => {
  it("accepts real Cairo date keys", () => {
    expect(normalizeDateKey("2026-11-24")).toEqual({
      ok: true,
      value: "2026-11-24",
    });
    expect(normalizeDateKey("2026-02-30")).toEqual({
      ok: false,
      error: "invalidDate",
    });
  });

  it("allows no phone, or an international number", () => {
    expect(normalizePhone("")).toEqual({ ok: true, value: undefined });
    expect(normalizePhone("+20 100 123 4567")).toEqual({
      ok: true,
      value: "+20 100 123 4567",
    });
    expect(normalizePhone("123")).toEqual({ ok: false, error: "invalidPhone" });
  });
});
````

### `test/emulator/rules.test.ts`  ·  71 lines

````typescript
import { readFileSync } from "node:fs";
import { afterAll, beforeAll, describe, it } from "vitest";
import {
  assertFails,
  assertSucceeds,
  initializeTestEnvironment,
  type RulesTestEnvironment,
} from "@firebase/rules-unit-testing";
import { doc, getDoc, setDoc } from "firebase/firestore";

let testEnv: RulesTestEnvironment;

beforeAll(async () => {
  testEnv = await initializeTestEnvironment({
    projectId: "demo-tutor-manager",
    firestore: {
      rules: readFileSync("firestore.rules", "utf8"),
      host: "127.0.0.1",
      port: 8080,
    },
  });
});

afterAll(async () => {
  await testEnv.cleanup();
});

describe("firestore rules", () => {
  it("lets an owner read and write their own settings document", async () => {
    const db = testEnv.authenticatedContext("alice").firestore();
    await assertSucceeds(
      setDoc(doc(db, "users/alice"), { settings: { cycleLength: 4 } }),
    );
    await assertSucceeds(getDoc(doc(db, "users/alice")));
  });

  it("lets an owner write students, sessions, attendance and payments", async () => {
    const db = testEnv.authenticatedContext("alice").firestore();
    await assertSucceeds(
      setDoc(doc(db, "users/alice/students/s1"), { name: "Ahmed" }),
    );
    await assertSucceeds(
      setDoc(doc(db, "users/alice/sessions/g1_20261124"), { status: "scheduled" }),
    );
    await assertSucceeds(
      setDoc(doc(db, "users/alice/attendance/g1_20261124_s1"), {
        status: "present",
      }),
    );
    await assertSucceeds(
      setDoc(doc(db, "users/alice/payments/p1"), { amount: 200 }),
    );
  });

  it("denies another signed-in user", async () => {
    const db = testEnv.authenticatedContext("mallory").firestore();
    await assertFails(getDoc(doc(db, "users/alice")));
    await assertFails(getDoc(doc(db, "users/alice/students/s1")));
    await assertFails(
      setDoc(doc(db, "users/alice/students/s2"), { name: "Mallory" }),
    );
  });

  it("denies anonymous access", async () => {
    const db = testEnv.unauthenticatedContext().firestore();
    await assertFails(getDoc(doc(db, "users/alice")));
    await assertFails(
      setDoc(doc(db, "users/anonymous/students/s1"), { name: "x" }),
    );
  });
});
````

### `test/integration/ai.test.ts`  ·  125 lines

````typescript
import { readFileSync } from "node:fs";
import { afterAll, beforeAll, describe, expect, it, vi } from "vitest";
import { NextRequest } from "next/server";
import { initializeTestEnvironment, type RulesTestEnvironment } from "@firebase/rules-unit-testing";
import { createUserWithEmailAndPassword } from "firebase/auth";

// The auth emulator mints tokens for the project in .firebaserc; the admin SDK
// must verify against that same audience.
process.env.GOOGLE_CLOUD_PROJECT = "tutor-manager-demo";

const mock = vi.hoisted(() => ({ calls: 0, answering: true }));

vi.mock("@google/genai", () => ({
  GoogleGenAI: class {
    models = {
      generateContent: async () => {
        mock.calls += 1;
        if (mock.calls === 1) {
          return {
            functionCalls: [{ name: "get_dues", args: {} }],
            text: "",
          };
        }
        return {
          functionCalls: [],
          text: JSON.stringify({
            answer: mock.answering
              ? "عليه 200 ج.م"
              : "ما قدرش أجاوب النهاردة",
            proposals: [],
          }),
        };
      },
    };
  },
}));

import { POST } from "@/app/api/ai/assistant/route";
import { getFirebase } from "@/data/firebase/client";
import { createGroup } from "@/data/services/groupService";
import { createStudent } from "@/data/services/studentService";
import { todayKey } from "@/domain/dateKey";

describe("AI assistant route (emulator + mocked model)", () => {
  let testEnv: RulesTestEnvironment;
  let token = "";

  beforeAll(async () => {
    testEnv = await initializeTestEnvironment({
      projectId: "demo-tutor-manager",
      firestore: {
        rules: readFileSync("firestore.rules", "utf8"),
        host: "127.0.0.1",
        port: 8080,
      },
    });
    await testEnv.clearFirestore();

    const { auth } = getFirebase();
    const credentials = await createUserWithEmailAndPassword(
      auth,
      `ai-${Date.now()}@example.com`,
      "password123",
    );
    token = await credentials.user.getIdToken();

    const groupId = await createGroup(credentials.user.uid, {
      name: "رياضيات",
      slots: [],
    });
    await createStudent(credentials.user.uid, {
      name: "أحمد",
      groupId,
      pricePerSession: 50,
      startedAtKey: todayKey(),
      initialCounted: 6,
      initialPaid: 0,
      phone: "01001234567",
    });
  }, 30_000);

  afterAll(async () => {
    await testEnv.cleanup();
  });

  function request(body: unknown, bearer = token) {
    return new NextRequest("http://localhost:3000/api/ai/assistant", {
      method: "POST",
      headers: {
        "content-type": "application/json",
        ...(bearer ? { authorization: `Bearer ${bearer}` } : {}),
      },
      body: JSON.stringify(body),
    });
  }

  it("rejects unauthenticated callers", async () => {
    const response = await POST(
      request({ text: "مين عليه دفعات؟", locale: "ar", clientToday: todayKey() }, ""),
    );
    expect(response.status).toBe(401);
  });

  it("answers from tool results and never includes the parent's phone", async () => {
    const response = await POST(
      request({ text: "مين عليه دفعات؟", locale: "ar", clientToday: todayKey() }),
    );
    const body = await response.json();

    expect(response.status).toBe(200);
    expect(body.answer).toBe("عليه 200 ج.م");
    expect(JSON.stringify(body)).not.toContain("01001234567");
    expect(mock.calls).toBeGreaterThanOrEqual(2);
  }, 30_000);

  it("enforces the daily cap", async () => {
    process.env.AI_DAILY_LIMIT = "2";
    // Two more calls hit the limit configured for this tutor/day.
    await POST(request({ text: "سؤال", locale: "ar", clientToday: todayKey() }));
    const response = await POST(
      request({ text: "سؤال تاني", locale: "ar", clientToday: todayKey() }),
    );
    expect(response.status).toBe(429);
  }, 30_000);
});
````

### `test/integration/attendance.test.ts`  ·  233 lines

````typescript
import { readFileSync } from "node:fs";
import { afterAll, beforeAll, describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
import { collection, doc, getDoc, getDocs, setDoc, type Firestore } from "firebase/firestore";
import { createUserWithEmailAndPassword, signOut } from "firebase/auth";
import { initializeTestEnvironment, type RulesTestEnvironment } from "@firebase/rules-unit-testing";
import { getFirebase } from "@/data/firebase/client";
import {
  attendanceDocPath,
  attendancePath,
  paymentsPath,
  sessionDocPath,
  sessionsPath,
  studentDocPath,
  studentsPath,
} from "@/data/paths";
import {
  mapAttendance,
  mapPayment,
  mapSession,
  mapStudent,
} from "@/data/mappers";
import { createGroup } from "@/data/services/groupService";
import { createStudent } from "@/data/services/studentService";
import { addExtraSession, cancelSession } from "@/data/services/sessionService";
import {
  saveLesson,
  setAttendanceStatus,
} from "@/data/services/attendanceService";
import {
  reconcileCounters,
  type CounterSource,
} from "@/data/services/counterService";
import { corruptCounters } from "@/data/services/devSeedService";
import { todayKey, weekdayOfKey } from "@/domain/dateKey";
import { attendanceId, regularSessionId } from "@/domain/sessionId";
import { DEFAULT_SETTINGS, type TutorSettings } from "@/domain/types";

describe("attendance + counters (Firestore emulator)", () => {
  let testEnv: RulesTestEnvironment;
  let db: Firestore;
  let uid = "";
  let groupId = "";
  const today = todayKey();

  beforeAll(async () => {
    testEnv = await initializeTestEnvironment({
      projectId: "demo-tutor-manager",
      firestore: {
        rules: readFileSync("firestore.rules", "utf8"),
        host: "127.0.0.1",
        port: 8080,
      },
    });
    await testEnv.clearFirestore();

    const firebase = getFirebase();
    db = firebase.db;
    const credentials = await createUserWithEmailAndPassword(
      firebase.auth,
      `attendance-${Date.now()}@example.com`,
      "password123",
    );
    uid = credentials.user.uid;
    groupId = await createGroup(uid, {
      name: "رياضيات",
      slots: [{ weekday: weekdayOfKey(today), time: "17:00", durationMin: 60 }],
    });
  }, 30_000);

  afterAll(async () => {
    await signOut(getFirebase().auth);
    await testEnv.cleanup();
  });

  async function readSource(settings: TutorSettings = DEFAULT_SETTINGS): Promise<CounterSource> {
    const [studentSnap, sessionSnap, attendanceSnap, paymentSnap] =
      await Promise.all([
        getDocs(collection(db, studentsPath(uid))),
        getDocs(collection(db, sessionsPath(uid))),
        getDocs(collection(db, attendancePath(uid))),
        getDocs(collection(db, paymentsPath(uid))),
      ]);
    return {
      students: studentSnap.docs.map((item) => mapStudent(item.id, item.data())),
      sessionsById: new Map(
        sessionSnap.docs.map((item) => {
          const session = mapSession(item.id, item.data());
          return [session.id, session];
        }),
      ),
      attendance: attendanceSnap.docs.map((item) =>
        mapAttendance(item.id, item.data()),
      ),
      payments: paymentSnap.docs.map((item) => mapPayment(item.id, item.data())),
      settings,
    };
  }

  async function newStudent(name: string, initialCounted = 0): Promise<string> {
    return createStudent(uid, {
      name,
      groupId,
      pricePerSession: 50,
      startedAtKey: today,
      initialCounted,
      initialPaid: 0,
    });
  }

  async function newExtra(time: string): Promise<string> {
    return addExtraSession(uid, {
      groupId,
      groupName: "رياضيات",
      dateKey: today,
      time,
      existingIds: (await readSource()).sessionsById.keys(),
    });
  }

  it("saves all attendance, completion and counters in one batch", async () => {
    const a = await newStudent("حاضر");
    const b = await newStudent("غايب");
    const sessionId = regularSessionId(groupId, today);
    await setDoc(doc(db, sessionDocPath(uid, sessionId)), {
      id: sessionId,
      groupId,
      groupName: "رياضيات",
      dateKey: today,
      time: "17:00",
      type: "regular",
      status: "scheduled",
    });
    const result = await saveLesson(uid, {
      sessionId,
      absentIds: [b],
      note: "حصة اختبار",
      source: await readSource(),
    });

    expect(result).toEqual({ countedStudents: 2, newlyDue: [] });
    expect((await getDoc(doc(db, sessionDocPath(uid, sessionId)))).data()).toMatchObject({
      status: "completed",
      note: "حصة اختبار",
    });
    expect(
      (await getDoc(doc(db, attendanceDocPath(uid, attendanceId(sessionId, a))))).data()?.status,
    ).toBe("present");
    expect(
      (await getDoc(doc(db, attendanceDocPath(uid, attendanceId(sessionId, b))))).data()?.status,
    ).toBe("absent");
    for (const id of [a, b]) {
      // One completed lesson; absence counts by default.
      expect((await getDoc(doc(db, studentDocPath(uid, id)))).data()?.sessionsCounted).toBe(1);
    }
  }, 20_000);

  it("reports students who became due in this lesson", async () => {
    const almostDue = await newStudent("قرب يتحق", 3);
    const sessionId = await newExtra("19:00");

    const result = await saveLesson(uid, {
      sessionId,
      absentIds: [],
      source: await readSource(),
    });

    expect(result.newlyDue).toContain(almostDue);
    expect((await getDoc(doc(db, studentDocPath(uid, almostDue)))).data()?.sessionsCounted).toBe(4);
  }, 20_000);

  it("recomputes on status edit and drops counters on cancellation", async () => {
    const present = await newStudent("تعديل حاضر");
    const absentStudent = await newStudent("تعديل غايب");
    const sessionId = await newExtra("20:00");
    const noAbsenceCounts = {
      ...DEFAULT_SETTINGS,
      absenceCounts: false,
    };

    await saveLesson(uid, {
      sessionId,
      absentIds: [absentStudent],
      source: await readSource(noAbsenceCounts),
    });
    expect(
      (await getDoc(doc(db, studentDocPath(uid, present)))).data()?.sessionsCounted,
    ).toBe(1);
    expect(
      (await getDoc(doc(db, studentDocPath(uid, absentStudent)))).data()?.sessionsCounted,
    ).toBe(0);

    await setAttendanceStatus(uid, {
      attendanceId: attendanceId(sessionId, absentStudent),
      sessionId,
      studentId: absentStudent,
      groupId,
      dateKey: today,
      status: "present",
      source: await readSource(noAbsenceCounts),
    });
    expect(
      (await getDoc(doc(db, studentDocPath(uid, absentStudent)))).data()?.sessionsCounted,
    ).toBe(1);

    await cancelSession(uid, sessionId, "ملغية", await readSource(noAbsenceCounts));
    expect((await getDoc(doc(db, sessionDocPath(uid, sessionId)))).data()?.status).toBe(
      "cancelled",
    );
    for (const id of [present, absentStudent]) {
      expect((await getDoc(doc(db, studentDocPath(uid, id)))).data()?.sessionsCounted).toBe(0);
    }
  }, 20_000);

  it("heals a deliberately corrupted cached counter", async () => {
    const studentId = await newStudent("تصحيح عداد");
    const sessionId = await newExtra("21:00");
    await saveLesson(uid, {
      sessionId,
      absentIds: [],
      source: await readSource(),
    });
    await corruptCounters(uid, studentId);
    expect(
      (await getDoc(doc(db, studentDocPath(uid, studentId)))).data()?.sessionsCounted,
    ).toBe(999);

    const changed = await reconcileCounters(uid, await readSource());
    expect(changed).toBeGreaterThan(0);
    expect(
      (await getDoc(doc(db, studentDocPath(uid, studentId)))).data()?.sessionsCounted,
    ).toBe(1);
  }, 20_000);
});
````

### `test/integration/crud.test.ts`  ·  199 lines

````typescript
import { readFileSync } from "node:fs";
import { afterAll, beforeAll, describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
import {
  collection,
  doc,
  getDoc,
  getDocs,
  setDoc,
  type Firestore,
} from "firebase/firestore";
import {
  createUserWithEmailAndPassword,
  signOut,
} from "firebase/auth";
import { initializeTestEnvironment, type RulesTestEnvironment } from "@firebase/rules-unit-testing";
import { getFirebase } from "@/data/firebase/client";
import {
  attendanceDocPath,
  attendancePath,
  paymentsPath,
  sessionDocPath,
  sessionsPath,
  studentDocPath,
  studentsPath,
} from "@/data/paths";
import {
  mapAttendance,
  mapPayment,
  mapSession,
  mapStudent,
} from "@/data/mappers";
import { createGroup } from "@/data/services/groupService";
import {
  enrollStudent,
  moveStudentAndReconcile,
  setStudentActiveAndReconcile,
} from "@/data/services/enrollmentService";
import type { CounterSource } from "@/data/services/counterService";
import { attendanceId, regularSessionId } from "@/domain/sessionId";
import { DEFAULT_SETTINGS } from "@/domain/types";

const DATE_KEY = "2026-11-24";

describe("groups + students CRUD (Firestore emulator)", () => {
  let testEnv: RulesTestEnvironment;
  let db: Firestore;
  let uid = "";
  let groupId = "";
  let secondGroupId = "";
  let studentId = "";

  beforeAll(async () => {
    testEnv = await initializeTestEnvironment({
      projectId: "demo-tutor-manager",
      firestore: {
        rules: readFileSync("firestore.rules", "utf8"),
        host: "127.0.0.1",
        port: 8080,
      },
    });
    await testEnv.clearFirestore();

    const firebase = getFirebase();
    db = firebase.db;
    const credentials = await createUserWithEmailAndPassword(
      firebase.auth,
      `tutor-${Date.now()}@example.com`,
      "password123",
    );
    uid = credentials.user.uid;
  }, 30_000);

  afterAll(async () => {
    const firebase = getFirebase();
    await signOut(firebase.auth);
    await testEnv.cleanup();
  });

  async function readSource(): Promise<CounterSource> {
    const [studentSnap, sessionSnap, attendanceSnap, paymentSnap] =
      await Promise.all([
        getDocs(collection(db, studentsPath(uid))),
        getDocs(collection(db, sessionsPath(uid))),
        getDocs(collection(db, attendancePath(uid))),
        getDocs(collection(db, paymentsPath(uid))),
      ]);

    return {
      students: studentSnap.docs.map((item) => mapStudent(item.id, item.data())),
      sessionsById: new Map(
        sessionSnap.docs.map((item) => {
          const session = mapSession(item.id, item.data());
          return [session.id, session];
        }),
      ),
      attendance: attendanceSnap.docs.map((item) =>
        mapAttendance(item.id, item.data()),
      ),
      payments: paymentSnap.docs.map((item) => mapPayment(item.id, item.data())),
      settings: DEFAULT_SETTINGS,
    };
  }

  it("creates a group and a student with carried-over counters", async () => {
    groupId = await createGroup(uid, {
      name: "رياضيات",
      slots: [{ weekday: 6, time: "17:00", durationMin: 60 }],
    });
    studentId = await enrollStudent(
      uid,
      {
        name: "أحمد",
        groupId,
        pricePerSession: 50,
        startedAtKey: "2026-01-01",
        initialCounted: 3,
        initialPaid: 2,
      },
      {
        students: [],
        sessionsById: new Map(),
        attendance: [],
        payments: [],
        settings: DEFAULT_SETTINGS,
      },
    );

    const snapshot = await getDoc(doc(db, studentDocPath(uid, studentId)));
    expect(snapshot.exists()).toBe(true);
    expect(snapshot.data()).toMatchObject({
      name: "أحمد",
      groupId,
      pricePerSession: 50,
      initialCounted: 3,
      initialPaid: 2,
      sessionsCounted: 3,
      sessionsPaid: 2,
    });
  }, 20_000);

  it("moves a student without rewriting attendance group history", async () => {
    secondGroupId = await createGroup(uid, { name: "إنجليزي", slots: [] });

    const sessionId = regularSessionId(groupId, DATE_KEY);
    await setDoc(doc(db, sessionDocPath(uid, sessionId)), {
      groupId,
      groupName: "رياضيات",
      dateKey: DATE_KEY,
      time: "17:00",
      type: "regular",
      status: "completed",
    });
    await setDoc(
      doc(db, attendanceDocPath(uid, attendanceId(sessionId, studentId))),
      {
        sessionId,
        studentId,
        groupId,
        dateKey: DATE_KEY,
        status: "present",
      },
    );

    await moveStudentAndReconcile(
      uid,
      studentId,
      secondGroupId,
      await readSource(),
    );

    const student = await getDoc(doc(db, studentDocPath(uid, studentId)));
    expect(student.data()?.groupId).toBe(secondGroupId);
    // 3 carried over + 1 completed lesson.
    expect(student.data()?.sessionsCounted).toBe(4);
    expect(student.data()?.sessionsPaid).toBe(2);

    const attendance = await getDoc(
      doc(db, attendanceDocPath(uid, attendanceId(sessionId, studentId))),
    );
    expect(attendance.data()?.groupId).toBe(groupId);
  }, 20_000);

  it("archives a student but keeps the document and counters", async () => {
    await setStudentActiveAndReconcile(
      uid,
      studentId,
      false,
      await readSource(),
    );

    const snapshot = await getDoc(doc(db, studentDocPath(uid, studentId)));
    expect(snapshot.exists()).toBe(true);
    expect(snapshot.data()).toMatchObject({
      active: false,
      sessionsCounted: 4,
      sessionsPaid: 2,
    });
  }, 20_000);
});
````

### `test/integration/payments.test.ts`  ·  159 lines

````typescript
import { readFileSync } from "node:fs";
import { afterAll, beforeAll, describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
import { collection, doc, getDoc, getDocs, type Firestore } from "firebase/firestore";
import { createUserWithEmailAndPassword, signOut } from "firebase/auth";
import { initializeTestEnvironment, type RulesTestEnvironment } from "@firebase/rules-unit-testing";
import { getFirebase } from "@/data/firebase/client";
import {
  attendancePath,
  paymentsPath,
  sessionsPath,
  studentDocPath,
  studentsPath,
} from "@/data/paths";
import {
  mapAttendance,
  mapPayment,
  mapSession,
  mapStudent,
} from "@/data/mappers";
import { createGroup } from "@/data/services/groupService";
import { createStudent } from "@/data/services/studentService";
import {
  recordPayment,
  removePayment,
  updatePayment,
} from "@/data/services/paymentService";
import { computeStudentBalance } from "@/domain/balanceCalculator";
import { todayKey } from "@/domain/dateKey";
import { DEFAULT_SETTINGS, type TutorSettings } from "@/domain/types";
import type { CounterSource } from "@/data/services/counterService";

describe("payments (Firestore emulator)", () => {
  let testEnv: RulesTestEnvironment;
  let db: Firestore;
  let uid = "";
  let studentId = "";
  let firstPaymentId = "";

  beforeAll(async () => {
    testEnv = await initializeTestEnvironment({
      projectId: "demo-tutor-manager",
      firestore: {
        rules: readFileSync("firestore.rules", "utf8"),
        host: "127.0.0.1",
        port: 8080,
      },
    });
    await testEnv.clearFirestore();

    const firebase = getFirebase();
    db = firebase.db;
    const credentials = await createUserWithEmailAndPassword(
      firebase.auth,
      `payments-${Date.now()}@example.com`,
      "password123",
    );
    uid = credentials.user.uid;
    const groupId = await createGroup(uid, { name: "رياضيات", slots: [] });
    studentId = await createStudent(uid, {
      name: "طالب الدفع",
      groupId,
      pricePerSession: 50,
      startedAtKey: todayKey(),
      initialCounted: 6,
      initialPaid: 0,
    });
  }, 30_000);

  afterAll(async () => {
    await signOut(getFirebase().auth);
    await testEnv.cleanup();
  });

  async function readSource(settings: TutorSettings = DEFAULT_SETTINGS): Promise<CounterSource> {
    const [studentSnap, sessionSnap, attendanceSnap, paymentSnap] =
      await Promise.all([
        getDocs(collection(db, studentsPath(uid))),
        getDocs(collection(db, sessionsPath(uid))),
        getDocs(collection(db, attendancePath(uid))),
        getDocs(collection(db, paymentsPath(uid))),
      ]);
    return {
      students: studentSnap.docs.map((item) => mapStudent(item.id, item.data())),
      sessionsById: new Map(
        sessionSnap.docs.map((item) => {
          const session = mapSession(item.id, item.data());
          return [session.id, session];
        }),
      ),
      attendance: attendanceSnap.docs.map((item) => mapAttendance(item.id, item.data())),
      payments: paymentSnap.docs.map((item) => mapPayment(item.id, item.data())),
      settings,
    };
  }

  async function cached() {
    const snapshot = await getDoc(doc(db, studentDocPath(uid, studentId)));
    return {
      counted: snapshot.data()?.sessionsCounted as number,
      paid: snapshot.data()?.sessionsPaid as number,
    };
  }

  it("records a discounted payment and persists the new paid counter", async () => {
    firstPaymentId = await recordPayment(
      uid,
      {
        studentId,
        dateKey: todayKey(),
        amount: 160,
        sessionsCovered: 4,
        note: "خصم",
      },
      await readSource(),
    );

    const payment = await getDoc(doc(db, `${paymentsPath(uid)}/${firstPaymentId}`));
    expect(payment.data()).toMatchObject({ amount: 160, sessionsCovered: 4, note: "خصم" });
    expect(await cached()).toEqual({ counted: 6, paid: 4 });

    const student = (await readSource()).students[0];
    expect(computeStudentBalance(student, await cached(), DEFAULT_SETTINGS)).toMatchObject({
      state: "IN_PROGRESS",
      balance: 2,
    });
  }, 20_000);

  it("editing the amount leaves counters and balance untouched", async () => {
    await updatePayment(
      uid,
      firstPaymentId,
      { amount: 200 },
      await readSource(),
    );

    expect((await getDoc(doc(db, `${paymentsPath(uid)}/${firstPaymentId}`))).data()?.amount).toBe(200);
    expect(await cached()).toEqual({ counted: 6, paid: 4 });
  }, 20_000);

  it("a two-cycle payment makes the student paid ahead; deleting restores the balance", async () => {
    const secondId = await recordPayment(
      uid,
      { studentId, dateKey: todayKey(), amount: 400, sessionsCovered: 8 },
      await readSource(),
    );
    expect(await cached()).toEqual({ counted: 6, paid: 12 });

    const student = (await readSource()).students[0];
    expect(computeStudentBalance(student, await cached(), DEFAULT_SETTINGS)).toMatchObject({
      state: "PAID_AHEAD",
      paidAheadBy: 6,
    });

    await removePayment(uid, secondId, await readSource());
    expect(await cached()).toEqual({ counted: 6, paid: 4 });
    const after = (await readSource()).students[0];
    expect(computeStudentBalance(after, await cached(), DEFAULT_SETTINGS).balance).toBe(2);
  }, 20_000);
});
````

### `test/integration/sessions.test.ts`  ·  161 lines

````typescript
import { readFileSync } from "node:fs";
import { afterAll, beforeAll, describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
import {
  collection,
  doc,
  getDoc,
  getDocs,
  type Firestore,
} from "firebase/firestore";
import { createUserWithEmailAndPassword, signOut } from "firebase/auth";
import { initializeTestEnvironment, type RulesTestEnvironment } from "@firebase/rules-unit-testing";
import { getFirebase } from "@/data/firebase/client";
import { groupsPath, sessionDocPath, sessionsPath } from "@/data/paths";
import { mapGroup, mapSession } from "@/data/mappers";
import { createGroup, updateGroup } from "@/data/services/groupService";
import { ensureGenerated } from "@/data/services/sessionGenerator";
import {
  addExtraSession,
  cancelSession,
} from "@/data/services/sessionService";
import { addDaysKey, todayKey, weekdayOfKey } from "@/domain/dateKey";
import { extraSessionId, regularSessionId } from "@/domain/sessionId";
import type { Group, Session, Weekday } from "@/domain/types";

describe("session generation (Firestore emulator)", () => {
  let testEnv: RulesTestEnvironment;
  let db: Firestore;
  let uid = "";
  let groupId = "";
  let weekday: Weekday = 0;
  let today = "";
  let todaySessionId = "";

  beforeAll(async () => {
    testEnv = await initializeTestEnvironment({
      projectId: "demo-tutor-manager",
      firestore: {
        rules: readFileSync("firestore.rules", "utf8"),
        host: "127.0.0.1",
        port: 8080,
      },
    });
    await testEnv.clearFirestore();

    const firebase = getFirebase();
    db = firebase.db;
    const credentials = await createUserWithEmailAndPassword(
      firebase.auth,
      `sessions-${Date.now()}@example.com`,
      "password123",
    );
    uid = credentials.user.uid;
    today = todayKey();
    weekday = weekdayOfKey(today);
  }, 30_000);

  afterAll(async () => {
    await signOut(getFirebase().auth);
    await testEnv.cleanup();
  });

  async function readGroups(): Promise<Group[]> {
    const snapshot = await getDocs(collection(db, groupsPath(uid)));
    return snapshot.docs.map((item) => mapGroup(item.id, item.data()));
  }

  async function readSessions(): Promise<Session[]> {
    const snapshot = await getDocs(collection(db, sessionsPath(uid)));
    return snapshot.docs.map((item) => mapSession(item.id, item.data()));
  }

  it("generates the horizon once and is idempotent on a second run", async () => {
    groupId = await createGroup(uid, {
      name: "رياضيات",
      slots: [{ weekday, time: "17:00", durationMin: 60 }],
    });

    const firstWrites = await ensureGenerated(uid, {
      groups: await readGroups(),
      sessions: await readSessions(),
      today,
    });
    expect(firstWrites).toBeGreaterThan(1);

    const sessions = await readSessions();
    todaySessionId = regularSessionId(groupId, today);
    expect(sessions.some((session) => session.id === todaySessionId)).toBe(true);
    expect(sessions.every((session) => session.status === "scheduled")).toBe(true);

    const secondWrites = await ensureGenerated(uid, {
      groups: await readGroups(),
      sessions,
      today,
    });
    expect(secondWrites).toBe(0);
    expect((await readSessions()).length).toBe(sessions.length);
  }, 20_000);

  it("never resurrects a cancelled lesson or duplicates extras", async () => {
    await cancelSession(uid, todaySessionId, "اختبار");

    const sessions = await readSessions();
    await addExtraSession(uid, {
      groupId,
      groupName: "رياضيات",
      dateKey: today,
      time: "19:00",
      existingIds: sessions.map((session) => session.id),
    });
    await addExtraSession(uid, {
      groupId,
      groupName: "رياضيات",
      dateKey: today,
      time: "20:00",
      existingIds: (await readSessions()).map((session) => session.id),
    });

    const withExtras = await readSessions();
    expect(withExtras.some((session) => session.id === extraSessionId(groupId, today, 2))).toBe(true);
    expect(withExtras.some((session) => session.id === extraSessionId(groupId, today, 3))).toBe(true);

    expect(
      await ensureGenerated(uid, {
        groups: await readGroups(),
        sessions: withExtras,
        today,
      }),
    ).toBe(0);

    const after = await readSessions();
    expect((await getDoc(doc(db, sessionDocPath(uid, todaySessionId)))).data()?.status).toBe(
      "cancelled",
    );
    expect(after.filter((session) => session.type === "extra").length).toBe(2);
  }, 20_000);

  it("updates a future scheduled lesson when the slot time changes", async () => {
    const futureKey = addDaysKey(today, 7);
    await updateGroup(uid, groupId, {
      slots: [{ weekday, time: "18:30", durationMin: 60 }],
    });

    const writes = await ensureGenerated(uid, {
      groups: await readGroups(),
      sessions: await readSessions(),
      today,
    });
    expect(writes).toBeGreaterThan(0);

    const future = await getDoc(
      doc(db, sessionDocPath(uid, regularSessionId(groupId, futureKey))),
    );
    expect(future.data()?.time).toBe("18:30");

    // Today's cancelled lesson keeps its original time and status.
    const cancelled = await getDoc(
      doc(db, sessionDocPath(uid, todaySessionId)),
    );
    expect(cancelled.data()).toMatchObject({ status: "cancelled", time: "17:00" });
  }, 20_000);
});
````

````typescript
/**
 * Emulator environment for the integration suite. Must run before any module
 * that imports `src/data/firebase/client.ts` (that module snapshots env vars at
 * import time).
 */
process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_USE_EMULATORS = "1";
process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_FIREBASE_API_KEY = "fake-api-key-for-emulator";
process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_FIREBASE_AUTH_DOMAIN =
  "demo-tutor-manager.firebaseapp.com";
process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_FIREBASE_PROJECT_ID = "demo-tutor-manager";
process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_FIREBASE_APP_ID = "1:123456789:web:emulator";
// Server-side AI route talks to the same emulators without real credentials.
process.env.FIREBASE_AUTH_EMULATOR_HOST = "127.0.0.1:9099";
process.env.FIRESTORE_EMULATOR_HOST = "127.0.0.1:8080";
process.env.GEMINI_API_KEY = "fake-test-key";
process.env.GEMINI_MODEL = "gemini-3.5-flash-lite";
process.env.AI_DAILY_LIMIT = "5";
````

````typescript
import { fileURLToPath } from "node:url";
import { defineConfig } from "vitest/config";

export default defineConfig({
  resolve: {
    alias: {
      "@": fileURLToPath(new URL("./src", import.meta.url)),
    },
  },
  test: {
    environment: "node",
    include: [
      "test/emulator/**/*.test.ts",
      "test/integration/**/*.test.ts",
    ],
    // Both suites talk to the same emulator project; never run them in parallel.
    fileParallelism: false,
    setupFiles: ["test/integration/setup.ts"],
    testTimeout: 30_000,
    hookTimeout: 40_000,
  },
});
````
